*Mehdi Heydari Shahna,Seihun Kim,Soyi Jung,Soohyun Park,Jouni Mattila,Joongheon Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对由大语言模型指导的挖掘机的动作执行边界授权机制，通过一次性租约、原子调度和定期监管来确保动作、生成的运动、任务合同和准入容差之间的绑定。实验结果表明该方法能够有效防止未授权的操作，并在撤销过程中减少最大沉积量及降低摆动扭矩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型（LLM）被应用于指导挖掘机等机械设备操作，可能出现机器执行的动作目的地或权限不再符合操作员意图的情况。为解决这一潜在的安全与控制问题，研究提出了一个执行边界授权框架，旨在保证操作指令从批准到执行过程中的安全性与一致性。

Method: 本研究设计了执行边界授权框架，其中包含三个核心组件：1) 一次性租约，将具体动作与其产生的运动、任务合约以及准入容差绑定；2) 原子调度，在每次派遣前重新验证当前权限并检查关节偏移情况；3) 定期监督，用于检测撤销请求并通过一系列参数检查选择合适的停止点。

Result: 实验评估涵盖了672个界面案例、四个负载挖掘场景下的同步模拟测试、无负载条件下的MuJoCo/Pinocchio迁移测试以及两个基于LLM的指挥级规划器。结果显示，在良性案例中所有授权请求均被正确处理且攻击场景下没有发生任何未经授权的派遣行为。加载状态下，撤销期间的最大现场沉积体积显著减少，同时摆动所需扭矩也得到了有效控制。

Conclusion: 提出的执行边界授权机制有效地增强了由LLM驱动的挖掘机操作的安全性和可控性，特别是在面对非预期或恶意指令时能够快速准确地响应以避免潜在风险。

Abstract: An LLM-directed excavator can execute mechanically feasible motion whose destination or authority no longer matches the operator's approval. We propose execution-bound authorization for the approval--dispatch--execution interval. A one-use lease binds the action, generated motion, mission contract and admission tolerance; atomic dispatch rechecks current authority and measured joint drift. Periodic supervision detects revocation and requests a quintic stop with a carry-pitch endpoint, selected through sampled joint, stroke, flow and reference-acceleration checks. The framework connects exact motion identity to dispatch integrity and load-aware interruption. Evaluation combines a 672-case interface suite, four loaded digging cases in synchronous simulation, unloaded MuJoCo/Pinocchio transfers and two command-level LLM planners. The gateway admitted all 120 benign interface cases and produced zero unauthorized dispatches in the attack suite. Loaded dispatch-window tests produced zero unauthorized deposition. During simulated dump-phase revocation, maximum closed-site deposition decreased from 1.23 to 0.33\,m$^3$; loaded-slew stops used less than 75\% of nominal swing torque. These results demonstrate motion-bound authorization and quantify the retention and stopping behavior of supervised excavation skills.

</details>


### [26] [Akule: Fast and Scalable Multi-Robot Motion Planning via Sparse Interaction Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14248)
*Manav Vora,Gokul Puthumanaillam,Aditya Penumarti,Jane Shin,Melkior Ornik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Akule的稀疏交互扩散框架，用于多机器人运动规划。该框架通过三个学习模块增强了一个冻结的单机器人先验模型：一个可重用的成对修正模型、一个上下文选择器和一个组合器。训练过程中无需指定的地面实况交互图。实验表明，Akule在多种MRMP基准测试中达到了竞争性的规划成功率，并且能够显著提高规划速度，支持零样本群体迁移以及处理多达256个机器人的场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对多机器人运动规划（MRMP）领域内扩展扩散模型至大型团队时面临的挑战，即如何在不进行昂贵的联合计算的情况下协调个体动作。

Method: 引入了名为Akule的框架，该框架基于一个固定的单机器人先验模型，并增加了三个学习组件：一个可重复使用的配对校正模型$R_φ$、一个决定哪些校正需要评估的上下文选择器$U_ψ$，以及一个通过依赖于上下文的带符号权重来结合这些效果的组合器$G_ω$。首先使用匹配的配对轨迹优化来训练$R_φ$，然后从物理优化的联合轨迹中学习$G_ω$和$U_ψ$，而不需要预设的真实交互图。

Result: Akule在各种MRMP基准上实现了具有竞争力的规划成功，完整的规划器速度提高了最多25倍，实现了零样本的人口转移，并且能够成功地规划多达256个机器人的任务。消融研究表明，与启发式选择和统一求和相比，学习的选择和组合减少了修复前的冲突和下游修复工作量。

Conclusion: 通过提出的Akule框架，可以在保持高效的同时解决大规模多机器人系统中的运动规划问题，展示了其在加速规划过程、减少冲突及促进复杂场景下有效协作方面的潜力。

Abstract: Diffusion models provide expressive trajectory priors for multi-robot motion planning (MRMP), but scaling them to large teams requires coordinating individual motions without expensive joint computation. We introduce Akule, a sparse interaction diffusion framework that augments a frozen single-robot prior with three learned modules: a reusable pairwise correction model $R_φ$, a contextual selector $U_ψ$ that determines which corrections to evaluate, and a composer $G_ω$ that combines their effects through context-dependent signed weights. This decomposition separates which interactions merit computation from how their effects should be combined into coordinated denoising updates. We train $R_φ$ using matched pairwise trajectory optimizations, then learn $G_ω$ and $U_ψ$ from physically optimized joint trajectories without requiring a prescribed ground-truth interaction graph. The resulting joint proposals are passed to a downstream search-based planner for validation and repair. Across diverse MRMP benchmarks, Akule achieves competitive planning success, complete-planner speedups of up to $25\times$, zero-shot population transfer, and successful planning with up to $256$ robots. Ablations further demonstrate that learned selection and composition reduce pre-repair conflicts and downstream repair effort relative to heuristic selection and uniform summation.

</details>


### [27] [PILOT: Guiding Long-Horizon World-Model Planning for Robot Manipulation with Visual Subgoals and Policy Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14335)
*Yang Liu,Hanchen Cui,Karthik Desingh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的方法PILOT，它通过视频模型生成中间视觉子目标，并利用学习到的策略初始化交叉熵法（CEM），从而在长距离规划中提高机器人操作的成功率和效率。实验表明，该方法在实际机器人任务和模拟环境中都取得了显著的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期规划对于基于潜在世界模型的目标条件机器人操作来说仍然是一个挑战，因为缺乏时间指导和低效的动作搜索。远距离视觉目标为预备动作提供的指导很少，而无信息的动作采样使局部优化变得低效。

Method: 提出了PILOT（Policy-Initialized Local Optimization with Temporal subgoals），使用视频模型来生成中间视觉子目标，并且采用学习得到的策略初始化交叉熵方法(CEM)。CEM通过动作条件下的世界模型，在后退地平线控制循环中朝向每个子目标细化候选动作序列。

Result: 在对四个UR5e操纵任务的实际机器人评估中，PILOT达到了68.8%的成功率，相比之下，没有策略初始化的情况下成功率为55.0%，仅执行策略时为13.8%，直接最终目标规划则完全失败。此外，在较长的任务上，如Push-T和LIBERO-10，PILOT同样表现出色，分别比单纯执行相同策略高出30个百分点和22个百分点。同时，策略初始化加快了局部优化的速度：在Push-T任务上，CEM只需要4次迭代就能达到通用初始化10次迭代后的成本水平，并在同一抽样预算下降低了24.1%的最终规划成本。

Conclusion: 结合时间子目标与策略引导的动作搜索可以同时改善长时间任务执行效果及局部规划效率。

Abstract: Latent world-model planning has shown promising results in goal-conditioned robotic manipulation, but remains challenging for long-horizon planning due to limited temporal guidance and inefficient action search. A distant visual goal provides little guidance for preparatory actions, while uninformed action sampling makes local optimization inefficient. We propose PILOT (Policy-Initialized Local Optimization with Temporal subgoals), which uses a video model to generate intermediate visual subgoals and a learned policy to initialize the cross-entropy method (CEM). CEM refines candidate action sequences toward each subgoal using an action-conditioned world model in a receding-horizon control loop. In our primary real-robot evaluation across four UR5e manipulation tasks, PILOT achieves 68.8% success, compared with 55.0% without policy initialization, 13.8% for policy-only execution, and 0.0% for direct final-goal planning. Simulation results show that these gains persist over longer horizons: PILOT exceeds direct execution of the same policy by 30 percentage points on long Push-T tasks and by 22 percentage points on LIBERO-10. Policy initialization also accelerates local optimization: on Push-T, CEM matches the cost reached by generic initialization after 10 iterations in only 4 iterations and reduces the final planning cost by 24.1% under the same sampling budget. These results show that combining temporal subgoals with policy-guided action search improves both long-horizon task execution and local planning efficiency. Videos are available on the webpage: https://pilot-planner.github.io/.

</details>


### [28] [CoPlanVLM: Coordinated Path Planning for a Robot Team using Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14341)
*David R. Martin,Jared Paquet,Mohammad Abdullah Al Faruque,Pramod Khargonekar,Kyle Volle,J. Humberto Ramos,Scott Nivison*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了CoPlanVLM，它利用视觉语言模型（VLM）将自然语言指令和图像转化为两机器人团队的协调路径。通过改进VLM的空间推理和路径规划能力，CoPlanVLM能够完成复杂的协作操作，并且在Gazebo模拟和实际Turtlebot 4硬件上得到了验证，平均成功率达到了84.4%，比最接近的基线方法高出不少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管视觉语言模型（VLMs）为指挥机器人团队提供了灵活、自然语言界面，但其较弱的空间推理能力大大限制了基于VLM的路径规划应用。为了改善这一情况，研究者开发了CoPlanVLM以增强VLM在空间推理及路径规划方面的能力。

Method: CoPlanVLM通过三个组成部分增强了VLM的空间推理与路径规划能力：(1) 图像叠加层，用于标记自由空间与障碍物；(2) 任务分类机制，根据每个指令选择最适合的低级规划器和推理提示；(3) 针对特定任务的链式思维推理，促使VLM识别关键对象、分配角色并确认路线无碰撞后再制定计划。

Result: 实验表明，在Gazebo仿真环境及TurtleBot 4物理机器人上的测试中，CoPlanVLM取得了平均84.4%的成功率，相较于最相近的基线方法提高了显著。对于两种方法均能成功的情况，CoPlanVLM规划出的路径平均缩短了27%。

Conclusion: CoPlanVLM成功地提升了视觉语言模型处理机器人路径规划问题时的空间推理能力，允许机器人团队执行更加复杂且高效的协同操作。此外，它还能支持涉及具体路线、循环动作以及大面积覆盖等细节操作的任务。

Abstract: We present CoPlanVLM, which uses a Vision Language Model (VLM) to translate a single natural-language instruction and image into coordinated paths for a team of two robots. While VLMs offer a flexible, natural-language interface for commanding robot teams, their weak spatial reasoning has greatly limited VLM-based path planning. CoPlanVLM improves the VLM's spatial reasoning and path planning abilities through three components: (1) an image overlay marking both free space and obstacles, (2) task classification that selects the most appropriate low-level planner and reasoning prompt for each instruction, and (3) task-specific chain-of-thought reasoning that prompts the VLM to identify key objects, allocate roles, and verify routes are collision-free before committing to a plan. Together, these components enable the team to complete complex coordinated maneuvers requiring both robots and divide large tasks between robots to increase efficiency. CoPlanVLM also supports detailed maneuvers involving specific routes, loops, and coverage of large areas, as well as tasks requiring directional reasoning such as north/south or left/right. We validate CoPlanVLM in both Gazebo simulation and on physical Turtlebot 4 hardware. CoPlanVLM attains an average success rate of 84.4% compared to 50.0% for the closest baseline. On trials where both methods succeed, CoPlanVLM's paths are 27% shorter on average. Project code is available at https://github.com/AICPS/CoPlanVLM.

</details>


### [29] [DexFLEX: Contact-Aware Foundation Controller for Command-Guided Dexterity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14372)
*Weikang Wan,Zeyu Lai,Jianyu Hou,Yuchen Zhou,Jiawei Fu,Henrik I Christensen,Hao Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 介绍了DexFLEX，一种接触感知基础控制器，能够将指尖运动草图转化为一致的关节指令，从而提高机器人手操作的成功率和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决灵巧机器人手在执行任务时面临的接触决策问题，即上游命令很少明确指定的问题，研究引入了DexFLEX控制器来增强动作执行的一致性和稳定性。

Method: DexFLEX采用了一种从当前触觉-本体感觉状态出发的方法，提出可行的短期运动片段、预测其接触结果，并选择最佳跟随命令同时保持未来接触稳定性的候选方案。该方法通过简单的“草案-设想-选择”过程结合纯先验建议以实现恢复与响应性，随后解码选定动作为可执行的目标关节空间值。

Result: 实验表明，在模拟环境下的退化命令处理、现实世界的共享控制以及视觉运动策略学习中，相同的训练控制器无需重新训练即可提升鲁棒性，显著提高了实际远程操作成功率（从29.2%增加到78.3%），并达到了直接关节动作预测3.5倍的成功率，比仅使用先验校正提高了1.56倍。

Conclusion: DexFLEX展示了如何通过接触感知控制机制显著改善灵巧机器人手的操作性能，证明了这种方法对于提高复杂操作任务中的执行成功率非常有效。

Abstract: Dexterous robot hands can receive useful motion intent from teleoperation or learned policies, but successful execution also depends on contact decisions that these commands rarely specify. We introduce DexFLEX, a contact-aware foundation controller that turns upstream fingertip-motion drafts into contact-consistent joint commands. Instead of treating a draft as a trajectory to copy, DexFLEX treats it as evidence about intent: from the current tactile-proprioceptive state, it proposes feasible short-horizon motion chunks, predicts their contact consequences, and selects the candidate that best follows the command while preserving future contact stability. At inference time, a simple Draft-Dream-Select procedure combines pure-prior proposals for recovery with draft-seeded proposals for responsiveness, then decodes the selected motion into executable joint-space targets. Across degraded-command simulation, real-world shared control, and visuomotor policy learning, the same trained controller improves robustness without retraining, increasing real-world teleoperation success from 29.2% to 78.3% and reaching 46.7% policy-learning success, 3.5 times direct joint-action prediction, and 1.56 times prior-only correction. Videos are available at dex-flex.github.io.

</details>


### [30] [OATH: Object-Agnostic Tracking for Dexterous Hands, Enabling Rapid Physics-based Retargeting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14379)
*Solvin Sigurdson,Prasanna Sriganesh,Juan Borbon,Joel Burdick,Dingqi Zhang,Jiuguang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于物理的重定向方法OATH，通过将物理重定向重构为残差学习问题，并采用结构化的状态表示来实现对新物体几何形状的零样本泛化。该方法在模拟中实现了比实时快2.4倍的表现，与基于采样的方法相比，计算成本降低了60-350倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的将人类演示重定目标为动态可行的手部轨迹的方法要么忽略了物理约束，要么依赖于计算成本高昂的基于采样的优化。强化学习虽然可以处理接触动力学，但标准策略需要针对特定对象进行训练且无法很好地泛化到任意几何形状上。

Method: 本研究将基于物理的重定向重新定义为一个残差学习问题，通过构造观察空间包括局部接触误差信号来引导每个机器人链接至物体表面上的目标坐标点，使得单一的统一残差策略能够成功抓取新颖、未见过的物体几何形状。这种方法被命名为OATH，在模拟环境中实施时表现出色。

Result: OATH系统达到了每秒参考0.4秒计算量的速度，即比实时快2.4倍的表现，并且相较于基于采样的方法，其计算成本减少了60-350倍。对于保留和未见物体DexYCB轨迹，它分别恢复了58%和54%，同时保持低于2厘米的跟踪误差。

Conclusion: 通过引入一种新的基于残差学习的方法OATH，研究解决了现有技术中关于复杂接触物理处理的问题，显著提高了处理速度并降低了计算成本，同时展示了良好的跨物体泛化能力。

Abstract: Retargeting human demonstrations into dynamically feasible dexterous hand trajectories requires resolving complex contact physics, yet current methods either omit physical constraints or rely on computationally expensive sampling-based optimization in simulation. Reinforcement learning has previously been used to achieve planning and control through contact dynamics; however, standard policies require object-specific training and fail to generalize across arbitrary geometries. This work reframes physics-based retargeting as a residual learning problem, where we demonstrate that zero-shot generalization across objects relies on structured state representations. Specifically, formulating the observation space to include a localized contact-error signal guiding each robot link to its target coordinate on the object surface enables a single, unified residual policy to successfully grasp novel, unseen object geometries. Rolling out this policy in simulation on a kinematic reference trajectory yields a physics-based retargeter, OATH. Operating at 0.4 seconds of compute per second of reference, OATH achieves 2.4x faster-than-real-time performance and a 60-350x reduction in computational cost compared to sampling-based methods. The system recovers 58% of held-out and 54% of unseen-object DexYCB trajectories while maintaining a sub-2 cm tracking error.

</details>


### [31] [Memory Without Curation: Learning Multi-Stream Latent Memory for Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14406)
*Yilin Wu,Rutav Shah,Shuaiyi Huang,Sumedh A. Sontakke,Homanga Bharadhwaj,Andrea Bajcsy,Max Simchowitz,Guanya Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为IMPRINT的新方法，该方法是一种与策略无关的潜在记忆框架，通过多流记忆增强视觉-语言-动作模型，而不需要明确限制策略应记住的内容。IMPRINT在需要记忆的任务中表现出色，提高了至少26%的成功率，并保持了最低的推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人策略虽然利用了长期记忆来执行复杂任务，但直接基于所有过去的观察来制定策略会导致学习到虚假的捷径，并在策略部署时引入较大的延迟。当前的记忆增强型策略往往只保留最近的画面、选定的关键帧或文本摘要，或者仅从视觉流中学习潜在表示。本文旨在提供一种新的解决方案，以解决上述问题。

Method: IMPRINT是一个策略无关的潜在记忆框架，它能够通过多流记忆（包括视觉、语言和动作）来增强视觉-语言-动作模型，而不必明确规定策略应该记住什么。IMPRINT通过逐步压缩不同数据流至一个潜在记忆空间内，在推理过程中实现增量更新，从而达到低延迟的目的。为了避免学习捷径的问题，IMPRINT采用了两阶段训练过程：首先监督记忆重构过去的信息流（如观察、行动、本体感觉）以及估计当前任务进度；然后是端到端微调，与策略一起优化动作预测。

Result: IMPRINT在模拟和真实世界中需要记忆的任务上均显示出了优于基准VLA模型的表现，平均成功率提升了至少26%，同时也超过了其他流行的方法，并且保持了最低的推理延迟。通过详细的消融实验进一步验证了将多样化信息流整合进机器人记忆中的价值。

Conclusion: IMPRINT为增强机器人策略的记忆能力提供了一个有效的新途径，不仅避免了学习捷径的问题，还显著提高了任务完成的成功率并降低了推理延迟。此外，研究表明结合多种类型的数据流对于提高机器人记忆的有效性至关重要。

Abstract: It is widely appreciated that robotic policies require long-term memory to perform complex tasks. However, directly conditioning policies on all past observations leaves policy learning susceptible to learning spurious shortcuts, and introduces large latency at policy deployment. As such, today's memory-augmented policies curate what to remember, either by retaining only recent frames, selected keyframes, or textual summaries, or by learning latent representations of the visual stream alone. In this work, we propose an alternative approach IMPRINT, a policy-agnostic latent memory framework that augments vision-language-action (VLA) models with multi-stream memory without explicitly constraining what the policy should remember. IMPRINT compresses different streams into a latent memory that is updated incrementally at inference, enabling low latency. To avoid shortcut learning, IMPRINT adopts a two-stage training procedure, first supervising memory to reconstruct multiple streams (observations, actions, proprioception) of past information, as well as estimate current task progress, followed by end-to-end finetuning, jointly with the policy, on action prediction. Across both simulation and real-world tasks demanding memory, IMPRINT improves average success over the base VLA by at least 26%, outperforming popular alternatives, and maintaining lowest inference latency among memory-augmented VLAs. Through careful ablations, we demonstrate the utility of incorporating diverse information streams into robotic memory, validating the advantages of foregoing history curation. Project website: https://yilin-wu98.github.io/imprint_website/.

</details>


### [32] [Red-Teaming of Vision-Language-Action Models via Quality Diversity Environment Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14415)
*Shihan Zhao,Henry Chen,Ya-Chuan Hsu,Minjune Hwang,Siddharth Srikanth,Freddie Liang,Akanksha Saran,Aaquib Tabrez,Stefanos Nikolaidis,Daniel Seita*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Q-DEG的方法，通过生成对抗性环境并在此基础上对VLA模型进行微调来提高其鲁棒性。实验表明，该方法在面对分布外的对抗性环境时能够有效提升VLA模型的鲁棒性，并有助于模拟到现实世界的迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在大量机器人演示数据上训练表现出色，但它们往往难以应对测试时细微的变化，比如物体位置、光线、纹理或摄像机角度的变化。为了改善这一情况，研究者们提出了Quality Diversity for Diverse Environment Generation (Q-DEG) 方法。

Method: Q-DEG利用质量多样性(QD)优化技术寻找一组导致高失败率的环境配置，并通过从VLA提取策略嵌入促进这些环境配置间的差异性。随后，通过对自身成功执行案例的学习调整，即自我模仿微调，来缓解暴露出来的脆弱点，从而提高VLA模型的鲁棒性。

Result: 实验显示，在三种代表性VLA模型上的测试中，与基础版VLA及采用两种红队基线收集的数据进行微调后的版本相比，使用Q-DEG方法可以更好地增强模型对于分布外(OOD)对抗性环境的鲁棒性。此外，还证明了通过Q-DEG增强VLA鲁棒性有利于实现从仿真到实际应用的成功迁移。

Conclusion: Q-DEG提供了一种有效的途径来提高VLA模型面对未知挑战时的表现力和适应能力，促进了更广泛的场景下通用型机器人的发展。

Abstract: A long-standing goal in robotics is to develop generalist robots that can perform tasks across a wide range of settings, flexibly specified with images and text. Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models offer a promising path toward this goal by training on large collections of robot demonstrations. However, while VLAs perform well in-distribution, they often fail under minor test-time shifts, such as changes in object positions, lighting, textures, and camera poses. To address this, we propose Quality Diversity for Diverse Environment Generation (Q-DEG), a method for improving VLA robustness by generating adversarial environments and fine-tuning the VLA on its own successful rollouts. Q-DEG uses Quality Diversity (QD) optimization to find a set of high-failure environments, while promoting diversity using policy embeddings extracted from the VLA. These environments expose vulnerabilities that can be mitigated through self-imitation fine-tuning, thereby improving the VLA's robustness. In simulation, we conduct experiments across three representative VLA models, and our results indicate that Q-DEG improves robustness to out-of-distribution (OOD) adversarial environments compared to the base VLA and VLAs fine-tuned using data collected from two red-teaming baselines. Furthermore, we demonstrate that improving VLA robustness through Q-DEG facilitates sim-to-real transfer. Our anonymous project website is at: https://qd-env.github.io/qdeg/.

</details>


### [33] [Shared-Control Teleoperation of Omnidirectional Aerial Robots for Continuous Valve Rotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14478)
*Jinjie Li,Yicheng Chen,Haokun Liu,Moju Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对全方位空中机器人在执行连续旋转任务时遇到的人类前臂运动范围限制问题，提出了一种当人类前臂角度超过某一阈值时切换至连续旋转控制的方法，以增强人机协作下的空中遥操作能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管全方位空中机器人能够在复杂环境中提供6自由度的位置和方向独立控制，但在进行如阀门转动等需要连续旋转的任务时，由于人类操作员的前臂运动范围有限而受到了限制。为了解决这一问题并拓展此类机器人的工业应用潜力，研究者们寻求改进现有系统。

Method: 研究团队提出了一种新的方法：当检测到人类操作员的前臂角度超过了预设阈值时，自动启动机器人的连续旋转模式。这种方法结合了人机交互中的共享控制概念，允许根据实际情况灵活地在人与机器人之间转换控制权。

Result: 通过引入基于前臂角度阈值的控制策略，该方法成功克服了因人体生理限制而导致的操作局限性，使得全方位空中机器人能够完成更加复杂的连续旋转任务。

Conclusion: 这项工作展示了如何通过优化人机交互机制来提高全方位空中机器人在特定任务中的表现，特别是对于那些要求持续旋转动作的应用场景而言具有重要意义。

Abstract: Omnidirectional aerial robots offer full 6-DoF independent control over position and orientation, making them popular for aerial manipulation. Although there have been advancements in robotic autonomy, human operation remains essential in complex aerial environments. Previously, we proposed an aerial teleoperation system that brings the rotational flexibility of human hands into the unbounded aerial workspace, which was evaluated through a vertically mounted valve-turning task. However, due to the limited range of human forearm's motion, the robot could not continuously rotate the valve, hindering its application in industry. In this work, we propose an approach to initiate continuous rotation when the forearm angle exceeds a threshold. This switch of control authority exemplifies the concept shared control from human-robot interaction, marking a further step in aerial teleoperation.

</details>


### [34] [From Field to Planning: Learning Temporal Polar Gradient Fields for Vision-based AAV Trajectory Planning in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14482)
*Haowen Gao,Zhan Chen,Yinuo Xu,Yifeng Niu,Mengyun Wang,Chao Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种两阶段的Field-to-Planning (F2P)学习框架，用于在动态环境中为自主飞行器提供准确且高效的安全梯度以规划轨迹。通过使用自我中心极坐标占用表示和时间极坐标梯度场，并结合ESDF引导的学习策略，在保证实时性和鲁棒性的同时减轻了计算负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自主飞行器轨迹规划方法依赖静态地图和动态障碍物追踪来构建梯度场，这导致了较大的计算负担并产生了过于保守的轨迹。为了克服这些局限性，需要一种能够在动态环境中提供精确、高效安全梯度的新方法。

Method: 开发了一个名为Field-to-Planning (F2P)的学习框架，分为两个阶段：1）第一阶段引入了Ego-centric Polar Occupancy (EPO)表示法，它与机载相机视锥几何自然匹配，支持非结构化场景建模；接着，通过多核可分离高斯卷积创建一个Temporal Polar Gradient Field (TPGF)，无需明确区分静态或动态障碍物。此外，采用ESDF指导的离线学习策略减少极坐标网格中应用卷积带来的空间模糊问题。2）第二阶段则利用校准后的TPGF以自监督方式指导轨迹规划策略。

Result: 与其他方法相比，所提出的方法表现出色。大量的真实世界实验表明，在多种杂乱动态环境下，该方法能够实现实时性能和稳健性，尤其是在资源受限的机载系统上。

Conclusion: 本研究提出的F2P学习框架有效解决了自主飞行器在动态环境下的轨迹规划挑战，不仅提高了安全性梯度估计的效率与准确性，而且确保了算法在实际部署中的可行性。

Abstract: Autonomous Aerial Vehicle (AAV) trajectory planning requires real-time and accurate safety gradient guidance, especially in dynamic environments. Existing methods typically rely on static maps and dynamic obstacle tracking to construct gradient fields, imposing heavy computational burdens and yielding conservative trajectories. To address these limitations, we propose a two-stage Field-to-Planning (F2P) learning framework to provide accurate and efficient safety gradients for trajectory planning in dynamic environments. In the first stage, we introduce an Ego-centric Polar Occupancy (EPO) representation that naturally matches the geometry of the onboard camera frustum, while supporting unstructured scene modeling. Subsequently, we formulate a Temporal Polar Gradient Field (TPGF) via multi-kernel separable Gaussian convolution to provide unified safety gradients, without explicitly distinguishing between static and dynamic obstacles. To mitigate the inherent spatial ambiguity introduced by applying convolutions within the polar grid, we propose an ESDF-guided offline learning strategy that incorporates geometric priors into TPGF. In the second stage, we employ the calibrated TPGF to guide the trajectory planning policy in a self-supervised learning manner. Comparative results with other approaches demonstrate the superiority of our method. Extensive real-world experiments within various cluttered dynamic environments validate the real-time performance and robustness on resource-constrained onboard systems.

</details>


### [35] [Multi-modal Representation Fusion for Long-term Microsurgical Anastomosis Trajectory Anticipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14492)
*Yuxuan Liu,Yating Luo,Yunfei Luan,Xinyao Zhou,Yuyang Zhuge,Yao Guo,Guang-Zhong Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种跨模态特征融合框架，用于长期2D和3D显微手术吻合轨迹预测。该框架结合了手术对象的语义识别与连续运动估计作为多模态视觉线索，并采用由粗到精逐步融合策略来捕捉时间依赖性，从而改善了对手术器械-组织交互特性的理解。实验结果表明，该方法在不同手术场景下均表现出色，优于现有方法，显示出了其在智能机器人辅助及自主手术系统中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的显微手术吻合方法主要依赖于从手术视频和历史轨迹中提取的特征，而忽视了能够为复杂的手术器械-组织交互提供更具辨别力的多模态表示的丰富语义和动作信息。因此，有必要开发一种新的方法，利用这些被忽略的信息以提高手术场景的理解、术中规划、机器人操作以及行为分析的可靠性。

Method: 本文提出的方法包括一个多模态表征提取器和一个跨模态特征融合网络。通过整合手术对象的语义识别与连续运动估计作为补充的空间和时间指导信息，对器械-组织交互进行刻画。此外，引入了一种带有长短时记忆机制的由粗到细渐进式融合策略，逐步捕捉时间上的依赖关系。

Result: 广泛定量与定性实验表明，所提框架在不同手术场景中相对于已有方法展现出了更好的性能与鲁棒性。这不仅证明了其在处理复杂手术环境下的有效性，也突出了其应用于智能机器人辅助及自主手术系统的潜力。

Conclusion: 提出的跨模态特征融合框架成功地提升了2D和3D显微手术吻合轨迹预测的准确性与稳定性，为未来智能化医疗设备的发展提供了新的思路和技术支持。

Abstract: Microsurgical anastomosis is one of the most challenging and sophisticated procedures for surgical robotics, requiring accurate manipulation of surgical instruments within a limited space to achieve precise operations. Accurate surgical trajectory anticipation is essential for surgical scene understanding, intraoperative planning, robotic manipulation, and reliable behaviour analysis. However, existing approaches mainly focus on features extracted from surgical videos and historical trajectories, while overlooking rich semantic and motion information that can provide more discriminative multi-modal representations for complex surgical instrument-tissue interactions. In this work, we propose a cross-modal feature fusion framework for long-term 2D and 3D microsurgical anastomosis trajectory anticipation. The proposed framework consists of a multi-modal representation extractor and a cross-modal feature fusion network. Semantic recognition of surgical objects and consecutive motion estimation are incorporated as multi-modal visual cues, providing complementary spatial and temporal guidance for instrument-tissue interaction characterization. A coarse-to-fine progressive fusion strategy with a long-short-term memory mechanism is introduced to capture temporal dependencies step by step. The training and evaluation are conducted on our established robotic platform and a comprehensive dataset containing both phantom and ex-vivo surgical scenarios is constructed. Extensive quantitative and qualitative experiments demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently outperforms existing methods and exhibits improved robustness across different surgical scenes, highlighting its potential for both intelligent robot-assisted and autonomous surgical systems.

</details>


### [36] [Safety-Critical Quadrotor Navigation Using Fourth-Order Control Barrier Functions with Full Nonlinear Quadrotor Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14522)
*Yanze Liu,Yaşar İdikut,Dhruv Madhusudan Agrawal,Wei Xiao,Guanrui Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于四阶控制屏障函数(CBF)的安全控制器，用于解决安全关键型四旋翼飞行器在复杂环境中的导航问题。该方法直接整合了包括姿态动力学和扭矩级执行在内的全非线性四旋翼动力学，通过高阶积分CBF技术解决了约束公式中的混合相对度问题，从而实现了障碍物避让同时优化控制扭矩。实验表明，该方法能够有效满足约束条件并成功避开障碍物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的分层架构中，高层安全过滤器与单独的姿态控制器之间的分离可能导致平移安全目标与姿态演变之间出现不匹配。为了解决这个问题，研究提出了一个结合了四旋翼全部非线性动态特性的新安全控制器。

Method: 使用第四阶控制屏障函数（CBFs）来构建安全控制器，并采用积分高阶CBF技术处理约束定义中的混合相对度问题。此方法允许安全层直接控制姿态行为，从而消除了参考跟踪安全过滤器固有的层级断开问题。

Result: 在高保真模拟和实际飞行测试中验证了所提方法的有效性，展示了可靠的约束满足能力和成功的障碍物规避表现。

Conclusion: 研究表明，扭矩级别的高阶CBFs为在考虑完整刚体动力学下的四旋翼飞行器提供了更安全的导航路径。

Abstract: Safety-critical quadrotor navigation in cluttered environments typically relies on hierarchical architectures in which a high-level safety filter modifies position references that are then tracked by a separate attitude controller. However, this separation can create a mismatch between translational safety objectives and attitude evolution. To address this gap, we propose a safety controller based on fourth-order control barrier functions (CBFs) that explicitly incorporates the full nonlinear quadrotor dynamics, including attitude dynamics and torque-level actuation. Furthermore, we apply the integral high-order CBF technique to resolve the mixed-relative degree issue in the derived constraint formulation. The resulting formulation enforces obstacle-avoidance constraints while optimizing control torques, granting the safety layer direct authority over attitude behavior and eliminating the hierarchical disconnect inherent in reference-tracking safety filters. We validate the approaches in both high-fidelity simulation and real-world flight experiments, demonstrating reliable constraint satisfaction and successful obstacle avoidance. These results suggest that torque-level high-order CBFs provide a practical pathway to provably safer quadrotor navigation under full rigid-body dynamics.

</details>


### [37] [Who to Trust in Multi-Robot Systems: Joint Robot and Object Hypothesis Trust Inference via Neural-Symbolic Cross-Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14530)
*Ruiyang Wang,Hao-Lun Hsu,Haocheng Meng,Songqiao Xie,Miroslav Pajic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种名为NeST-Bayes的神经符号框架，该框架能够在多机器人系统中仅基于机器人间观测一致性来推断机器人和假设的信任度，而不需要预先设定的信任关系或额外的身份信号。在多种测试场景下，NeST-Bayes表现出色，特别是在面对攻击时依然能够保持较高的准确率与召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多机器人系统合作方案往往依赖于预设的信任关系或侧信道信息，在实际部署中可能不可靠或不可用。为解决这个问题，研究提出了一种新的方法，旨在不依赖这些前提条件的情况下实现可靠的合作。

Method: 提出了NeST-Bayes框架，通过构建以自我为中心的关系图来推理跨机器人的观察一致性，并据此推断出对机器人及其报告假设的即时信任证据分数。这些证据分数随时间积累并通过贝塔分布信任信念的贝叶斯更新进行调整，以维持时间上一致的信任估计。

Result: 实验评估表明，在所有基准设置下，相较于其他基于信任的方法，NeST-Bayes达到了最高的平均物体精度和机器人召回率；在大多数情况下也取得了最好的机器人精度和物体召回率成绩。此外，在遭受强烈优化攻击时仍能保持98.8%的机器人精度（召回率为93.8%）及98.3%的物体精度（召回率为95.4%）。即使是在障碍物丰富的Webots仓库环境中，也能保持良好的表现。

Conclusion: NeST-Bayes提供了一种有效的方式，可以在没有预设信任关系或额外身份信号的情况下提高多机器人系统的合作可靠性与信息覆盖范围。

Abstract: Multi-robot systems (MRS) offer significant advantages over single-robot platforms by enabling information sharing and coordination for different tasks in complex environments. However, reliable cooperation critically depends on inferring trust over both information sources and their reported hypotheses, particularly under partial observability and potential adversarial interference. Existing approaches frequently depend on pre-established trust relationships or side-channel information, which may not be available or reliable in realistic MRS deployments. This work introduces NeST-Bayes, a neural-symbolic framework that jointly infers robot and hypothesis trust using only inter-robot observation consistency, without requiring preassigned robot trust, reputation information, or side-channel identity signals. NeST-Bayes infers instantaneous trust evidence scores for robots and object hypotheses by reasoning over ego-centric relational graphs constructed from cross-robot observation consistency. These evidence scores are accumulated over time through Bayesian updates of Beta-distributed trust beliefs to maintain temporally consistent trust estimates. Extensive evaluations show that NeST-Bayes achieves the highest mean object precision and robot recall across all benchmark settings, and the highest robot precision and object recall in most settings, compared to trust-based methods. Under aggressive optimized attacks, it achieves 98.8\% robot precision with 93.8\% recall and 98.3\% object precision with 95.4\% recall. In an occlusion-rich Webots warehouse, it maintains 92.5\% robot precision with 92.0\% recall and 97.3\% object precision with 85.0\% recall. These results demonstrate joint improvements in the reliability and coverage of retained information.

</details>


### [38] [GG-Nav: Goal-oriented Grounding Chain-of-Thought Elicits Reasoning in Navigation Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14541)
*Shaohao Zhu,Wensi Huang,Siqi Zhang,Yuqiang Yang,Meng Wei,Chenyang Wan,Tianle Liu,Tai Wang,Jinming Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了GG-Nav框架，通过监督微调学习结构化的思维链机制，以改善视觉-语言模型在目标导向导航设置中的性能。实验表明，该方法在多个基准上优于现有技术，并展示了在未见真实环境中的零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前强大的视觉-语言模型在目标导向的导航设置中表现不佳，主要问题在于流式观察利用不足和目标定位不精确。

Method: 设计了名为GG-Nav的导航框架，采用两阶段的结构化思维链（CoT）机制：观察到证据阶段用于从连续帧间提取有用的目标相关信息；证据到决策阶段则结合当前观测与累积证据来指导目标定位及生成导航动作或澄清问题。整个过程通过监督微调实现。

Result: GG-Nav在HM3D ObjectNav、VL-LN以及CoIN基准测试上的成功率分别提高了3.1%、16.6%和5.3%。消融研究表明，从中间观测积累证据可减少11.6%的证据缺失失败，而明确的定位减少了49.4%的停止错误。此外，当部署于Unitree Go2机器人时，展示出了对未知现实环境的良好适应性。

Conclusion: 本研究提出的GG-Nav为解决视觉-语言模型在导航任务中存在的挑战提供了一个有效方案，不仅提升了导航的成功率，还增强了模型处理新环境的能力。

Abstract: Emerging strong Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have advanced navigation foundation models. However, these models still have unsatisfactory performance on goal-oriented navigation settings. In this paper, we first conduct a motivating study revealing that the key issue lies in the insufficient utilization of streaming observations and imprecise target localization. To tackle these challenges, we propose GG-Nav, a simple yet effective navigation framework that learns a structured chain-of-thought (CoT) mechanism via supervised fine-tuning to explicitly localize goal-relevant instances, accumulate grounding evidence, and support more reliable decision-making. Specifically, the structured CoT is designed with two key stages: (i) Observation-to-Evidence, which reasons over all intermediate frames between adjacent decision steps to retain useful goal-related evidence; and (ii) Evidence-to-Decision, which integrates the current observation with accumulated evidence to ground the target and support the generation of navigation actions or clarification questions. Extensive experiments show that GG-Nav outperforms the state-of-the-art methods in terms of success rates on the HM3D ObjectNav, VL-LN, and CoIN benchmarks by 3.1%, 16.6%, and 5.3%, respectively. Ablation studies further show that accumulating evidence from intermediate observations reduces evidence-missing failures by 11.6%, while explicit grounding reduces stopping errors by 49.4%. We also deploy GG-Nav on a Unitree Go2 robot, demonstrating its zero-shot generalization capability in unseen real-world environments.

</details>

*Zihao Li,Yanan Zhou,Ranpeng Qiu,Guoqiang Ren,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CARE（合作行动-响应信念估计）的框架，用于从局部历史中学习团队响应信念。通过预测信念编码器、匹配反应和重放延续以及候选条件读出等技术，CARE能够改善多机器人操作中的动作选择，并在多个实验场景下表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多机器人操作中，一个动作的价值取决于队友如何响应。为了更好地理解和利用这种相互作用，作者开发了CARE框架来学习并预测队友对特定行为的响应方式，从而优化动作选择过程。

Method: CARE框架包括几个关键组件：1) 一个预测信念编码器用来表示互动情况并对基础动作进行条件设定；2) 匹配反应与重放延续分离每个候选动作直接优势与其因队友适应而贡献的效用；3) 候选条件读出模块学习响应分布及总优势；4) 校准选择机制决定是否采用替代方案或保持原动作。此外，该研究还分析了响应改变优选动作的情况以及预测错误对可恢复收益的影响。

Result: 实验表明，在RoboFactory、MARS控制轨道及物理协作任务等多个测试环境中，相比其他策略，CARE实现了最高的平均成功率。特别是在那些需要队友调整才能改变最优决策的情境下，明确响应监督有助于提高候选排序质量并减少选择遗憾。

Conclusion: 结果证明，CARE能够将关于队友适应性的预测转化为有效的分散式动作选择策略，结合了一个强大的基本策略与有选择性的协调调整，在多机器人协作领域展示了显著的应用潜力。

Abstract: In multi-robot manipulation, an action's value depends on how teammates respond. We present \method{} (Cooperative Action--Response Belief Estimation), a framework for learning \emph{team-response beliefs} from local history. A predictive belief encoder represents the interaction and conditions a nominal action. Matched reactive and replay continuations separate each candidate's direct advantage from the utility contributed by teammate adaptation. A candidate-conditioned readout learns this response distribution alongside total advantage, and calibrated selection chooses an alternative or retains the nominal action. Our analysis characterizes when response changes the preferred action and how prediction and source errors limit the recoverable gain. With the representation, proposals, and data fixed, explicit response supervision improves candidate ranking and reduces selection regret relative to learning the same information through branch utilities. The benefit is concentrated in decisions where teammate adaptation changes the preferred action. Across RoboFactory, the MARS Control Track, and physical collaboration tasks, CARE achieves the highest average success among the evaluated policies, combining a capable nominal policy with selective coordination adjustments. Together, these results show that CARE turns predictions of teammate adaptation into effective decentralized action selection. Project page: https://jeong-zju.github.io/care/.

</details>


### [40] [From Video to Vision: Learning Generalizable Humanoid Carrying from Human Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14561)
*Ziluo Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: A novel multi-stage RL framework, MOVER, is introduced for humanoid object carrying, enhancing generalization and eliminating the need for motion-capture systems.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To overcome the limitations of existing methods in humanoid object carrying, which include the need for real-time reference trajectory generation and poor spatial generalization, as well as the restriction to evaluation within a motion-capture studio, aiming to improve the adaptability and practicality of humanoid actions in different scenarios.

Method: MOVER, a Teacher-Assistant-Student reinforcement learning (RL) training framework, uses GENMO and GMR for data retargeting from video data, combines with data augmentation, and follows a three-stage training process. The first stage trains the humanoid to follow specific trajectories, the second relaxes trajectory adherence for varied starting positions, and the third employs Dagger-PPO to remove dependency on global position information, allowing direct perception of objects via an RGB-D camera.

Result: Simulation experiments showed improved success rates across the stages, especially from Stages 1 to 2, and before versus after the distillation process. Additionally, the policy was successfully transferred to a real humanoid, demonstrating its ability to transport objects at varying positions without a motion-capture system.

Conclusion: The proposed MOVER framework, through a multi-stage training process, effectively enhances the spatial generalization of humanoid object carrying tasks and enables policy execution without reliance on motion-capture systems, as demonstrated by simulation experiments and real-world application.

Abstract: Most existing approaches rely on video or motion-capture data for reinforcement learning (RL) training; however, all of these methods require an upper-level module to generate reference trajectories in real time. In object carrying tasks, the humanoid can only lift objects from designated locations along the reference trajectory, resulting in poor spatial generalization. Moreover, existing policies can only be evaluated inside a motion-capture studio. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{MOVER}, a multi-stage, Teacher-Assistant-Student, RL training framework for humanoid object carrying. We first collect data from videos and employ GENMO and GMR to retarget the video data, combining this with data augmentation techniques to generalize the data across objects of varying shapes and sizes. In the \textbf{first stage}, building upon the DeepMimic \cite{peng2018deepmimic} motion-training framework, we train the humanoid to transport objects from specified locations along designated trajectories. In the \textbf{second stage}, we relax the tracking of the reference motion and adjust the reward so that the humanoid learns to transport objects from different positions. In the \textbf{third stage}, we apply a Dagger-PPO distillation method to distill away the proprioceptive position observations and reference joint position of the humanoid. This allows the deployed policy to operate without relying on global position information from a motion-capture system; instead, the humanoid can directly perceive the object's pose from its onboard RGB-D camera to complete the carrying task. We design a series of simulation experiments to compare the success rates across stages (Stages 1 vs. 2) and before versus after distillation. We further transfer the policy to a real humanoid, successfully transporting objects at varying positions in a motion-capture-free setting.

</details>

*Weixi Song,Donglin Wang,Yong-Lu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于块世界的模型强化学习方法，通过一次性前向传递生成假设性滚动预测，并直接优化价值扩展，从而避免了成本高昂且不稳定的BPTT。该方法在19个高维度的人形任务上进行了评估，并应用于现实世界的四足两足行走。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于模型的强化学习方法虽然能够实现较快的学习速度，但都面临着累积误差、推理时延高以及时间反向传播不稳定的问题。

Method: 提出的方法使用块世界模型作为可微训练时评估器来优化动作执行者，无需反复调用模型。通过世界模型和策略的协同块预测，可以在一次前向传递中生成H步假设滚动，并直接优化价值扩展。

Result: 所提方法在HumanoidBench中的19项具有挑战性的高维人形任务上得到了验证，并成功部署到现实世界用于四足机器人的两足行走。

Conclusion: 本研究提出的基于块世界的模型强化学习方法有效提高了样本效率，减少了传统方法中存在的问题，为解决复杂环境下的连续控制问题提供了一个新视角。

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement learning has shown superior sample efficiency by leveraging the learned latent dynamics either in the form of derivative-free planning/imagined rollout training or dynamics gradient-based policy updates. While these approaches achieve comparatively fast learning, they all suffer, to varying degrees, from issues of recurrent compounding errors, high inference-time latency, and instability in backward propagation through time (BPTT). In this work, we propose a model-based RL approach that uses the chunk world model as a differentiable training-time evaluator to optimize the chunk actor without recurrent model invocation. Through the collaborative chunk-wise prediction of the world model and policy, we can generate H-step hypothetical rollouts in a single forward pass and directly optimize value expansion, avoiding the costly and unstable BPTT. We evaluate our method on 19 challenging high-dimensional humanoid tasks from HumanoidBench and deploy our method to the real-world for quadruped bipedal walking. Project Website: https://song-wx.github.io/cmpo.github.io/

</details>


### [42] [In View or Out of View? A Vision-Aware Filter that Infers and Reacts to the Human's Field of View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14577)
*Mengxue Fu,Ya-Chuan Hsu,Yinlong Dai,Erdem Bıyık,Dylan P. Losey*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种视觉感知过滤器，使机器人能够根据人类视野中的可见性调整其行为。通过在线估计和过滤过程，该方法能不断更新对人类视野的理解，并基于此局部调整机器人的动作，从而在协作烹饪和驾驶协调等任务中改善了性能和用户偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人机交互过程中，观察对于理解机器人行为和意图至关重要。现有规划者要么忽略人的视野范围，假设人能看到整个场景，要么设计让机器人始终处于人的视线内。本文旨在让机器人推断出人在交互过程中看到或未看到的内容（即人的视野），并据此调整自身行为以更好地适应人的视觉感知。

Method: 首先，建立一个关于人与机器人互动的模型；然后，采用一种名义上的机器人策略，在短期内预测机器人状态的变化；接着，根据这些动作如何影响机器人被预测的可见度重新分配权重：使得进入（或远离）人类视线的动作获得更高的权重；最后，框架提供了一个在线估计和过滤的过程，持续更新对人类视野的理解，并相应地局部调整机器人动作。

Result: 实验结果表明，在协作烹饪任务中使用视觉感知过滤器时，参与者完成的子任务数量是无过滤情况下的三倍多；而在驾驶环境中，与特意保持在视线之外的汽车相撞的情况也有所减少。

Conclusion: 视觉感知过滤器不仅提高了人机合作任务中的性能指标，还提升了用户体验，表明考虑人类视野范围的人机交互设计具有重要意义。

Abstract: During human-robot interaction, sight plays an important role. When the human sees a robot, they can observe the robot's actions and better predict the robot's future behaviors. Conversely, when the human cannot see the robot, they become uncertain about the robot's intent and plans. In this paper, we focus on the human's field of view. Existing human-aware planners either ignore this field of view and assume the human can observe the entire scene, or intentionally design their interactions so the robot is always visible to the human. By contrast, we enable robots to infer what the human has and has not seen during interaction, i.e., the human's field of view. We then apply this estimate as a vision-aware filter on the robot's actions. Given a nominal robot policy and a model of the interaction, we roll out the policy over a short horizon to see how the robot's state updates. We then reweight the actions based on how those actions impact the robot's predicted visibility: higher weights are assigned to actions that bring the robot into (or away from) the human's line of sight. In practice, our framework provides an online estimation and filtering process that repeatedly updates its understanding of the human's field of view and then locally perturbs robot actions based on the human's vision. We evaluate our vision-aware filter on robots that assist humans in collaborative cooking and coordinate with them in driving environments. Across simulations and a 12-participant user study, we find that our vision-aware filter changes both performance and preference: participants completed over three times as many subtasks with in-view assistance while cooking, and had fewer collisions with cars that purposely stayed out of their view while driving.

</details>


### [43] [Remembering What Matters for Long-Horizon Robotic World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14583)
*Hao Wu,Tianyu Shao,Shengju Qian,Yizhou Zhao,Fan Zhang,Penghao Zhao,Weiyan Wang,Yufei Huang,Fan Xu,Kun Wang,Xin Wang,Ming-Hsuan Yang,Yuxuan Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为ReCAP（保留锚点的回退上下文）的方法，用于改善长时序机器人视频世界模型的预测质量。该方法通过保持初始场景锚点和最近完整的视觉-动作块窗口，同时剔除旧生成的历史，来解决在全上下文解码中由于不同预测角色的信息竞争注意力而导致的问题。实验表明，ReCAP在多个基准测试上提高了视觉保真度、条件一致性以及计算效率，且无需重新训练模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归机器人视频世界模型基于离散视觉令牌，在长时间序列预测时表现不稳定。研究者发现，保存整个生成历史并不一定能够提高预测质量，因为在全上下文解码过程中，持久场景信息、最近的视觉-动作状态与陈旧的生成历史之间存在记忆角色冲突，这影响了模型性能。

Method: 提出了ReCAP（Receding Context with Anchor Preservation），这是一种无需额外训练的上下文调度方法。ReCAP维持预训练的分词器、世界模型和视觉解码器不变，通过保留初始场景锚点及一系列最新的完整视觉-动作区块，并去除较旧的生成内容，以优化长期预测。这种方法保持了区块内部结构及时序顺序，不需引入新模块或参数更新，还能独立于展开长度限制活跃上下文的增长。

Result: 通过对RT-1、BridgeV2、LIBERO-90和CALVIN等数据集上的冻结自回归世界模型进行评估，结果表明ReCAP相对于全上下文处理方式，在视觉逼真度、动作条件一致性以及机器人结构保持方面有所提升。特别是在BridgeV2上LPIPS降低了63.3%，系统基准测试（H=64）中逻辑KV占用减少了73.3%、p50延迟降低了6.51%。注意力分析显示对保留的近期状态给予了更多关注，而干预实验则揭示了大量陈旧上下文与更高预测误差之间的关联。

Conclusion: 研究表明，通过在推理阶段调整上下文安排，可以作为一种实用方法来提高长时序机器人视频预测的质量和计算效率，而不需要重新训练模型。

Abstract: Autoregressive robot video world models based on discrete visual tokens remain unreliable over long rollout horizons. Retaining the entire generated history does not necessarily improve prediction quality. We investigate a possible memory-role conflict in Full Context decoding: persistent scene information, recent visual-action states, and stale generated history compete for attention despite their different predictive roles. This perspective motivates ReCAP (Receding Context with Anchor Preservation), a training-free context-scheduling method for long-horizon robot video world models. ReCAP keeps the pretrained tokenizer, world model, and visual decoder frozen. It preserves the initial scene anchor and a contiguous window of recent complete visual-action blocks while evicting older generated history. The schedule preserves within-block structure and temporal order, requires no additional modules or parameter updates, and bounds active-context growth independently of rollout length. We evaluate ReCAP on RT-1, BridgeV2, LIBERO-90, and CALVIN using frozen autoregressive world models. Across evaluated environments and horizons, ReCAP improves visual fidelity, action-conditioned consistency, and robot structure preservation relative to Full Context. On BridgeV2, it reduces LPIPS by 63.3%. In the system benchmark at H=64, it reduces the logical KV footprint by 73.3% and p50 latency by 6.51%. Attention analyses show increased attention to retained recent states, while history interventions associate larger amounts of stale context with higher prediction error. These observations are consistent with the memory-role-conflict hypothesis, although they do not isolate it from context-length and position-handling effects. Our results support inference-time context scheduling as a practical approach to improving long-horizon robot video prediction and computational efficiency without retraining.

</details>


### [44] [PC7 Model: Dynamics of Expanding and Contracting Soft Robots using Piece-wise Constant 7D Deformation Based on Similarity Transformation Group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14586)
*Yamato Kudo,Taiki Ishigaki,Ko Ayusawa,Ko Yamamoto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为分段常数7D变形（PC7）模型的新软体机器人模型，该模型能够表示例如人工肌肉执行器的膨胀和收缩变形。通过引入SIM(3)变换群中的缩放参数，新定义了7D变形向量，从而扩展了先前基于SE(3)变换群提出的分段常数应变（PCS）模型的功能。我们还基于欧拉-庞加莱方程推导了PC7模型的动力学方程，并通过动态仿真验证了该模型的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更准确地描述软体机器人中的人工肌肉执行器等组件在膨胀和收缩时的变形情况，研究者们希望超越现有的基于SE(3)变换群的PCS模型限制，利用SIM(3)变换群来更好地捕捉这些特性。

Method: 本研究首先定义了一个新的7维变形向量，其中新增的一维用于表达由SIM(3)中的缩放参数所代表的膨胀与收缩变形。接着，利用SE(3)与SIM(3)之间共有的数学性质，将PCS模型下的运动学及动力学理论直接拓展到了PC7模型上，并严格依据欧拉-庞加莱方程推导出了适用于PC7模型的动力学方程。

Result: 通过假设薄圆柱结构或McKibben型人工肌肉执行器进行的动态仿真表明，所提PC7模型能有效模拟此类软体结构的膨胀收缩行为。

Conclusion: PC7模型为描述软体机器人的复杂变形提供了一个有力工具，特别是在处理涉及体积变化的情形时。这标志着从传统刚性建模方法向更加灵活多样的软体机器人建模方式转变的重要一步。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel soft robot model named piece-wise constant 7D deformation (PC7) model that can represent expanding and contracting deformation of, for example, artificial muscle actuator. In the proposed model, we newly define the 7D deformation vector based on SIM(3) - 3D similarity transformation group - whereas a 6D deformation vector was defined for Cosserat rod based on SE(3) - the special Euclid group - in the piece-wise constant strain (PCS) model proposed in the previous study. The additional dimension of the 7D deformation vector represents expanding and contracting deformation, which comes from the scaling parameter in SIM(3). Common mathematical properties between SE(3) and SIM(3) allow us to directly extend the kinematics and dynamics of the PCS model to those of the PC7 model. In particular, we derive the dynamical equation of the PC7 model based on Euler-Poincare equation in a mathematically precise manner. We validate the proposed model through dynamical simulations assuming a thin cylinder structure or McKibben-type artificial muscle actuator.

</details>


### [45] [Flow-Support-Weighted Proposals for Test-Time Verified VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14588)
*Chen Zhao,Zipeng Ye,Haitao Shen,Guanlin Li,Yuchen Xie,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为行动动态采样（ADS）的方法，旨在解决测试时采样产生的候选动作高度冗余的问题。通过在训练过程中整合额外的轨迹并调整策略的动作分布，ADS能够在不增加部署成本的情况下提高提案的多样性，并在模拟和实际机器人任务中显示出性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时采样本应提供一组可由验证者选择的不同执行候选方案，但下游微调会导致模型生成的动作预测变得过于集中，使得多次采样的结果非常相似，限制了多样性。这种现象被称为提案收缩，是测试时扩展的关键约束之一。

Method: 提出了Action Dynamic Sampling (ADS) 方法，在训练期间每个步骤集成K条来自当前策略的新基噪声轨迹，使用Hutchinson迹估计器对每条路径进行评分，并通过分离的有限集KL项修正策略的局部动作分布朝向加权集合。该方法增加了训练成本但保持了部署成本不变。

Result: ADS方法在模拟环境及四个物理机器人任务上进行了评估，结果显示它能够提高提案的多样性，同时避免了高斯扰动带来的成功率下降问题；在RoboCasa基准上的最佳8选成功率从57.6%提升到了63.0%，对于固定验证者在模拟环境中提升了2.4至4.9个百分点的表现，在真实世界操作中TokenPB指标最高提升了13.0个百分点。

Conclusion: ADS为解决测试时采样产生的动作候选方案高度冗余问题提供了有效途径，通过增强提案多样性来改善整体系统表现，且适用于不同场景下的应用。

Abstract: Test-time sampling promises to turn one action prediction into a set of distinct executable candidates that a verifier can choose among. Downstream fine-tuning works against that promise. A vision-language-action model trained to solve one task distribution concentrates its flow-matching decoder around the dominant demonstrated action, so repeated samples differ only marginally and the verifier receives many copies of one decision. We call this proposal contraction and identify it as a key constraint on test-time scaling: repeated samples can remain highly redundant as the candidate budget grows. Action Dynamic Sampling (ADS) alleviates the constraint on the proposal side, during training. At each training step, ADS integrates $K$ additional trajectories from the current policy under fresh base noise, scores each by the flow support along its path with a Hutchinson trace estimator, and corrects the policy's local action distribution toward this weighted set through a detached finite-set KL term. Training cost rises; deployment cost is unchanged. Across simulation and four physical-robot tasks, ADS raises proposal spread without the success penalty that Gaussian perturbation incurs, lifts oracle best-of-8 success on RoboCasa from 57.6% to 63.0%, improves fixed verifiers by 2.4 to 4.9 points in simulation, and raises TokenPB by up to 13.0 points on real-world manipulation.

</details>

*Xu Yang,Wenhao Li,Zhengyu Wei,Zening Li,Longxiang Huang,Bo Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

Method: 本文提出了ToF-轮式里程计(TWO)，这是一种紧密耦合的滤波框架，它将轮子运动学与来自飞行时间(ToF)相机的几何约束相结合。主要贡献是一种退化感知、方向协方差成形策略，该策略用基于几何条件的膨胀取代了启发式的在线协方差调整。核心思想是从严格的可靠性检查中的几何校正推断过程噪声。

Result: 广泛的实验表明，在具有挑战性的场景中，TWO相比静态和自适应高斯滤波提高了轨迹精度和鲁棒性，稳健地处理了运动学可靠性和有限传感能力之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 通过引入一种新的基于ToF相机数据和轮式里程计信息融合的方法，TWO能够更准确地估计机器人的位置，并且在面对环境变化时展现出更高的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Wheel odometry provides an efficient motion prior for ground robots but is prone to transient violations such as wheel slip or terrain-induced tilt. In filtering-based odometry, these violations frequently trigger divergence when they coincide with constrained geometric observability (e.g., featureless corridors). We address this challenge by proposing ToF-Wheel Odometry (TWO), a tightly-coupled filtering framework that integrates wheel kinematics with geometric constraints from Time-of-Flight (ToF) cameras. The primary contribution of this work is a degeneracy-aware, directional covariance-shaping strategy that replaces heuristic online covariance tuning with geometry-conditioned inflation. The core idea is to infer process noise from geometric corrections with a strict reliability check. We decompose the posterior-to-prior correction into a wheel-explainable subspace and an orthogonal remainder. The process covariance is anisotropically adjusted according to the observability of the ToF update: if the geometry is degenerate in non-kinematic directions, the remainder is suppressed as sensor noise; if the geometry is distinct, it is admitted as a valid kinematic violation. Additionally, we introduce an intra-frame feedback loop that retrospectively repairs the process covariance within the current frame to reduce structural lag during bursty disturbances. Extensive experiments on challenging scenarios demonstrate that TWO improves trajectory accuracy and robustness compared to static and adaptive Gaussian filtering, robustly navigating the trade-off between kinematic reliability and limited sensing capabilities.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 本研究采用两阶段方法：第一阶段，利用强化学习训练LOADNav，这是一种能够根据携带物体几何尺寸自适应调整路径和避障行为的负载感知导航策略；第二阶段，通过模仿学习训练CoGE，在线几何预测器从偶尔发生的接触事件中估计未知负载的几何形状，并据此调整导航行为。

Result: 实验结果表明，提出的导航策略展示了明显的负载感知行为，同时在线几何预测器能有效从接触事件中估计物体几何形状并据此细化估计值，进而调整导航行为。无需事先了解负载信息的情况下，该方法缩小了与几何感知策略之间的差距。

Conclusion: 这项工作证明了结合负载感知导航策略与在线几何预测可以显著改善四足机器人在携带物品穿越拥挤环境时的表现，提高了导航系统的灵活性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Navigating cluttered environments while carrying objects remains a challenging problem for quadruped robots. Compared with works that focused on load-aware locomotion adaptation to maintain gait stability, existing autonomous navigation systems generally ignore the influence of carried objects' geometries and use fixed footprint inflation, leading to overly conservative navigation. To fill this knowledge gap, we propose a two-phase load-aware navigation learning framework. In Phase 1, we train LOADNav, a load-aware navigation policy via reinforcement learning, enabling the robot to adapt its trajectory and clearance behavior according to the geometric size of the carried object. In Phase 2, we train CoGE, an online geometry predictor via imitation learning, which estimates unknown payload geometry from occasional contact events. We evaluate the framework in cluttered environments with loads of varying sizes and shapes. The results show that our navigation policy exhibits clear load-aware behaviors, and the online geometry predictor estimates object geometry from occasional contact events, refining its estimate and adapting the navigation behavior accordingly. Without requiring any prior knowledge of the payload, it narrows the gap to geometry-aware policies.

</details>


### [48] [StateSkill-Agent: An Effective and Efficient Embodied Agent via State-conditioned Skill](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14601)
*Changxu Wei,Jingyi Zhang,Zhiyu Zheng,Tianyi Lin,Yixian Zhang,Yunpeng Qing,Jingyuan Liu,Zhi Hou,Jiaxing Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为StateSkill的新技能表示方法，通过将有限状态机的概念引入到具身代理的技能建模中，以实现有效且高效的技能构建与学习。此外，还开发了StateSkill-Evolve机制和StateGRPO框架，旨在不断优化技能并通过实验验证了所提方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了使具身多模态语言模型（MLLM）代理能够理解并解决具身任务，本文致力于开发一种新的技能表示方式，它可以通过学习、抽象以及重用可转移且泛化的行动模式来达成这一目标。

Method: 提出了StateSkill，这是一种基于有限状态机概念的新技能表示法，用于组织围绕一系列任务进展状态、状态转换及状态条件指导的行为。进一步开发了StateSkill-Evolve机制，该机制通过对新成功与失败的状态引导轨迹进行回顾性分析来迭代地改进StateSkill。最后，介绍了StateGRPO，它将StateSkill进化与通过状态条件GRPO的模型优化集成到了一个统一的学习循环中。

Result: 广泛的实验表明，在多种具身任务上，所提出的方法及其训练得到的模型（即StateSkill-Agent）均表现出色。

Conclusion: 研究结果证明了StateSkill作为一种新型技能表示的有效性，同时展示出通过StateSkill-Evolve和StateGRPO框架持续优化技能的可能性，为未来在更复杂环境中提升具身代理能力的研究开辟了道路。

Abstract: In this work, we aim to develop an embodied MLLM agent that understands and solves embodied tasks by learning, abstracting, and reusing transferrable and generalizable behavioral patterns. To this end, we propose StateSkill, a new skill representation for embodied agents, which introduces the concept of "finite-state machines" into skill modeling for effective and efficient skill construction and learning. The core idea of StateSkill is to model embodied interaction experiences as a finite-state machine, where a skill is organized around a set of task-progress states, state transitions, and state-conditioned guidance, enabling embodied agents to dynamically adjust their skills as the task progresses while keeping skill contexts compact and stage-relevant. We further develop StateSkill-Evolve, an experience-driven evolution mechanism that iteratively refines StateSkill through retrospective analysis of new successful and failed state-guided trajectories. Upon this, we introduce StateGRPO, which integrates StateSkill evolution and model optimization via state-conditioned GRPO into a unified iterative learning loop, enabling state-conditioned skills and model capabilities to co-evolve and reinforce each other. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed methods and trained model (i.e., StateSkill-Agent) across various embodied tasks.

</details>


### [49] [AnyRank: Reliable Anytime Policy Ranking via Sequential Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14603)
*Apurva Badithela,David Snyder,Tenny Yin,Jade Sceats,George Pappas,Nikolai Matni,Masha Itkina,Haruki Nishimura,Anirudha Majumdar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为AnyRank的顺序方法，用于多策略排序。该方法能够自适应地为难以区分的策略分配试验次数，可以在任何时间点提供部分排序，并可选择性地利用先验信息来更高效地进行硬件评估。实验表明，与现有统计严格的方法相比，AnyRank最多可以减少50%的硬件评估需求，同时避免了当前基准测试中启发式方法可能引起的错误排序问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于硬件实验成本高昂，机器人策略主要通过有限次数的硬件试验来评估其性能。然而，样本数量有限导致的统计不确定性可能会导致错误结论，尤其是在使用固定批次的数据对多个策略进行排名时。为此，本文提出了一种新的方法解决这一挑战。

Method: 提出了AnyRank，一种针对多策略排序的顺序方法，它能根据需要自适应地向难以区别的策略分配试验；在任意停止时刻提供（部分）排序的同时保证了错误排序的概率界限；并且可以选择性地利用来自专家直觉、少量预留硬件评估结果或模拟器中的策略执行等来源的先验信息以提高效率。即使这些先验信息不完全准确，也不会影响到AnyRank提供的统计保障。

Result: 仿真和真实世界策略评估基准测试显示，相较于其他统计严谨的方法，AnyRank能够减少高达50%的硬件评估需求，同时还避免了现有基准测试所使用的启发式方法可能导致的错误排序。

Conclusion: AnyRank通过结合序列测试与多重假设图形测试方法的新理论贡献实现了其特性，不仅提高了硬件评估效率，还增强了评估过程中的可靠性。

Abstract: Due to the high cost of hardware experiments, robot policies are predominantly evaluated by comparing their empirical performance on a limited number of hardware trials. However, finite-sample statistical uncertainty can lead to erroneous conclusions, especially when ranking multiple policies using a fixed batch of evaluation data. We propose AnyRank: a sequential method for multi-policy ranking that (i) adaptively allocates trials to hard-to-separate policies, (ii) provides (partial) rankings at any stopping time while bounding the probability of an incorrect ranking, and (iii) optionally exploits prior information to prioritize hardware evaluations more efficiently. This prior information could be informed from expert intuition, a small set of held-out hardware evaluations, or from policy rollouts in a simulator or an action-conditioned video world model. Notably, the prior can be imperfect; while a strong prior can provide efficiency gains on hardware, any inaccuracies in prior policy rankings do not invalidate the statistical assurances of AnyRank. We achieve the aforementioned properties through novel theoretical contributions that combine sequential testing with multiple-hypothesis graphical testing approaches. Experiments in simulation and real-world policy evaluation benchmarks show that AnyRank requires up to 50% fewer hardware evaluations compared to existing statistically rigorous approaches, while guarding against the incorrect rankings produced by heuristic methods used by state-of-the-art benchmarks.

</details>


### [50] [VbotNav-0: Asynchronous Cloud-Edge Dual-Policy Navigation across Indoor and Outdoor Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14634)
*Shiqi Tan,Yujun Zhang,Keyu Li,Shuaixi Pan,Siyu Zhou,Hailong Qin,Yinan Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为VbotNav-0的云端-边缘双策略框架，它通过异步策略间条件作用来连接目的地级推理与基于当前观察的移动决策。该系统在258个跨145个场景的闭环评估中达到了55.04%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续的室内外导航需要将目标级别的推理与必须响应当前观测值的动作决策相结合。为了实现这一目的，作者提出了一个能够处理视觉历史、导航指令和路径令牌以生成子目标指导，并结合即时RGB和LiDAR观测数据生成局部轨迹的框架。

Method: VbotNav-0采用了云-边缘双策略架构，其中云端策略根据视觉历史、导航说明和路线标记提供子目标指引；而边缘端策略则综合可用的引导信息以及实时RGB图像和LiDAR观测结果来产生局部运动轨迹。整个训练过程融合了导航演示、校正数据及基于视觉的问题解答（VQA）。

Result: 在覆盖145个不同场景的258次试验中，VbotNav-0实现了55.04%的任务成功率，按路径长度加权后的成功率为55.03%。

Conclusion: VbotNav-0通过引入一种新颖的云-边缘协作机制，在一定程度上解决了连续室内外导航中的挑战，尽管其表现还有改进空间，但已经显示出对于实际应用的良好潜力。

Abstract: Continuous indoor-outdoor navigation connects destination-level reasoning with motion decisions that must respond to current observations. We present VbotNav-0, a cloud-edge dual-policy framework that links these processes through asynchronous policy-to-policy conditioning. The cloud policy produces subgoal guidance from visual history, navigation instructions, and route tokens; the edge policy combines available guidance with current RGB and LiDAR observations to generate local trajectories. This formulation allows the local perception-action loop to continue between cloud updates. A common route representation connects indoor paths and outdoor map routes, and the training recipe combines navigation demonstrations, corrective data, and grounded visual question answering (VQA). In the reported closed-loop evaluation of 258 episodes across 145 scenes, VbotNav-0 achieves 55.04% success rate and 55.03% success weighted by path length.

</details>


### [51] [Global Optimality of Pose Graph Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14655)
*Shuan Ding,Ruishuang Chen,Mengfan Cao,Zaiyue Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了位姿图优化(PGO)的优化景观，提出了一种在噪声小于某一明确界限时保证全局最优性的充分条件。通过建立一个适当的吸引盆(BOA)，分析其几何特性，并结合PGO的一阶最优性条件，证明当噪声低于某明确界限时，在BOA内存在唯一的局部极小值且该极小值为全局最优。数值模拟验证了所提理论的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 位姿图优化(PGO)是机器人状态估计中的经典问题，对定位、建图和导航至关重要。然而，由于其本质上是非凸的，可能存在许多局部极小点，这使得分析变得困难。本研究旨在理论上证实，如果噪声小于某个可事先确定的界限，则优化景观在全局最小值周围是有利且有吸引力的。

Method: 首先定义了一个包含全局最小值的适当吸引盆(BOA)，并展示了其半径如何受噪声影响；接着，通过探讨PGO的一阶最优性条件，证明了满足特定噪声控制标准的局部最小值即为全局最优；最后，综合以上两点结果，证明当噪声低于某个明确且可事先验证的界限时，在BOAO中仅存在一个局部最小值，且这个最小值就是全局最优解。

Result: 研究表明，在给定条件下，即使面对非凸优化问题如PGO，也能够通过控制噪声水平来确保找到全局最优解。此外，数值仿真表明提出的条件确实可以作为有效准则用于验证PGO全局最优性。

Conclusion: 本论文为位姿图优化提供了一套新的理论框架，用以理解和保证其全局最优性，特别是在低噪声环境下。所开发的方法不仅加深了我们对PGO优化景观的理解，也为实际应用中解决相关问题提供了有用的指导原则。

Abstract: Pose Graph Optimization (PGO) is a classical and widely applied problem in state estimation for robotics, playing a fundamental role in localization, mapping, and navigation. Yet, it is nonconvex in nature and may have many local minimizers, thus difficult to analyze. In this paper, we investigate the optimization landscape and establish a sufficient condition of global optimality for PGO. The ultimate goal of this paper is to theoretically confirm that the optimization landscape is benign and attractive around the global minimizer, if noise is smaller than an explicit and a priori bound. To reveal such an insight, this paper develops three main technical results. First, we identify a proper basin of attraction (BOA) containing global minimizer, and characterize its geometric property by showing that its radius is controlled by noise in an explicit way. Then, by exploring the first order optimality conditions of PGO, we prove that a local minimizer is globally optimal if a noise-controlled criterion is satisfied. Finally, by combining above two results, we prove that one and only one local minimizer exists in the BOA and this minimizer is globally optimal, when the noise is smaller than an explicit bound that can be verified a priori. Numerical simulations validate the proposed theory under various random cases, showing that the obtained conditions provide an effective criterion for certifying the global optimality of PGO.

</details>


### [52] [Successful but Unsafe: An Analytical Benchmark for Revealing Collisions in Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14682)
*Hieu D. Pham,Quoc-An Luong,Dung D. Le*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究引入了VLN-Safe基准，用于在滑动和阻挡两种评估机制下分析碰撞安全。研究发现，在R2R-CE上测试的六个已发布代理中，切换到阻挡机制后成功率下降了11.7至36.1个百分点。基于这些发现，提出了一种无需训练的碰撞防护措施，可以减少碰撞步骤并提高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言导航(VLN)评价标准主要关注目标达成及路线遵循效率，但很少考虑碰撞行为的影响。此外，常用的滑动机制可能掩盖了不安全动作对导航表现的真实影响。因此，需要一种新的方法来更直接地衡量和改善导航中的安全性问题。

Method: 通过建立名为VLN-Safe的新基准，该研究在滑动（允许沿障碍物滑行）与阻挡（阻止因碰撞导致的前进）两种互补的评估机制下分析了导航任务中的碰撞安全性。此外，还开发了一个不需要额外训练的碰撞防护系统，该系统能够拒绝执行有风险的动作、执行避让操作，并最终将控制权交还给原始策略。

Result: 实验表明，在从滑动模式转换为阻挡模式时，所有测试代理的成功率均有所下降；而所提出的碰撞防护措施则显著减少了碰撞次数，并提高了多种类型代理在阻挡模式下的成功率。即使是在保持原有成功率不变的情况下，也能增加无碰撞成功案例的比例。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了完成导航任务与实现碰撞安全之间存在明显差距，并证明了轻量级碰撞感知干预不仅有助于提升安全性，同时也能增强整体导航性能。

Abstract: Vision-and-language navigation (VLN) agents are typically evaluated by whether they reach a target and how efficiently they follow an instructed route, yet these metrics provide little information about collision behavior. Moreover, continuous VLN evaluation commonly uses a sliding regime, where an agent can slide along obstacles after a collision, potentially masking the effect of unsafe actions on navigation performance. We introduce VLN-Safe, a benchmark for analyzing collision safety under two complementary evaluation regimes: sliding, reflecting common VLN evaluation practice, and blocking, where collision-inducing actions prevent forward motion and more directly expose their consequences. Across six published agents on R2R-CE, 38.5-56.8% of successful episodes contain contact under sliding, while switching to blocking decreases success rate by 11.7-36.1 percentage points. We further analyze signals for anticipating imminent collisions and find that depth geometry and recent movement history are more informative than policy-derived signals, while safe alternatives near the policy's preferred action are rarely available. Motivated by these findings, we introduce a training-free collision guard that vetoes risky forward actions, executes an escape maneuver, and returns control to the original policy. Under blocking, the guard reduces collision steps by 62-98% and improves success rate by 6.9-29.3 percentage points across five recurrent, topological, and vision-language-action agents. Under sliding, it leaves success rate unchanged while raising the share of collision-free successes by 4.1-12.4 points. These results reveal a substantial gap between task success and collision-safe navigation and show that lightweight collision-aware intervention can improve both safety and navigation performance.

</details>


### [53] [DexCast: Learning Real-World Dexterous Control from Human Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14691)
*Zihan Gao,Yuhong Zhang,Mengzhe Wang,Kaisheng Liu,Shengpeng Li,Siyao Xiao,Runqing Cheng,Xiao Lin,Zhifang Liu,Haoqian Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了DEXCAST框架，通过利用单目RGB-D视频中的手-物体交互重建直接学习灵巧的机器人手控制，无需远程操作。此外，还设计了DexHand运动感知模块(DMPM)来捕捉关节间的协调性和时间依赖性，以指导扩散策略。真实世界的实验表明，该方法在多种灵巧操控任务中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的灵巧模仿学习方法通常依赖于成本高昂且需要人工远程操作的机器人演示，这要求大量的人力投入。为了解决这个问题，研究旨在开发一种能够直接从人类视频中学习而不需要任何远程操作的新方法。同时，如何建模高维机器人手部动作以及提取时间上下文来告知未来的动作生成也是一个关键挑战。

Method: 构建了名为DEXCAST的框架，它能够从单目RGB-D视频中直接学习灵巧的机器人手控技术，借助联合的手-物体互动重构来达成目标。并且提出了一种DexHand Motion Perception Module (DMPM)，用于在紧凑的潜在表示中捕捉关节间协调和时间依赖性，这些潜在表示将作为我们扩散策略的条件。

Result: 广泛的现实世界实验证明，DEXCAST在各种灵巧操作任务上实现了卓越的表现。

Conclusion: 本研究提出的DEXCAST框架及其核心组件DMPM展示了从低成本输入源——单目RGB-D视频——学习灵巧机器人技能的能力，为减少对昂贵及劳动密集型数据收集方法的需求提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in 3D hand-object interaction (HOI) perception have opened new opportunities for learning dexterous robotic skills from human videos. However, existing dexterous imitation learning methods often rely on costly and manually teleoperated robot demonstrations, requiring substantial human effort. Therefore, we build DEXCAST, a framework that learns dexterous robotic hand control directly from monocular RGB-D videos by leveraging joint HOI reconstructions, without requiring any teleoperation. Furthermore, how to model high-dimensional robotic hand motion and extract temporal context to inform future action generation remains a key challenge. To this end, we propose a DexHand Motion Perception Module (DMPM) to capture inter-joint coordination and temporal dependencies in compact latent representations that condition our diffusion policy. Extensive real-world experiments demonstrate that DEXCAST achieves exceptional performance across diverse dexterous manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [54] [BGC-Nav: Cross-Altitude Multi-UAV Collaborative Navigation via Belief-Guided Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14702)
*Jiuwu Hao,Yueyang Wu,Liguo Sun,Haolin Song,Zao Zhang,Pin Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练的跨高度协作框架BGC-Nav，通过信念引导耦合机制改善了无人机在搜救任务中的导航表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的空中视觉-语言导航方法在户外搜索和救援场景中面临部分可观测性的问题，而当前的协作导航方式依赖于精确的目标定位和刚性的任务分配模式，忽略了高空观测的不确定性以及抑制了低空探索的自主性。

Method: 开发了BGC-Nav框架，利用信念引导耦合（Belief-Guided Coupling, BGC）实现稳健导航。高海拔无人机维护一个编码任务条件先验的全局信念记忆，低海拔代理则通过局部耗尽记忆(LEM)、局部吸引增强(LAA)以及方向信念优先(DBP)作为建议偏置而非硬命令来纳入决策制定过程中。

Result: ESARBench上的广泛实验表明，与最先进的单智能体及协同方法相比，BGC-Nav始终表现出更优性能。

Conclusion: BGC-Nav提供了一个有效的解决方案来解决单无人机搜救任务中存在的问题，并通过促进不同高度间的协作提高了整体效率。

Abstract: Despite recent advances in Aerial Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN), single Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) search-and-rescue in outdoor scenarios remains challenging due to partial observability, making cross-altitude collaboration a promising direction. However, existing collaborative navigation methods rely on precise target localization and rigid goal-dispatching paradigms, ignoring high-altitude observation uncertainty and suppressing low-altitude exploration autonomy. To address these issues, we introduce BGC-Nav, a training-free cross-altitude collaboration framework that enables robust navigation via Belief-Guided Coupling (BGC). Specifically, the high-altitude UAV maintains a Global Belief Memory encoding task-conditioned priors, which the low-altitude agent incorporates into decision-making via Local Exhaustion Memory (LEM), Local Attraction Augmentation (LAA), and Directional Belief Prior (DBP) as an advisory bias rather than a rigid command. Extensive experiments on ESARBench show that BGC-Nav consistently outperforms state-of-the-art single-agent and collaborative methods. Code is available at https://github.com/uestchjw/BGC-Nav.

</details>


### [55] [PRISM: Precision Forcing for Generalist Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14704)
*Chenghao Yue,Peiyan Li,Hao Wang,Lei Lv,Jiahao Chen,Huaping Liu,Yu Luo,Yuanlong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为PRISM的方法，旨在解决当前视觉-语言-动作模型在执行高精度任务时的不足。通过调整监督分配和表示学习以适应不同情境下的精度需求，从而提高机器人在如电缆插入等精细操作中的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在面对需要极高精确度的任务时表现不佳，主要原因是训练过程中对不同情境及时间点上的流匹配误差采取平均处理的方式，导致关键瞬间未能获得足够的监督。

Method: 提出了一种称为PRISM（Precision-aware Reweighting and Internalization for Skilled Manipulation）的技术方案，它能够离线评估精度需求，并强化那些错误后果严重且动作信号清晰可见的时间段内的流动监督，同时将这种需求内化于视觉-语言模型的状态表示中。

Result: 实验结果显示，在模拟环境和真实机器人测试中，PRISM不仅保持或提升了普通任务的表现，而且显著提高了特定高精度任务的成功率，例如驱动连接器插入、双手连接器配对以及螺丝拧紧等任务的成功率从38.3%提升到了86.7%。

Conclusion: PRISM方法有效地解决了现有身体化策略面临的精度限制问题，无需引入新的传感器或观察方式即可大幅度扩展其适用范围。

Abstract: Coupling a pretrained vision-language model (VLM) with a diffusion or flow-matching action expert has become the mainstream vision-language-action (VLA) paradigm for embodied policies, yet they remain unreliable once a task demands precise manipulation, e.g., submillimeter cable insertion. We attribute this weakness to a training objective that averages flow-matching errors across contexts and flow times, allocating little supervision to the few decisive moments determining task success. Along the generative path, inference integrates a locally supervised velocity field, so equally penalized errors at different flow times can affect the executed action unequally. Consequently, precision-task performance deteriorates as physical tolerance tightens by orders of magnitude. We call this supervision imbalance across contexts and flow times Flow-Time Starvation. The remedy is to jointly align supervision allocation and representation learning with context-dependent precision demand, enabling the policy to recognize when greater precision is needed. We instantiate this precision-forcing strategy as Precision-aware Reweighting and Internalization for Skilled Manipulation (PRISM), which scores precision demand offline, strengthens flow supervision where errors are consequential and action signals are visible, and internalizes that demand in the VLM condition representation. Simulation and real-robot experiments show that PRISM preserves or improves general-task performance while raising mean end-to-end success from 38.3% to 86.7% across drive connector insertion, bimanual connector mating, and screw tightening, substantially extending the precision boundary of existing embodied policies without new sensors or observation modalities.

</details>


### [56] [C2Flight: A Closed-Loop Co-Simulation Framework for Early-Stage Evaluation of Heterogeneous UAV Computing Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14715)
*Xizhu Chen,Shuyuan Zhang,Yixuan Li,Huazhong Yang,Yu Wang,Jincheng Yu,Hongyang Jia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为C2Flight的闭环协同仿真框架，用于在异构无人机计算系统设计早期阶段评估算法、硬件、通信和系统拓扑对闭环飞行的影响。该框架通过参数化的数据流图表示目标系统，并将设计中的硬件模型与物理硬件结合，提供了高精度的时间模拟和闭环预测准确性，支持早期设计决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在异构无人机计算系统的早期设计阶段，需要在完整系统可用之前评估算法、硬件、通信以及系统拓扑对闭环飞行性能的影响。由于设计选择之间通过机器人反馈环路相互作用，使得仅凭单独组件的指标难以预测任务表现；而现有的执行驱动方法则推迟了系统级设计反馈直到计算系统集成之后。为了解决这一问题，作者提出了C2Flight框架。

Method: C2Flight是一个闭环协同仿真框架，它采用参数化数据流图来表示目标系统，并通过统一节点接口整合正在设计中的硬件模型与可以独立访问的物理硬件。该框架的图运行时能够捕捉到缓冲、激活、资源共享、通信以及本地时间等因素对闭环飞行行为的综合影响。

Result: 通过与机载及无线外部物理计算管道进行对比验证，证实了C2Flight具有较高的时间保真度和闭环预测准确性。两个案例研究展示了该框架如何帮助探索部署策略及加速器位宽选择等早期设计决策。

Conclusion: C2Flight能够在全系统集成前揭示系统级设计折衷情况，从而实现快速的设计优化。

Abstract: Early-stage design of heterogeneous UAV computing systems requires evaluating how algorithm, hardware, communication, and system topology affect closed-loop flight before the complete system is available. However, design choices interact through the robotic feedback loop, making task performance difficult to predict from isolated component metrics, while existing execution-driven approaches postpone system-level design feedback until the computing system is integrated. This work presents C2Flight, a closed-loop co-simulation framework that represents target systems as parameterized dataflow graphs and integrates models of hardware under design with independently accessible physical hardware through a unified node interface. Its graph runtime captures the combined effects of buffering, activation, resource sharing, communication, and local timing on closed-loop flight behavior. Validation against onboard and wireless offboard physical computing pipelines confirms its timing fidelity and closed-loop prediction accuracy. Two case studies demonstrate early-stage exploration of deployment and accelerator bit-width choices. These results show that C2Flight exposes system-level design trade-offs before full-system integration, enabling rapid early-stage design refinement.

</details>


### [57] [CAG-WAM: Causal Action-Gain World Action Models for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14720)
*Peizheng Guo,Cheng Zhang,Xingyu Zhang,Changwen Zheng,Wenwen Qiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究针对世界动作模型中直接评估未来可能导致不准确的问题，通过构建因果模型来区分动作与环境因素对未来变化的影响，并提出了一种新的模型CAG-WAM用以提高动作评估精度，实验表明该方法有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更准确地评估候选动作对未来的影响，考虑到未来变化既包括动作引起的也包括环境驱动的变化。直接评估预测的未来可能无法完全描述与候选动作相关的未来变化。因此，引入了因果结构模型（SCM）来正式化这两种变化如何共同塑造未来评估，并揭示了环境演变影响候选动作未来评估的后门路径。

Method: 提出了因果动作增益世界动作模型（CAG-WAM），该模型为每个候选动作预测了一个基于动作条件的未来和一个在相同当前观察下的环境未来，并将它们的评价差异定义为因果动作增益(CAG)，以表征与候选动作相关的未来变化。训练过程中，通过将CAG纳入群组相对策略优化（GRPO）来指导机器人策略学习；推理时，则利用CAG评估初始动作，并根据需要使用预测的未来信息对其进行修正。

Result: 多个基准测试实验支持了CAG-WAM的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过采用SCM分析并提出CAG-WAM方法，能够更好地处理动作引起与环境驱动的变化对未来评估的影响，从而改进了机器人的决策过程。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) combine action generation with future prediction, allowing predicted futures to support action evaluation and robot decision making. However, the future associated with a candidate action may contain action-induced and environment-driven changes. Directly evaluating the predicted future may not fully characterize the future change associated with the candidate action. For example, a candidate action that worsens a favorable environmental future may still yield a favorable outcome, while one that improves an unfavorable environmental future may still yield an unfavorable outcome. To analyze this issue, we formulate a Structural Causal Model (SCM) for action evaluation in WAMs. The SCM formalizes how action-induced and environment-driven changes jointly shape future evaluation, and reveals a backdoor path through which environmental evolution can also affect the future evaluation associated with a candidate action. Intervention and backdoor adjustment account for this path, providing a causal basis for characterizing the future change associated with each candidate action. Based on this analysis, we propose Causal Action-Gain World Action Model (CAG-WAM). For each candidate action, CAG-WAM predicts an action-conditioned future and an environmental future under the same current observation, and defines their evaluation difference as causal action gain (CAG) for characterizing the future change associated with the candidate action. During training, we incorporate CAG into Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) to guide robot policy learning. During inference, we evaluate the initial action with CAG and, when needed, revise it using predicted future information. Experiments on multiple benchmarks support the effectiveness of CAG-WAM.

</details>


### [58] [B-Human '26 - Scalable Team Play up to 11 vs. 11](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14733)
*Arne Hasselbring,Tim Laue,Thomas Röfer,Philip Reichenberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: B-Human successfully adapted to the Humanoid Soccer League, winning competitions and conducting 11-robot matches, by evolving their behavior architecture from experiences in other RoboCup leagues.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation behind this work was to showcase the B-Human team's ability to adapt to a new league with different robot specifications and to demonstrate their capability in playing with a full team of 11 humanoid robots, which required significant adjustments and innovation in their approach.

Method: The method involved adapting the B-Human team's strategy and technical setup from the Standard Platform League to the Humanoid Soccer League, where robots are larger and heavier. This included developing a new behavior architecture based on experience from RoboCup leagues that have been using 11 players for a longer time.

Result: The results were positive, with the B-Human team achieving success in both regular and 11-player demo matches, proving the effectiveness of their adjusted strategies and the robustness of their robotic system in the Humanoid Soccer League.

Conclusion: The B-Human team's transition to the Humanoid Soccer League was successful, with the team winning multiple competitions and demonstrating tactical flexibility and system robustness in 11-player demo matches. The paper suggests that concepts from other RoboCup leagues played a key role in their behavior architecture.

Abstract: B-Human won multiple competitions in the Middle Division of the new Humanoid Soccer League in 2026. In addition to regular matches with three or five players per team, we ventured into uncharted territory by playing demo matches with eleven humanoid robots, highlighting the tactical flexibility of our behavior and the overall robustness of our system.
