As B-Human formerly competed in the Standard Platform League with robots half the size and less than a third the weight, we begin by outlining the adjustments needed for this transition. The central section of this paper details our behavior architecture, drawing on concepts from RoboCup leagues that have used 11 players for much longer.

</details>


### [59] [BridgeWAM: Bridging Video and Action Experts via Latent Bridge Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14746)
*Jiaxin Fang,Ruitong Liu,Zeyu Lai,Xingyu Wang,Mingyuan Jia,Pingyue Sheng,Weikang Wan,Mengdi Xu,Yuxiang Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Bridge-of-Experts (BoE)的新模型，通过32个潜在桥接查询(LBQs)在共享的有限潜在空间中聚合视觉、语言和本体感觉先验信息。该模型允许动作梯度引导视频专家学习更适合动作预测的表示，同时动作专家学会通过交叉注意力选择性地检索任务相关信息。实验结果表明，相比Fast-WAM，新模型不仅在LIBERO等基准测试上取得了更好的表现，而且显著降低了推理计算量和实际机器人延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的World Action Models（WAMs）通常通过密集的层间Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT)注意力机制将视频与动作专家结合在一起，这种方法不仅使动作预测暴露于不相关的视觉特征之下，还强制要求专家具有相同的深度，从而阻碍了对视觉特征的选择性使用，并引入了冗余计算。

Method: 提出了Bridge-of-Experts (BoE)，具体实现为BridgeWAM模型，它利用32个Latent Bridge Queries (LBQs)在共享且受限的潜在空间内整合视觉、语言及本体感知先验知识。通过联合视频-动作监督，使得动作梯度能够指导Video Expert生成更利于动作预测的表示形式；同时，Action Expert学会了如何通过交叉注意机制挑选出对于当前任务至关重要的信息。此外，解耦专家网络深度并重复使用LBQs以优化动作降噪过程。

Result: 所提出的5.06亿参数规模模型，包含30层的Video Expert和2层的Action Expert，在LIBERO数据集上达到了98.25%的成功率，在LIBERO-Plus上则有72.24%的整体成功率，展示了优秀的任务执行能力和强大的泛化能力，特别是拥有一个非常轻量级的动作头部。相较于Fast-WAM，BridgeWAM在单样本推理FLOPs方面减少了28.72%，模拟分析显示真实机器人环境下的延时降低至77.1毫秒（速度提升了2.47倍）。定性实验结果进一步表明该模型能够在多种干扰条件下优先关注目标物体和任务终点。

Conclusion: 研究证明了解耦专家网络深度并通过统一的LBQ条件进行联合表征适应的有效性。BridgeWAM不仅提高了任务执行效率和泛化性能，还大幅减少了计算成本和延迟时间，显示出其在实时交互场景中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) commonly couple video and action experts through dense, layer-wise Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) attention, exposing action prediction to irrelevant visual features while enforcing matched expert depths. This coupling hinders selective visual-feature use and introduces redundant computation. We introduce Bridge-of-Experts (BoE), instantiated as BridgeWAM, which aggregates visual, language, and proprioceptive priors through 32 Latent Bridge Queries (LBQs) into a shared, limited latent space. Joint video-action supervision lets action gradients guide the Video Expert toward representations better suited for action prediction, while the Action Expert learns to selectively retrieve task-relevant information through cross-attention. Decoupling expert depths and reusing LBQs during action denoising yield a 5.06B model with a 30-layer Video Expert and a two-layer Action Expert, achieving 98.25% success on LIBERO and 72.24% pooled success on LIBERO-Plus, demonstrating effective task execution and strong generalization with an ultralight action head. Ablations support joint representation adaptation and unified LBQ conditioning. Relative to Fast-WAM, BridgeWAM reduces single-sample inference FLOPs by 28.72% in simulation profiling and real-robot latency to 77.1 ms (a 2.47-fold speedup). Qualitative experimental results further indicate preferential attention to target objects and task destinations across perturbations.

</details>


### [60] [Beyond Fixed Visual History: Learning Motion-Adaptive Temporal Receptive Fields for Complex-Terrain Quadruped Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14753)
*Hao Zhang,Yuping Yan,Yitian Hong,Luying Feng,Tianhong Wang,Fengqi Dai,Wei Chen,Yaochu Jin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为TempoLoco的体感动态视觉感知框架，该框架可以根据机器人的当前运动状态调整视觉记忆，从而提高四足机器人在复杂地形上的移动性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉控制器通常使用固定的时间模式来组织深度历史，但忽略了随着机器人速度、姿态和步态阶段的变化，相同的时间跨度对应着不同的空间范围。因此，在一种运动状态下足够的视觉历史在另一种状态下可能变得过时或错位。

Method: 提出了TempoLoco，这是一种体感动态视觉感知框架，它根据机器人的当前运动状态来组织视觉记忆。TempoLoco从长深度缓冲区学习多种互补的时序视图，并在一个共享特征空间内编码这些视图，然后利用本体感觉历史和多尺度地形证据动态地聚合它们。

Result: 实验分析表明，TempoLoco的成功率达到78.4%，而最强的固定历史基线为58.2%，均匀多尺度融合为62.1%。在单独的能力评估中，它完成了横跨九种地形类型的45次模拟试验中的41次，以及配备固定臂的Unitree B2上的25次实际试验中的23次，展示了通过体感动态视觉感知实现的稳健且可部署的移动性。

Conclusion: TempoLoco能够使单一策略将视觉预期与机器人当前的运动能力对齐，从而在包括楼梯、平台、间隙、离散障碍物、粗糙地面和斜坡在内的复杂地形上表现良好。

Abstract: Versatile quadruped locomotion depends on a tight coupling between what the robot perceives and what its body can execute. Yet existing visual controllers typically organize depth history using fixed temporal patterns, overlooking that the same temporal span corresponds to different spatial ranges as the robot's speed, posture, and gait phase change. Consequently, the same visual history may be insufficient in one motion state but outdated or misaligned in another. To address this limitation, we propose TempoLoco, a body-aware dynamic visual perception framework that organizes visual memory according to the robot's current locomotion state. TempoLoco learns multiple complementary temporal views from a long depth buffer, encodes them within a shared feature space, and dynamically aggregates them using proprioceptive history and multi-scale terrain evidence. This enables a single policy to align visual anticipation with the robot's current motion capabilities across complex terrains, including stairs, platforms, gaps, discrete obstacles, rough ground, and slopes. From the experiment analysis, TempoLoco achieves a 78.4% success rate, compared with 58.2% for the strongest fixed-history baseline and 62.1% for uniform multi-scale fusion. In separate capability evaluations, it completes 41/45 simulated trials across nine terrain types and 23/25 real-world trials on a Unitree B2 with a fixed arm, demonstrating robust and deployable locomotion through body-aware dynamic visual perception.

</details>


### [61] [Scaling Latent Motor Adaptation to Coordinated Bimanual Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14833)
*Seung-Bin Joo,Alessandro Albini,Luca Wiehe,Perla Maiolino,Daniel Olmeda Reino,João F. Henriques*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于潜变量调节的双臂操作策略（Bimanual RMA）在面对不确定物体动态和几何特性时的表现，发现该方法能够较好地适应不同物体属性，在模拟到真实的双臂提升任务中直接转移效果良好，但对于长时间任务仍需进一步的时间结构支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的机器人技术面临的一个核心挑战是在物体动力学和几何形状不确定的情况下进行鲁棒操作。现有策略往往在物体质量、摩擦力、尺寸或形状发生变化时表现不佳。

Method: 通过采用基于嵌入式的适应方法，如快速运动适应（RMA），根据从交互历史推断出的环境动力学潜在表示来调节动作。本研究将这种模式扩展到了需要协调的双臂操作场景中，并使用ManiSkill平台上的三个接触密集型任务评估了双臂RMA的表现。

Result: 双臂RMA利用观察-行动历史和双腕深度图像，在双臂拾取和提升任务上保持了优越性能；而一个未经过潜变量调节的匹配静态PPO策略则成功率较低。所有方法在长周期堆叠任务上仍存在局限性。此外，实验还展示了无需真实世界微调的直接模拟到真实世界的转换能力。

Conclusion: 潜变量调节为提高协调双臂系统控制的鲁棒性提供了有用的框架，但针对更长期的任务还需要额外考虑时间上的结构安排。

Abstract: Robust manipulation under uncertain object dynamics and geometry remains a core challenge in real-world robotics. Policies trained under fixed assumptions often degrade when object mass, friction, scale, or shape vary. Embedding-based adaptation methods such as Rapid Motor Adaptation (RMA) address this by conditioning actions on latent representations of environment dynamics inferred from interaction history. We investigate whether this paradigm scales from single arm settings to coordinated bimanual manipulation, where higher dimensional control, coupled contacts, and shared object forces introduce additional complexity. We evaluate bimanual RMA on three contact-rich ManiSkill tasks with randomized YCB object properties. Bimanual RMA, using observation-action history and dual wrist depth images, retains privileged policy performance on bimanual picking and lifting, while an otherwise matched stationary PPO policy without latent conditioning achieves low success; all methods remain limited on long-horizon stacking. We further demonstrate direct sim-to-real transfer on a dual Franka lifting task without real-world finetuning. Together, these results suggest that latent conditioning provides useful structure for scaling robust control to coordinated bimanual systems, while longer horizon tasks require additional temporal structure.

</details>


### [62] [Push the Limit: Learning High-Speed Sprinting and Aggressive Cornering on a Full-Scale Humanoid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14840)
*Huaxing Huang,Ruidong Li,Jinchao Han,Ziyang Dong,Wenhao Cui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究提出了一种基于学习的框架，使双足机器人能够以超过7m/s的速度冲刺并执行高速急转弯。关键创新包括因果多头注意力模块、新设计的方向奖励以及技能条件下的多对抗运动先验判别器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现全尺寸双足人形机器人在超过7米/秒的速度下进行冲刺，并以高达25度的显著躯干倾斜执行高速急转弯，研究者们面临了几个挑战。当速度达到约4米/秒以上时，周期性的脚步冲击会导致严重的高频偏航率振荡，这与高速步态固有的特性有关。这种高频率的时间结构会破坏平坦多层感知器策略所依赖的本体感受反馈历史，同时使传统的偏航率跟踪奖励变得适得其反，因为它们对抗着机器人的自然振荡动力学。此外，单个跨技能共享的风格先验无法区分命令的具体技能，并且内在地损害了低速技能的保真度。

Method: 为克服上述限制，研究人员采用了因果多头注意力（MHA）模块来显式建模高频时间反馈。他们设计了一个方向奖励来代替振荡偏航率，只监督低频速度方向，使得能够在不抑制自然步态振荡的情况下实现高速敏捷转弯。此外，引入了技能条件下的多对抗运动先验（MAMP）判别器，将风格奖励条件化于被命令的技能上。消融实验确认了每种机制都是必不可少的：用平坦历史MLP替换结构化时间编码会限制最高速度；恢复到偏航率跟踪会阻止高速转弯；单一共享判别器会使原地转弯崩溃成准静态姿态。

Result: 该框架在原始冲刺速度和参考运动保真度之间架起了桥梁，在仿真和实际机器人部署中均展示了稳定的高速冲刺和激进转弯的能力。

Conclusion: 通过采用因果多头注意力模块、设计新的方向奖励以及使用技能条件下的多对抗运动先验判别器，研究成功地解决了高速移动双足机器人面临的挑战，实现了既快又灵活的运动表现。

Abstract: We present a learning-based framework that enables a full-scale bipedal humanoid to sprint at over 7 m/s with motion-prior-guided gait and perform aggressive cornering at high speed with a pronounced trunk lean of up to $25^\circ$. Achieving this combination is challenging: above approximately 4 m/s, periodic foot impacts induce severe, high-frequency yaw-rate oscillations intrinsic to high-speed gaits. This high-frequency temporal structure corrupts the history of proprioceptive feedback that flat-MLP policies rely on, while rendering conventional yaw-rate tracking rewards counterproductive, as they fight against the robot's natural oscillatory dynamics. Moreover, a single style prior shared across skills cannot tell which skill is commanded and inherently compromises the low-speed skill fidelity. To overcome these limitations, we adopt a causal multi-head attention (MHA) module to explicitly model the high-frequency temporal feedback. In place of the oscillatory yaw rate, we design a heading-direction reward that supervises only the low-frequency velocity direction, allowing agile turning at high speed without suppressing natural gait oscillations. We further introduce skill-conditioned multi-adversarial-motion-prior (MAMP) discriminators that condition the style reward on the commanded skill. Ablation experiments confirm that each mechanism is essential: replacing structured temporal encoding with a flat-history MLP caps the top speed; reverting to yaw-rate tracking prevents high-speed turning; and a single shared discriminator makes in-place turning collapse into a quasi-static stance. Our framework bridges the gap between raw sprinting speed and reference-motion fidelity, demonstrating stable high-speed sprinting and aggressive cornering in both simulation and real-robot deployment.

</details>


### [63] [Learning Dynamic Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14848)
*Nga Kiu Kelly Ho,Yilin Liu,Hao Xu,Niloy J. Mitra*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从单个初始手-物体状态生成完整抓取动作的新框架，该框架将手部运动生成与物体运动恢复解耦，并通过条件流匹配预测接触图和手部姿态。相比现有方法，本方法在各种设置下都能产生更平滑的手部和物体运动，同时保持较高的计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的合成方法通常只模拟最终的静态抓握，或者依赖于额外的条件（如语言、目标抓握或物体轨迹）来指定互动。因此，研究仅从单一初始手-物体状态生成整个抓取动作的问题显得尤为重要。

Method: 提出了一个框架，该框架首先对手部运动生成与物体运动恢复进行解耦。给定手腕帧中的物体点云以及参数化的初始手部配置，通过条件流匹配预测序列级接触图和每个标准化序列时间点的手部姿态。然后，通过无训练、基于接触引导的加权配准推断附着框架并恢复刚体运动。

Result: 实验结果表明，在不同场景下，所提方法能够产生比LatentHOI更流畅的手部和物体运动，同时在野外条件下保持或改进了接触和穿透指标。此外，本方法每帧生成速度大约快30倍。用户研究表明，在强制选择的成对比较中，约三分之二的情况下参与者更喜欢本方法产生的运动。

Conclusion: 本文介绍的方法为从单个起始状态生成完整的4D抓取序列提供了一个有效方案，展示了优于现有技术的表现，特别是在提高运动平滑度和计算效率方面。然而，对于新几何形状的精确接触闭合仍然是未来需要解决的关键挑战之一。

Abstract: Synthesizing believable 4D hand-object grasping sequences is important for character animation, extended reality, and embodied agents. Grasping requires a hand to approach, contact, and lift or manipulate an object coherently. Existing synthesis methods often model only the final static grasp or rely on additional conditions, such as language, target grasps, or object trajectories, to specify the interaction. We instead study the underconstrained problem of generating a complete grasping motion from a single initial hand-object state. Our framework decouples hand-motion generation from object-motion recovery. Given an object point cloud in the initial wrist frame and the initial parameterized hand configuration, conditional flow matching predicts a sequence-level contact map and a hand pose at each normalized sequence time. This time-indexed formulation avoids the error accumulation of autoregressive rollout while representing the complete motion. We then infer an attachment frame and recover rigid-object motion through training-free, contact-guided weighted registration. We evaluate on held-out DexYCB objects and unseen object geometries reconstructed from in-the-wild videos by Open-CHOIR. Compared with LatentHOI, our method produces smoother hand and object motion in every setting, matches or improves on its contact and penetration metrics in the wild under a shared initialization, and is about 30x faster per generated frame. In a 15-participant study, our motions were preferred in roughly two thirds of (forced-choice pairwise) comparisons. Precise contact closure on novel geometry, however, remains a key open challenge.

</details>


### [64] [DEFT-VLA: Stage-Localized Force Specialization for Contact-Rich Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14854)
*Zan Huang,Yibo Qiu,Kan Ding,Xuanping Zhang,Ao Tian,Ronald X Xu,Mingzhai Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为DEFT-VLA的新方法，旨在解决接触丰富操作中的感官冲突问题，通过使用两个专门化的专家分别处理非接触与接触阶段的任务，实验证明其能有效提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在接触丰富的操作中存在阶段性的感官冲突：在视觉和本体感觉对于定位和接近至关重要时，力的信息量不大且可能分散注意力；而在接触之后，力变得关键，此时无关的视觉细节和相关的机器人状态可能会成为捷径。

Method: 提出了DEFT-VLA（针对视觉-语言-动作操作的双专家力量专业化训练），该方法保留了为非接触行为定制的任务适应基线专家，并引入了一个对力敏感的接触专家来处理关键的接触阶段，将基于力条件的调整限定在需要的地方。此外，还介绍了一种接触局部化的力量关注课程，仅在接触专家上应用后续的力量关注训练，逐步恢复视觉并掩盖状态令牌以抑制捷径。

Result: 真实机器人实验显示，在吸头安装、双手吸液和白板擦除任务中，DEFT-VLA能够有效利用力反馈改善接触阶段的表现，同时保持强大的非接触行为以及对未见握持高度的鲁棒性。它在每个任务上的整体任务成功率比最强基准提高了20到40个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过DEFT-VLA方法，可以在保持非接触阶段性能的同时显著提高涉及物理接触的操作任务的成功率。此方法证明了专门化处理不同操作阶段需求的重要性。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation poses a stage-dependent sensory conflict: vision and proprioception are essential for localization and approach, when force is largely uninformative and potentially distracting, whereas force becomes critical after contact, when irrelevant visual details and correlated robot states may become shortcuts. We propose DEFT-VLA (Dual-Expert Force-specialized Training for Vision-Language-Action Manipulation), which retains the task-adapted base expert for vision-dominated non-contact behavior and introduces a force-aware contact expert for critical contact stages, localizing force-conditioned adaptation to where it is needed. We further introduce a contact-localized force-attending curriculum that applies force-attending post-training only to the contact expert, progressively restoring vision and masking state tokens to suppress shortcuts. Real-robot experiments on pipette tip attachment, bimanual pipette aspiration, and whiteboard wiping show that DEFT-VLA effectively exploits force feedback to improve contact-stage performance while maintaining strong non-contact behavior and robustness to held-out grasp heights. It improves overall task success by 20-40 percentage points over the strongest baseline on each task.

</details>


### [65] [Predict in Chunks, Revise in Spans: History-conditioned Action Regeneration for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14864)
*Chenyang Wang,Yuntian Wang,Jiaying Tang,Chunzheng Li,Yang Liu,Siao Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了Contextual Masked Action Policy (CMAP)，一种通过历史条件局部再生来优化连续动作块的视觉运动策略，该策略能够改善模仿学习中的时间连贯性，并在模拟和真实世界的操作任务中表现出优于其他基线方法的整体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的许多策略在遇到局部错误时会重新生成整个动作序列，这可能会替换掉原本有效的预测结果。为解决这一问题，研究旨在开发一种新的方法，能够在保证其他有效预测不变的情况下，仅对高不确定性区域进行局部修正，以提高模仿学习中动作预测的时间连贯性和准确性。

Method: 引入了Contextual Masked Action Policy (CMAP) 方法，它利用基于历史条件的动作带将已执行命令与未来位置掩码结合，生成粗略动作预测及每步的不确定性分数。随后，采用不确定性引导跨度再生机制选取一个高不确定性的连续区间，并利用执行历史和保留的未来预测作为上下文对该区间进行重建。

Result: 通过对九个模拟任务和四个现实世界操控任务的广泛实验表明，CMAP相比多种竞争基线实现了更优的整体表现。消融研究表明其关键组成部分具有互补贡献；而预留轨迹分析显示局部再生确实减少了选定范围内的预测误差。

Conclusion: CMAP提供了一种有效的方法来增强模仿学习过程中动作序列的时间连贯性，通过局部更新而不是完全重置动作块，从而提高了预测精度和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Action chunking improves temporal coherence in visuomotor imitation learning by jointly predicting sequences of future actions. However, many existing policies regenerate entire action chunks even when errors are localized, potentially replacing otherwise valid predictions. We introduce Contextual Masked Action Policy (CMAP), a visuomotor policy that refines continuous action chunks through history-conditioned local regeneration. Specifically, CMAP introduces a history-conditioned action tape that combines executed commands with masked future positions to produce coarse action predictions and per-step uncertainty scores. An uncertainty-guided span regeneration mechanism then selects a high-uncertainty contiguous span and reconstructs it using the executed history and retained future predictions as context. By jointly reconstructing neighboring actions, CMAP models local temporal dependencies while preserving all predictions outside the selected span. Extensive experiments on nine simulated and four real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate that CMAP achieves superior overall performance over several competitive baselines. Ablation studies validate the complementary contributions of its key components, while held-out trajectory analysis shows that local regeneration reduces prediction errors within selected spans.

</details>


### [66] [Verify, Act, Distill: Turning Test-Time Compute into Test-Time Adaptation for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14885)
*Kaneyoshi Hiratsuka,Satoshi Yamamori,Jun Morimoto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Verify, Act, Distill的框架，该框架通过将测试时计算（TTC）的增益整合到视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型中来提高其执行性能。通过对成功的适应阶段直接提炼观察结果和聚合片段到LoRA参数中，这种方法能够保留TTC在执行过程中已经产生的监督信息，而无需额外的强化学习或专门用于生成教师的rollouts。实验表明，此方法不仅保持了TTC带来的改进，还进一步放大了后续TTC的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的方法在利用测试时计算(TTC)提升视觉-语言-动作(VLA)模型表现时，通常需要在每次执行时重新计算收益，并且策略保持不变。这限制了效率与效果。

Method: 提出了Verify, Act, Distill框架，该框架通过固定验证器对由流匹配VLA生成的候选动作进行评分，然后执行加权聚合的结果。成功适应阶段的观察结果及聚合片段被直接蒸馏进LoRA参数里，使用标准流匹配损失。

Result: 在40项LIBERO任务上，仅从每项任务10个回合中提炼，就使无验证者单候选生成的成功率从54.1%提高到了70.6%，大约保留了聚合八个候选所获得改进的74%。对蒸馏后的策略应用相同的聚合方法，成功率进一步从76.4%提高至82.3%，尤其是在LIBERO-Long任务上的提升显著（44.0%到57.8%）。此外，单候选策略也在包含分布偏移的LIBERO-plus上有所改善。

Conclusion: 使用验证作为学习信号，而不仅仅是在执行时的选择工具，不仅可以保持TTC带来的改进，还能增强后续TTC的效果。

Abstract: Test-time compute (TTC), which scores multiple candidate actions at inference time, is a powerful way to improve the execution performance of vision-language-action (VLA) models, but existing methods keep the policy frozen, so the gain must be recomputed at every execution. We propose Verify, Act, Distill, a framework that consolidates the gains of TTC into the VLA itself. A fixed verifier scores candidates generated by a flow-matching VLA, the weighted aggregate is executed, and the observations and aggregated chunks from successful adaptation episodes are distilled directly into LoRA parameters with the standard flow-matching loss. This reuses supervision that TTC has already produced during execution, without additional reinforcement learning or rollouts dedicated to teacher generation. On the 40 LIBERO tasks with a SmolVLA 5k checkpoint, distilling from only 10 episodes per task raises the mean success rate of verifier-free single-candidate generation from 54.1% to 70.6%, retaining about 74% of the improvement obtained by aggregating eight candidates. Applying the same aggregation to the distilled policy further raises the success rate from 76.4% to 82.3%, with a particularly large gain on LIBERO-Long (44.0% to 57.8%), and the single-candidate policy also improves on LIBERO-plus, which contains distribution shifts. These results show that using verification as a learning signal, rather than only as an execution-time selector, both retains the improvements of TTC and re-amplifies the effect of subsequent TTC.

</details>


### [67] [LETS: Low-Overhead External-Torque Sensing for Force-Feedback Bilateral Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14889)
*Zonghao Guo,Dongchen Guan,Yubo Wang,Peng Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为LETS的物理导向学习方法，用于提高双边遥操作中外部扭矩估计的质量。该方法仅利用机器人的URDF模型和关节反馈，在没有专门的六轴力/扭矩传感器的情况下也能实现稳定的力反馈，且其自由空间误差比制造商提供的估计低69%，接触误差低28%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双边遥操作中力反馈的质量越来越受到重视，而外部扭矩估计的质量对于此过程至关重要。然而，商业机器人臂上制造商提供的扰动扭矩估计往往不准确，同时末端执行器力/扭矩传感器的应用增加了成本和负载。

Method: 提出了一个称为LETS的方法，该方法将刚体动力学分配给递归牛顿-欧拉逆向动力学，并将学习仅保留给特定于机器人的残差部分。LETS仅使用机器人的URDF模型和关节反馈，有意排除了控制命令，从而将外部扭矩估计与控制命令输入解耦。

Result: LETS在12分钟的自由运动数据上进行训练只需不到一分钟的时间，无需专用的末端执行器六轴力/扭矩传感器。在双边遥操作中，LETS实现了实时外部扭矩估计，使力反馈稳定。其自由空间误差比制造商提供的估计低69%，大大减少了领导者和跟随者之间的耦合振荡；其接触误差降低了28%，提供了更精确的触觉反馈。

Conclusion: 通过引入LETS这一创新方案，显著提升了外部扭矩估计的准确性，进而改善了双边遥操作中的力反馈性能，为降低系统复杂度及成本提供了一个有效途径。

Abstract: Bilateral teleoperation with force feedback is becoming increasingly important, and the quality of external-torque estimation is critical to this process. However, manufacturer-provided disturbance-torque estimates on commercial robotic arms are often inaccurate, while the use of end-effector force/torque sensors adds cost and payload. We propose LETS, a physics-guided learning approach that assigns rigid-body dynamics to recursive Newton-Euler inverse dynamics and reserves learning for only the robot-specific residual. LETS uses only the robot's URDF model and joint feedback, deliberately excluding control commands and thereby decoupling external-torque estimation from control-command inputs. It trains on 12 minutes of free-motion data in under one minute, without requiring a dedicated end-effector six-axis force/torque sensor. In bilateral teleoperation, LETS achieves real-time external-torque estimation that enables stable force feedback. Its free-space error is 69% lower than the manufacturer-provided estimate, substantially reducing coupled oscillations between leader and follower; its contact error is 28% lower, yielding more accurate haptic feedback.

</details>


### [68] [Test-Time Compute for Humanoid Behavior Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14896)
*Weiji Xie,Junsong Wu,Zibo Zhou,Yueru Chen,Xingyi Wang,Zhenhua Xiong,Weinan Zhang,Chenjia Bai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种名为测试时命令优化(TTCO)的方法，用于提高人形行为基础模型(BFMs)在执行动作时的全身位置准确性。通过采样优化和闭环模拟，TTCO能够调整输入指令以更接近理想的运动轨迹，显著减少了模拟中的平均身体位置误差，并支持跨BFM重用现有演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的人形行为基础模型（BFMs）虽然可以执行令人印象深刻的动作，但在下游应用如位移操作中需要较高的全身位置精度。现有的BFMs存在系统性的命令执行差距问题，即实际执行的动作与期望的动作之间存在偏差。为了解决这一问题，作者提出了一种无需重新训练BFM即可改善执行效果的方法。

Method: 1. 提出了测试时命令优化（TTCO），通过适应特定BFM的输入命令来生成更接近理想运动轨迹的执行轨迹。
2. TTCO利用基于采样的优化方法，通过BFM和机器人动力学的闭环模拟找到最佳命令。
3. 分析了测试时计算量的增长情况，并提出了一个近似的最优计算策略，在增加计算预算时仍能保持性能提升。
4. 展示了TTCO可以从记录的机器人轨迹生成BFM特有的动作标签，而不需要原始遥操作体动命令，这对于训练高级位移操作策略非常有效。

Result: 1. 在AMASS数据集上的三个BFM上，TTCO将模拟中的平均全球身体位置误差降低了79-84%。
2. 该方法还支持跨不同BFM重用现有演示。
3. 实验表明，使用TTCO生成的动作标签对于训练高层级位移操纵策略是有效的。

Conclusion: 通过引入测试时命令优化（TTCO），研究者们成功地缩小了人形行为基础模型在执行复杂任务时的实际动作与预期动作之间的差距。这种方法不仅提高了动作执行的准确性，同时也为如何有效地利用额外的测试时间计算资源提供了一个新思路。此外，它还能促进已有演示数据在不同BFM间的迁移利用。

Abstract: Humanoid Behavior Foundation Models (BFMs) can execute impressive motions, but downstream applications such as loco-manipulation demand high whole-body positional accuracy. Existing BFMs suffer from a systematic command-execution gap, where executed motions deviate from desired ones. We show that additional test-time compute can improve execution without retraining the BFM. First, we introduce Test-Time Command Optimization (TTCO), which adapts the input command to the specific BFM to produce an executed trajectory closer to the desired motion. TTCO finds such commands by sampling-based optimization through closed-loop simulations of the BFM and robot dynamics. Across three BFMs on the AMASS dataset, TTCO reduces mean global body-position error by 79-84% in simulation. Second, we analyze test-time compute scaling and derive an approximate compute-optimal strategy that can sustain performance gains as the compute budget increases. Third, TTCO can generate BFM-specific action labels from recorded robot trajectories without requiring the original teleoperation body-motion commands. Simulation and real-robot experiments show that these labels are effective for training high-level loco-manipulation policies. This method also supports reusing existing demonstrations across BFMs.

</details>


### [69] [Scalable Multi-Human Cooperative Manipulation via Commander-Executor Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14911)
*Buzhen Huang,Xingchen Wu,Yuanwang Yang,Chenyi Xie,Kun Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种指挥-执行框架来改进多人协同物体操作的表现，在多种任务上的测试表明该方法优于现有技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的方法虽然能够处理简单的协作搬运任务，但在处理角色依赖、长周期和顺序性的协作操作时存在困难。为了解决这个问题，本文提出了一种分层的指挥-执行框架，以使多个代理能够以不同的方式完成复杂的协作任务。

Method: 所提出的框架中，指挥者结合指令和场景信息进行联合语义和几何规划，并将任务分解为特定于角色的子任务，生成共享的对象中心协调计划，指定每个代理的执行时机、目标位置和操作动作。基于指挥者的高层次策略和每个代理的局部观察，执行者通过从单独到合作的课程学习低层次的基于物理的策略，实现协调的人-物互动。

Result: 该方法在多种物体操作任务上进行了评估，展示了其执行结合不同类型协作的长期任务的能力，并且可以从双代理扩展到多代理合作。广泛的实验进一步表明，与最先进的方法相比，该方法达到了更高的成功率。

Conclusion: 提出的指挥-执行架构有效提升了多个人类协同操作物体的能力，特别是在处理复杂、长周期以及需要角色间配合的任务时表现出了明显优势。

Abstract: Multi-human cooperative object manipulation is a highly challenging topic in humanoid control. While existing methods can handle simple collaborative carrying tasks, they struggle with role-dependent, long-horizon, and sequential cooperative manipulation. To address this issue, we propose a hierarchical commander-executor framework that enables multiple agents to accomplish complex collaborative tasks in distinct ways. Specifically, a commander combines instructions and scene information for joint semantic and geometric planning. It then decomposes the task into role-specific subtasks and generates shared object-centric coordination plans, specifying execution timing, target positions, and manipulation actions for each agent. Conditioned on the commander's high-level strategy and each agent's local observations, the executor learns a low-level physics-based policy through a solo-to-cooperative curriculum to realize coordinated human-object interactions. The planned object trajectory provides a shared coordination target, while root positions and grasping instructions specify role-dependent execution cues. We evaluate our method on a variety of object manipulation tasks, demonstrating its ability to execute long-horizon tasks that combine different types of collaboration and to scale from two-agent to multi-agent cooperation. Extensive experiments further show that our approach achieves higher success rates compared to state-of-the-art methods. Code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/TJU-3DV/Co-Manipulation.

</details>


### [70] [Funnel planning and control for manipulators using neural Signed Configuration Distance Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14917)
*Bernhard Wullt,Christos K. Verginis,Per Mattsson,Mikael Norrlöf,Thomas B. Schön*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的漏斗规划和控制解决方案，利用神经符号配置距离函数(nSCDF)来高效估计无碰撞球体和梯度，从而在保持安全性和鲁棒性的同时，提高了路径规划的速度、缩短了路径长度并增加了避障间隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的Kinodynamic Motion Planning via Funnel Control (KDF)方法虽然具备吸引人的理论特性，如确保闭环演化位于一个无碰撞区域（即漏斗）内，但其计算漏斗的过程计算量大，限制了表达能力和性能。因此，需要一种更有效的漏斗规划与控制方案来克服这些局限。

Method: 通过引入神经符号配置距离函数(nSCDF)，能够以计算效率高的方式估计无碰撞球体及其梯度；基于nSCDF设计了一个漏斗控制器，该控制器利用nSCDF提供的几何信息来实现具有避碰保证的轨迹跟踪。

Result: 实验结果显示，所提出的规划器生成名义路径的速度快了2倍，同时路径长度减少了20%，避障间隙增加了5倍。此外，在使用nSCDF进行漏斗控制时，还观察到了运动性能和鲁棒性的提升。

Conclusion: 本研究通过结合nSCDF提供了一种改进的漏斗规划与控制方法，有效提升了机器人操纵器在存在扰动情况下的安全执行与无碰撞移动能力，显著改善了路径规划速度、路径长度及避障性能。

Abstract: Robust motion planning and control for manipulators is the problem of guaranteeing safe execution and collision-free motion under disturbances. The Kinodynamic Motion Planning via Funnel Control (KDF) is a recently proposed planner that comes with robustness guarantees and is particularly attractive for collision-free control as it guarantees closed-loop evolution inside a funnel, i.e., a collision-free region around a nominal trajectory. Despite its attractive theoretical properties, the current approach for computing funnels is computationally demanding, which limits its expressiveness and performance. We address its current limitations by proposing a novel funnel planning and control solution. Our key tool to enable this is a neural Signed Configuration Distance Function (nSCDF) which allows us to estimate collision-free balls and gradients in a computationally efficient way. We use the nSCDF in our planner to compute a nominal trajectory, where path length and clearance are balanced. To track the trajectory with collision-avoidance guarantees, we design a funnel controller that exploits the geometric information from the nSCDF. From numerical experiments, we observe that our planner produces nominal paths $2$ times faster, while at the same time obtaining $20 \%$ shorter path length and $5$ times the clearance. Finally, we also observe improved motion performance and robustness properties when using the nSCDF for funnel control.

</details>


### [71] [Edge-Native Temporal--Graph Dual-Expert Inference for Dexterous Grasp Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14926)
*Jiajian Zheng,Bin Zhang,Wen Fan,Yanan Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文介绍了一种边缘原生处理流程，利用内置关节角度和预使用校准的执行器负载反馈来识别抓取的对象，并为每个手指预测一个独立的停止事件。通过结合时间卷积网络(TCN)和手指图滤波网络(FGFN)的优势，系统实现了高精度的对象分类和停止判断，在实际部署中表现出了低功耗和快速响应的特点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多指手在接触物体时存在不同步问题，全局关闭指令可能导致早期接触的手指过载，而其他手指仍在移动。本研究旨在通过仅依赖于机器人手部内部传感器的数据来解决这一难题，避免引入复杂的触觉阵列硬件及相关开销。

Method: 提出了一种名为DualExpert的异构双专家架构，其中TCN负责建模短期接触演变并提供所有五个停止概率；FGFN则专注于编码手指间的拓扑关系以辅助对象分类。此外，还设计了一个基于边界的轻量级推理流程，确保了实时性和能源效率。

Result: 实验结果表明，在225次保留测试中，固定0.7/0.3类别概率融合策略达到了93.78%的轨迹级别分类准确率，分别比单独使用TCN和FGFN提高了4.00和2.22个百分点。对于停止判断任务，TCN路径获得了95.25%的F1分数。

Conclusion: 所提出的系统不仅能够有效提升多指抓握操作中的同步性与安全性，而且在真实世界应用中表现出色，具有较高的识别率（96.00%）和抓取成功率（98.00%），同时保持较低的能耗（约0.80W）。

Abstract: Multi-finger hands contact objects at different times, so a global close command can overload early-contact fingers while others are still moving. Dense tactile arrays address this problem but add hardware, calibration, bandwidth, and computation. We present an edge-native pipeline that uses only built-in joint-angle and pre-use-calibrated actuator-load feedback to recognize the grasped object and predict an independent Stop event for each finger. Four samples and their first differences form a 4-by-20 contact-evolution window. At its core is a heterogeneous dual-expert architecture, termed DualExpert, that assigns complementary tasks to a Temporal Convolutional Network (TCN) and a Finger Graph Filter Network (FGFN). The TCN models short-term contact evolution and supplies all five Stop probabilities, while the FGFN encodes finger topology and contributes only to object classification. Fixed 0.7/0.3 class-probability fusion reaches 93.78\% trajectory-level classification accuracy on 225 held-out trials, improving the standalone TCN and FGFN by 4.00 and 2.22 percentage points, respectively. The TCN Stop path attains 95.25\% F1. The dataset contains 1,500 automatically collected trajectories from five object instances. In Flash deployment, mixed accelerator execution retains 93.78\% classification accuracy and 94.84\% Stop F1 with 2.890~ms mean inference time and 9.172~ms mean sample-to-stop latency. Across 50 physical trials, the system achieves 96.00\% recognition and 98.00\% grasp success while the complete embedded board draws approximately 0.80~W.

</details>


### [72] [CFG-Policy: Unified Diffusion Policy Learning Framework via Classifier-free Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14936)
*Shutong Ding,Zhaoyang Liu,Yimiao Zhou,Jingya Wang,Jiayuan Gu,Ye Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种名为CFG-Policy的新框架，该框架旨在通过将探索和利用分离为两个生成策略并在采样过程中结合它们的得分或速度场来解决扩散策略在强化学习中的行为多样性保持问题。它既适用于在线RL也适用于离线到在线RL，并在多个任务上证明了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散策略在机器人控制、视觉-语言-动作模型以及基于世界模型的代理中越来越受到重视。然而，使用强化学习优化这些策略可能会减少行为多样性并限制其探索能力。直接微调预训练策略可能破坏已获得的能力，而动作空间残差可能不足以支持多模态探索。这些问题揭示了在线及从离线到在线扩散强化学习中的共同挑战：即如何在提升任务性能的同时保留所需的行为多样性和适应性。

Method: 提出了一种称为CFG-Policy（无分类器引导策略）的统一插件式框架，该框架能够将探索与利用分为两个独立的生成策略，并通过无分类器引导技术在采样时合并二者的分数或速度场。对于在线RL场景，冻结的探索分支负责维持行为多样性，而利用分支则根据任务奖励进行学习；在离线至在线RL转换期间，则保持预训练的利用分支不变，同时可训练分支指导组合行为的发展。

Result: CFG-Policy方法成功地在5个MuJoCo任务上采用QSM和QVPO进行了性能改进，在4个RoboTwin操作任务上利用Flow-SDE+PPO同样得到了验证。这表明该方法能够在不牺牲底层RL目标的情况下提高表现力。

Conclusion: 研究展示了一种创新的方法——CFG-Policy，它有效地解决了强化学习领域内扩散策略面临的挑战，包括保持行为多样性的问题。通过实验结果证实，无论是在纯在线还是由离线转向在线的学习过程中，CFG-Policy都能够显著改善系统的表现，同时保证了持续适应环境变化所需的多样化行为特征。

Abstract: Diffusion policies are increasingly used in robotic control and manipulation, vision-language-action models, and world-model-based agents. Their ability to represent multimodal action distributions offers rich opportunities for exploration in reinforcement learning (RL). However, optimizing diffusion policies with RL can reduce the behavioral diversity and further limit their exploration capability. In RL post-training, directly fine-tuning a pretrained policy may also disrupt its acquired capabilities, while action-space residuals may lack the expressive power for multimodal exploration. Together, these issues reveal a common challenge across online and offline-to-online diffusion RL: improving task performance while retaining the behavioral diversity and capabilities needed for continued adaptation. We introduce CFG-Policy, a unified, plug-and-play framework that separates exploration and exploitation into two generative policies and combines their score or velocity fields during sampling through classifier-free guidance. For online RL, a frozen exploration branch sustains behavioral diversity while an exploitation branch learns from task rewards; for offline-to-online RL, a pretrained exploitation branch remains frozen while a trainable branch steers the composed behavior. CFG-Policy preserves the underlying RL objectives and improves performance with QSM and QVPO on 5 MuJoCo tasks and with Flow-SDE+PPO on 4 RoboTwin manipulation tasks. Project page is at https://zhy-liu.github.io/cfg-policy-page.

</details>


### [73] [FoundationVIO: Enforcing Metric Scale in Feed-forward Visual-Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14951)
*Jin Rhee,Yuhao Zhang,Frank Dellaert,Maurice Fallon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种名为FoundationVIO的方法，该方法通过将惯性感知与几何基础模型(GFM)预测相结合来恢复度量尺度重建。它在里程估计上表现优异，并且能够创建全局准确的重建和姿态估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于GFM的SLAM方法生成的轨迹和3D重建面临的主要挑战是预测的几何比例模糊问题，即局部一致但缺乏直接物理测量约束。受经典视觉-惯性里程计启发，作者提出结合惯性传感器数据解决这一问题。

Method: 提出了一种名为FoundationVIO的新方法，采用因子图方法整合了惯性感应与GFM预测以恢复具有度量尺度的重建。此外还添加了一个简单的基于GFM的闭环机制，构建完整的视觉-惯性SLAM系统。

Result: 实验结果表明，FoundationVIO在里程估计方面可与最先进的VIO方法相媲美，在密集重建方面优于现有的GFM支持的SLAM方法。此方法可以在消费级笔记本电脑GPU上接近实时运行。

Conclusion: 通过结合惯性测量，FoundationVIO不仅解决了GFM预测中的规模不确定性问题，而且提供了一种新的高性能视觉-惯性SLAM解决方案。

Abstract: Feed-forward geometric foundation models (GFMs) directly predict dense geometry and camera poses from batches of images. Emerging visual SLAM methods chain together consecutive GFM batches to form dense point cloud maps. A core challenge is how to address the ambiguous geometric scale of GFM predictions: independent predictions are locally consistent up-to-scale but not constrained by any direct physical measurements. Models trained on data with known scale improve the consistency of GFM reconstructions but again do not directly measure it. Consequently, trajectories and 3D reconstructions from GFM-enabled SLAM methods inherit this issue. Motivated by parallels with classical visual-inertial odometry, we introduce FoundationVIO which integrates inertial sensing with GFM predictions to recover metric scale reconstruction using a factor-graph based approach. We demonstrate odometry estimates competitive with classical state-of-the-art VIO methods and accurate dense reconstructions that outperform GFM-enabled SLAM methods. We also add a minimal GFM-based loop-closure mechanism to create a full Visual-Inertial SLAM system capable of creating globally accurate reconstructions and pose estimates. We evaluate our method on three datasets with well known sensor systems and demonstrate its compatibility on ubiquitous camera and IMU sensors. The method runs at approximately real-time on a consumer-grade laptop GPU. Code: https://github.com/ori-drs/FoundationVIO.

</details>


### [74] [EnactOpt: Diagnosis-Guided Harness Evolution for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14977)
*ZhiYuan Feng,Zhuoshi Pan,Zhaolu Kang,Zhongxia Chen,Shuo Yang,Sen Ye,Heng Wang,Han Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EnactOpt enhances embodied agent performance by evolving textual skills and context-construction code using limited feedback, achieving up to 27% better task success across multiple benchmarks compared to initial setups and other methods.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the observation that failures in embodied agents can occur due to both limited model capabilities and the way an execution harness presents information and guides its use. Since collecting task-specific interaction experience is costly, there's a need for a method that can adapt the external mechanisms of the model (i.e., the harness) without requiring updates to the model parameters, and that can differentiate between issues arising from missing evidence and inadequate use of available evidence.

Method: The method introduces EnactOpt, which leverages limited execution feedback to simultaneously evolve textual skills and context-construction code without altering the underlying models or runtime. It involves diagnosing adjacent decisions by linking action feedback with model input and subsequent actions, using structured episode summaries to guide revisions through prompt-level edit operators, and performing compatibility checks and validation-based retention on candidate harnesses.

Result: Across various benchmarks including VLABench, ALFWorld, and Embodied Agent Interface (EAI), EnactOpt demonstrates significant improvements in task success, ranging from approximately 10-27 percentage points, over the initial harnesses as well as outperforming other methods such as GEPA, TextGrad, and Trace2Skill. The improvements are also noted in task conditions that were not part of the harness evolution or candidate selection process, indicating the broad applicability of the developed technique.

Conclusion: EnactOpt provides a new approach to improving the performance of embodied agents by jointly refining how evidence is presented and acted upon, leading to significant improvements in task success rates over initial harnesses and other evaluated baselines. These benefits are observed even in task conditions not used for harness evolution or candidate selection, suggesting that EnactOpt effectively turns limited execution experience into reusable behavioral improvements.

Abstract: Embodied agents built on foundation models rely on an execution harness to organize observations, guide actions, and incorporate execution feedback into subsequent decisions. Failures can arise not only from limited model capabilities, but also from how the harness presents evidence and guides its use. These model-external mechanisms provide an adaptation target without updating model parameters, but task-specific interaction experience can be costly to collect. Task-level outcomes alone do not reveal whether relevant evidence was missing from the model input or was available but inadequately used. Moreover, these two aspects are coupled: effective action guidance may require evidence that the current context omits, while exposing that evidence alone does not ensure an appropriate response. We introduce EnactOpt, which uses limited execution feedback to jointly evolve reusable textual skills and context-construction code while keeping the underlying models and runtime fixed. It diagnoses adjacent decisions by relating action feedback to the actual model input and subsequent action. Structured episode summaries guide joint revisions through prompt-level edit operators, and candidate harnesses undergo compatibility checks and validation-based retention. Across VLABench, ALFWorld, and Embodied Agent Interface (EAI), EnactOpt improves task success by approximately 10-27 percentage points over the initial harnesses and outperforms the evaluated GEPA, TextGrad, and Trace2Skill baselines. These gains extend to task conditions not used for harness evolution or candidate selection. The results support a complementary route to embodied adaptation: turning limited execution experience into reusable behavioral improvements by jointly refining how evidence is presented and acted upon.

</details>


### [75] [Barrier-Constrained Guidance For Real-time Asynchronous VLA Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14997)
*Wenbo Yu,Jifan Li,Wenke Xia,Yuchen Li,Yizhuo Zhang,Pei Ren,Zhengping Che,Di hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了FreeBar，一种无需训练的实时推理插件，通过引入轻量级的控制屏障函数启发的一致性约束来解决异步执行中出现的边界不对齐问题，同时保持了生成效率和动作灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步执行虽然使VLA模型能够在实时环境中运行，但会导致与已执行动作流之间的边界不匹配问题。现有解决方案要么需要大量重训练、架构修改，要么采用固定前缀补全方法增加计算负担，特别是在低延迟至关重要的情况下。

Method: FreeBar通过在基于流的VLA中将块对齐转换为障碍引导的动作空间约束来实现鲁棒的异步执行。它引入了一个轻量级的控制屏障函数（CBF）启发的一致性约束，并以闭式解的形式解决了由此产生的校正问题，避免了通过VLA动作头进行昂贵的反向传播。

Result: FreeBar在Kinetix、RoboCasa以及多种现实世界机器人平台上测试显示，与简单的异步执行相比，成功率平均提高了10%至15%，尤其是在高度动态场景下表现更佳。此外，FreeBar还保持了实时计算效率，相较于RTC每段动作的推理延迟降低了超过45%。

Conclusion: 作为一种通用插件，FreeBar不仅有效提升了异步执行的成功率，而且在不同延迟环境、基础策略及超参数设置下均表现出色，证明了其广泛适用性和稳健性。

Abstract: While asynchronous execution enables VLA models to operate in real-time environments by predicting the next chunk during execution, it inevitably introduces boundary misalignment with the committed action stream. Existing delay-aware solutions address this at the prohibitive cost of extensive retraining, architectural modifications, or fixed-prefix inpainting that inflates computation when low latency is vital. To address these problems, we propose FreeBar, a training-free real-time inference plugin that enables robust asynchronous execution with only marginal additional inference cost by casting chunk alignment in flow-based VLAs as barrier-guided action-space constraint. Specifically, FreeBar introduces a lightweight Control Barrier Function (CBF)-inspired consistency constraint between consecutive action chunks and solves the resulting correction in closed form, avoiding costly backpropagation through the VLA action head. By progressively tightening the consistency bound during generation, FreeBar aligns generated chunks with the committed execution stream while preserving early-stage generative and within-chunk future action flexibility,. Across Kinetix, RoboCasa, and diverse real-world robotic platforms with multiple VLA backbones, FreeBar improves success rates over naive asynchronous execution by 10%--15% on average, with larger gains in highly dynamic scenarios. It also preserves real-time computational efficiency, decreasing inference latency per action chunk over 45% than RTC. Additionally, as a universal plugin, FreeBar remains robust across distinct latency environments, base policies, and hyperparameter settings.

</details>


### [76] [Design and Evaluation of a Collaborative Teleoperation Interface for Multi-Operator Multi-Robot Systems in Challenging Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15001)
*Alperen Kenan,Juan José García Cárdenas,Adriana Tapus,Paul Bremner,Manuel Giuliani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究设计并评估了一种共享可视化协作遥操作界面，以提高多操作员、多机器人系统中的操作员协调性、态势感知和任务表现。通过比较用户研究发现，共处一地且使用共享界面的操作条件在完成任务速度、减少碰撞次数以及提高态势感知准确性方面表现最佳，并且与远程操作相比，这种设置下的认知负荷更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的多机器人部署依赖于每个机器人配备一个或一组操作员的方式，而这些操作员之间需要手动沟通来协调不同平台的工作。这种方式增加了操作员认知负担，并限制了来自不同制造商机器人的可扩展性。为了解决这些问题，研究旨在开发一种能够改善操作员间协调、提升态势感知能力及整体任务执行效率的新方法。

Method: 研究采用了包含16名参与者的一个对比实验设计，测试了三种不同的遥操作条件：共处一地且使用共享界面、远程操作但使用共享界面、以及共处一地但各自使用独立界面。参与者控制两个模拟机器人，在一个核电厂数字孪生环境中执行灾难响应任务。期间记录了性能指标、生理数据和行为测量结果，用于评估各条件下任务执行情况、认知负荷及操作员意识水平。

Result: 共处一地并采用共享界面的操作条件取得了最快的使命完成时间（292.8秒），优于远程操作（351.6秒）和个人界面（385.1秒）。此外，该条件下还实现了最少的碰撞次数（每试验0.9次）和最高的态势感知准确率（91.1%）。NASA-TLX得分显示，从共处一地到远程操作的认知负荷有所增加，分别为38.2分和57.7分，这得到了一致的生理指标支持。

Conclusion: 研究表明，物理上的共存与共享界面相结合可以提供互补的好处，分别支持工作量调节和相互间的意识增强，在多操作员、多机器人遥操作系统中尤为重要。

Abstract: Multi-robot systems that combine the complementary capabilities of specialised platforms are critical for complex missions in challenging, inaccessible environments. Current deployments assign an operator or operator team to each robot, with collaboration relying on operator-to-operator communication to manually coordinate heterogeneous platforms with incompatible interfaces. This operator-dependent approach increases cognitive load and limits scalability across robots from different manufacturers. This study designs and evaluates a shared-visualisation collaborative teleoperation interface to improve operator coordination, situational awareness, and the task performance in multi-operator, multi-robot systems.
  A comparative user study with 16 participants compared three teleoperation conditions: co-located operators with a shared interface, remote operators with a shared interface, and co-located operators with individual interfaces. Participants controlled two simulated robots in a nuclear power plant digital twin to complete disaster response tasks. Performance metrics, physiological data, and behavioural measures were recorded to assess task performance, cognitive load, and operator awareness across conditions. The co-located shared-interface condition achieved the fastest mission completion (292.8 s), outperforming remote operation (351.6 s) and individual interfaces (385.1 s), with the fewest collisions (0.9 per trial) and highest situational awareness accuracy (91.1 %). Physical co-presence was associated with reduced cognitive load, with NASA-TLX scores increasing from 38.2 in co-located to 57.7 in remote operation, supported by consistent physiological indicators. These results demonstrate that co-presence and shared interfaces provide complementary benefits, supporting workload regulation and mutual awareness respectively in multi-operator, multi-robot teleoperation.

</details>


### [77] [Training-Free Multi-Agent Cooperative Embodied Search from Uncertain Vision-Language Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15038)
*Fan Pu,Keshu Wu,Jiwan Jiang,Shiyu Shen,Zhiwen Fan,Zhengzhong Tu,Jiachen Li,Yang Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级、无需训练且具有不确定性感知能力的框架，用于无地图环境下的多智能体多目标搜索任务。通过将高层语义推理与低层执行分离，并采用图优化方法处理由多模态大语言模型生成的假设信息，该框架能够提高目标覆盖率和搜索效率，同时支持不同数量的智能体和目标、适应新环境以及兼容不同的感知模块和底层控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的视觉-语言导航（VLN）需要代理在部分可观察性下行动，在这种情况下，基础模型可能无法完全或正确地推断出地图结构、语义线索和目标位置。为了解决这一挑战，本文旨在开发一种能够在缺乏完整地图信息的情况下有效工作的解决方案，特别是针对多智能体协同寻找多个目标的任务。

Method: 提出的方法是一个不需要额外训练的框架，它将高级别的语义推理与低级别的具体执行相分离，使用了一个可扩展的图优化后端来实现这一点。多模态大型语言模型被用来生成关于可能的目标区域及其连接方式的假设，这些假设被视为不确定性的先验知识，并结合在线观测数据和图上下文证据进一步精炼。之后，合作导航问题被建模成一个滚动视界上的多智能体混合整数线性规划问题，其目标首先是最大化朝向目标的奖励，然后是减少行程距离、不确定边缘的使用次数及重复访问次数。

Result: 实验基于100个Matterport3D测试案例表明，所提出的框架相比基准方法提高了目标覆盖范围和搜索效率。此外，共享信息和联合规划使得各智能体之间的工作负载更加均衡。

Conclusion: 本研究展示了一种新颖而有效的多智能体视觉-语言导航方法，它不仅改善了目标发现率和搜索过程中的效率，还具备良好的通用性和灵活性，适用于不同规模的团队、未知环境以及多种传感器配置。

Abstract: Real-world Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) requires agents to act under partial observability, where map structure, semantic cues, and target locations may be incomplete or incorrectly inferred by foundation models. This paper presents a lightweight, training-free, and uncertainty-aware framework for cooperative multi-agent multi-target search in mapless environments. Instead of learning an end-to-end navigation policy, the framework separates high-level semantic reasoning from low-level execution through a scalable graph-optimization backbone. Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) serve only as hypothesis generators, proposing semantic zones, target-location cues, and possible navigable connections. These outputs are treated as uncertain priors and further refined using online observations and graph-context evidence. Cooperative navigation is then formulated as a rolling-horizon multi-agent mixed-integer linear program with a lexicographic objective that first maximizes target-directed reward and then reduces travel distance, uncertain-edge use, and repeated visits. Experiments derived from 100 Matterport3D test cases show that the proposed framework improves target coverage and search efficiency over representative baseline methods, while shared information and joint planning enable more balanced workloads across agents. By operating on an abstract graph without relying on embodiment-specific policy parameters, the framework scales across varying numbers of agents and targets, generalizes to unseen environments, and accommodates different perception modules and low-level controllers without task-specific retraining.

</details>


### [78] [Modeling and Control of a Rigid-soft Hybrid-link Humanoid Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15073)
*Zewen He,Taiki Ishigaki,Ko Yamamoto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于逆运动学和顺应性优化控制的框架，用于具有刚柔混合链接系统的人形机器人。通过前向动力学仿真和实验验证了该方法在站立、跳跃和行走动作中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理灵活性不仅对人类运动至关重要，而且对于机器人运动也非常重要。虽然结合被动和柔性元素的人形机器人可以在动态运动中储存能量并减轻冲击，但这种混合链接系统的运动设计和控制却极具挑战性。

Method: 研究者们提出了一种新的控制框架，它基于逆运动学与顺应性优化，并采用分段常应变(PCS)模型来表示柔性结构的连续变形。此外，还构建了一个包含叶片弹簧型假肢腿的双足人形机器人的实验装置，用以测量假肢的变形情况并通过动捕系统记录数据。

Result: 所提方法通过进行站立、跳跃及步行等动作的前向动力学模拟得到了验证；同时，在双足支撑与单足支撑两种情况下，通过实际实验也验证了其对于保持平衡的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的控制框架为解决刚柔混合链接人形机器人在多种动态运动下的设计与控制难题提供了一条新途径，展示了良好的应用前景。

Abstract: Physical flexibility plays a significant role not only in human motions but also in robot motions. For example, a humanoid robot with passive and flexible elements, which can be regarded as a rigid--soft hybrid-link system, has the advantages of storing energy and mitigating impact in a dynamic motion. However, the motion design and control of a hybrid-link system are challenging to achieve and have only been discussed in the literature. In this study, we propose the framework of inverse kinematics and compliance optimization-based control for a hybrid-link humanoid system that represents the continuous deformation of a flexible structure using the piece-wise constant strain (PCS) model. The proposed method was validated using forward dynamics simulations for standing, jumping, and walking motions. Then, we built an experimental setup using a biped humanoid robot with leaf-spring type prosthesis leg, in which the prosthesis deformation was measured by a motion capture system. Using this setup, we experimentally validated the proposed method for standing balance control in both double- and single-support cases.

</details>


### [79] [Targeting the Right Situations: Mutation Testing of Fuzzy-Powered Situation Hyperspace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15122)
*Nawshin Mannan Proma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于模糊逻辑的风险估计方法是否能有效关联到真实系统故障，通过在SCALOFT模拟框架内进行变异测试实验。结果表明，与随机测试相比，基于模糊逻辑引导的方法能够达到更高的变异分数，并更有效地暴露难以检测的故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于地下矿井操作设计领域（ODD）广阔且不确定，传统的验证方法如随机测试往往导致情况覆盖效率低下，未能充分揭示关键边缘案例，因此需要寻找一种更有效的测试策略来确保自主无人机的安全性。

Method: 采用基于模糊逻辑的风险优先情景生成方法，并在一个固定软件突变集合上进行了系统性的注入和评估，比较了均匀随机情境生成与模糊引导风险优先情境生成两种测试策略的效果。

Result: 实验结果显示，模糊引导的方法比随机测试更能高效地识别出难以发现的错误，并且实现了更高的变异得分。

Conclusion: 基于模糊逻辑的风险估计方法对于提高地下矿山中自主无人机的安全性验证具有显著效果，能够更好地识别潜在的系统故障。

Abstract: Ensuring the safety of autonomous Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) operating in underground mines is challenging due to the vast and uncertain Operational Design Domain (ODD). Traditional validation approaches, such as random testing, often result in inefficient situation coverage, allocating computational resources to low-risk scenarios while failing to expose critical edge cases. Building on prior work in risk-prioritized Situation Coverage, this paper investigates whether Fuzzy Logic-based risk estimation effectively correlates with real system failures. To this end, we conduct a mutation testing study within our SCALOFT simulation framework. A fixed set of software mutants is systematically injected and evaluated under two testing strategies: (i) uniformly random situation generation and (ii) Fuzzy-Guided risk-prioritized situation generation. Experimental results show that the Fuzzy-Guided approach achieves a higher mutation score and is more effective at exposing difficult-to-detect faults compared to random testing.

</details>


### [80] [RootSLAM: Square Root Bundle Adjustment for Visual-Inertial SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15157)
*Tomás Santucci,Mateo de Mayo,Daniel Cremers,Taihú Pire*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 介绍了RootSLAM，一种基于Basalt构建的多摄像头视觉惯性SLAM系统。它通过创新地将运动结构中的平方根束调整公式重新应用于多摄像头视觉惯性SLAM环境中，实现了与最先进方法相当的精度，同时运行速度最高可达20倍，特别适合于资源受限环境下的感知系统的部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决学习型SLAM系统在计算需求高以及传统几何方法虽然高效但可能牺牲一定准确性的问题，研究提出了RootSLAM。旨在保持高准确性的同时大幅度提高效率，使之更适合小型低功耗设备的应用场景，如机器人学、混合现实、自动驾驶和无人机等。

Method: 采用了一种新颖的方法来重构来自运动结构领域的平方根束调整公式，使其适应多摄像头视觉惯性SLAM设置。该方法利用了系统的位姿图，并允许全局优化器以单精度浮点数运行，从而提升性能。整个系统包括地图管理、回环检测模块、位姿图优化器及全局映射组件。

Result: 实验结果表明，RootSLAM达到了与现有技术相媲美的精度水平，同时其运行速度可以达到现有方案的20倍之快。此外，还进行了详细的基准测试与消融研究来验证各个组成部分的效果。

Conclusion: 通过引入RootSLAM，研究人员成功开发出一个既快速又准确的视觉-惯性SLAM解决方案，适用于多种资源受限的应用场合。该工作的贡献不仅在于算法层面的创新，还包括开源实现，促进了相关领域的发展。

Abstract: Efficient perception is a prerequisite for the small, low-power devices targeted by robotics, mixed reality, autonomous driving, and drone applications, all of which rely on Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) to estimate their motion within their surroundings. Learning-based SLAM systems achieve state-of-the-art accuracy but are demanding in compute, whereas classical geometry-based methods retain a strong efficiency advantage with little to no loss in accuracy. In this work we present RootSLAM, a fully-featured visual-inertial SLAM system for multi-camera setups built on Basalt. RootSLAM achieves accuracy competitive with the state of the art while running up to 20 times faster, facilitating the deployment of perception systems in resource constrained environments. We adapt the square-root bundle adjustment formulation from structure from motion into the multi-camera visual-inertial SLAM setting through a novel reformulation that leverages the system's pose-graph, letting the global optimizer run in single-precision floating point, thus doubling its performance. We provide a thorough benchmark against the state of the art together with an ablation study of our contributions. Our new implementation, including its map management and loop detection module, pose-graph optimizer, and global mapping components, will be open-sourced.

</details>


### [81] [nanoflann: A Header-Only KD-Tree Library for Exact Nearest-Neighbor Search on Compile-Time Product Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15215)
*José Luis Blanco-Claraco*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个轻量级的C++ KD树库，用于在用户自定义的产品流形上进行精确最近邻搜索。该库支持多种基础流形，并且能够合成一种可证明是可接受的度量方法。实验表明，与对相同产品度量下的穷举扫描相比，该方法能保持100%的召回率，并且比暴力搜索快了三个数量级。此外，还提出了一种适用于点集频繁更新场景（例如滑动窗口LiDAR地图或$SE(3)$轨迹）的增量式自我平衡单树索引。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多机器人算法依赖于最近邻搜索，但不是所有的状态空间都是欧几里得的，比如方向存在于$SO(2)$或$SO(3)$中，姿态存在于$SE(2)$或$SE(3)$中。如果直接使用这些非欧氏空间的原始坐标来构建KD树，将会导致错误的结果。因此，需要开发一种能够在编译时声明的用户自定义乘积流形上执行准确最近邻搜索的方法。

Method: 研究者们创建了一个无外部依赖、仅包含头文件的C++ KD树库，它允许用户通过简单的类型别名组合基础流形（如$\mathbb{R}^n$、$SO(2)$、$SO(3)$、$S^n$等），并且自动合成一个适合这些流形结构的度量函数。这个度量函数在处理循环坐标时考虑了环绕特性，在处理单位四元数时则考虑到了双覆盖问题。此外，他们还为高动态环境下的点云数据设计了一种增量式自我平衡索引结构。

Result: 实验证明了所提方法的有效性，对于包含$m$个旋转块的数据集，相较于采用基于原始坐标的欧氏距离计算方式（其召回率约为$0.5^m$），本方法能够实现100%的正确召回率。同时，在查询速度方面，相比于暴力搜索法，新方法展现出了高达三个数量级的速度提升。对于经常变化的数据集，提出的单树索引不仅内存占用少而且更新效率更高。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种有效的解决方案，针对非欧几里得空间中的最近邻搜索问题，尤其是当涉及到复杂流形如$SO(2), SO(3), SE(2), SE(3)$等时。所开发的开源库不仅保证了搜索结果的准确性，同时也大幅度提高了搜索效率，特别是在处理大规模和/或高动态性的数据集时表现尤为出色。

Abstract: Nearest-neighbor (NN) search underlies countless robotics algorithms, but many state spaces are not Euclidean: orientations live on $SO(2)$ or $SO(3)$ and poses on $SE(2)$ or $SE(3)$, and a Euclidean KD-tree on their raw coordinates returns wrong neighbors. We present a header-only, dependency-free C++ KD-tree library for exact NN search on user-defined product manifolds declared at compile time: the space is composed from base manifolds ($\mathbb{R}^n$, $SO(2)$, $SO(3)$, $S^n$; products $SE(2)$, $SE(3)$, tori) with a single type alias, and the library synthesizes a metric whose pruning is provably admissible, wrap-aware for circular coordinates and double-cover-aware for unit quaternions. Experiments confirm exactness (recall $1.0$ vs an exhaustive scan under the same product metric), where the Euclidean-on-raw shortcut collapses to recall $\approx 0.5^{m}$ for $m$ rotation blocks, with up to three orders of magnitude speedup over brute force. We further contribute an incremental, self-balancing single-tree index for points under heavy churn (e.g. sliding-window LiDAR maps) or $SE(3)$ trajectories: bounded memory and faster updates than prior work, with an optional asynchronous tail-latency-bounding mode. The implementation is open source: https://github.com/jlblancoc/nanoflann

</details>


### [82] [Reinforcement Learning Based Heterogeneous Task Allocation Under Spatio-Temporal Environmental Uncertainty for Multi-Robot Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15237)
*Xi Lin,Yaxuan Li,Paul Szenher,Brendan Englot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于去中心化强化学习的策略网络，用于解决在海上干扰引起的不确定性条件下，具有异构能力的自主水面舰艇(ASVs)团队如何有效执行不同类型和出现时间的任务分配问题。实验表明，该策略能够实现最低完成时间，并可应用于包含100个机器人和400项任务的情境中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着近年来越来越多地部署自主水面舰艇来执行各种海洋任务，对于需要能够适应异构任务和海洋干扰的可靠任务分配策略的需求日益增长。

Method: 研究者们构建了一个多机器人任务分配问题框架，在此框架下，一组具备不同能力的ASVs需要在未知随机时空不确定性的海洋干扰条件下完成类型和出现时间各异的任务。为此，他们提出了一种基于去中心化强化学习(RL)的策略网络，旨在训练该网络以对抗因不确定性导致的观测偏差，并能够根据个体决策达成高效的任务执行行为，满足多样化的任务需求。

Result: 通过改变不同类型任务的比例、任务数量、机器人种类以及每类机器人的数量，在模拟实验中对所提出的策略及四种基线方法进行了评估。结果表明，所提出的策略达到了最短的完成时间（makespan），并且适用于涉及多达100个机器人和400项任务的情况。

Conclusion: 本研究提出的基于去中心化RL的策略为解决存在不确定性的多ASV任务分配问题提供了一种有效途径，能够在复杂环境中促进更高效的任务执行。

Abstract: Autonomous Surface Vehicles (ASVs) have been increasingly deployed to perform different kinds of maritime tasks in recent years, which motivates the need for a reliable task allocation policy that can adapt to heterogeneous tasks and maritime disturbances. In this work, we formulate a multi-robot task allocation problem in which a team of ASVs with heterogeneous capabilities needs to complete tasks of different types and appearance times under unknown stochastic spatio-temporal uncertainties from maritime disturbances. We propose a decentralized Reinforcement Learning (RL) based policy network that is trained to be robust towards uncertainty-induced observation deviations, and is able to perform individual decision making that leads to efficient execution behaviors toward heterogeneous task requirements. We evaluate the proposed policy and four baseline methods in simulated experiments with varied proportions of different tasks types, numbers of tasks, robot species and robots per species. Experiment results show that our proposed policy achieves the lowest makespan and can be deployed to address scenarios with 100 robots and 400 tasks.

</details>


### [83] [Embodiment-Aware Inference-Time Search for Human Reaction Selection in Humanoid Retargeting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15274)
*Yongpeng Cao,Masahiro Hirano,Yuji Yamakawa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种在推理时搜索反应的框架，该框架利用生成反应模型进行随机采样，并同时考虑反应质量和人形机器人实体的可行性。实验结果表明，所提出的框架在保持人类反应质量的同时，提高了针对不同实体重定向的人形机器人反应的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练类人机器人学习人类行为需要昂贵的数据收集过程，而且人机交互数据的收集可能更加困难和成本高昂。为每个类人机器人实体训练专门的反应模型也可能非常耗时且资源密集。人类到类人机器人的动作重定向提供了一个更具扩展性的替代方案，允许将人与人之间的反应数据作为不同类人机器人实体的通用动作表示。然而，由于人类与类人机器人之间存在形态学和运动学上的差异，在人类动作空间中优选的反应可能并不适合特定机器人的执行。

Method: 提出一种基于生成反应模型的随机采样的推理时间反应搜索框架，该框架综合考虑了反应质量和类人机器人实体的可行性。给定观察到的互动，框架会生成多个合理的反应候选者。每个候选者都被重新定位到目标类人机器人上，并使用互补的互动标准和特定于实体的可行性准则进行评估，从而能够在符合目标实体物理约束的情况下选择候选者。

Result: 实验结果显示，所提出的框架能够保持整体的人类反应质量，同时提高针对不同实体重定向的人形机器人反应的可行性。人形机器人重定向可行性提高了1.61%。此外，在78.98%的样本中，人类空间和G1最佳候选者的选择不一致，这表明了实质性的实体依赖性。

Conclusion: 通过引入这种新的反应搜索框架，可以更好地适应不同类人机器人实体的需求，而无需对反应生成器进行再训练。该方法为人机交互研究提供了新途径，使得从人类行为到类人机器人行为的转换更加高效可行。

Abstract: Training humanoid robots to learn human behaviors requires expensive data collection, and human-robot interaction data can be even more difficult and costly to collect. Training a specialized reaction model for each humanoid embodiment can also be time-consuming and resource-intensive. Human-to-humanoid motion retargeting provides a more scalable alternative, allowing human-human reaction data to serve as a common motion representation for different humanoid embodiments. However, due to morphological and kinematic differences between humans and humanoid robots, a reaction preferred in human motion space may not be the most suitable one for execution on a particular robot embodiment.
  In this work, we propose an inference-time reaction search framework that leverages stochastic sampling from a generative reaction model and jointly considers reaction quality and humanoid embodiment feasibility. Given the observed interaction, our framework generates multiple plausible reaction candidates. Each candidate is retargeted to the target humanoid and evaluated using complementary interaction and embodiment-specific feasibility criteria, allowing candidate selection under the physical constraints of the target embodiment. Since the reaction generator remains frozen, the framework can adapt to different humanoid embodiments at inference time without retraining. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed framework maintains overall human reaction quality while improving the feasibility of retargeted humanoid reactions across different embodiments. The proposed pipeline improves humanoid retargeting feasibility by 1.61%. Moreover, human-space and G1 best-candidate selections disagree in 78.98% of samples, demonstrating substantial embodiment dependence. Code will be released.

</details>


### [84] [ProWorld: Multi-View World Model with Proprioceptive Feedback for Closed-Loop Policy Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15294)
*Zijun Wang,Tianyi Yan,Haokun Lin,Panwen Hu,Bohang He,Siyu Ren,Xiaoyu Guo,Weijia Liufu,Shuoshuo Xue,Fengjiao Chen,Zipeng Ye,Xuezhi Cao,Zutao Jiang,Hanhui Li,Xiaodan Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种名为ProWorld的动作条件世界模型，用于评估机器人操作策略。该模型能够预测多视角视频和机器人状态，并将两者反馈给策略以实现重复交互。通过结合视觉-本体感受反馈与失败感知训练，ProWorld在生成环境与真实成功率之间达到了高度的相关性（皮尔逊相关系数0.95，斯皮尔曼相关系数0.94），表明其作为策略评估工具的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理试验中反复测试机器人操作策略的成本很高。研究旨在探索是否可以使用一种动作条件下的世界模型来作为闭环测试环境，这种环境中的策略成功率能够很好地反映实际机器人上的表现。

Method: 提出了ProWorld模型，它是一个联合预测多视角视频和机器人状态的世界模型，然后将这些信息反馈给冻结的策略进行连续互动。该模型包括一个用于联合视频-状态流预测的共享transformer以及Shared-Time View Encoding技术，用以区分不同摄像机同时保持时间同步。此外，通过增加从高保真模拟中收集的失败交互数据来减少导致误判成功的交互幻觉。

Result: ProWorld获得了TriWorldBench评分中最高的总体得分。对于三种策略和四个局部操作任务，它实现了生成成功率与实际成功率之间高达0.95的皮尔逊相关性和0.94的斯皮尔曼相关性。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持了基于联合视觉-本体感觉反馈及考虑到失败情况训练的世界模型可以有效地作为策略评价的基础。

Abstract: Evaluating robot manipulation policies through repeated physical trials is costly. We study whether an action-conditioned world model can serve as a closed-loop test environment whose policy success rates track those on real robots. Beyond realistic video, the model must return the camera views and proprioceptive state needed for the policy's next action and preserve failed-action outcomes to avoid overestimating policy capability. We introduce ProWorld, a world model that jointly predicts multi-view video and robot state, then feeds both back to a frozen policy for repeated interaction. ProWorld comprises a shared transformer for joint video-state flow prediction and Shared-Time View Encoding to distinguish cameras while preserving temporal synchronization. To reduce interaction hallucinations that turn missed grasps into successful lifts, we augment training with failed interactions collected in high-fidelity simulation, reducing inflated success rates in the generated environment. ProWorld achieves the highest overall TriWorldBench score among the compared methods. Across three policies and four local manipulation tasks, it attains a Pearson correlation of 0.95 and a Spearman correlation of 0.94 between generated and real success rates. These results support joint visual-proprioceptive feedback and failure-aware training as a basis for world-model-based policy evaluation.

</details>


### [85] [RM-WAM: Reliable Memory-Augmented World Action Models for Long-Horizon Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15305)
*Cui Miao,Junhao Yu,Ping Liu,Ning Yang,Xuhua Chen,He Zheng,Xiaodong Wang,Tao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种可靠的增强记忆世界行动模型RM-WAM，通过区分短期视觉状态记忆和潜在事件记忆，并采用反事实记忆学习方案来估计历史记忆的上下文依赖性效用，以提高长期任务中的机器人操作可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大多数方法主要基于当前观察做出决策，这限制了它们在需要考虑不同交互历史和任务阶段的长期任务中的可靠性。直接引入历史观察可能导致信息陈旧、阶段不匹配或错误污染，从而导致世界预测不准确和闭环误差累积。

Method: 提出了RM-WAM模型，该模型将记忆视为上下文相关的预测资源，而非普遍有用的历史背景。它利用互补的短期视觉状态记忆与潜在事件记忆来表示历史，前者捕捉最近的交互动态，后者记录更长期的状态转换。此外，通过反事实记忆学习方案调整可访问的历史，同时保持当前输入和预测目标不变，让模型能够学习到关于历史记忆的上下文依赖性效用估计。基于这一估计，一个感知效用的门控机制可以自适应地控制向世界-动作骨干中注入的记忆量。

Result: 实验表明，RM-WAM强调了在长期任务中可靠且有选择性的记忆利用的重要性。

Conclusion: RM-WAM通过引入一种新的方式来处理历史信息，提高了机器人在执行长时间任务时的性能，证明了在这样的场景下可靠并有选择性地使用记忆对于改进未来视觉状态和动作预测至关重要。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) improve robotic manipulation by jointly predicting future visual states and actions, yet most existing methods make decisions primarily from the current observation. This limits their reliability in long-horizon tasks, where visually similar states may correspond to different interaction histories and task stages. Moreover, directly incorporating historical observations can introduce stale, stage-mismatched, or error-contaminated information, leading to inaccurate world prediction and accumulated closed-loop errors. We propose RM-WAM, a reliable memory-augmented World Action Model that treats memory as a context-dependent predictive resource rather than universally useful historical context. RM-WAM represents history using complementary short-term visual state memory and latent event memory, which respectively capture recent interaction dynamics and longer-term state transitions. A counterfactual memory learning scheme varies the accessible history while keeping the current inputs and prediction targets fixed, enabling the model to learn a context-dependent utility estimate for historical memory. Based on this estimate, a utility-aware gating mechanism adaptively controls memory injection into the world-action backbone. Experiments demonstrate that RM-WAM highlights the importance of reliable and selective memory utilization in long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [86] [An Open MuJoCo Simulation Framework for a Quadrotor Carrying a Tendon-Driven Soft Continuum Arm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15308)
*Haitham El-Hussieny*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个基于MuJoCo物理引擎的开源仿真框架，用于模拟配备有肌腱驱动软体连续机械臂的四旋翼飞行器。该框架支持多种仿真场景，并能够以接近实时的速度运行，所有模型、控制器和脚本均已公开发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将空中机器人与软体连续操作器结合在一起有望实现与环境的安全柔顺交互，但自由飞行基座与高度可变形、欠驱动手臂之间的耦合动力学难以在没有合适模拟器的情况下进行研究。

Method: 论文描述了一个基于MuJoCo物理引擎构建的开源仿真框架，适用于带有底部肌腱驱动软体连续臂的四旋翼无人机。该手臂被建模为由十二个节段组成的伪刚性链，通过成对正交的柔性铰链连接。三个间隔120度且贯穿每个节段的肌腱可以在任何方向上弯曲它。采用SE(3)上的几何跟踪控制器来操控飞行器；其转子分配矩阵直接从模型描述生成。一个封闭形式的肌腱张力分配方案保持每个张力在其限制内，并在所有方向提供相同的弯矩。

Result: 该框架在四种仿真场景下进行了评估：一个包含八字形路径和肌腱驱动尖端扫掠的36秒任务，姿态增益研究，准静态弯曲工作空间特征分析，以及施加于手臂尖端的冲击扰动。使用标称增益时，飞行器的任务跟踪具有3.1厘米的RMS位置误差，而手臂尖端相对于机体轴线的最大摆动距离可达37厘米。增益研究表明，低带宽姿态回路会让手臂激发持续振荡。模拟运行速度大约是实时速度的15倍。

Conclusion: 提出了一个开放源代码仿真框架，用于研究四旋翼飞行器携带的肌腱驱动软体连续臂的动力学特性，该框架经过不同场景下的测试验证了其有效性，并且提供了全部的建模选择和参数，以及再现图形的所有模型、控制器和脚本。

Abstract: Combining aerial robots with soft continuum manipulators promises safe, compliant interaction with the environment, but the coupled dynamics of a free-flying base and a highly deformable, underactuated arm are difficult to study without a suitable simulator. This paper describes an open-source simulation framework, built on the MuJoCo physics engine, for a quadrotor that carries a tendon-driven soft continuum arm underneath its body. The arm is modelled as a pseudo-rigid-body chain of twelve segments joined by pairs of orthogonal compliant hinges. Three tendons, spaced \SI{120}{\degree} apart and routed through every segment, bend it in any direction. A geometric tracking controller on $SE(3)$ flies the vehicle; its rotor allocation matrix is generated directly from the model description. A closed-form tendon-tension allocation keeps every tension within its limits and gives the same bending moment in every direction. We report the full set of modelling choices and parameters, and we evaluate the framework in four simulated scenarios: a \SI{36}{\second} mission with a figure-eight and a tendon-driven tip sweep, an attitude-gain study, a quasi-static characterization of the bending workspace, and an impulsive disturbance applied to the arm tip. With the nominal gains the vehicle tracks the mission with \SI{3.1}{\centi\meter} RMS position error while the arm tip swings up to \SI{37}{\centi\meter} from the body axis. The gain study shows that a low-bandwidth attitude loop lets the arm excite sustained oscillations. The simulation runs about $15\times$ faster than real time on a laptop CPU. All models, controllers and scripts that reproduce the figures are released.

</details>


### [87] [CoLA: Multi-VLA Coordination via Learned Adapters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15337)
*Ahaskar Kashyap,Jiawen Zong,Boyu Ma,Chuhao Zhou,Jingliang Li,Stefano V. Albrecht,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出了CoLA（通过学习适配器进行协调），这是一种在多代理共享工作空间时促进协作的方法。它保持每个代理的视觉-语言-行动(VLA)主干冻结，仅训练轻量级消息通道和每臂扩散动作头，实现了完全去中心化的执行。实验表明，在信息为私有时，消息通道对于任务成功至关重要；并且CoLA能够以较少的可训练参数达到与微调基线相当的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉-语言-行动(VLA)模型主要作为孤立的单代理策略部署。当多个这样的代理共享一个工作区时，它们可以通过观察彼此或交换消息来进行隐式或显式协调，但对于何时需要明确沟通尚不清楚。

Method: 引入了CoLA (Coordination via Learned Adapters)，该方法保持每个代理的VLA骨干网络固定，并在此基础上训练了一个轻量级的消息通道和针对每个手臂的动作生成头部。整个过程采用完全分散的方式执行。

Result: 在三个不同可见度的任务中测试了CoLA的效果。结果显示，在信息私密的情况下，消息传递对于任务完成特别重要。比如在一个遮挡托盘传递任务中，只有特定代理能看到盒子的颜色标记，此时使用CoLA达到了76.0%的成功率，而如果在整个训练过程中不使用消息则成功率仅为1.3%。此外，在双臂及三臂传递任务中，如果没有共同的上视摄像头，则去除消息交流会显著降低成功率。

Conclusion: CoLA架构不仅适用于不同的VLA基础模型，而且能够在仅有少量可训练参数的情况下达到与经过LoRA微调基准相仿甚至更好的表现，特别是在选择正确托盘的任务上显示出明显优势。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are typically deployed as isolated single-agent policies. When several such agents share a workspace, they can coordinate implicitly, by observing one another, or explicitly, by exchanging messages, but it remains unclear when explicit communication becomes necessary. We introduce CoLA (Coordination via Learned Adapters), which keeps each agent's VLA backbone frozen and trains a lightweight message channel and a per-arm diffusion action head on top, with fully decentralised execution. Across three tasks that differ in how much each agent can observe, the channel matters only when information is private. On an occluded tray handover, where the box's colour marker is visible to one agent only, CoLA reaches 76.0% success versus 1.3% with messages zeroed throughout training. On two-arm and three-arm handover tasks, removing messages has no measurable effect with a shared overhead camera (90.6% vs. 88.3% at two arms; 63.5% vs. 60.0% at three), but lowers success on the two-arm task's wrist-only variant (74.8% vs. 64.3%). This architecture generalises across different VLA backbones as well. With a frozen $π_{0.5}$ backbone on the same occluded tray task, CoLA matches the overall success rate of a LoRA-fine-tuned baseline (37.3% vs. 34.7%) using a fraction of the trainable parameters. When CoLA places the box, it selects the correct tray 71.8% of the time, whereas the independently fine-tuned baseline, whose placing arm never sees the marker, selects it at chance level (34.7% against 33.3%).

</details>


### [88] [LERR-Nav: Learning to Coordinate Exploration, Recovery, and Reminiscing for Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15362)
*Jingzhi Huang,Junkai Huang,Yuhang Peng,Keyu Mu,Hailong Huang,Haoyang Yang,Renbo Luo,Lujia Wang,Yi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为LERR-Nav的对象导航框架，通过学习到的高级策略来协调探索、恢复和回忆四种互补的导航策略，以解决在未知环境中导航时遇到的问题。实验表明该方法在HM3D和MP3D数据集上达到了最先进的导航性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于大语言模型（LLM）和视觉-语言模型（VLM）的进步虽然提高了语义映射、空间记忆以及路径点选择的能力，但成功导航还需要识别停滞进度、摆脱无效行为及重新访问被忽视的目标提示等问题。因此，研究者受到失败导航案例分析的启发，提出了一个能够通过学习到的高级策略来协调多种专门导航策略执行的新框架。

Method: LERR-Nav框架包括四个互补的导航策略：价值函数引导的探索、LLM引导的探索、从停滞或重复行为中恢复、以及基于记忆的回忆。为了支持具有历史意识的协调，保持了一个包含所有策略执行文本摘要与地图序列滑动窗口的执行记忆。高级策略的学习是通过首先使用Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct初始化一个高级视觉-语言代理，然后经过两阶段课程训练实现的。第一阶段利用地图中心的监督微调建立空间基础；第二阶段则对可变长度的导航片段进行监督微调，根据当前地图和混合执行记忆决定接下来要调用哪种导航策略。

Result: 在HM3D和MP3D上的实验显示了最新的导航表现，并且消融研究表明了各个组成部分的有效性。

Conclusion: LERR-Nav提供了一种新的方式来处理对象导航中的挑战，特别是对于需要在新环境中泛化的场景。通过结合不同类型的导航策略并通过学习到的政策有效地协调它们，这种方法不仅提升了导航效率还增强了系统处理复杂环境变化的能力。

Abstract: Object Navigation in unseen environments remains a fundamental challenge in embodied AI, as it requires agents to generalize visual and spatial reasoning to novel layouts. While recent advances in large language model (LLM) and vision-language model (VLM) have enhanced semantic mapping, spatial memory, and waypoint selecting for navigation, successful navigation requires more than selecting the next promising waypoint. A robot must also recognize stalled progress, escape unproductive behaviors, and revisit overlooked target cues. Addressing these challenges requires both specialized navigation strategies and a high-level policy that coordinates their execution. Motivated by the analysis of failed navigation episodes, we propose LERR-Nav, an object navigation framework that coordinates exploration, recovery, and reminiscing through a learned high-level policy. The framework comprises four complementary navigation strategies: value-function-guided exploration, LLM-guided exploration, recovery from stalled or repetitive behavior, and memory-guided reminiscing. To support history-aware coordination, we maintain the execution memory that preserves textual summaries of all strategy executions alongside a sliding window of map sequences. To learn the coordination policy, we initialize a high-level vision-language agent from Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct and train it through a two-stage curriculum. The first stage uses map-centric supervised fine-tuning (SFT) to establish spatial grounding. The second stage performs SFT on variable-length navigation segments to learn which navigation strategy to invoke next based on current maps and the hybrid execution memory. Experiments on HM3D and MP3D demonstrate state-of-the-art navigation performance, while ablation studies validate the contributions of individual components.

</details>


### [89] [Have You Seen It Clearly? Uncertainty-Guided Belief Refinement for Zero-Shot Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15375)
*Jingzhi Huang,Junkai Huang,Yuhang Peng,Keyu Mu,Jiaru Zhong,Yi Wang,Lujia Wang,Lap-Pui Chau,Siyang Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为RefBeliefMap的不确定性引导映射框架，用于在零样本对象导航（ZSON）任务中改进目标定位信念，并基于此框架开发了RefBeliefNav来优化探索和重访策略。实验显示该方法在HM3D和MP3D数据集上达到了最先进性能，并通过实际机器人测试证明了其实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的零样本对象导航方法未能充分解决证据是否足以支持导航决策的问题，以及新证据如何改变先前访问位置的搜索价值。

Method: 提出了RefBeliefMap框架，它能够区分目标位置信念与空间和语义证据不确定性，并通过联合建模观察内的不确定性和跨观察差异来评估证据渠道的可靠性。此外，基于改进后的地图，开发了RefBeliefNav，以综合评价未探索边界和历史路点的目标发现潜力、预期信息增益及导航成本。

Result: 在HM3D和MP3D数据集上的实验表明，所提方法相比其他零样本基线具有最先进的表现。消融研究进一步分析了各个模块的作用。真实世界中的Unitree Go2四足机器人实验也展示了从模拟到现实的良好迁移能力。

Conclusion: 通过引入考虑不确定性因素的RefBeliefMap和利用统一目标函数进行探索-重访协调的RefBeliefNav，本文为解决零样本对象导航挑战提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Zero-Shot Object Navigation (ZSON) requires an agent to locate target objects in unfamiliar environments without additional training for the test categories or environments. Recent approaches leverage large language models and vision-language models to construct spatial representations and guide exploration. However, whether the available evidence sufficiently supports a navigation choice, and how new evidence changes the search value of previously visited locations, remain insufficiently addressed. We propose RefBeliefMap, an uncertainty-guided mapping framework that distinguishes target-location belief from spatial and semantic evidence uncertainty. By jointly modeling intra-observation uncertainty and inter-observation disagreement over a sliding memory window, RefBeliefMap assesses the reliability of both evidence channels. When competing frontiers remain insufficiently resolved, these uncertainty estimates guide active observation and semantic reasoning to refine the evidence supporting target-location beliefs before navigation commitment. Building on the refined map, we introduce RefBeliefNav, which jointly evaluates unexplored frontiers and historical waypoints according to their target-finding potential, expected information gain, and navigation cost. This unified objective allows historical locations to be reconsidered as new evidence changes their search value, coordinating exploration and revisitation. Experiments on HM3D and MP3D demonstrate state-of-the-art performance among the evaluated zero-shot baselines, with ablation studies examining the contributions of modules. Real-world experiments on a Unitree Go2 quadruped robot further demonstrate the sim-to-real transferability of our method.

</details>


### [90] [A Reinforcement Learning Framework for Safety-Aware Haptic Shared Control in Surgical Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15422)
*Zihao Zhang,Karl Handwerker,Balint Varga*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过使用多智能体强化学习框架，研究了在手术机器人中触觉共享控制下几何安全选择对任务执行和力协调的影响。实验比较了几种不同的方法，包括MADDPG、rMAPPO以及带图解约束的方法，并得出了各自在仿真与实际操作者输入下的表现差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在手术机器人的触觉共享控制系统中，尽管已有人类侧与机器人侧力量结合的研究，但关于几何安全选择如何影响任务执行和力协调的问题仍不清楚。本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 研究采用了无需完整分析交互模型的无模型多智能体强化学习框架来支持联合策略适应，对比了MADDPG、rMAPPO与一种基于图解约束的方法在共同几何任务定义下的表现。

Result: 仿真结果显示，rMAPPO通常获得了最高的回报和跟踪性能；而基于图解约束的方法产生了更加保守的动作及更低的几何暴露度，在几何转移后未观察到碰撞或危险区域暴露。当引入真实操作者输入时，采用固定阻抗训练的策略表现出更快的速度并在所有记录试验中均取得成功。

Conclusion: 该工作为研究触觉共享控制中人类控制表示及处理约束之间的权衡提供了基准。

Abstract: Haptic shared control in surgical robotics combines human-side and robot-side forces at a shared end effector while preserving operator involvement. However, the effects of geometric safety choices on task execution and force coordination remain unclear. We model the interaction as a two-agent cooperative Markov game under centralized training and decentralized execution, comparing learned human control with a task-tuned fixed-impedance baseline. This model-free multi-agent reinforcement learning framework supports joint policy adaptation without requiring a complete analytical interaction model. It compares MADDPG and rMAPPO with an epigraph-constrained approach under common geometric task definitions. In simulation, rMAPPO generally achieved the highest return and tracking performance, whereas the epigraph approach produced more conservative motions and lower geometric exposure, including no observed collision or dangerous-region exposure after geometry transfer. With real operator input, fixed-impedance-trained policies were faster and successful in all recorded trials. Policies trained with a learned partner exhibited greater command opposition, while the fixed epigraph condition produced the most aligned commands. This work provides a benchmark for studying human-control representation and constraint-handling trade-offs in haptic shared control.

</details>


### [91] [Exploring the Limits of Combinatorial Generalization in Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15425)
*Xupeng Zhang,Yantai Yang,Chang Guo,Zhaokai Yin,Zhipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究引入了Hierarchical Subtask Execution (HSE)框架和RoboCombine基准，用于在没有组合技能训练轨迹的情况下评估技能重组。通过Pose Robust Task Initialization (PRTI)策略来解决起始状态差距问题，从而提高长视野操作中技能重组的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
