To bridge scene-level dynamics with robotic manipulation, we propose a modular 3D flow learning framework with dual-level flow supervision. Specifically, point-level flow guides the model to forecast the future displacements of task-critical 3D points, while TCP-level flow aggregates these motions to align with the robot's TCP trajectories. This dual-level flow supervision encourages the model to focus on semantically meaningful dynamic regions, effectively coupling scene-level motion with precise control signals. Extensive evaluations across simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate that FlowVLA consistently improves manipulation performance, zero-shot robustness, and optimizes data efficiency. FlowVLA adds only lightweight auxiliary heads during training and discards them at inference. Therefore, FlowVLA introduces no additional 3D sensor inputs, architectural modifications, or inference-time computation.

</details>


### [123] [Extending Wire-Driven Parallel Robots to Multi-Floating-Link Systems with an Inter-Body Wire](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16173)
*Shintaro Inoue,Kento Kawaharazuka,Temma Suzuki,Sota Yuzaki,Keita Yoneda,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究通过引入体间线实现了多浮动链路的并行线驱动，展示了其在操作大型物体和救援支持等更广泛任务中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的并行线驱动系统通常假设单个末端执行器，由于共享工作空间中线与链接之间的干扰，很难同时处理多个浮动链接。

Method: 本研究通过引入一种体间线来实现多浮动链接的并行线驱动，并通过实际机器人实验验证了这一方法的有效性。

Result: 两个身体能够支撑、抬起一个人体模型，并将其转移到担架上作为救援动作。

Conclusion: 并行线驱动可以从单一末端执行器扩展到多浮动链接系统，并表明它具有操作大型物体和提供救援支持等更广泛任务的潜力。

Abstract: Parallel wire-driven systems are a framework in which multiple wires are connected to an end effector, and the end effector is driven by the tensions of those wires. Because they can transmit large forces, up to the breaking strength of the wires, over lengths on the order of several tens of meters, they can realize powerful actuation in large workspaces. In recent years, studies have also investigated connecting wires to external environments such as trees and columns and forming parallel wire actuation on site. However, conventional parallel wire-driven systems often assume a single end effector, and because of interference between wires and links in a shared workspace, it has been difficult to handle multiple floating links simultaneously. In this study, we realize multi-floating-link parallel wire actuation by introducing an inter-body wire. In real-robot experiments, the two bodies supported and lifted a mannequin and transferred it onto a stretcher as a rescue motion. This shows that parallel wire actuation can be extended from a single end effector to a multi-floating-link system, and indicates its potential for broader tasks such as the manipulation of large objects and rescue support.

</details>


### [124] [One-Shot Learning for Double-Shot Latte Art: Adapting Cobot Pouring Skills with Dynamic Movement Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16179)
*Zain Khan,Victor Hernandez Moreno,Dinh Tung Le*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究通过将一次拿铁拉花演示分解为五个阶段，每个阶段用独立的笛卡尔动态运动基元(CDMP)编码，显著提高了拉花图案的精确度和适应不同大小杯子的能力。相比单一CDMP方法，此法在降低过渡终点误差、改善图案形状以及保持倾倒角度方面表现更优，并且能够应用于其他需要分阶段执行的任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提高拿铁艺术这种既复杂又具商业价值任务的操作精度与灵活性，研究提出了一种基于单次学习系统的解决方案，旨在通过细分操作步骤来增强再现性和可调整性。

Method: 采用一种单次学习系统，其中单个咖啡师的演示被划分为五个功能上不同的阶段，每个阶段都被编码为具有自身参数的独立笛卡尔动态运动基元（CDMP）。这种方法允许对每个阶段进行优化处理，从而提高整体表现。

Result: 相比于使用单一CDMP作为基准的方法，按阶段编码的方式可以将过渡点错误减少3.2至4.5倍，艺术笔触形状误差减少2.7倍，在倒水阶段将壶倾斜度再现到低于1度的均方根值。此外，所学得的倒水技巧可以通过简单的空间偏移和比例配方从280毫升扩展到400毫升的杯子，无需重新演示。

Conclusion: 基于阶段的CDMP方法不仅有效提升了拿铁艺术的表现质量，还展示了其对于任何具有独特空间或时间需求阶段的任务的通用性，表明了该技术在适应不同任务要求方面的潜力。

Abstract: Latte art is a complex task requiring dexterity and precision, making it a challenging and commercially relevant task. A one-shot learning system is employed in which a single barista demonstration is segmented into five functionally distinct phases, each encoded as an independent Cartesian Dynamic Movement Primitive (CDMP) with its own parameters. Against a single-CDMP baseline, per-phase encoding reduces transition endpoint error by 3.2-4.5 times and art-stroke shape error by 2.7 times, reproducing jug tilt to below 1 degree RMS in the pouring phases. The learnt pour scales from a 280mL to a 400mL cup through a single spatial offset and a scaled recipe, with no re-demonstration. The phase-based CDMP approach generalises beyond latte art to any demonstration task whose phases have distinct spatial or temporal requirements whilst demonstrating the ability to adapt reproductions to suit different task requirements.

</details>


### [125] [MSC: Multi-Stage Caster-aware Control Framework with Uncertainty-Aware NMPC and RL-based Execution Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16199)
*Jinho Park,Jeongtae Lee,Jeonghyeok Lim,Keun Ha Choi,Kyung-Soo Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为多阶段转向轮感知控制（MSC）的层级框架，通过结合不确定性感知NMPC与基于强化学习的状态估计执行层，有效改善了低速或高曲率操作中因转向轮错位导致的问题，从而提高了跟踪鲁棒性和状态估计准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由旋转的转向轮由于其机械简单性和承载能力，在工业AMR/SDV中被广泛采用。然而，在低速或高曲率操控过程中，转向轮错位会导致强烈的接触非线性问题，扩大了指令$(v, ω)$与实际运动之间的差距。现有针对转向轮的方法高度依赖于状态估计的可靠性，在不确定性过渡状态下容易造成不稳定执行。

Method: 研究提出了多阶段转向轮感知控制（MSC），一种规划-执行集成的层级框架，包含两个核心机制：1）一个不确定性感知NMPC（CU-MPC），利用预测不确定性动态调度转向轮感知成本和输入正则化，抑制过于激进的操作；2）一个基于强化学习的执行层，在部分可观测条件下估计并预测转向轮状态，将高层级速度命令转化为适应当前接触条件的实际可执行轮速。

Result: Isaac Sim仿真及真实机器人验证表明，相比现有的解耦补偿方法，MSC在显著提高转向轮状态估计精度、减少错位以及增强对转向轮敏感操作中的跟踪鲁棒性方面具有明显优势。

Conclusion: 提出的MSC框架为解决转向轮错位带来的挑战提供了一个有效的解决方案，特别是在需要精确导航与控制的应用场景下。

Abstract: Freely swiveling casters are widely adopted in industrial AMRs/SDVs for their mechanical simplicity and load capacity. However, caster misalignment during low-speed or high-curvature maneuvers induces strong contact nonlinearities, widening the planning-execution gap between commanded $(v, ω)$ and realized motion. Existing caster-aware strategies are highly sensitive to state-estimation reliability, causing unstable execution in uncertain transition regimes. To address this, we present Multi-Stage Caster-aware Control (MSC), a planning-execution integrated hierarchical framework featuring two core mechanisms. First, an uncertainty-aware NMPC (CU-MPC) dynamically schedules caster-aware costs and input regularization using predictive uncertainty to suppress overly aggressive maneuvers. Second, a reinforcement learning-based execution layer estimates and predicts caster states under partial observability, transforming high-level velocity commands into physically executable wheel speeds adapted to current contact conditions. Validations in Isaac Sim and real robots demonstrate that MSC offers a clear comparative advantage over existing decoupled compensation approaches by significantly improving caster-state estimation accuracy, minimizing misalignment, and enhancing tracking robustness in caster-sensitive maneuvers.

</details>


### [126] [Embodied Skill Bench: Benchmarking How Well Agents with Embodied Skills Perform on Physical Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16238)
*Zhi Han,Zihan Guo,Chenxi Zeng,Zicai Cui,Yue Xin,Ming Zhou,Dongzhan Zhou,Weinan Zhang,Yang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一个名为EmbodiedSkillBench的评估框架，旨在测试不同模式下（包括新提出的ADR模式）机器人技能对多种LLM代理执行物理任务的影响。结果显示，对于较弱的代理而言，外部技能可以大幅提高其性能；而对于较强的代理，则主要帮助在于节省资源消耗而非进一步提高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM代理越来越多地使用VLA策略、感知模型和程序性知识来控制机器人，这些模块由于接口、具身假设及使用条件的不同而难以复用，并且它们在物理任务中的好处尚不清楚。

Method: 研究者将这些模块打包成具有明确具身假设和使用条件的具身技能，从1052个资源中构建了250种行动、感知和经验技能。基于此引入了EmbodiedSkillBench，该平台允许代理在一个封闭的观察-行动循环中运行，并记录成功率、动作、令牌数以及实际耗时。它支持三种模式：原生控制、仅技能执行以及作者提出的Agent-Directed Reuse (ADR)模式，在这种模式下代理保持其原生控制，并决定是否调用、修改或忽略每个技能。

Result: 通过对四个代理在100个扰动过的LIBERO-Pro任务上的测试发现，只有当技能超越代理本身的能力时，才能对代理有所帮助。仅技能执行模式使GPT-5.6-Sol的成功率从11%提升至63%，Kimi-K3和Claude-Opus-5分别提升了约20个百分点，同时减少了58%到85%的令牌使用量。然而所有四个代理最终都落在了61%到64%之间，GPT-6-Astra在原生控制下的表现从92%下降到了61%。这表明，随着代理能力增强，具身技能的作用从提供缺失的能力转变为降低成本。通过允许选择性调用技能的ADR模式，GPT-6-Astra恢复到了85%的成功率，比原生控制低7个百分点，但使用了少14.1%的令牌和少11.9%的时间。结合Jev作为选择运动方向和夹持器动作的技能，成功率达到了81%，同时令牌使用量减少了一半，时间减少了57%。

Conclusion: 研究表明，对于已经很强大的代理来说，具身技能更多是在于优化成本而非显著提高性能。通过提出的新ADR模式，既保留了代理原有的控制权又能有效利用外部技能，从而在降低计算成本的同时维持了较高的任务完成度。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly control robots using VLA policies, perception models, and procedural knowledge. These modules are difficult to reuse because their interfaces, embodiment assumptions, and conditions of use differ, and their benefits on physical tasks remain unclear. We package them as embodied skills that extend agent skills with explicit embodiment assumptions and conditions of use, building 250 action, perception, and experience skills from 1,052 resources. On this basis we introduce EmbodiedSkillBench, which runs an agent in a closed observation-action loop and records success, actions, tokens, and wall-clock time. It supports three modes: native control, skill-only execution, and Agent-Directed Reuse (ADR), a mode we propose in which the agent keeps its native controls and decides for each skill whether to invoke, modify, or ignore it. Across four agents on 100 perturbed LIBERO-Pro tasks, we find that skills help an agent only as far as they exceed what it can already do. Skill-only execution lifts GPT-5.6-Sol from 11% to 63% and Kimi-K3 and Claude-Opus-5 by about 20 points, while cutting their token usage by 58% to 85%. Yet all four agents then end up between 61% and 64%, and GPT-6-Astra falls from 92% under native control to 61%. This suggests that as agents grow stronger, the role of embodied skills shifts from supplying missing capability to reducing cost: GPT-6-Astra already solves most tasks through native control, so restricting it to skills removes more than it adds. Under ADR, which lets it keep native control and call skills selectively, GPT-6-Astra recovers to 85%, within 7 points of native control, while using 14.1% fewer tokens and 11.9% less time. With Jev as a skill choosing motion directions and gripper actions, it reaches 81% with 50% fewer tokens and 57% less time. The code is available at https://github.com/HanZhi306/EmbodiedSkill-Bench.

</details>


### [127] [Same Goal, Different Words: Spatial Instruction Sensitivity in World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16242)
*Ali-Adeeb Abbas,Esmaeil Seraj,Behrad Toghi,Taskin Padir*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究评估了864次实验中世界-动作模型在不同描述下保持目标一致性的能力，发现通过参考对象而非移动对象来描述关系平均降低了12.2个百分点的成功率。政策的语言理解测试显示添加图像可能会干扰纯文本的正确解释。这些结果表明需要对世界-动作模型的方向控制、语言理解和基础进行更全面的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探讨世界-动作模型是否能在等价的空间目标描述之间保持预期操作的一致性，以及这种一致性如何受到指令重述的影响。

Method: 通过在四个RoboLab场景中对三种模型执行864次展开实验，固定初始状态和任务目标不变，比较了基于不同参照物描述空间关系的效果，并单独测试了策略的语言理解能力。

Result: 结果显示，以参考对象而不是被移动的对象来描述关系会使得稳定放置的成功率平均下降12.2个百分点；策略的语言理解测试揭示了关系反转现象，并指出加入图片可能扰乱原本正确的纯文本解读；此外，移动错误的对象有时也能满足请求的关系，导致从任何时间点到回合结束时的稳定放置率由43.2%降至21.8%。

Conclusion: 研究建议对于世界-动作模型的指导能力、语言理解和根基进行更加彻底的评价，包括检查跨等效指令的一致性、验证是否移动了预期的对象及所要求的布局是否能持续到最后。

Abstract: World-action models should preserve the intended manipulation across equivalent descriptions of a spatial goal. We evaluate this consistency in 864 rollouts from three models across four RoboLab scenes, holding initial states and task goals fixed. Describing relations through the reference object rather than the object-to-move lowers the rate of reaching a stable placement by 12.2 percentage points on average. The effect of instruction rephrasing varies across spatial relations, so aggregate scores can hide sensitivity to wording. Standalone tests of the policies' language understanding identify relation reversals and show that adding images can disrupt otherwise correct text-only interpretations. Rollout analysis also distinguishes spatial placement from instruction following: moving the wrong object can satisfy the requested relation, and rates of stable placement fall from 43.2% at any time to 21.8% at episode end. These findings motivate a more thorough evaluation of world-action models' steerability, language understanding, and grounding. Evaluations should test consistency across equivalent instructions, verify that the intended object moved, and check whether the requested arrangement persists to the end of the rollout.

</details>


### [128] [PACE: Temporal-Role Tactile Conditioning for Chunk-Based Insertion Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16308)
*Zhiyuan Chen,Shixiong Xu,Shuai Chen,Yangwei You*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了PACE，一个针对接触丰富插入任务设计的新框架，通过将触觉反馈细分为三个时间角色以改善机器人的表现，实验证明其有效性和广泛适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于视觉触觉块的策略在处理接触丰富的插入任务时，将触觉反馈整合到单一的生成时间融合路径中，未能明确区分持续接触状态、突然接触事件以及未来接触后果。这导致机器人在执行接触密集型插入任务时无法有效地结合视觉对齐和触觉调整。

Method: 提出了一种名为PACE（Phase-Aware Contact-Event Conditioning）的时间角色触觉条件框架，用于基于块的插入策略。该框架将共享触觉流组织为三个时间角色：持续接触状态用于动作块生成的条件，突然接触事件修正生成的动作块，而接触结果预测则在训练过程中塑造动作表示。

Result: 在五个真实的机器人插入任务中，PACE达到了平均90.7%的成功率，相比仅基于视觉和生成时间触觉融合的基线方法提高了超过44个百分点；在ManipulationNet基准测试上，PACE在包括工业级0.1毫米间隙在内的六种几何间隙配置中保持了88.7%的成功率。消融实验验证了三种时间角色的互补贡献，机制分析显示PACE能够区分正常的插入进程与需要立即校正的接触事件，跨信号和跨架构实验表明这一时间角色原则适用于不同的物理反馈信号和策略主干。

Conclusion: PACE通过引入一种新的触觉信息处理方式，即按照其在任务中的时间角色来分配不同功能，显著提升了机器人在执行接触密集型插入任务时的表现。它不仅增强了机器人应对复杂接触情况的能力，而且证明了这种方法对于提高机器人操作灵活性和准确性具有普遍适用性。

Abstract: Contact-rich insertion requires robots to combine visual alignment with tactile adjustment. However, existing visuotactile chunk-based policies collapse tactile feedback into a single generation-time fusion pathway, leaving persistent contact states, abrupt contact events, and future contact consequences implicitly modeled in a single representation. We present PACE (Phase-Aware Contact-Event Conditioning), a temporal-role tactile conditioning framework for chunk-based insertion policies. PACE organizes a shared tactile stream into three temporal roles: persistent contact states condition action-chunk generation, abrupt contact events correct the generated chunk, and contact-consequence prediction shapes action representations during training. This design preserves chunk-level action continuity while assigning distinct pathways to different temporal roles of contact feedback. Across five real-robot insertion tasks, PACE achieves an average success rate of 90.7%, outperforming vision-only and generation-time tactile-fusion baselines by over 44 percentage points; on the ManipulationNet benchmark, PACE maintains 88.7% success across six geometry-clearance configurations, including an industrial-grade 0.1-mm clearance. Ablations confirm the complementary contributions of the three temporal roles, mechanistic analyses show that PACE separates normal insertion progress from contact events requiring immediate correction, and cross-signal and cross-architecture experiments indicate that this temporal-role principle extends across different physical feedback signals and policy backbones. Videos and materials are available at https://pace-insertion.github.io/.

</details>


### [129] [ReAP: From Visual Failure Recognition to Action-Conditioned Risk Prediction for Runtime Failure Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16309)
*Jingyuan Liu,Yunpeng Qing,Changxu Wei,Zhi Hou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为ReAP的未来感知运行时监控框架，它结合了视觉失败识别和动作条件下的失败预测来提前评估风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人策略在执行过程中仍然容易出错，而当前的监控系统通常只基于当前观测或策略信号估计风险，未能有效预测行动后的潜在后果。为了在行动前预估这些风险，提出了新的解决方案。

Method: ReAP首先通过成功与失败的运行数据训练一个视觉检测器，以学习与失败相关的表示空间并估算当前风险；然后使用动作条件下的预测器在这个表示空间中预测未来的状态，从而估计未来的失败风险。

Result: 实验表明，无论是在仿真还是真实世界中的多种机器人策略上，ReAP都能有效地平衡故障检测准确性与警告及时性。

Conclusion: 通过结合即时的失败识别与面向未来的风险评估机制，ReAP为提高机器人策略部署的安全性和可靠性提供了一个有效的手段。

Abstract: Despite rapid progress, generalist robot policies remain brittle during execution. Reliable deployment therefore requires runtime monitoring that provides timely warnings of failure. Existing monitors commonly estimate risk from current observations or policy signals without explicitly predicting the future consequences of proposed actions. To anticipate such risks before action execution, we introduce ReAP, a future-aware runtime monitoring framework that combines visual failure recognition with action-conditioned failure prediction. Specifically, ReAP first trains a visual detector on successful and failed rollouts to learn a failure-relevant representation space and estimate current risk. An action-conditioned predictor then forecasts the resulting future representation in this space to estimate future failure risk. At runtime, the detector and predictor provide complementary signals for current-state failure recognition and future-aware risk assessment. Experiments across diverse robot policies in simulation and the real world demonstrate that ReAP effectively balances failure-detection accuracy and warning timeliness.

</details>


### [130] [Listen Before Act: Audio-Conditioned Robot Manipulation with ListenVLA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16313)
*Jiaqi Fan,Rui Xu,Jiakun Xia,Xuyu Fan,Shuailei Ma,Yuefeng Wang,Qing Wang,Jiarui Zhang,Yili Fu,Xu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了ListenVLA，一种基于GR00T N1.7的声音引导操作策略。它结合了语义音频嵌入与声波衍生的听觉线索，并通过音频标记和特征调制来调节连续动作生成。此外，还引入了一个音频-动作绑定目标，并开发了LIBERO-Audio基准测试，以评估模型在声音导向任务中的表现。实验结果表明该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多操纵任务依赖于视觉观察无法可靠推断的听觉线索，这可能需要通过物理互动来激发。为了解决这个差距，研究旨在开发一种能够根据听觉输入指导机器人完成复杂任务的方法。

Method: ListenVLA策略使用了语义音频嵌入以及从因果音频历史中提取的声学提示，并且通过音频令牌和特征调整来调节动作生成。另外，研究者们设计了一种音频-动作绑定目标函数，利用上下文匹配的示范来将不同的听觉信号与对应的动作差异关联起来。同时，创建了LIBERO-Audio作为包含八个任务的评测基准，用于检测系统对基于声音的目标选择、响应听觉事件及揭示声学证据的物理交互能力。

Result: 实验结果表明，在LIBERO-Audio基准测试和三个实际世界操作任务上，所提出的方法表现出色。消融研究表明，绑定监督和音频调节对于提高性能起到了重要作用，而音频交换测试则显示声音确实影响了动作预测。

Conclusion: 本研究表明，通过整合听觉信息并采用专门设计的学习机制，可以显著提升机器人在执行需依赖声音的任务时的表现。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have advanced robotic manipulation by connecting visual perception and language instructions to action generation. However, many manipulation tasks depend on auditory cues that cannot be reliably inferred from visual observations and may need to be elicited through physical interaction. To address this gap, we present ListenVLA, an audio-conditioned VLA policy built on GR00T N1.7 for sound-guided manipulation. ListenVLA combines semantic audio embeddings with waveform-derived acoustic cues over a causal audio history and conditions continuous action generation through audio tokens and feature modulation. Moreover, we introduce an audio-action binding objective that uses context-matched demonstrations to link auditory differences to demonstrated action differences. The objective supervises short-horizon differences between flow-velocity predictions under different audio conditions, with shared pure-noise action inputs and fixed non-audio observations, alongside full-chunk action supervision. Furthermore, we develop LIBERO-Audio, an eight-task benchmark covering sound-based target selection, responses to auditory events, and physical interactions that reveal acoustic evidence. Experiments on LIBERO-Audio and three real-world manipulation tasks show excellent performance. Ablation studies support the contributions of binding supervision and audio modulation, while audio swap tests show that sound influences action predictions. Our code and videos are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/listenvla-F18D

</details>


### [131] [Constraint-Decomposed Impedance Control from Generative Policy for Articulated Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16316)
*Kyungseo Park,Byeongdo Lim,Jungbin Lim,Minseok Choi,Frank C. Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种约束分解的阻抗控制方法，通过生成策略的轨迹多样性来估计在扭转空间中的可行运动方向，并利用主成分分析确定可行的扭转子空间以形成6x6阻抗增益矩阵。实验表明该方法在保持任务执行的同时，减少了违反约束的接触载荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对机器人末端执行器在操作铰接物体时可能遇到的运动学约束问题，现有基于神经网络的策略难以满足这些约束条件。而传统的阻抗控制虽然可以调节虚拟惯性、阻尼和刚度，但在选择合适的任务阻抗增益方面仍然存在挑战。因此，本研究旨在解决在操作铰接物体时为机器人策略选择适当阻抗增益的问题。

Method: 采用约束分解的阻抗控制方法，运用生成策略产生的轨迹多样性来估计在扭转空间中可行的运动方向。通过主成分分析（PCA）处理由同一观察点生成的一系列轨迹，从而推断出扭转变换的可行子空间与受限子空间，并据此形成6x6的阻抗增益矩阵。对于常见的铰接物体，可行的扭转子空间通常独立于物体配置，故仅需一次估计并固定使用；而对于不常见的情况，则估计一个更高维度但配置无关的扭转子空间，使得同样能够采取固定增益策略。

Result: 实验结果表明，在模拟环境和真实世界中，无论是单自由度还是多自由度物体，所提出的约束分解阻抗控制方法相比各向同性的阻抗控制方式，能够在维持任务执行的同时显著减少违反约束条件的接触负荷。

Conclusion: 本文介绍的方法提供了一种有效途径来应对铰接物体操作过程中遇到的运动学约束问题，通过优化阻抗控制参数提高了机器人在完成此类任务时的表现。

Abstract: Articulated object manipulation tasks such as opening doors, tightening bolts, and pulling drawers impose kinematic constraints on a robot end-effector's motion that neural policies may fail to satisfy. Unlike hybrid force-position control that strictly separates force and position control into orthogonal subspaces, impedance control modulates a mechanical impedance system in the form of a virtual inertia, damping, and stiffness. However, selecting task-appropriate impedance gains remains challenging for robot policies. In this paper, we address this problem for the case of manipulating articulated objects. Our constraint-decomposed impedance control method uses the sampling diversity of a generative policy to estimate the feasible motion directions in twist space. The key idea is to infer the feasible and constrained subspaces of twists from an ensemble of trajectories generated by the policy from the same observation. Principal component analysis (PCA) of the end-effector twists determines the feasible twist subspace, which is then used to form 6x6 impedance gain matrices. For many common articulated objects, the feasible twist subspace is independent of the object configuration, so the subspace and resulting gains are estimated once and held fixed during manipulation, minimizing computational overhead. For less common cases where this independence does not hold, we instead estimate a configuration-independent twist subspace of higher dimension that contains the local feasible subspaces, allowing the same fixed-gain strategy to be used. Experiments across simulated and real-world single- and multi-DoF objects show that the proposed method consistently reduces constraint-violating contact loads relative to isotropic impedance control while maintaining task execution.

</details>


### [132] [Imitation Learning of Avoidance Modes for Efficient Multimodal Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16326)
*Changyu Lee,Byung-Kwan Lee,Kanghoon Yoon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SideNet-MPC通过图神经网络模仿MM-MPC进行避障决策，以减少计算复杂度。实验显示其在维持较高成功率的同时大幅降低了计算需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了克服多模态模型预测控制（MM-MPC）在移动障碍物中避免局部最小值时，随着决策关键障碍物数量增加而指数增长的计算成本问题。

Method: 提出了一种名为SideNet-MPC的方法，该方法通过模仿作为专家的MM-MPC学习来做出模式决定，取代了原本对每个避让侧面组合求解一个最优控制问题的方式。使用图神经网络预测专家选择的最低成本模式，对于每个障碍物给出一个避让侧面。边编码每一对障碍物之间车辆首次到达任一障碍物时的预计间隙，以便当邻居阻挡了首选路径时能够反转建议的侧面。保留了专家的选择规则，并用网络提案替换了最低成本模式。因此，在每个控制周期内最多解决两个OCP，且所有应用的输入仍然来自具有控制屏障函数约束的OCP而非直接从网络获得。

Result: 在100次种子匹配闭环蒙特卡洛运行中的混合交通测试表明，SideNet-MPC达到了专家成功率的93%，并且在一个单线程上每个控制周期仅需18.6毫秒，比专家在十六个并行线程上的35.5毫秒减少了48%。在受控间隙扫描中，其建议的侧边在与专家相同的间隙区间内反转，而没有成对边的数据训练者则永远不会反转，在确定性场景的被阻塞入口处全部陷入死锁。

Conclusion: SideNet-MPC提供了一种有效降低计算负担同时保持高成功率的方法，适用于需要快速响应和准确避障的应用场景。相比传统MM-MPC，它显著减少了所需处理时间而不牺牲太多性能。

Abstract: Multimodal model predictive control (MM-MPC) avoids local minima among moving obstacles by solving one optimal control problem (OCP) per combination of avoidance sides, so its computational cost grows exponentially with the number of decision-critical obstacles. We propose SideNet-MPC, which replaces this enumeration with a mode decision learned by imitating MM-MPC as an expert. A graph neural network is trained to predict the expert's minimum-cost mode, one avoidance side per obstacle. Its edges encode, for each obstacle pair, the gap predicted for the moment the vehicle first reaches either obstacle, so a neighbor that blocks a preferred passage can reverse the proposed side. The expert's selection rule compares its minimum-cost mode with the previously executed mode; SideNet-MPC keeps this rule and substitutes the network proposal for the minimum-cost mode. Hence at most two OCPs are solved per control period, and every applied input still comes from an OCP with control barrier function constraints rather than from the network. In $100$ seed-matched closed-loop Monte Carlo runs of mixed traffic, SideNet-MPC reaches $93\%$ of the expert's success rate and needs \SI{18.6}{ms} per control period on a single thread, 48\% less than the expert's \SI{35.5}{ms} on sixteen parallel threads. In a controlled gap sweep, its proposed side reverses in the same gap interval as the expert's, whereas proposers without pairwise edges, trained on the same data, never reverse and all deadlock at the blocked gates of a deterministic scene.

</details>


### [133] [Toward Study on the Effects of Robot's Humorous Apology on Human's Trust Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16334)
*Gayoung Kim,Go-Eum Cha,Wonse Jo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究通过2x2被试间因子设计实验，探讨了幽默型机器人道歉在不同错误严重程度下对用户信任恢复的影响，假设幽默型道歉在低严重性错误后比通用型道歉更能促进信任恢复，但在高严重性错误后这种优势会减弱或逆转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人错误会通过降低用户的信任度和依赖意愿来干扰人机协作。虽然机器人的道歉有助于信任的恢复，但其效果可能因道歉策略和错误严重程度而异。因此，有必要研究不同情境下（特别是错误严重程度）幽默型道歉相较于普通道歉对用户信任恢复的具体影响。

Method: 采用2x2被试间因子设计方法，以错误严重程度（低与高）和道歉策略（通用型与幽默型）为自变量，计划使用开源的仓库机器人交互模拟器进行在线实验。参与者将完成一个协作任务，在经历机器人错误及随后的道歉之后，报告他们的信任水平、信任恢复情况、互动体验以及再次使用的意愿。

Result: 研究尚未进行，结果待定。预期发现是：对于低严重性错误，幽默型道歉相比通用型道歉能够带来更大的信任恢复；而对于高严重性错误，则认为幽默的优势可能会减弱甚至反转。

Conclusion: 该研究表明选择合适的道歉策略对于修复由于机器人错误造成的信任损失至关重要，特别是在考虑错误严重程度时。幽默型道歉可能不是所有情况下最有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Robot errors can disrupt human-robot collaboration by reducing user trust and willingness to rely on the robot. Robot apologies can support trust recovery after such errors, but their effects may vary with apology strategy and error severity. This study therefore presents a user experiment based on a 2x2 between-subjects factorial design to examine the effect of humorous robot apologies on user trust recovery and the moderating role of error severity. The design crosses robot error severity (low vs. high) with apology strategy (generic vs. humorous). An online experiment will be conducted using the open-source Warehouse Robot Interaction Simulator. Participants will complete a collaborative task, experience a robot error followed by an apology, and report trust, trust recovery, interaction experience and reuse intention. We hypothesize that humorous apologies will produce greater trust recovery than generic apologies after low-severity errors, but that the advantage of humor will weaken or reverse after high-severity errors.

</details>


### [134] [PINT: A Unified Model with Shared Predictive Intent for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16355)
*Xinyu Guo,Chao Jing,Li Yi,Xingyu Chen,Zhengxing Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种名为PINT的统一模型，该模型通过共享的预测意图来协调语义推理、世界建模和动作生成，同时保留专家特定路径。PINT利用预训练的视觉-语言模型（VLM）和视频生成模型（VGM）的优势，并通过两阶段训练方法实现强大的分布内性能及显著提升的分布外泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的直接通过多层共享注意力机制融合视觉-语言模型（VLMs）与视频生成模型（VGMs）的方法难以充分利用各自的专长能力。为了解决这一问题并提高机器人操作任务中的通用性和泛化性，研究者提出了PINT模型。

Method: PINT模型通过在VLM序列中插入可学习的意图令牌形成共享块级表示——预测意图，以此条件化VGM和动作专家（AE）。训练过程分为两个阶段：第一阶段冻结VGM以让未来视频预测形塑带有任务相关未来动态的预测意图；第二阶段固定已学得的意图调整VGM和AE。

Result: PINT模型不仅达到了强劲的分布内表现，在RoboTwin 2.0-Hard上达到15.5%的成绩，在LIBERO-Plus上则有79.5%的表现，还大幅提升了分布外泛化能力。对于五个现实世界中的双手机器人任务，PINT实现了68%的分布内成功率和24%的分布外成功率。

Conclusion: PINT模型证明了通过共享预测意图协调不同专业模块的有效性，尤其在增强机器人的泛化能力方面表现出色。它提供了一种新的方法来利用预训练模型的优点，同时保持各自领域的专门知识。

Abstract: Generalist robot manipulation can benefit from pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) for semantic reasoning and video generation models (VGMs) for world modeling. However, existing unified models that directly fuse them through shared attention at multiple layers can make it harder to draw on their specialized capabilities. We introduce PINT, a unified model that coordinates semantic reasoning, world modeling, and action generation through a shared chunk-level Predictive Intent while preserving expert-specific pathways. PINT inserts learnable Intent Tokens into the VLM sequence to form this representation, conditioning both the VGM and an Action Expert (AE). It replaces only the VGM's prompt conditioning and retains the VGM at inference, where the AE reads its representations through one-way attention. Rather than prescribing what Predictive Intent should encode, PINT learns it jointly from the native prediction objectives of the VLM, VGM, and AE, which provide complementary views of the same upcoming task execution. Training uses two stages: Stage 1 freezes the VGM so that future video prediction shapes Predictive Intent with task-relevant future dynamics, while Stage 2 fixes the learned Intent and adapts the VGM and AE. Without embodied pretraining, PINT achieves strong in-distribution (ID) performance and substantially improves out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization, reaching 15.5% on RoboTwin 2.0-Hard compared with 1.8% for Fast-WAM and 3.2% for StarVLA, and 79.5% on LIBERO-Plus compared with 51.5% and 75.0%, respectively. Across five real-world bimanual tasks, PINT reaches 68% ID and 24% OOD success, compared with 35%/9% for pretrained Motus and 67%/35% for pretrained $π_{0.5}$.

</details>


### [135] [RoboBugBench: Benchmarking Automated Repair of Real Robot Software Under an Audited Behavioral Oracle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16369)
*Alexey Tuzikov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了RoboBugBench，一个包含33个真实缺陷的ROS 2导航堆栈基准测试集，用于评估语言代理修复机器人软件的能力。研究发现，当前模型在基于过程存活与行为表现的实例上修复成功率差异显著，并探讨了如何通过分布规则而非单纯加强标准来提高对伪造补丁的识别率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对于语言代理是否能够有效修复机器人软件中的缺陷尚无明确衡量方法，主要原因在于机器人错误通常表现为行为问题，而现存的机器人代码库中几乎没有任何捕捉这些行为问题的测试案例。

Method: 构建了一个名为RoboBugBench的基准测试集，包含了33个经过验证的真实缺陷。对于候选补丁，通过编译并在物理模拟环境中驱动机器人来评分，而不是运行单元测试。此外，还进行了对抗性审计以检验评价标准的有效性。

Result: 三个前沿模型在尝试修复每个实例时的成功率为24.2%-36.4%，且彼此间没有显著差异；修复难度更多取决于评价标准而非领域特性，在基于过程存活的实例上的成功率为66.7%，而在基于机器人行为的实例上仅为22.2%。另外，发现现有规则容易接受那些掩盖失败但未真正修复缺陷的虚假补丁。

Conclusion: 本研究表明，通过对相同数据应用分布规则可以减少接受虚假补丁的情况而不增加误拒率。为了提高区分度，需要通过增强监控手段而不是仅靠严格标准。所提供的修复率受到观察限制的影响，实际成功率可能高于或低于报告值。

Abstract: Language agents increasingly modify robot software, but whether they can repair it is unmeasured, because the domain supplies no oracle to inherit: a robot bug manifests as behavior, and robot repositories ship almost no tests that capture it. We present RoboBugBench, a benchmark of 33 real, execution-validated defects in a pinned ROS 2 navigation stack, in which a candidate patch is graded by compiling it and driving the robot through a physics simulation rather than by running a unit test. Three frontier models, each given 1 attempt per instance, repair 24.2%-36.4%, with no pairwise difference significant; difficulty tracks the oracle rather than the domain, at 66.7% on instances graded by process survival against 22.2% on those graded by what the robot did (instance-level p = 0.0027). Because the oracle is constructed rather than inherited we audit it adversarially, and report the decomposition that audit yields: counterfeit patches which suppress a failure without repairing the defect are accepted by the shipped rule 11/11, while a distributional rule over the same data rejects 4 at no cost in false rejects. The residual is an observability bound (what the observation channel discards, no predicate over it can recover), so discrimination must be bought with instrumentation rather than stricter criteria, and our repair rates are bounds: inflated by what the oracle cannot distinguish, deflated by unanimity's rejection of correct fixes under run-to-run noise. We release the corpus, criterion batteries, counterfeit suite and cached telemetry.

</details>


### [136] [LABIT: Long-Horizon Robotic Assembly Benchmark for Industrial Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16398)
*Constantin Schempp,Christian Friedrich,Luca Kofler,Laura Suhm*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的工业机器人装配基准LABIT，用于评估长时操作下的装配任务。该基准涵盖了DIN标准的装配任务，如拧螺丝、放置、插入和卡扣，并提供了一套评估协议及性能度量指标，旨在推动智能机器人系统的研究和发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基准测试主要针对单一任务设计，无法充分反映现代制造过程中的复杂性和相互关联性。随着机器人学习在顺应控制和机器人装配领域的发展，需要一个能够覆盖更广泛装配与子装配步骤的新基准。

Method: 提出了LABIT，这是一个面向长时操作任务的工业机器人装配基准。它包括了多种类型的装配任务（如拧螺丝、放置等），并考虑了不同容差条件。此外，还定义了一个基于装配复杂性分类法的新性能度量标准，并提供了所有零件及其CAD模型的详尽列表。

Result: 通过使用传统的力-位置控制方法对所提基准进行了初步评估，并报告了每个基本操作、子任务、装配类别以及子装配体的可靠性与得分指标。

Conclusion: LABIT为研究者们提供了一个评估智能机器人系统鲁棒性的有效工具，特别是对于那些涉及连续装配步骤的任务而言，其中早期错误可能会影响到后续的操作结果。

Abstract: Robot learning has undergone significant advancements in the domain of compliant control and robotic assembly. The development of novel methodologies has transcended the capacity to address discrete tasks, such as grasping or inserting, and has evolved to encompass the comprehensive construction of entire assemblies. Conventional benchmarks, which are predominantly focused on single tasks, are no longer adequate to address the complexity and interconnected nature of modern manufacturing processes. Consequently, the necessity for benchmarks to encompass a broader spectrum of assembly and subassembly steps is becoming apparent. In light of these developments, we propose LABIT, an industrial robotic assembly benchmark for long-horizon manipulation. This benchmark encompasses DIN-norm assembly tasks, including screwing, placing, inserting, and snapping, under both high and low tolerance conditions. The successive nature of these assembly steps implies that prior errors can influence subsequent manipulation. Consequently, this benchmark is particularly relevant for assessing the robustness of intelligent robotic systems. The proposed benchmarking protocols and novel performance metric, based on a taxonomy of assembly complexity in conjunction with the comprehensive list of parts and their corresponding CAD models, serve as essential tools for research and development in the field. To provide a baseline, we evaluate the benchmark using traditional force-position control and report reliability and score metrics per primitive, subtask, assembly category, and subassembly. Accompanying data is available at https://irp-hka.github.io/robotic-assembly-benchmark/.

</details>


### [137] [Estimating the Causal Impact of Social Navigation Signals in Corridor Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16411)
*Francesco Baldo,Luca Castri,Gloria Beraldo,Nicola Bellotto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了在人机交互场景中，使用因果模型估计社交导航信号是否能实际上提高通过成功率。结果显示，考虑混杂变量后，可以发现原本基于观察的分析错误地认为社交信号是有害的，而因果估计则表明它是有益的，突出了因果模型在社会机器人导航中的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人类共享环境中部署机器人时，不仅需要决定如何导航，还需要判断何时发出专门的社会信号。传统的基于观察的推断在这种情况下可能会产生误导：混杂因素可能会影响对信号与任务结果之间关系的估计，导致决策失误。

Method: 研究者们设计了一个走廊内的人机交互场景，并利用因果模型来评估一个社交导航信号是否确实能够提升通行的成功率。他们将该互动表示为一个涉及机器人动作、行人占用情况、可用空间、任务结果以及一个受控混杂因素（静态障碍物）在内的因果有向无环图(DAG)。接着，比较了干预估计与仅基于机器人行为条件下的观察估计之间的差异，后者没有明确建模干预措施。

Result: 结果显示，如果考虑到混杂变量，则会改变从简单观察分析得出的结论：观察估计错误地指出社交信号是不利的；相反，因果估计显示它实际上是有利于提高通过成功率的。这种差异强调了忽略混杂因素可能导致不正确的结论，并展示了因果模型对于情境感知信号决策的重要性。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，在做出关于社交信号价值的决策时，采用因果推理方法而非单纯依赖观察数据是非常重要的。正确处理混杂变量可以帮助我们更准确地理解哪些因素真正影响了机器人的表现，从而指导更有效的社交导航策略开发。

Abstract: Deploying robots in human-shared environments requires deciding not only how to navigate, but also when dedicated social signals are worth emitting. Classic observation-based inference can be misleading in such settings: confounding factors may bias the estimated relationship between a signal and the task outcome, leading to incorrect decisions. Causal inference provides a framework for disentangling these relationships by modelling the mechanisms linking observations, actions, and outcomes, and by estimating interventional effects after accounting for confounding paths. We study a human-robot interaction scenario in a corridor and use a causal model to estimate whether a social navigation signal causally improves transit success. We represent the interaction as a causal DAG involving robot action, pedestrian occupancy, available space, task outcome, and a controlled confounder (static obstacles). We compare the interventional estimate with the observational estimate obtained by conditioning only on the robot action, which does not explicitly model interventions. Our results show that accounting for confounding variables can reverse the conclusions drawn from naive observational analysis: the observational estimate wrongly suggests that the social signal is detrimental, whereas the causal estimate shows that it is beneficial. This discrepancy highlights how failing to account for confounding can lead to incorrect conclusions and demonstrates the importance of causal models for context-aware signalling decisions in social robot navigation.

</details>


### [138] [SHOES: Sim-to-real Hardware-adaptation via On-policy Expansion of Skills for Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16457)
*Kota Tsuruhara,Tsung-Yuan Chen,Kento Kawaharazuka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了SHOES框架，通过高效地调整策略来适应因穿着鞋类导致的形态变化，从而恢复移动性能。同时引入了SafeBox以确保在线调整在用户定义的工作空间内进行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决因穿戴不同鞋类（改变脚部几何形状、质量和摩擦力）而导致的移动控制器性能下降问题。

Method: 开发了SHOES框架，该框架冻结模拟训练的基础策略，并仅对轻量级的调节模块进行微调，以及使用名为SafeBox的技术来保证调整过程中的安全性和有效性。

Result: 仿真结果显示，在有限的额外调整下，SHOES能够显著恢复穿鞋后形态变化带来的移动表现降低；与其它方法相比，SHOES在相同调整预算下表现更优。真实世界实验也证明了其有效性。

Conclusion: SHOES提供了一种有效的解决方案，用于适应机器人由于穿戴不同鞋类所引起的形态变化，提高了跨地形移动的能力。

Abstract: Humans expand their locomotion capability by wearing appropriate footwear, such as hiking boots for mountains or skates for ice. Footwear changes the ground-contact interface and enables traversal of otherwise difficult terrain. In contrast, locomotion controllers are typically trained for a fixed morphology, and their performance can degrade when shoes alter foot geometry, mass, and friction. We propose SHOES, a sample-efficient framework for adapting locomotion policies to footwear-induced morphology changes. SHOES freezes a simulation-trained nominal policy and fine-tunes only lightweight, identity-initialized modulation modules on the robot, preserving the initial behavior while enabling efficient hardware adaptation. We also introduce SafeBox, a workspace-aware waypoint sampler that constrains online adaptation within a user-defined workspace. The simulation results with a shoe-equipped Unitree Go2 show that SHOES substantially recovers locomotion performance after footwear-induced morphology changes using limited additional adaptation. Under the same adaptation budget, SHOES outperforms FiLM-only, Linear Adapter-only, and residual-RL baselines. Real-world experiments on a Unitree Go2 fitted with human shoes further evaluate the effectiveness of SHOES for hardware deployment.

</details>


### [139] [DeTac: Dual-branch Decoupling of Texture and Force Signals in Vision-Based Tactile Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16463)
*Zhirui Zhang,Yunkai Tang,Jiahang Li,Fan Fei,Jinnuo Zhang,Kairan Ding,Jieji Ren,Yandong Guo,Boxin Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为DeTac的双分支生成框架，能够从单个混合触觉图像中同时提取清晰的阴影和标记图像，以解决现有基于视觉的触觉传感器中存在的信号耦合问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于标记的设计将表面纹理和接触力信号紧密耦合在单一观察中，而现有的数据驱动解耦方法存在由于物理压痕效应、误差累积以及缺乏真实配对数据进行监督导致的问题。

Method: 通过全图像合成自然绕过掩码依赖性，并引入跨分支协同优化机制实现互监督，防止误差累积。此外，还构建了一个严格对齐的真实世界配对触觉数据集用于高质量监督。

Result: 广泛的实验表明，DeTac达到了最先进的解耦精度，显著促进了下游的表面重建和力估计任务，并表现出跨传感器泛化能力。

Conclusion: DeTac为基于视觉的触觉感知提供了一种有效的方法来分离表面纹理与接触力信息，提升了相关机器人操作任务中的表现。

Abstract: Vision-based tactile sensors enable fine-grained perception for dexterous robotic manipulation, but current marker-based designs tightly couple surface texture and contact force signals within a single observation. Existing data-driven decoupling methods rely on serial mask-based inpainting, which suffers from mask-sizing dilemmas caused by physical indentation effects, error retention, and lack of real paired data for supervision. To address these challenges, we propose DeTac, a novel dual-branch generative framework that concurrently extracts clean shading and marker images from a single mixed tactile image. DeTac leverages full-image synthesis to naturally bypass mask dependency and introduces a cross-branch synergistic optimization that enables mutual supervision, effectively preventing error retention. Furthermore, we construct a strictly aligned, real-world paired tactile dataset using custom hardware to provide high-quality supervision. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DeTac achieves state-of-the-art decoupling accuracy, significantly facilitating downstream surface reconstruction and force estimation tasks while exhibiting cross-sensor generalization.

</details>


### [140] [Trust-Calibrated VLA Policy Post-Training with Evolving Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16517)
*Wenzhan Li,Yiran Qin,Heng Zhou,Huirui Wang,Yulan Guo,Ruimao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种信任校准框架MERL，结合策略优化和在线更新的模拟器，以解决视觉-语言-行动模型在强化学习微调过程中可靠性和效率之间的紧张关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-行动（VLA）模型在超出演示覆盖范围部署时，需要通过强化学习进行后续训练，但当前的方法要么过于依赖交互（无模型方法），要么依赖于不可靠的想象（基于模型的方法）。为了克服这些限制，研究者提出了MERL方法来平衡可靠性和效率。

Method: MERL是一个结合了策略优化与包含世界模型和奖励代理头的在线更新模拟器的信任校准框架。它根据模拟器的可靠性调整想象数据的比例和展开地平线；在每次展开中，块级信任控制重放缓存选择和优化权重。对于递归想象，在没有精确接地延续的情况下，剩余预测器可以不查询未来真实情况估计信任度。

Result: 在受干扰的LIBERO-PRO任务上，MERL达到了79.7%的平均成功率，并且比匹配的无模型控制提高了10.9%的高阈值学习速度。此外，在三个训练种子上，MERL的表现优于使用相同协议的外部基线。从每个任务50个演示开始的固定数据适应性训练，在两个物理机器人任务上实现了80.0%的平均成功率。

Conclusion: MERL为视觉-语言-行动模型提供了一个有效提升其性能同时保持高效性的解决方案，尤其是在面对超出初始训练环境的情况时。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are often pretrained via imitation learning, making reinforcement learning (RL) post-training important when deployment moves beyond demonstration coverage. Existing refinement paradigms expose a reliability--efficiency tension: model-free refinement avoids simulator bias but is interaction-intensive, whereas model-based refinement can exploit unreliable imagination even as its simulator adapts. We propose Model-Evolving Reinforcement Learning (MERL), a trust-calibrated framework that couples policy optimization with an online-updated simulator comprising a world model and a reward proxy head. Across stages, simulator reliability controls the imagined-data ratio and rollout horizon; within rollouts, chunk-level trust controls replay selection and optimization weight. For recursive imagination without exact grounded continuations, a residual predictor estimates trust without querying future ground truth. On perturbed LIBERO-PRO tasks, MERL reaches 79.7% average success, improves high-threshold learning speed by 10.9% over the matched model-free control, and outperforms same-protocol external baselines across three training seeds. Fixed-data adaptation from 50 demonstrations per task achieves 80.0% average success on two physical-robot tasks.

</details>


### [141] [Learning Reachability-Based Safety Representations for Real-Time Robot Navigation in Unknown and Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16522)
*Bojan Derajić*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种方法，通过离线训练神经隐式表示来学习到达性分析解，从而为基于优化的控制器提供实时的安全信息。这种方法能够提高机器人在未知动态环境中的避障性能，特别是针对短时预测和移动障碍物的情况。此外，还介绍了一种新的离线求解器方法，适用于更高维度的系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的几何碰撞检测不足以保证机器人在未知且动态变化环境中的安全导航；HJ可达性分析虽然有效但在线计算成本过高。因此，需要一种可以在实时中提供此类安全信息的方法。

Method: 首先，通过离线训练神经网络学习从到达性解决方案得到的价值函数修正量，确保即使存在近似误差也能将障碍物保持在预期的安全集合之外。然后，提出了五种方法来应用学习到的功能：前两种作为模型预测控制中的终端约束处理静态局部环境及预测障碍物运动；接下来两种则将其作为控制屏障函数安全过滤器中的候选屏障，分别处理静态环境以及使用相对坐标中的成对屏障处理多个移动障碍物。最后一种方法引入了新的离线求解策略，以支持更高维度的动力学模型。

Result: 仿真与硬件实验表明，在地面和空中机器人上，所提方法相较于几何和手工设计基线方案展现了更好的避障性能，特别是在处理短时视界内的决策和移动障碍物方面。对于具有多面体输入的控制仿射动力学，新提出的离线求解技术可生成用于训练的数据标签，并为多达13个状态的系统提供屏障候选方案。

Conclusion: 通过结合机器学习与传统控制理论，本文提出的方法不仅能够在复杂环境中提高机器人的安全性，而且展示了处理更高级别维度问题的可能性。

Abstract: Safe robot navigation in unknown, dynamic environments requires more than geometric collision checking: a robot still far from an obstacle may be unable to avoid it because of its speed, orientation, or the obstacle's motion. Hamilton--Jacobi (HJ) reachability analysis captures this in a value function whose sign separates states that admit a safe continuation from those where collision is unavoidable, but computing it online for every newly observed environment is too expensive. This dissertation trains neural implicit representations offline on reachability solutions to give optimization-based controllers this information in real time. Since the value function never exceeds the constraint, it equals a directly computed constraint, such as a signed distance, minus a nonnegative dynamics correction. Learning only this correction keeps represented obstacles outside the predicted safe set despite approximation errors. The first two of five methods use the learned function as a terminal constraint in model predictive control, for static local environments and predicted obstacle motion. The next two use it as a barrier candidate in control barrier function safety filters, for static environments and, through pairwise barriers in relative coordinates, for a varying number of moving obstacles. Simulations and hardware experiments on ground and aerial robots show better collision avoidance than geometric and hand-designed baselines, especially for short horizons and moving obstacles. The fifth method replaces the grid-based offline solver, which limits the first four to low-dimensional models. For control-affine dynamics with polytopic inputs, the HJ Hamiltonian is maximized at a vertex, so a parallelizable tree search over vertex inputs can generate training labels. With a loss from the HJ variational inequality, these labels yield barrier candidates for systems with up to 13 states.

</details>


### [142] [Design and Analysis of a Nonlinear Parallel Mechanism Coupling for Dynamic Humanoid Legs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16604)
*Rohit Kumar,Heiner Peters,Felix Wiebe,Franek Stark,Janne Janzen,Dennis Mronga,Shubham Vyas,Frank Kirchner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型串联-并联腿部设计，通过将所有执行器靠近髋部放置，并结合膝关节和踝关节的并联机制与非线性耦合，实现了在保持扭矩能力和高效力传输的同时降低远端惯性。这种设计有助于动态人形机器人的轻量化腿机构造，展示了非常低的远端腿部惯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了使人形机器人具备敏捷、动态且节能的移动能力，需要设计出重量轻的腿部结构。减少远端惯性同时维持紧凑传动装置的扭矩性能及有效力量传递是当前面临的一个挑战。

Method: 论文中介绍了一种新的系列-平行腿设计，该设计包括一个膝部平行机制、一个踝部平行机制以及一种膝-踝非线性耦合方式，所有的驱动器都更接近于髋部位置布置。当膝盖弯曲时，这种耦合能够被动地帮助脚踝背屈以实现抬脚动作；而当膝盖伸展时，则帮助跖屈完成蹬踏动作，从而通过膝关节传动重新分配了脚踝所承受的负载。研究者们还推导出了这种耦合的非线性传动模型，并通过仿真和硬件实验对其进行了验证。

Result: 实验结果表明，共享传动系统允许两个关节相互利用对方驱动器的能力：在负载下减轻了膝关节的最大扭矩需求，在快速运动期间降低了脚踝的最大速度需求。结合近端驱动器布局，该腿对于6kg重的腿来说，在足部达到了0.37kg的有效反射质量，显示出了极低的远端腿部惯性。

Conclusion: 这项工作展示了一个创新的腿设计，它有效地解决了如何在不牺牲扭矩能力的情况下减少远端肢体质量的问题，为开发更加动态和高效的仿人机器人提供了新思路。

Abstract: Humanoids increasingly demand lightweight leg mechanisms for agile, dynamic, and energy-efficient locomotion. Reducing distal inertia through proximal actuation has thus become a common design goal. Yet, compact transmissions that preserve torque capability and efficient force transmission remain hard to achieve. This paper presents a novel series-parallel leg design combining a knee parallel mechanism, an ankle parallel mechanism, and a nonlinear knee-ankle coupling where all actuators placed closer to the hip. As the knee flexes, the coupling passively assists ankle dorsiflexion for foot clearance and, as it extends it assists plantarflexion for push-off, redistributing ankle load through the knee transmission. We derive the coupling's nonlinear transmission models and validate them in simulation and on hardware. The design shows that the shared transmission lets both joints draw on each other's actuator capacity by easing the knee's peak torque demand under load and the ankle's peak speed demand during fast motion. %Together With the leg's proximal actuator placement, the leg achieves an effective reflected mass of 0.37kg at the foot for a 6kg leg, demonstrating very low distal leg inertia for dynamic humanoid robots.

</details>


### [143] [Language2Drive: Do Vision-Language-Action Models Really Follow the Instructions in Autonomous Driving?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16634)
*Zihan You,Zehua Fan,Wenqi Lyu,Aoqi Wang,Ni Wang,Yifei Yang,Linge Zhao,Huan Li,Sining Ang,Yuguang Yang,Jueyu Chen,Xiangyu Li,Qianshan Wei,Jieyuan Pei,Yuxin Wang,Zhiwen Chen,Jiaqi Li,Wenxuan Song,Yanyuan Qiao,Benjin Zhu,Haoang Li,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个新的基准测试\ltd，旨在直接评估基于自然语言指令的自动驾驶模型是否真正按照乘客指令执行行为。通过使用配对反事实单元（PCU）设计，研究者能够在保持驾驶场景不变的情况下仅改变指令，从而区分出单一指令的成功与配对反事实条件下的成功，并揭示了当前模型在遵循语言指示方面存在的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着视觉-语言-行动(VLA)模型越来越多地利用自然语言指令来调节自动驾驶行为，现有闭环基准主要衡量的是路线完成、安全性和规则遵守情况，但未能解决一个根本问题：语言是否真的决定了车辆执行的具体行为？为了解决这个问题，研究者提出了一个新的基准\ltd，专门用于检测这种能力。

Method: 本研究引入了\ltd基准测试，其核心评估单元是配对反事实单元(PCU)，该单元固定初始驾驶场景而只变更指令，允许研究人员区分单独指令成功和跨四个指令跟随范围内的反事实配对成功。此外，为了验证这种区别是否跨越不同评价体系存在，研究者在互补的闭环CARLA模拟、重建的真实世界场景以及开环驾驶基准中实现了Language2Drive，包括发布了一个带有目的建造地图的CARLA-Language2Drive模拟器。

Result: 通过对代表性驾驶VLA模型在这些设置中的评估显示，强大的驾驶能力和弱的语言控制之间存在着显著不匹配现象：93.2%的成功行驶案例在面对可替代的可行指令时并没有相应成功的对应案例。这种失败主要表现为单边完成，即模型反复执行一种偏好行为但在相同场景需要采取反事实替代方案时失败。

Conclusion: 结果表明，目前广泛使用的传统驾驶基准——孤立的成功和驾驶得分，可能会严重高估语言跟随能力；同时，当前的驾驶VLA自身在相匹配的反事实测试下缺乏可靠的语言指令跟随能力。

Abstract: Recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models increasingly use natural-language instructions to condition autonomous driving behavior. Yet existing closed-loop benchmarks primarily measure route completion, safety, and rule compliance, leaving a fundamental question unresolved: does language actually determine which feasible behavior the vehicle executes? A trajectory may be safe and successful while still violating or even ignoring the passenger's instruction. To address this, we introduce \ltd, a benchmark designed to directly test this capability. Its key evaluation unit, the paired counterfactual unit (PCU), holds the initial driving scene fixed while changing only the instruction. This design enables us to distinguish isolated instruction success from counterfactual paired success across four instruction-following scopes. To test whether this distinction persists across evaluation regimes, we instantiate Language2Drive in complementary closed-loop CARLA simulation, reconstructed real-world scenes, and open-loop driving benchmarks, including our released CARLA-Language2Drive simulator with purpose-built maps. Evaluating representative driving VLAs across these settings reveals a striking mismatch between strong driving competence and weak language control: 93.2% of successful rollouts do not have a successful counterpart under the alternative feasible instruction. This failure is dominated by one-sided completion, where a model repeatedly executes one preferred behavior but fails when the same scene requires the counterfactual alternative. These results expose two fundamental limitations of current evaluation: isolated success and driving scores, which are widely used by conventional driving benchmarks, can substantially overstate language-following capability, while current driving VLAs themselves lack reliable instruction following under matched counterfactual tests.

</details>


### [144] [What Limits Cross-Task Generalization in World Action Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16641)
*Chen Zhao,Zipeng Ye,Yihan Lin,Yuchen Xie,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的方法，通过解耦世界行动模型(WAM)为世界模型和逆动力学模型(IDM)，并引入了Align Once, Evaluate Everywhere (AoE)来改善跨任务泛化性能。同时，提出了视觉到动作(VITA)损失函数以优化生成的未来图像与实际操作相关细节之间的差异，从而提高了执行成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的世界行动模型（WAMs）在跨任务泛化方面存在困难，即便它们预测的未来情景看起来是合理的操纵行为。为了改进这一点，研究者们希望通过一种新的方式来提高模型对未来未知任务的适应能力，并确保这些模型能够更准确地反映对物体的实际操控情况。

Method: 1. 将世界行动模型分解成世界模型和逆动力学模型。
2. 引入Align Once, Evaluate Everywhere (AoE)，这是一个基于大规模机器人数据预训练的相机空间逆动力学模型，它可以在不同任务和环境间提供一致的恢复接口。
3. 通过固定逆动力学模型而改变未来图像源的方法，发现直接从记录下来的未见任务的未来图像中恢复出来的动作几乎可以像重放演示那样好执行，但由世界模型预测的未来图像表现不佳。
4. 提出视觉到动作(VITA)损失函数，该函数通过冻结的AoE传递生成的未来图像，并惩罚恢复的动作中的差异，以此引导生成过程更加关注于与动作相关的细节。

Result: 使用VITA损失函数后，在两个世界模型和三个操作基准测试上，生成未来图像的执行成功率从接近零提高到了47.0%。此外，同样的损失函数也可以应用于WAMs，并在其原生控制中产生一致的增益。

Conclusion: 本研究表明，通过专门针对手爪和交互几何进行监督以及利用视觉到动作损失函数来调整生成重点，能够显著提升世界行动模型对于新任务的泛化能力和执行效果。

Abstract: Cross-task generalization remains difficult for world action models (WAMs), even when their predicted futures depict plausible manipulation. This gap can be attributed directly: we make this gap directly testable by decoupling a WAM into a world model and an inverse-dynamics model (IDM), then holding the IDM fixed while replacing the source of future images. We introduce Align Once, Evaluate Everywhere (AoE), a camera-space inverse-dynamics model pretrained on large-scale robot data, which provides this recovery interface for different future sources across tasks and environments. Through it, we find that actions recovered from recorded futures of unseen tasks execute almost as well as replayed demonstrations, whereas futures predicted by world models collapse across benchmarks. The failures are not generic: they concentrate on the gripper and interaction geometry while generation capacity is spent on task-irrelevant background. This diagnosis suggests that supervision should target exactly these regions. We therefore introduce the vision-to-action (VITA) loss, which passes generated futures through the frozen AoE and penalizes discrepancies in the recovered actions, redirecting generation toward action-relevant detail. Across two world models and three manipulation benchmarks, this simple change raises the execution success of generated futures from near zero to 47.0%. The same loss also transfers to WAMs and yields consistent gains in native control.

</details>


### [145] [FEM-Validated Real-Time Modelling of Soft Tactile Deformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16667)
*Silvia Terrile,Kaichen Shi,Taejun Kwon,Jiseok Lee,Nathan Lepora,Yeejin Lee,Saekwang Nam,Efi Psomopoulou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数据高效框架，结合了有限的实际验证、高保真有限元方法(FEM)和图神经网络(GNNs)，用于平面软触觉结构中二维触觉变形的实时准确预测。通过仅使用82个相机跟踪接触配置来调整和验证ANSYS FEM模型，然后生成768个合成接触场景用于GNN训练，实现了从标记变形到接触定位的正向预测以及从接触配置到标记变形的逆向推理。所提出的模型在真实测量上保持了亚毫米级精度，并被集成到了一个实时可视化管道中，支持快速变形预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于有限元方法（FEM）计算成本高昂，而实时模拟器通常缺乏对超弹性变形的逼真度，同时大规模物理数据集收集不切实际，因为重复测试可能会损坏脆弱的传感器，因此准确定模化软触觉传感器仍然具有挑战性。

Method: 研究者提出了一种结合有限实际世界验证、高保真FEM及图神经网络（GNNs）的数据高效框架，用以实现二维触觉变形的精确实时预测。该方法首先利用82组经由摄像机追踪得到的接触配置专门用于调校与验证ANSYS FEM模型；随后，基于已验证过的FEM模型生成768种合成接触情境作为GNN的训练资料。

Result: 验证过的FEM模型在超过90%的验证案例中达到了低于0.5毫米的误差水平，而学习得到的GNN替代模型则达到了0.32毫米的平均绝对误差，其推断速度为毫秒级别，远快于分钟级别的FEM模拟。完全基于合成FEM数据训练的模型，在2D触觉结构的真实测量上依然保持着亚毫米级别的准确性。

Conclusion: 本研究表明，所提框架能够有效结合少量实际数据与大量合成数据进行训练，从而使得GNN能够在保持高精度的同时显著提高预测速度，适用于虚拟原型设计及基于模拟的设计探索，无需大量的物理测试。

Abstract: Accurate modelling of soft tactile sensors remains difficult because finite element methods (FEM) are computa- tionally expensive, while real-time simulators often lack fidelity for hyperelastic deformation. Large physical datasets are also impractical to collect because repeated testing can damage fragile sensors. We present a data-efficient framework that combines limited real-world validation, high-fidelity FEM, and graph neural networks (GNNs) for accurate real-time prediction of two-dimensional tactile deformation in a planar soft tactile structure. The key idea is to decouple validation from training: 82 camera-tracked contact configurations are used only to tune and validate an ANSYS FEM model, which then generates 768 synthetic contact scenarios for GNN training. We evaluate both forward prediction from marker deformation to contact localisation and inverse inference from contact configuration to marker deformation. The validated FEM achieves sub-0.5 mm error in more than 90% of validation cases, while the learned GNN surrogate reaches a mean absolute error of 0.32 mm with millisecond-scale inference, compared with minute-scale FEM simulation. Models trained entirely on synthetic FEM data retain sub-millimetre accuracy on real measurements from the 2D tactile structure, demonstrating strong sim-to-real transfer within the tested planar setting. We further integrate the learned model into a real-time visualisation pipeline, enabling rapid deformation prediction for virtual prototyping and simulator-based design exploration without extensive physical testing.

</details>


### [146] [Belief Space Planning for Risk Signal Temporal Logic via Mixed-Integer Convex Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16696)
*Shaohang Han,Joris Verhagen,Kaier Liang,Jana Tumova*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在高斯信念空间中使用Risk Signal Temporal Logic (RiSTL)进行规划的方法，通过信念的信息形式参数化和信念动态的凸松弛，将问题转化为混合整数凸程序(MICP)，证明了该方法的有效性、完备性和全局最优解的保持性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的机器人任务规划中，利用信号时间逻辑(STL)可以满足时空要求，并且其鲁棒性语义允许通过轨迹优化来进行规划。然而，在实际操作中，由于机器人状态估计受到噪声测量的影响，当从测量中获取的信息依赖于状态时，机器人可以通过在信念空间中规划来主动减少不确定性。但这样会导致非凸问题的产生，基于梯度的方法既不完整也不能保证全局最优。为解决这一问题，本文提出了在高斯信念空间内使用风险信号时间逻辑(RiSTL)进行规划的新方法。

Method: 采用信息形式参数化描述机器人的信念状态，并结合对信念动态过程的凸松弛技术，成功地将原本复杂的非凸规划问题转化为了一个混合整数凸程序(MICP)问题。这种方法不仅能够有效处理由不确定性和复杂环境引起的挑战，而且理论上保证了解决方案的完备性和全局最优性。

Result: 研究表明，所提出的MICP方法是合理的、完整的，并且能够保持全局最优输入轨迹。通过数值仿真验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本研究展示了一种新的在高斯信念空间下使用RiSTL进行机器人规划的方式，通过转化为MICP实现了对不确定性的有效管理同时确保解决方案的质量。此方法为未来更复杂环境下机器人自主导航提供了有力支持。

Abstract: Signal Temporal Logic (STL) specifies robot tasks with spatio-temporal requirements, and its robustness semantics allow planning by trajectory optimization. In practice, robots estimate their state from noisy measurements, and when the information gained from a measurement depends on the state, the robot can actively reduce its uncertainty by planning in the belief space. The resulting problem is, however, generally non-convex, for which gradient-based methods are neither complete nor globally optimal. In this paper, we consider Risk STL (RiSTL) planning in Gaussian belief space and show that an information-form parameterization of the belief, together with a convex relaxation of the belief dynamics, yields a mixed-integer convex program (MICP). We prove that the MICP is sound and complete, and that the relaxation preserves the globally optimal input trajectories. We validate the effectiveness of our method in numerical simulations.

</details>


### [147] [CopyQuad: Quadrupedal Skill Transfer from a Single Side-View Video Without a Depth Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16737)
*Mohamad H. Danesh,Raina Hu,Mengxue Xu,Hsiu-Chin Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种名为CopyQuad的方法，它省略了传统视频到四足机器人模仿流程中的深度恢复阶段，直接基于机器人的姿态宽度来确定缺失的轴。通过冻结的2D关键点检测器和固定的分析重定向方法，将未经校准的单个视频片段转换为机器人参考轨迹。这种方法能够在不进行每段剪辑轨迹优化的情况下学习多种动作模式，并在硬件上实现零样本部署。实验表明，忽略该轴对最终效果的影响在可接受范围内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的视频到四足机器人模仿流程需要一个额外的学习步骤来恢复侧视图相机无法捕捉到的一个维度的信息（通常是深度）。这增加了系统复杂性和计算成本。本文旨在提出一种更简单且有效的解决方案，即直接使用机器人自身的姿态宽度作为替代，从而避免复杂的3D重建或单目深度估计过程。

Method: 1. 使用冻结的2D关键点检测器从未经校准的视频片段中提取出关键点。
2. 采用固定分析重定向技术将这些关键点转换为适合于机器人的运动参考。
3. 利用单一相位索引奖励结构训练模型，其中每个行为仅需调整三个标量参数即可完成学习。
4. 对不同类型的行走方式（如狗步、马步）及非周期性手势进行实验验证。

Result: 1. 实现了包括狗走、马走、马小跑以及两种非周期性手势在内的多种动作模式的学习与零样本部署。
2. 通过对视频深度模型和单图像度量模型的测试发现，即使忽略了特定轴信息，所导致的位置偏差也小于现有方法产生的误差范围。
3. 证明了即使尝试修正深度信息也无法优于本方法中的常数设定；而不加修正的估计则会导致机器人倒地。

Conclusion: 本文提出的CopyQuad方法能够有效简化视频到四足机器人模仿的过程，同时保证了良好的性能表现。研究结果表明，在某些情况下，省略传统流程中用于恢复隐藏维度的步骤并不会显著影响最终的动作模仿质量。

Abstract: Video-to-robot pipelines for quadrupedal imitation insert a learned lifting stage, either a monocular depth model or a per-clip 3D reconstruction, to recover the one axis a side-view camera cannot see. We present CopyQuad, which omits that stage and pins the missing axis to the robot's own stance width instead. The setting is side-view footage of sagittally dominated motion, where limb motion is almost entirely in the image plane. A frozen 2D keypoint detector and a fixed analytic retargeting then turn a single uncalibrated clip into a robot reference whose foot targets are reachable by construction, so no per-clip trajectory optimization is needed. One phase-indexed reward structure, whose only per-behavior freedom is three scalars from a shared sweep, learns a dog walk, a horse walk, a horse trot and two non-periodic gestures, all deployed zero-shot on hardware. We then measure what omitting the axis costs. In a training-free rigidity test, a video depth model varies the distance between the animal's hips by $40$ to $107\%$ of its own mean and a single-image metric model by $22$ to $58\%$, though that distance cannot move. Motion capture puts the true lateral paw travel of a dog walk at $4.5$ cm peak to peak, a mean deviation near $1.4$ cm against the $3.5$ cm of lateral foot error our policies already leave, so the discarded axis sits below the pipeline's resolution. Depth repaired to feasibility never beats the constant, and the unrepaired estimates put the robot on the ground.

</details>


### [148] [Human-Like Redundancy Resolution for Real-Time Bimanual Humanoid Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16855)
*Chao Tan,Ming Shin Cindy Sung,Shuxian Liang,Chaoqun Wan,Zhen Huang,Xian-Sheng Hua*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合自适应肘部引导与奇异鲁棒手腕跟踪的人类仿生冗余解析框架，旨在实现实时双手机器人遥操作中精确的手腕追踪同时保持手臂配置的可预测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时双手机器人遥操作需要在保证手腕准确追踪的同时维持手臂配置的可预测性。由于7自由度的手臂对于相同的手腕姿态存在多种可能的肘部配置，仅依赖手腕追踪可能会导致肘部漂移或配置突变。虽然固定的人体工程学约束可以稳定肘部姿态，但它们也可能阻碍接近奇异点时所需的冗余调整。

Method: 该研究提出了一个模仿人类行为的冗余解决框架，该框架将自适应肘部引导与奇异鲁棒手腕跟踪相结合。通过肩-腕几何重定向生成机器人尺度的手腕目标。自适应肘偏置标准利用偏置方向来指导姿态，并根据偏置大小调整这种引导的程度，随着人臂伸直逐渐释放冗余自由度。分层速度求解器优先考虑手腕跟踪，并将自适应肘部引导投射到手腕任务零空间内。

Result: 在固定手腕目标消融实验中，与去除此引导相比，自适应引导减少了54.4%的平均肘部方向误差。在工作空间边界压力测试中，奇异鲁棒跟踪与未阻尼伪逆相比，降低了97.9%的最大原始关节速度需求。实际的双手任务和跨平台重定向展示了其适用于不同形态的机器人。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架成功解决了实时双手机器人遥操作中的关键问题，既保证了手腕追踪精度又改善了肘部姿态控制，尤其在接近奇异点时表现出了优越性。此外，该方法还显示了良好的通用性和实用性。

Abstract: Real-time bimanual humanoid teleoperation requires accurate wrist tracking while keeping arm configurations predictable. Because a 7-DoF arm admits multiple elbow configurations for the same wrist pose, wrist-only tracking can cause elbow drift or abrupt configuration changes. Fixed anthropomorphic constraints can stabilize elbow posture, but they can also block the redundant adjustments needed near singular configurations. We propose a human-like redundancy-resolution framework that combines adaptive elbow guidance with singularity-robust wrist tracking. Shoulder-wrist geometric retargeting generates robot-scale wrist targets. An adaptive elbow-offset criterion uses the offset direction to guide posture and scales this guidance by the offset magnitude, so the redundant degree of freedom is progressively released as the human arm straightens. A hierarchical velocity solver prioritizes wrist tracking and projects the adaptive elbow guidance into the wrist-task null space. In a fixed-wrist-target ablation, adaptive guidance reduces mean elbow-direction error by 54.4% compared with removing this guidance. In a workspace-boundary stress test, singularity-robust tracking reduces peak raw joint-velocity demand by 97.9% compared with the undamped pseudoinverse. Real-world bimanual tasks and cross-platform retargeting show applicability across robot morphologies.

</details>


### [149] [UniLAP: A Unified Latent Action Policy for Cross-Embodiment Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16969)
*Lei Yao,Qiao Feng,Mutian Tong,Qihao Yun,Lap-Pui Chau,Lingjie Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种统一的连续潜在动作策略UniLAP，通过流匹配建模潜在空间，并在推理过程中不依赖未来帧。它继承了LAM教师的动作表示能力，同时保持可行行为的多模式分布，并能够适应新的平台。预训练模型在手部姿态预测、单臂和人形机器人操作以及实际部署等多个任务上超越了现有技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的潜在动作模型（LAMs）虽然可以从无动作视频中捕捉操作先验，但离散的潜在变量限制了它们对精细动作的表示能力；而连续型替代方案虽缓解了这一问题，但通常需要额外的规划步骤来生成可执行动作。因此，提出一种不需要依赖未来帧且能直接产生可执行动作的新方法变得十分重要。

Method: 提出了UniLAP，一种基于流匹配构建连续潜在动作空间的方法，可以直接从当前观察到的数据推断出动作而无需未来的帧信息。此外，通过采用自适应流动目标提炼LAM教师模型，使UniLAP能够学习并保留原始模型中的有效动作表示及多样化的行为分布。还引入了一个无标签运动目标提取过程，以去除与任务无关的外观因素，专注于关键的动力学线索。

Result: UniLAP经过1.1B参数规模下的多源视频预训练后，在手部姿态预测、单臂和类人机器人操控等场景下表现优于最先进的潜在动作及视觉-语言-动作(VLA)基线方法，并成功应用于真实世界环境中。

Conclusion: 通过引入UniLAP框架，我们不仅解决了传统LAMs在表示精细动作时遇到的问题，也克服了连续替代方案需额外规划步骤才能生成动作的局限性。实验结果表明，UniLAP能够在多种操作任务中实现优异性能，展示了其强大的泛化能力和实用性。

Abstract: Latent Action Models (LAMs) can capture manipulation priors from action-free videos, but discrete latents usually impose a granularity bottleneck, limiting their ability to represent fine-grained action. Recent continuous alternatives mitigate this, but they are typically used as proxies for world modeling and require a further planning step to produce executable actions. We thus introduce UniLAP, a unified continuous latent-action policy that models the latent space via flow matching and bypasses reliance on future frames during inference. By distilling a LAM teacher with a flow-adaptive objective, UniLAP inherits the teacher's action representation while retaining the multimodal distribution of feasible behaviors. To decouple latent representations from task-irrelevant appearance, we extract a label-free motion target to isolate relevant kinematic cues from spurious visual dynamics. With the established embodiment-agnostic latent manifold, UniLAP can be easily adapted to novel platforms. Pretrained on multi-source videos at a compact 1.1B parameter scale, UniLAP outperforms state-of-the-art latent-action and Vision-Language-Action (VLA) baselines across hand pose forecasting, single-arm and humanoid manipulation, and real-world deployment.

</details>


### [150] [Joint-Feasibility Margin Feedback for Bounded-Input Second-Order Control Barrier Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16978)
*Siyuan Yang,Shaoming He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种有符号的局部联合可行性裕度，用于识别当前障碍半空间是否与可接受输入集相交，并通过预测此裕度来选择最佳控制候选方案，从而在保持任务完成的同时确保了系统的可行性。实验表明，在关键任务中该方法能有效防止硬QP问题变得不可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于解决多个二阶控制屏障函数约束下共同控制权限可能耗尽的问题，即使当前状态仍处于可行范围内。文章旨在找到一种方法以提前预测并避免这种潜在的不可行情况。

Method: 定义了一个有符号的局部联合可行性裕度，其正负性可以精确标识当前障碍半空间是否与允许的输入集合相交。对于单位缩放的距离约束，该裕度具有加速度单位和支持函数对偶特性，能够识别加权约束冲突。通过预测下一个样本点的裕度值来从九个任务导向的候选方案中进行选择，或者选取能够达到期望裕度值且偏差最小的候选者，或者是具有最大预期裕度值的候选者，然后再解相同的硬高阶CBF二次规划问题。

Result: 通过一个受控单步案例显示，在硬QP命令预测的状态下，两个约束单独来看是可行的但联合起来变得不可行；而基于裕度反馈控制器则保持了正面的下一个裕度。此外，在20组配对初始条件下测试时，基于裕度反馈控制器能够在低压力任务中维持硬QP命令，在关键任务中于硬QP问题变为不可行之前采取行动，其轨迹在此期间保持可行并完成任务。

Conclusion: 提出的基于裕度反馈的方法有效地解决了多约束情况下可能出现的控制权限耗尽问题，确保了系统不仅在当前状态下而且在未来一段时间内都保持可行性，同时保证了任务的成功执行。

Abstract: Multiple second-order control barrier function constraints can exhaust their common control authority under bounded acceleration, even when the current state remains feasible. We define a signed local joint-feasibility margin whose sign exactly identifies whether the current barrier half-spaces intersect the admissible input set. For unit-scaled distance constraints, it has acceleration units and a support-function dual that identifies weighted constraint conflicts. We predict the margin one sample ahead for nine task-directed candidates, selecting either the least-deviating candidate that attains a desired margin or the candidate with the largest predicted margin before solving the same hard HOCBF quadratic program. A controlled one-step case shows that, at the state predicted under the hard-QP command, two constraints remain individually feasible but become jointly infeasible, whereas the margin-feedback controller retains a positive next margin. Separately, across 20 paired initial conditions, the margin-feedback controller retains the hard-QP command in a low-pressure task and acts at positive margin in a critical task before the hard-QP problem becomes infeasible; its trajectories remain feasible at that time and complete the task. Source code is publicly available. https://github.com/xirhxq/cbf/tree/robio2026-preventive-regulation

</details>


### [151] [MOSAIC: Mode- and Support-Aware Inference-Time Control of Robot Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17001)
*Sabrina Bodmer,Rahel Rickenbach,René Zurbrügg,Melanie N. Zeilinger,Colin Jones*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种名为MOSAIC的框架，该框架能够在不重新训练的情况下，对预训练的机器人扩散模型进行推理时控制，并能够适应新的部署约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推理时间指导方法要么依赖于可能将样本从学习到的先验中推开的梯度，要么依赖于可能效率低下、模式崩溃或将它们平均成分布外预测的采样过程。因此需要一种新方法来解决这些问题，使得生成性策略如扩散模型能更好地应对执行时遇到的新情况和约束。

Method: MOSAIC通过将初始噪声及所有随机反向步骤扰动视为预训练去噪过程的联合输入，并利用完全去噪输出计算的重要性权重来调整这一输入上的分布。它使用自适应惩罚来平衡物理可行性和与所学支持的一致性，同时通过按模式重要性加权更新来保持模式并避免破坏性平均。此外，相同的方法可以通过共享耦合成本共同引导多个独立训练的扩散模型，实现协调行为而不需要一个联合模型。

Result: MOSAIC在导航、灵巧抓握、具有未见几何形状的运动规划以及与独立训练的机器人先验合作操纵箱子等多个场景下进行了评估。结果表明，在这些设置中，MOSAIC能够在没有重新训练的情况下使冻结的扩散先验适应并组合新的部署约束条件。

Conclusion: MOSAIC提供了一个有效的解决方案，用于控制机器人扩散模型在面对新情况和约束条件时的行为，无需重新训练模型即可实现灵活适应。

Abstract: Generative policies such as diffusion models provide expressive data-driven priors for robot behavior, yet the situations a robot faces at execution time rarely match its training data and impose constraints and objectives the policy never learned. Existing inference-time guidance methods either rely on gradients that can push samples away from the learned prior, or on sampling procedures that can be inefficient, collapse modes, or average them into out-of-distribution predictions.
  We introduce MOSAIC, a mode- and support-aware framework for gradient-free inference-time control of robotic diffusion models. MOSAIC treats the initial noise and all stochastic reverse-step perturbations as a joint input to the pretrained denoising process and adapts a distribution over this input using importance weights computed from fully denoised outputs. Physical feasibility and consistency with the learned support are balanced using adaptive penalties, while mode-wise importance-weighted updates preserve modes and avoid destructive averaging.
  The same formulation can jointly steer multiple independently trained diffusion models through shared coupling costs, enabling coordinated behavior without requiring a joint model. We evaluate MOSAIC on navigation, dexterous grasping, motion planning with unseen geometry, and collaborative box manipulation with independently trained robot priors. Across these settings, MOSAIC adapts and composes frozen diffusion priors under new deployment constraints without retraining.

</details>


### [152] [Task-Space Motion-Force Nonlinear Model Predictive Control for Precise and Compliant Control under Uncertainties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17069)
*Quang-Nam Nguyen,Ludovic Righetti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种统一的非线性模型预测控制框架，该框架能够直接规划和控制机器人关节，满足任务空间中的运动和力需求，并能跨多个机器人平台和多样化任务无缝泛化。此外，研究了控制器内在的任务空间阻抗及其在未建模交互下的微分稳定性和微分无源性，从而解决了力反馈NMPC的长期挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前非线性模型预测控制(NMPC)中，控制律通过最优控制问题(OCP)解隐式决定，这要求设计时针对特定平台和任务进行调整，需要大量专家知识与调校工作。同时，基于优化的方法通常将交互视为刚性接触，导致扭矩解对机器人动力学及环境建模误差敏感。

Method: 本文提出一个统一的NMPC框架，直接规划和控制机器人关节以满足任务空间内的运动和力量需求，可以跨多种机器人平台和任务无缝迁移。进一步地，探讨了所提控制器固有的任务空间阻抗特性及其在未建模互动下的部分收缩、差异稳定性与差异被动性。

Result: 所提出的框架能够在高层规划层闭合力反馈回路，实现精确的运动-力量跟踪性能，解决了长期以来力反馈NMPC面临的挑战。最后，在物理硬件上验证了该框架的有效性，展示了冗余操作器上的混合运动-力量控制以及四足机器人的顺应性行走。

Conclusion: 本研究开发出一种通用性强、适应多种机器人平台与任务类型的NMPC框架，有效提高了系统对于未知交互情况下的实时规划能力，并保持了核心优势如硬约束满足、最佳冗余利用等。

Abstract: In Nonlinear Model Predictive Control (NMPC), the control law is implicitly determined by the solution of an Optimal Control Problem (OCP). Consequently, OCP design is often tailored to specific platforms and tasks, demanding extensive expert knowledge and tuning effort. To address this, we propose a unified NMPC framework that directly plans and controls the robot joints subject to task-space motion and force requirements and is capable of seamlessly generalizing across multiple robotic platforms and diverse tasks. Furthermore, optimization-based methods often treat interactions as rigid contacts, making the torque solution sensitive to the modeling errors of the robot dynamics and the environment. We study the intrinsic task-space impedance of our proposed controller, its partial contraction for differential stability and differential passivity under unmodeled interactions. As a result, our framework enables closing the force-feedback loop in the high-level planning layer to achieve precise motion-force tracking performance, addressing the long-standing challenge of force-feedback NMPC. Finally, we validate our framework on physical hardware, demonstrating hybrid motion-force control on a redundant manipulator and compliant locomotion on a quadruped, while preserving core NMPC advantages such as hard constraint satisfaction, optimal redundancy exploitation, and real-time planning under unforeseen interactions.

</details>


### [153] [Declarative Reactive Robot Behaviors from Imperative Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17083)
*Vincent Berenz,Tasha Kim,Bernhard Schölkopf*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究探索了大型语言模型（LLMs）是否能够生成适合机器人行为的声明性和反应性程序，这些程序通常需要持续且开放式的感官-运动交互模式。通过使用一种名为Playful的反应式脚本语言，研究人员展示了基于LLM的编码代理可以从自然语言描述中生成正确的声明性和反应性程序，并且这一过程可以通过对话逐步完善或一次性生成完整的行为逻辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大型语言模型主要针对命令式编程语言进行了训练，而机器人编程领域的工作也侧重于表达为计划的目标导向任务。然而，许多机器人应用场景需要的是行为——即没有终止条件的开放式、连续的感官-运动互动模式。这种类型的应用更适合采用声明性和反应性编程方法，但目前大型语言模型在这方面的训练几乎为空白。

Method: 研究者采用了Playful，这是一种反应式脚本语言，用以测试基于大型语言模型的编码代理能否从定义不完全的自然语言描述中生成正确的声明性和反应性程序。为了帮助代理从默认的命令式编程风格转向合适的反应式模式，研究团队提供了一套可复用的编程指南。该指南由专家开发，适用于不同的机器人和行为场景。此外，编码代理还能够同时设计程序的命令式部分（包括用于感知、行动及评估的Python模块）和声明式部分（将这些模块组合成行为逻辑的反应式脚本）。

Result: 实验结果表明，在适当上下文和一组可重用编程指南的支持下，基于大型语言模型的编码代理确实能够根据自然语言描述生成正确无误的声明性和反应性程序。这不仅支持通过多轮对话逐步改进程序，同时也允许一次性生成完整的机器人行为逻辑。

Conclusion: 这项研究表明，尽管大型语言模型在反应性编程方面缺乏训练，但通过适当的指导和支持材料，它们仍能有效地为机器人创建所需的行为程序。这意味着未来有可能更广泛地利用大型语言模型来促进机器人技术的发展，特别是在那些要求持续互动而非单一完成目标的任务中。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are predominantly trained on imperative programming languages, and existing work on LLM-based robot programming reflects this: it focuses on goal-directed tasks expressed as plans. However, many robotic applications require not tasks but behaviors--open-ended, continuous patterns of sensory-motor engagement with no termination condition (e.g., greeting visitors, patrolling an area). Such behaviors are better served by declarative and reactive programming, on which LLMs have virtually no training. We investigate whether this gap can be bridged. Using Playful, a reactive scripting language, we show that an LLM-based coding agent can generate correct declarative and reactive programs for robotic behaviors. These programs are produced from under-specified natural language descriptions, given suitable context and a set of reusable programming guidelines. The guidelines encode design principles that redirect the agent from imperative defaults toward proper reactive patterns. Developed once by an expert, they generalize across robots and behaviors. Starting from only the generic robot operating system API, the agent jointly designs both the imperative component (Python modules for sensing, acting, and evaluating) and the declarative component (the reactive script that composes them into behavior logic). The declarative nature of reactive programming supports both incremental refinement through conversational turns and one-shot generation of complete behaviors.

</details>


### [154] [Interactive Navigation Beyond Reachability: Benchmark and Agentic Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17089)
*Delun Li,Xueyao Wei,Rui Chen,Yifei Wang,Junzhi Yu,Wenjie Song,Xingyu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为MultINav-Bench的新基准，用于评估在需要与环境互动（如开门或打开容器）才能完成目标的导航任务中的表现。同时提出了一种新的代理框架MultINav-Agent，在多种任务类型中表现出优于现有方法的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的实体导航任务通常假设在一个情景过程中可通行性和物体状态是固定的。然而，要达到某个目标可能需要通过开启门来进入房间，或是打开容器来发现隐藏的目标。本研究旨在解决这种交互式导航问题，超越了简单的可达性要求，并统一考虑了通道导致的空间连通性变化、容器导致的目标可见性变化以及它们之间的顺序依赖关系。

Method: 提出了一个名为MultINav-Bench的新基准测试集，它包括3000个情景，涵盖了通道任务、容器任务和混合任务，并且附有必要的互动标注信息。此外，还设计了一个三阶段的智能体框架——MultINav-Agent，该框架基于持续的状态条件交互图构建而成。其中包括语义互动感知、基于视觉-语言模型的任务相关动作选择以及后互动验证等组件，以更新图形指导后续导航、探索或互动。

Result: 实验结果表明，在通道、容器及混合任务上，所提出的MultINav-Agent相比调整后的基线方法展现出了更一致的优势表现。

Conclusion: 这项工作为涉及环境交互的导航任务提供了一个新的评价标准及解决方案，显示了MultINav-Agent在处理此类复杂场景时的有效性和优越性能。

Abstract: Existing embodied navigation tasks commonly assume that traversability and object states remain fixed during an episode. Yet reaching a goal may require opening a door to access a room and then opening a container to reveal a hidden target. We formulate interactive navigation beyond reachability, unifying channel-induced changes in spatial connectivity, container-induced changes in target visibility, and their sequential dependencies. We introduce MultINav-Bench, a 3K-episode ObjectGoal benchmark in MolmoSpaces spanning channel, container, and mixed tasks, with interaction-necessity annotations covering required and unnecessary episodes. Its data generation pipeline automates task construction across multiple task types. The evaluation protocol separately measures navigation success, interaction effectiveness, and cost. We further propose MultINav-Agent, a three-stage agentic framework built on a persistent, state-conditioned interaction graph. Semantic interactive perception grounds entities and interaction states; a vision-language model selects task-relevant actions based on expected changes in connectivity and target observability; and post-interaction verification updates the graph to guide subsequent navigation, exploration, or interaction. Experiments on MultINav-Bench show that MultINav-Agent consistently outperforms adapted baselines across channel, container, and mixed tasks.

</details>


### [155] [D-World: An Explicit World-Language-Action Model with Recursive Self-Improvement under Dual Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17114)
*Xuan Wen,Baichao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: D-World是一个基于感知、规划和行动分解的显式世界-语言-动作模型，通过生成视觉和几何代码来构建可执行的数字孪生，并利用测量修正循环实现递归自我改进（RSI），允许在不重新训练大型语言模型的情况下提高机器人执行任务时的世界模型准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的前沿大语言模型能够很好地处理空间推理以编程机器人，但背后的机器人策略世界模型是隐式的，存储在网络权重或预测视频中，无法直接检查或纠正。因此，需要一个可以直观地被审视和修正的世界模型。

Method: 提出了D-World，这是一个基于感知、规划和行动分解的明确世界-语言-动作模型。感知代理使用生成的视觉和几何代码将场景写入为可执行的数字孪生体；规划代理则在模拟中搜索动作，并根据机器人测量的结果更新孪生体中的对象状态及命名物理参数。该过程包括了一个基于测量的修正循环，称为递归自我改进(RSI)。

Result: 实验表明，在双臂机器人上执行毛巾与烤面包机相关任务时，D-World能够通过局部修订显著减少误差，例如将面包渲染深度残差从22.4毫米降至1.5毫米，从而使得执行流程无需重建即可继续进行。此外，由于感知与规划仅通过数字孪生和语言交流，任一模块都可以被学习模型替换；而且随着更快模型的应用，整个循环速度加快。

Conclusion: D-World提供了一种有效的方法，用于创建可通过语言和数字孪生机制进行交互并持续改进的显式世界模型，支持机器人更准确地理解和操作它们所处的环境。

Abstract: Frontier large language models (LLMs) now reason about space well enough to program robots, yet the world models behind robot policies are implicit, held in network weights or predicted video, and cannot be inspected or corrected directly. We present D-World, an explicit world-language-action model built on the Sense, Plan and Act decomposition. A perception agent writes the scene as an executable digital twin using generated vision and geometry code. A planning agent searches for actions in simulation and, after each executed action chunk, revises the twin's object states and named physical parameters against what the robot measured. A parameter revision is accepted only if replaying the latest chunk does not increase any measured residual beyond its tolerance, and every chunk is checked in the updated twin before it runs. Structural mismatches return to perception for reconstruction. We call this measurement-grounded cycle of correction and revision recursive self-improvement (RSI), in which the LLM stays frozen while the world it acts in improves. We model the choice of actions that both advance the task and expose model error as a dual control problem and show that the cost of incorporating evidence into the twin sets the threshold at which probing pays, so cheap local revision counteracts the turn-off of cautious controllers. Each episode runs a real-to-sim-to-real loop, and its recorded twins can be refitted and re-rendered offline into higher-fidelity environments for post-training. Because perception and planning communicate only through the twin and language, either can be replaced by a learned model, and since inference dominates the loop, the loop speeds up with faster models. On a dual-arm robot, towel and toaster tasks show local revisions, such as one reducing the bread's rendered-depth residual from 22.4 to 1.5 mm, that let execution continue without reconstruction.

</details>


### [156] [Force-Modulated Dexterity: Unified Compliance for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17161)
*Ying Yuan,David Held,Jitendra Malik,Haozhi Qi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种新的方法FOMO-Dex，它通过在选定方向上施加力驱动的动作校正来减少机器人操作中的过度接触力，从而提高了远程操作的灵活性。该方法在卡片拾取、纸张切割、螺丝拧紧和螺母螺栓装配等任务中表现出色，并且有助于收集更成功的演示数据以供策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的远程操作技术可以让操作者指定运动，但在没有感受到机器人的实际接触情况下难以复制人类操作时细微的力量调节。这限制了高精度操作任务的表现。为了解决这个问题，研究提出了Force-Modulated Dexterity (FOMO-Dex)，旨在提高涉及丰富接触操作的任务性能。

Method: FOMO-Dex是一种统一的顺应性公式化方法，用于处理包含大量接触的操作任务。它根据任务几何形状和接触结构定义坐标下的标称动作，并沿着选定的方向应用力量驱动的运动修正，而保持其他命令组件不变。
