Result: 实验表明，在卡片拾取、纸张切割、螺丝拧紧以及螺母与螺栓组装等任务中，相比使用刚性控制，FOMO-Dex能够显著降低过大的接触力。此外，用户研究表明经过简短练习后，操作员每小时可以收集更多成功的演示数据。对于策略学习而言，模仿经修正后的动作比直接模仿原始操作者指令能获得更好的卡片拾取成功率。基于FOMO-Dex演示训练出的策略也能够在螺母与螺栓操作及螺丝拧紧任务中实现更大角度的旋转。

Conclusion: FOMO-Dex通过允许沿特定方向进行力驱动调整同时保持其他部分不变的方式，有效地改进了复杂操控任务中的表现。这种方法不仅减少了不必要地大接触力，还使得从人类示教中学到的策略更加有效。

Abstract: Teleoperation is widely used to collect high-quality demonstrations for robot learning. Dexterous manipulation, however, requires coordinated control of motion and contact forces. Operators can specify motion, but struggle to reproduce the subtle force adjustments of human manipulation without feeling the robot's contacts. We introduce Force-Modulated Dexterity (FOMO-Dex), a unified compliance formulation for contact-rich manipulation. FOMO-Dex expresses nominal actions in coordinates defined by task geometry and contact structure. It applies force-driven motion corrections along selected directions while leaving the remaining command components unchanged. Across card picking, paper cutting, screwdriving, and nut-and-bolt assembly, FOMO-Dex reduces excessive contact forces compared with stiff control. A user study shows that operators collect more successful demonstration data per hour after brief practice. For policy learning, imitating the corrected actions improves card-picking success over imitating raw operator commands. Policies trained on FOMO-Dex demonstrations also achieve more rotation in nut-and-bolt manipulation and screwdriving than those trained on demonstrations collected with the stiff controller. Videos are shown on https://yingyuan0414.github.io/fomo-dex/ .

</details>


### [157] [Generative Flow on Symbolic Abstractions for Embodiment Capability Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17174)
*Youwei Yu,Lantao Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为FOSA的新框架，用于通过学习基础任务生成器来发现特定于物理形态的能力。该方法可以跨不同机器人平台发现多样且可执行的任务，并在未见过的复杂和长期任务上提高了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的训练任务分布往往基于手工设计或从大型语言模型中采样而来，这依赖于语义上的可能性而非特定物理形态下的可行性，导致产生的课程可能过于困难、冗余或忽视了那些虽不明显但可学习的行为。

Method: 引入了Generative Flow on Symbolic Abstractions (FOSA)框架，其中抽象策略被参数化为一个生成流网络以构建符号化的任务骨架。每个骨架随后被编译进仿真环境中，在那里低级策略的学习表现提供了抽象策略的奖励信号。

Result: FOSA能够在不同的机器人平台上识别出多种多样的并且是可行的任务，这些任务接近机器人的实际能力边界。此外，将所发现的任务策略提炼成单一的语言条件策略后，FOSA在处理未见的复杂及长期任务时表现出更好的性能。

Conclusion: 通过采用FOSA框架，研究展示了如何有效发现与机器人物理特性相匹配的任务，进而促进实现更复杂的长远行为目标。

Abstract: Achieving complex, long-horizon robot behavior remains challenging because successful execution depends on skills grounded in a robot's physical affordances. Existing training-task distributions are often handcrafted or sampled from large language models (LLMs), relying on semantic plausibility rather than embodiment-specific feasibility. This mismatch can produce curricula that are too difficult, redundant, or blind to non-obvious but learnable behaviors. We propose Generative Flow on Symbolic Abstractions (FOSA), a framework for discovering embodiment-specific capabilities by learning a grounded task generator. FOSA parameterizes an abstract policy as a Generative Flow Network that constructs symbolic task skeletons. Each skeleton is compiled into a simulation environment, where low-level policy-learning performance provides the reward signal for the abstract policy. Across heterogeneous robot platforms, FOSA discovers diverse and executable tasks near the robot's empirical capability frontier. After distilling the discovered task policies into a single language-conditioned policy, FOSA improves performance on unseen complex and long-horizon tasks. The code and demonstration videos are available at https://www.youwei-yu.com/fosa.

</details>


### [158] [DexNinja: Learning Robust Dexterous Cutting Policy with a Real-to-Sim-to-Real Data Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17208)
*Haozhe Lou,Runyi Yang,Wanqi Zhong,Caiyun Liu,Yicheng Liu,Wenhao Liu,Wenlong Ma,Jiawei Xia,Kanzhi Wu,Danda Pani Paudel,Luc Van Gool,Hang Zhao,Yiming Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为DexNinja的框架，通过结合少量实际演示和大量模拟增强数据来提高机器人切割食物的成功率，并且生成的策略能够适应不同形状和大小的食物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了使机器人能够更好地执行像切割这样的复杂任务，面对因材料变形及弹性变化带来的挑战，同时解决收集现实世界数据成本高且浪费的问题以及仿真与现实之间存在的较大差距。

Method: 开发了DexNinja框架，它从真实捕捉中重建物体实例、在类别级约束下随机化物理意义参数，并利用自定义可微分模拟器产生增强的手巧操作片段，该模拟器结合了机器人动力学、触觉接触和可变形切割。

Result: 在灵巧的食物切片任务上评估DexNinja显示：1) 通过数十次实际演示加上数百次增强模拟数据，成功率可以超过60%；2) 增强的数据始终通过sim-to-real方式提高了仅使用实际数据时超过20%的成功率；3) 所得策略能推广至分布外具有不同形状和尺寸的对象。

Conclusion: DexNinja提供了一种有效的方法，用于改善机器人执行需要高度灵活性的任务如食品切割的表现，通过有限的真实示范与大量仿真数据相结合，显著提升了任务完成的成功率及其泛化能力。

Abstract: Cutting a piece of food looks effortless in human hands. For a robot, however, every cut is a high-contact interaction with a deformable, elasto-plastic object whose behavior changes with pose, contact mode, and cutting stage. Collecting real-world data is expensive and wasteful: each trial typically contaminates or damages the food, so obtaining scalable real world data for dexterous cutting is expensive and difficult. Simulation is an appealing alternative, but a large sim-to-real gap exists in both physics and perception. This gap is especially severe for topology-changing interactions like cutting, where small errors in contact, friction, or material and elasticity responses quickly compound. We propose \textsc{DexNinja}, a real2sim2real framework that turns a handful of real demonstrations into a dense and realistic training distribution. \textsc{DexNinja} (i) reconstructs object instances from real captures, (ii) randomizes physically meaningful parameters under category-level constraints, and (iii) produces augmented dexterous manipulation episodes using a custom differentiable simulator that couples robot dynamics, tactile contact, and deformable cutting. We evaluate \textsc{DexNinja} on a dexterous food-slicing task and show that (1) with tens of real demonstrations and hundreds of augmented simulator data, the success rate could reach more than 60\%. (2) the augmented data consistently improves success rates when only using real data with more than 20\% via sim-to-real, and (3) the resulting policies generalize to out-of-distribution objects with varying shapes and sizes.

</details>


### [159] [LiDAR-based Humanoid Navigation in Dynamic, Non-Convex Environments via Spatio-Temporal Safe Corridors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17232)
*Shubham S. Kumbhar,Panagiotis Artemiadis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种实时框架，用于人形机器人在动态非凸环境中的导航。该框架直接从基于LiDAR的粒子表示构建时空安全走廊，并结合基于线性倒立摆（LIP）的规划与全身控制（WBC），使得机器人能够在没有限制性几何假设的情况下绕过或进入移动中的非凸结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的方法通常通过简化的凸障碍物边界来忽略复杂的自由空间，并且未能考虑实时动态占用预测。这种处理方式限制了人形机器人在复杂、动态环境下的导航能力。

Method: 研究者们开发了一个新的实时框架，利用基于LiDAR的粒子表示直接创建时空安全走廊，并将这些走廊与基于线性倒立摆模型的步态规划及全身控制相结合，以支持机器人在移动非凸障碍物周围及内部的安全导航。

Result: 仿真结果显示，在24平方米的场地内，Digit人形机器人成功率达到96%，能够有效避开并穿越移动中的非凸障碍物。消融研究表明，所提出的迭代多面体生成法相比单次迭代基线在狭窄通道中表现更佳；评估还表明系统对杂乱环境、传感器噪声、控制延迟以及力干扰具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本研究提出的框架显著提高了人形机器人在动态非凸环境中导航的能力，展示了其在处理复杂场景时的有效性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Navigating humanoid robots through dynamic, non-convex environments requires tightly coupling onboard perception with footstep planning and dynamic locomotion control. Existing methods frequently discard complex free space through simplified convex obstacle bounds and fail to account for real-time dynamic occupancy predictions. This paper presents a real-time framework for humanoid navigation that directly constructs spatio-temporal safe corridors from LiDAR-based particle representations and couples them with Linear Inverted Pendulum (LIP)-based planning and Whole Body Control (WBC) to enable navigation around and within moving non-convex structures without restrictive geometric assumptions. Simulations on the Digit humanoid yield a 96% success rate in navigating around and inside moving non-convex obstacles in a 24 by 24 m^2 arena. Ablation studies confirm that the proposed iterative polytope generation significantly outperforms single-iteration baselines in narrow corridors, while evaluations demonstrate robustness to clutter, sensor noise, control delays, and force perturbations.

</details>


### [160] [Value-Preserving Action Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17252)
*Max Muchen Sun,Jueun Kwon,Benjamin Riviere,Todd Murphey*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种值保持动作量化（VAQ）方法，通过保持状态可达性来量化动作，并与蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）结合形成了一个值保持、基于搜索的策略学习框架VAQ-MCTS。实验表明，此方法在标准设置下达到了或超过了现有最先进行为克隆方法的表现，同时提高了数据效率和对异质演示的鲁棒性，并能实现对新环境的零样本适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于搜索的策略学习方法，如蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS），需要将连续的动作空间离散化以适应机器人学习任务。然而，仅仅依靠动作重建误差不足以保证离散化后的动作能够准确反映其对未来状态及价值的影响。因此，研究旨在找到一种更好的动作量化方法，以保持动作对未来状态和价值函数影响的同时提高学习算法的数据效率和适应性。

Method: 提出了值保持动作量化（Value-preserving Action Quantization, VAQ）的方法，它通过对状态可达性的重建误差进行限制来优化动作的离散化过程。之后，将VAQ与MCTS相结合形成VAQ-MCTS框架，用于作为基于搜索的行为克隆策略学习方法。

Result: 通过一系列对象推动任务作为基准测试，发现VAQ-MCTS不仅在常规条件下匹配甚至超越了当前最先进的行为克隆技术表现，而且显著提升了数据利用效率以及对于不同种类示例数据集的鲁棒性。此外，它还展示了无需额外训练即可适应新环境的能力。硬件实验进一步证实了VAQ-MCTS的有效性，在仅有10分钟示范数据的情况下成功完成了所有30次现实世界中的物体推动试验。

Conclusion: 本研究表明，通过关注于保持状态可达性的动作量化可以有效改善基于搜索的策略学习性能，特别是在机器人学习领域内。提出的VAQ-MCTS框架不仅提高了数据效率和模型适应能力，而且为处理真实世界中多样化的任务提供了一个强大且灵活的解决方案。

Abstract: Search-based policy learning, such as Monte Carlo tree search (MCTS), offers the flexibility to trade runtime computation for data efficiency and adaptation to novel environments. For robot learning, however, applying search requires discretizing the continuous action space and associated dynamics, typically through action quantization. While reconstruction-based quantization is sufficient for representing the continuous action space alone, policy learning additionally requires the quantized actions to preserve their effects on future states and values. We show that such requirement is not necessarily captured by action reconstruction error. Instead, we prove that the error in the value function resulting from action quantization is tightly bounded by the reconstruction error of state reachability under the quantized actions. Based on these results, we propose value-preserving action quantization (VAQ), which quantizes actions by preserving state reachability. We further combine VAQ with MCTS (VAQ-MCTS) as a value-preserving, search-based policy learning framework for behavioral cloning. Using a wide range of object pushing tasks as a controlled benchmark, we evaluate VAQ-MCTS against state-of-the-art behavioral cloning methods. VAQ-MCTS matches or exceeds their performance under standard settings, while substantially improving data efficiency and robustness to heterogeneous demonstrations, while enabling zero-shot adaptation to novel environments. Hardware experiments further demonstrate the effectiveness of VAQ-MCTS, completing all 30 real-world object pushing trials with only 10 minutes of demonstration data.

</details>


### [161] [Physical SLAM: Physics-aware Simultaneous Localisation and Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17260)
*Guangming Wang,Zhishuo Liu,Haibing Wu,Wanli Ma,Yixiong Jing,Olaf Wysocki,Brian Sheil*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种物理感知的方法——Physical SLAM，用于从RGB-D图像序列中构建可变形物体的4D高斯场，并预测它们在外部作用下的未来状态。通过将场景分为静态背景高斯场和动态物体高斯场，并引入虚拟观察力来结合视觉观测与物理动力学，该方法能够提高重建精度和预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了从移动摄像机捕捉到的单目视频中重构出物理世界的预测性4D表示，研究者们旨在联合重构动态高斯辐射场并推断其潜在的物理动力学。

Method: 提出了一种名为Physical SLAM的方法，它将场景分为两部分：一个用于相机定位的静态背景高斯场和一个由弹簧-质量模型驱动的动态物体高斯场。此外，还引入了虚拟观察力以及一种物理感知的高斯变换和表面对齐策略来整合视觉观测与物理动力学。

Result: 在PhysTwin基准测试中，该方法相较于PhysTwin提高了10.4%的重建PSNR和11.0%的预测PSNR；在TUM RGB-D基准测试中，平均相机跟踪误差降低了20.6%，SSIM提升了3.6%，LPIPS减少了8.3%。

Conclusion: Physical SLAM提供了一种有效的方法来描述可变形物体如何响应操控，并且在多个基准测试上展示了优于现有技术的表现。

Abstract: To reconstruct a predictive 4D representation of the physical world from monocular video captured by a moving camera, we jointly reconstruct dynamic Gaussian radiance fields and infer their underlying physical dynamics. This paper proposes \emph{Physical SLAM}, a physics-aware method for constructing 4D Gaussian fields of deformable objects and predicting their future state when subjected to external actions, given a sequence of RGB-D images. Our method separates the scene into a static background Gaussian field for camera localization and a dynamic object Gaussian field driven by a spring--mass physical model of elastic deformation, inertia, and damping. To integrate visual observations with physical dynamics, we introduce virtual observation forces that gradually pull simulated surface nodes toward observed depth points, avoiding abrupt replacement of simulated node positions. Meanwhile, the physical parameters of the spring--mass model are updated from simulation errors aggregated over the full reconstruction observation window, enabling the reconstructed dynamics to align with observations. To couple appearance, geometry, and physics, we further design a physics-aware Gaussian transformation and surface alignment strategy, where dynamic Gaussians are transformed along the deforming physical surface of the spring--mass model and constrained to remain aligned with the surface during rendering-based optimization. The resulting representation supports observed-frame rendering and action-conditioned simulation, extending SLAM maps to describe how deformable objects respond to manipulation. On the PhysTwin benchmark, our method improves reconstruction and prediction PSNR over PhysTwin by 10.4\% and 11.0\%, respectively. On the TUM RGB-D benchmark, it reduces average camera tracking error by 20.6\%, improves SSIM by 3.6\%, and reduces LPIPS by 8.3\% compared with 4DGS-SLAM.

</details>


### [162] [ReForesight: Occlusion-Robust Re-Identification and Future Occupancy Forecasting for Dynamic Semantic Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17279)
*Didar Seyidov,Faith Ogunwoye,Hajira Amjad,Danil Tolkachev,Yuri Sarkisov,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 介绍了ReForesight，一个开源的ROS 2工具包，它能够为基于粒子的语义映射管道添加实例级别的未来占用预测和遮挡后的重新识别功能。通过使用多层感知器(MLP)来预测行人或车辆未来3秒内的位置，并结合卡尔曼滤波器等技术确保准确性。此外，该系统还能在物体被遮挡后通过关键点描述符重新关联对象。实验结果显示，与仅使用卡尔曼滤波器相比，MLP方法显著降低了预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语义占用地图只能显示当前时刻人的位置信息，但为了使机器人能够在人行道上安全地移动，需要一种能够预测人们未来位置的方法。

Method: 开发了名为ReForesight的工具包，采用了一个具有36.6k参数的多层感知器(MLP)来对每个跟踪到的人或车辆进行最多3秒的未来位置预测。此预测过程受到卡尔曼滤波器作为后备方案以及静止门控机制的支持，以保证预测结果的可靠性。同时，对于因遮挡而暂时丢失的目标，系统利用已计算的关键点描述符和三维度量门控来进行重新识别。

Result: 在四个保留的CODa序列上的测试表明，相较于单独使用卡尔曼滤波器（0.728米），MLP方法将平均3秒最终位移误差降至0.662米；并且在所有测试序列中都优于大小相似的GRU模型（0.692米）。每实例处理时间仅为0.04毫秒，在单个CPU核心上运行。

Conclusion: ReForesight提供了一种有效提高行人轨迹预测精度的方法，有助于增强机器人导航系统的安全性。其开源特性也促进了相关领域研究的发展。

Abstract: Robots sharing walkways need to know where people will be, yet semantic occupancy maps show only where they are. We present ReForesight, an open-source ROS 2 toolkit that adds instance-level future occupancy and re-identification after occlusion to a particle-based semantic mapping pipeline. A 36.6k-parameter MLP predicts each tracked person or vehicle up to 3 s ahead, guarded by a Kalman fallback and a stationary gate, and the object's own points are moved to the forecast pose, forming a "ghost" point cloud in the map frame without an occupancy decoder. An optional tracker re-associates objects after occlusion using keypoint descriptors the pipeline already computes and a metric 3D gate. On four held-out CODa sequences, the MLP lowers the mean 3 s final displacement error from 0.728 m (Kalman) to 0.662 m and beats a similar-size GRU (0.692 m) on every sequence, at 0.04 ms per instance on one CPU core. Code, all models, and a ROS-free demo are released; field demonstrations use an outdoor cleaning robot.

</details>


### [163] [Phase-Conditioned Affordance Fields for SE(3)-Equivariant Visuomotor Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17281)
*Xinzhe Chen,Liqi Huang,Sihua Ren,Haowen Sun,Mingyang Li,Zeyang Liu,Xingyu Chen,Xuguang Lan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于相位条件的可承受场（PAF）的方法，用于改进SE(3)-等变视觉运动策略。该方法通过引入局部几何归纳偏置同时保持全局SE(3)等变性，提高了机器人在不同物体间执行任务时的成功率，并在多对象真实机器人任务中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的SE(3)-等变视觉运动策略依赖于全局等变假设，即将整个观察视为由单一刚性变换控制，这在实际操作任务中不适用，因为与任务相关的区域会随着不同物体而变化。为了解决这个问题，研究者开发了一种新的方法来增强策略对局部变化的适应能力。

Method: 提出了学习一种相位条件下的可承受场（PAF），它是一种针对观测点云中的每个点评分机制。此评分机制以两种互补方式被SE(3)-等变策略所利用：其评分加权质心作为学习到、相位条件下的锚点，用来标准化观察和动作围绕活动区域；评分本身也被作为每一点特征附加。这样既引入了局部几何归纳偏置，又保持了策略的全局SE(3)等变性质。

Result: 在多种MimicGen任务以及空间随机化条件下验证了该方法的有效性，结果显示PAF能够持续提高基础策略的成功率。对于涉及多个物体的真实机器人任务而言，PAF不仅优于基线方法，而且即使是在光照条件变化的情况下也能保持领先地位。

Conclusion: 通过引入相位条件下的可承受场（PAF），可以有效地改善SE(3)-等变视觉运动策略的表现，特别是在处理需要考虑局部变化的任务时。这一方法不仅增强了策略对于环境变化的鲁棒性，还提升了整体任务成功率。

Abstract: SE(3)-equivariance provides a useful geometric inductive bias for visuomotor policies, encoding that actions covary with rigid transformations of the observation. However, existing SE(3)-equivariant visuomotor policies build this bias on a global equivariance assumption that treats the entire observation as governed by a single rigid transformation, which breaks down in real-world manipulation tasks where the task-relevant region changes across different objects. We address this by learning a phase-conditioned affordance field (PAF) over the observed point cloud, a per-point score that the SE(3)-equivariant policy uses in two complementary ways. Its score-weighted centroid serves as a learned, phase-conditioned anchor that canonicalizes the observation and the action around the active region, replacing the fixed gripper-frame reference, and the score itself is appended as a per-point feature. This introduces a local geometric inductive bias, while preserving the policy's global SE(3) equivariance. We validate on multiple MimicGen tasks under varying spatial randomization, and PAF consistently improves the success rate of the underlying policy. On multi-object real-robot tasks, PAF outperforms baselines and keeps its lead under lighting variations.

</details>


### [164] [Improving Policy Learning from Delayed Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17298)
*Xiru Zhu,David Meger,Gregory Dudek*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在模仿学习系统中人类监督的机器人系统里专家干预延迟的影响，并提出了一种新的方法LfDI，通过学习基于专家干预发生的时间和地点的成本函数来改善学习效果。实验表明，该方法优于其他不考虑干预延迟的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于以往的模仿学习工作主要考察了次优专家（即会犯错的专家策略）的影响，但假设专家会在错误发生后立即进行干预。而实际上，专家干预可能存在延迟，这种延迟对学习效果的影响尚未被充分研究。

Method: 提出了一种名为Learning from Delayed Intervention (LfDI)的新方法，该方法根据专家干预的时间点与位置学习一个成本函数，并结合专家提供的关于干预延迟程度的信息，以此推断出没有专家数据状态下的额外指导信息，帮助策略避开可能引发干预的状态。

Result: 通过在模拟环境中评估LfDI方法，发现其相较于那些未考虑干预延迟情况的方法，在表现上更优。

Conclusion: 尽管延迟的专家干预可以被视为一种可能增强策略鲁棒性的噪音形式，但从整体上看，从延迟的专家干预中学习不如从未延迟的专家干预中学习有效；不过，通过LfDI方法可以在一定程度上缓解这一问题。

Abstract: In this work, we explore the idea of expert intervention delays in human-supervised robotic systems for imitation learning system. Previous works in imitation learning have primarily examined the impact of suboptimal experts, where the expert policy makes mistakes, but assumed expert interventions occur immediately after errors are made. Although delayed expert intervention can be viewed as a form of noise that might improve policy robustness, our results show that learning from delayed expert interventions is overall less effective than learning from non-delayed expert interventions; delayed expert interventions are less effective at demonstrating error avoidance behaviors.
  To address this, we propose Learning from Delayed Intervention (LfDI), which learns a cost function based on where and when the expert intervenes, combined with limited information about the extent of intervention delays provided by the expert. This cost function is then used to infer additional supervision for states without expert data, guiding the policy to avoid states that would likely trigger interventions. We evaluate our method, Learning from Delayed Intervention (LfDI), on simulated environments and show that it outperforms other methods that do not account for intervention delays.

</details>


### [165] [Evaluating Long-Horizon Robot Planning under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17326)
*Harsh Muriki,Gayathri Aravindan,Yuchen Zheng,Sonia Chernova*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了在Habitat中进行的七项长期家庭任务的基准测试，以评估ReAct、VLM-TAMP和Tru-POMDP三种规划器在不同初始机器人记忆和环境配置下的表现。实验结果表明，尽管ReAct在整体任务成功率（45.3%）和完成度（71.7%）上表现最佳，但准确的初始记忆并不总是为每个规划器带来最高的性能。通过执行轨迹分析，研究还指出了重复出现的位置错误、前提条件错误以及冗余动作问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探索家庭机器人如何在内部模型和外部世界的不完美条件下成功执行任务，并通过一系列长期任务来比较不同规划方法的效果与适应性。

Method: 研究人员设计了七个长期的家庭任务作为基准，在Habitat环境中实施，同时考虑了机器人的初始记忆状态和环境配置的变化。他们使用统一的执行框架对ReAct、VLM-TAMP及Tru-POMDP这三种规划方法进行了测试，覆盖了90种任务-环境-记忆组合情况，共计1,350个回合。

Result: 结果显示，ReAct在总体任务成功率(45.3%)和完成度(71.7%)方面表现最好；然而，拥有精确的初始记忆并不总是能保证所有规划者都能达到最高性能。此外，通过对执行轨迹的规则基础分析，确定了一些常见的位置错误、前提条件错误和冗余动作。

Conclusion: 这项研究表明，不同的规划算法对于利用初始知识和应对不确定性有着不同的方式。尽管某些情况下准确的记忆有助于提高性能，但这并不是决定性的因素，而有效处理未知或变化的情况同样重要。

Abstract: Household robots must successfully navigate im-perfect circumstances, both in their internal models and the external world. We introduce a benchmark of seven long-horizon household tasks in Habitat that vary initial robot memory and environment configurations. We evaluate adaptations of ReAct, VLM-TAMP, and Tru-POMDP within a shared execution framework across 90 tasks-environment-memory combinations, totaling 1,350 episodes. Our evaluation measures task success, partial completion, and action efficiency, along with rule-based analysis of execution traces. ReAct achieves the highest overall task success (45.3%) and completion (71.7%), while having accurate initial memory does not consistently yield the highest performance for every planner. We identify recurring placement errors, precondition errors, and redundant actions through trace analysis. These findings highlight differences in how the evaluated planners use initial knowledge and respond to uncertainty.

</details>


### [166] [Can a Frontier VLM Serve as a Robot Policy for Memory-Dependent Manipulation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17350)
*Jack B. Jedlicki,Amish Sethi,Heng Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究使用了GPT-6-Astra模型作为机器人策略，在RoboMME基准测试的16个依赖记忆的任务上达到了92.1%的成功率，超过了之前的人类成绩90.5%，并且远高于最佳排行榜记录（60.8%）和一个结合了相同模型与RoboMME训练策略的分层系统（79.1%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索通用模型在不进行特定任务训练的情况下能否有效地执行复杂的、需要记忆能力的机器人操作任务。

Method: 直接使用冻结的视觉-语言模型GPT-6-Astra作为机器人策略来完成RoboMME中的16项任务，该模型能够保持整个事件的情境，并输出每个末端执行器路径点和夹具命令，而无需针对RoboMME的具体训练。

Result: 模型在800次测试中成功率为92.1%，超过先前报道的人类得分90.5%，显著优于最好的排行榜条目(60.8%)以及配对了同样模型与RoboMME训练策略的层次化系统(79.1%)。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，单一通用目的模型可以在实体系统中接管长周期规划、记忆和末端执行器控制之间的传统界限大部分功能，这引发了关于此类模型在实体系统中能承担多大范围职责的问题。

Abstract: We use a frozen frontier vision-language model, GPT-6-Astra, directly as the robot policy on RoboMME, a benchmark of 16 memory-dependent manipulation tasks spanning temporal, spatial, object, and procedural memory. The model keeps the whole episode in context and outputs every end-effector waypoint and gripper command, with no RoboMME-specific training. On the 800 test episodes it succeeds in 92.1%, above the previously reported human score of 90.5%, which was obtained with an oracle skill interface, and far ahead of the best leaderboard entry (60.8%) and of a hierarchical system that pairs the same model with a RoboMME-trained policy (79.1%). It matches or exceeds the human score on 13 of the 16 tasks. Its 63 failures concentrate in two tasks and are mostly premature completions and mistimed or unintended button presses. These results blur the usual division between long-horizon planning, memory and end-effector control, and raise the question of how much of this stack a single general-purpose model can take over in embodied systems. Project website: https://embodied-astra.github.io. Code, prompts and all episode logs: https://github.com/jackbjed/embodied-astra-robomme.

</details>


### [167] [ClearNav: Perception-Aligned Quadrotor Flight Using Differential-Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17419)
*Aditya Azad,Arthur Huang,Guanrui Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种名为ClearNav的导航策略，通过模拟训练并结合三个奖励项来限制无人机向观察到的方向移动，从而减少在杂乱环境中的碰撞率。实验表明，在模拟和真实环境中，该策略均能有效降低碰撞率，并成功实现安全飞行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于模拟训练并在硬件上运行的导航策略虽然能在开阔空间中高速飞行，但在障碍物间距缩小的情况下容易发生碰撞。研究者认为这些碰撞主要是由于无人机朝向机载摄像头未观测到的方向移动所导致。因此，提出了新的导航策略以解决这一问题。

Method: 研究团队开发了ClearNav导航策略，采用两种奖励机制约束运动方向至被观察方向：一个前视航向目标和对侧向速度的惩罚；另外还加入了一个针对靠近障碍物时速度的惩罚项，确保视野内出现的障碍物能够避开。

Result: 在18个训练过程中未曾见过的场景下进行2304次配对飞行测试后，与现有最先进方法相比，ClearNav提高了6.8个百分点的成功率，减少了65%的碰撞率，同时保持最高5米/秒的速度。即使仅使用接近度惩罚项，在匹配命令速度情况下，碰撞减少效果依然达到70%。此外，在真实世界杂乱环境中也验证了该策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 研究表明，通过适当设计导航策略可以显著提高小型无人机在复杂环境下的安全性，即便是在有限传感器和计算资源条件下也能实现更安全的飞行。

Abstract: Autonomous quadrotor flight in cluttered environment is an key capability for tasks like inspection and search. Navigation policies trained in simulation and transferred to hardware can fly at high speed in open space, but they can still collide as obstacle clearance shrinks. We attribute most of these collisions to a single mechanism, namely motion in directions the onboard camera does not observe. Heading that is not aligned with the direction of travel and lateral excursion during obstacle avoidance both carry the vehicle toward obstacles outside its field of view. We introduce ClearNav, a navigation policy trained with two reward terms that constrain motion to the observed direction, a forward-looking heading target and a penalty on lateral velocity. A third term penalizes speed near obstacles so that an obstacle entering the field of view remains avoidable. In simulation, across 18 held-out scenarios with 2304 paired flights per policy unseen in training, ClearNav improves success by 6.8 points, cuts the collision rate by 65% relative to the state of the arts, and flies at maximum 5m/s. For the proximity term alone under matched commanded speeds, the collision reduction still holds at 70%. The effect is significant on static scenes and consistent in direction on moving obstacles, though not significant there at the suite level, showing that safer flight in clutter is possible even on the limited sensors and compute of small drones. We also validate our navigation policy in a real-world cluttered environment, showing that it enables the quadrotor to navigate through the cluttered environment successfully and safely.

</details>


### [168] [MIRROR: Reflecting Hidden Symmetries from Offline Robot Interaction Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17422)
*Shiqi Liu,Yihang Yao,Peide Huang,Mengdi Xu,Ding Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种名为MIRROR的新框架，该框架能够从离线交互数据中学习已知对称群如何在部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP）中的观察和动作上共同作用。通过对抗性元组匹配和显式的群一致性正则化，MIRROR减少了变换组合下的一致性错误，并且使用学到的变换进行行为克隆时，在四个Robosuite桌面操作任务上实现了更高的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的对称感知方法通常依赖于具有预定义变换的表示，这需要根据感知设置和任务进行定制化的预处理。对于像素观测而言，即使是简单的物理对称性也可能导致复杂的、视点依赖性的图像变换，这些变换很难手动指定。因此，研究旨在开发一种能自动学习观察-动作转换的方法，以适应不同的对称性需求。

Method: MIRROR框架利用对抗性元组匹配技术来关联观察与动作上的变换，并通过明确的群一致性正则化惩罚违反群运算公理的行为。它基于离线交互数据工作，适用于部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDPs）。

Result: 在四个Robosuite桌面操作任务上，MIRROR在三个对称质量度量指标中达到了最低的总误差，其中兼容性误差相比LaLiGAN基线降低了61%。当将学到的变换用于帧平均行为克隆时，所有四个任务的成功率均高于纯行为克隆，平均成功率从80.48%提高到85.12%，单个任务的最大增益达到9.66个百分点。此外，还在模拟环境中重复了对称发现和策略训练实验，并成功地零样本部署到了真实的Kinova GEN3机械臂上。

Conclusion: MIRROR提供了一个有效的方法来学习观察-动作变换，从而改进了机器人策略的学习。实验结果表明，MIRROR不仅能够在多个度量标准下减少对称性相关的错误，而且还能提升实际操作任务的成功率。

Abstract: Symmetry-aware robot policy learning requires coupled observation-action transformations. For pixel observations, even simple physical symmetries can induce complex, viewpoint-dependent image transformations that are difficult to specify by hand. Existing symmetry-aware methods often rely on representations with predefined transformations, requiring preprocessing tailored to the sensing setup and task. We introduce MIRROR, a framework that learns how a known symmetry group operates jointly on observations and actions in partially observable Markov decision processes (POMDPs) from offline interaction data. MIRROR couples these transformations through adversarial tuple matching over one-step transition tuples, while explicit group-consistency regularization penalizes violations of the group-operation axioms. On four Robosuite tabletop manipulation tasks, MIRROR achieves the lowest aggregate error on three of four symmetry-quality metrics, including a 61% reduction in compatibility error (which measures consistency under transformation composition) relative to an adapted LaLiGAN baseline. Using the learned transformations in frame-averaged behavior cloning (BC) yields higher mean success rates than vanilla BC on all four tasks, increasing average success from 80.48% to 85.12%, with per-task gains up to 9.66 percentage points. We also repeat symmetry discovery and policy training in simulation with heightmap and segmentation-mask observations, then deploy the resulting policy zero-shot on a real Kinova GEN3 arm. Project page: https://pomdp-mirror.github.io.

</details>


### [169] [Smoothness Ranks Skill, Not Success: An Audit of Published Trajectory-Smoothness Curation Metrics Under Episode-Length Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17427)
*Jaiveer Bassi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究测试了Spectral Arc Length (SAL)和Trajectory-Envelope Distance (TED)两种度量方法在真实机器人模仿学习数据上的有效性。结果显示，这两种方法的表现不如预期，特别是在控制了episode长度后，原始的平均急动度（jerk）反而表现更好。此外，使用TED筛选训练数据会显著降低策略的成功率，而SAL和急动度在效果上与不进行筛选无异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于验证之前提出的用于模仿学习演示筛选的轨迹平滑性指标（特别是Spectral Arc Length [SAL] 和 Trajectory-Envelope Distance [TED]）是否真的能在实际机器人数据中有效区分出高质量的演示。

Method: 通过分析真实robomimic数据集，在不同任务场景下对比SAL、TED与基本的急动度（jerk）指标。同时，还考察了基于这些指标筛选的数据子集对GMM行为克隆策略及扩散策略训练效果的影响。

Result: 实验发现，在考虑episode长度因素后，SAL和TED作为演示质量指标的有效性大大下降；尤其在特定条件下，TED甚至表现出负面效果。相反，简单的急动度测量显示出更好的相关性。此外，基于TED选择的数据子集导致较低的任务成功率。

Conclusion: 结果表明，尽管Spectral Arc Length (SAL) 和 Trajectory-Envelope Distance (TED) 在某些情况下可能提供有用的信息，但它们并不总是可靠的模仿学习演示筛选工具。在特定条件下，更简单的度量如急动度可能更为适用。

Abstract: Trajectory smoothness has been proposed as a cheap, training-free signal for curating imitation-learning demonstrations, most recently by rinse, which introduces Spectral Arc Length (SAL) and a contact-aware Trajectory-Envelope Distance (TED). We test whether these published metrics survive episode-length controls on real robomimic data. On can/mh, SAL separates better from worse operators with AUROC 0.952, but episode length alone achieves 0.957, and inside a duration-matched band SAL falls to 0.628 while raw mean jerk, the baseline rinse argues against, rises to 0.896. The confound replicates on square/mh. On can/mg, where every episode is exactly 150 steps, SAL is at chance (0.454) and TED is inverted (0.376) against task success, while jerk reaches 0.706. Training GMM behavior-cloning policies on each metric's curated subset (10 seeds), curating by TED costs roughly a third of downstream success relative to random selection of the same size (0.240 vs. 0.360, p=0.0001, surviving Bonferroni correction), while SAL and jerk are indistinguishable from not curating. Under a diffusion policy TED still ranks worst, though its deficit is not resolvable at five seeds, and jerk becomes significantly better than both baselines, so the curation decision does not transfer unchanged across the two policy configurations we tested. Inspecting trajectories shows why TED inverts: successful manipulation requires sustained high-deviation motion through contact, while failure is low-amplitude hovering that a smoothed envelope follows closely.

</details>


### [170] [Act4R: Action-conditioned 4D reconstruction for kinematics-free control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17462)
*Zhaoliang Zhang,Chenhao Yu,Zhimin Shao,Xijun Liu,Cheng Peng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了Act4R框架，该框架基于3D基础模型，通过2D观察提取的4D特征来预测机器人在给定动作下的3D场景流。实验表明，Act4R在场景流预测和逆向控制方面均优于现有技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确控制各种机器人形态的运动是一个长期存在的挑战。建模复杂的机器人结构需要大量时间、专用传感器和手动标记。最近在4D表示学习方面的进展提供了一种新的可能：能否利用从2D观察中提升的4D特征来根据动作信号密集地映射机器人的空间移动？

Method: 提出了一种名为Act4R的框架，它基于3D基础模型来预测机器人对于任何给定动作的3D场景流动。Act4R引入了空间-动作编码器，将当前2D机器人状态及其动作信号编码为能够代表未来3D机器人状态的嵌入。接着，当前和未来的空间嵌入被解码成点图以估计条件于动作的场景流动。为了有效地学习动作与未来状态之间的映射关系，Act4R在其基于转换器的编码器中引入了空间-动作融合，并且引入了一个空间对齐损失函数，用于指导编码器进行未来流动预测。

Result: 经过对特定物理形式的一些样本训练后，Act4R能够通过对信号进行优化来执行逆向控制，以达到所需的场景流动。研究者在一个公开的真实世界数据集以及模拟器中收集的合成数据上评估了Act4R。不同机器人平台上的实验表明，Act4R在场景流动预测方面超过了之前的最先进水平（SoTA）。此外，在逆向控制方面，无论是动作准确性指标还是闭合回路控制仿真测试中，我们的方法都表现得更好。

Conclusion: Act4R框架成功地展示了如何利用4D特征来改进机器人运动控制，特别是在预测3D场景流及逆向控制方面表现出色。这为解决复杂机器人结构建模提供了新的思路和技术手段。

Abstract: Precise control of movement across various robotic embodiments is a longstanding challenge. Modeling complex robotic structures requires significant time, dedicated sensors, and manual labeling. Recent advancements in 4D representation learning offer an interesting alternative: can we leverage 4D features lifted from 2D observations to densely map a robot's spatial movement based on the action signal? To this end, we propose Act4R, a framework that builds on 3D foundation model to predict a robot's 3D scene flow with any given action. Act4R introduces a Spatial-Action Encoder, which encodes the current 2D robot state and its action signals into embeddings that represent future robot state in 3D. Then, both the current and future spatial embeddings are decoded into point maps to estimate action-conditioned scene flow. To effectively learn the mapping between action and future state, Act4R introduces spatial-action fusion in its transformer-based encoder, and a spatial alignment loss that guides the encoder for future flow prediction. After training on some samples of a given embodiment, Act4R can perform inverse control by optimizing the signal to achieve the desired scene flow. We evaluate Act4R on a public real-world dataset and synthetic data collected in simulator. Experiments on various robotic platforms show that Act4R outperforms the previous SoTA in scene flow prediction. Our method also performs better in inverse control, both on the action accuracy metric and in closed-loop control in simulation.

</details>


### [171] [From Orbit Matching to Docking: A Hierarchical Reinforcement-Learning Approach to Multiphase Rendezvous Trajectory Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17468)
*Mariko Storey-Matsutani,Anatoly Zlotnik,Lee Holguin,Zigfried Hampel-Arias,Richard Linares*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分层强化学习框架，用于实现从轨道匹配到对接的全轨迹自主三自由度近地操作。通过训练五个专门策略来应对不同阶段的任务，并由一个高级管理策略根据航天器状态选择合适的控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着空间任务复杂性的增加，自主航天器导航必须适应具有不同动态、目标和约束条件的任务阶段之间的转换。尤其是近距离操作（RPO）覆盖了从轨道尺度匹配到最终对接的多个运行模式，而现有的强化学习方法通常只解决这一过程中的部分问题或依赖于手动定义的控制器切换条件。

Method: 研究中提出了一种适用于全轨迹三自由度RPO的分层强化学习框架。该框架包括针对轨道匹配、远距离接近、中距离接近、近距离接近以及对接等不同阶段训练出的专业化策略。同时，还有一个学习得到的高层次管理者策略，依据不断变化的航天器状态来选择使用哪一个特定阶段的控制器。

Result: 对所提出的层次结构方法在整个端到端会合序列上进行了评估，并与固定顺序调用启发式方法进行了对比分析，以评估轨迹性能及过渡行为。此外，还单独分析了各个阶段策略以及整个分层系统的训练和推理指标。

Conclusion: 本文展示了一个能够处理从轨道匹配到对接完整流程的分层强化学习系统，它在提高RPO任务自动化水平方面展现出潜力。

Abstract: As the complexity of space missions increases, autonomous spacecraft guidance must accommodate transitions between mission phases with distinct dynamics, objectives, and constraints. Rendezvous and proximity operations (RPO) are particularly challenging because they span multiple operating regimes, from orbital-scale matching and far-range approach to close-range maneuvering and terminal docking. Existing reinforcement-learning approaches generally address only a subset of this sequence or rely on manually defined switching conditions between controllers. This study presents a hierarchical reinforcement-learning framework for autonomous, three-degree-of-freedom RPO that spans the complete trajectory from orbit matching to docking. Five specialized policies are trained for orbit matching, far-range approach, mid-range approach, close-range approach, and docking. A learned high-level manager policy then selects among these phase-specific controllers based on the evolving spacecraft state. The hierarchical approach is evaluated over the full end-to-end rendezvous sequence and compared with a fixed sequential-invocation heuristic to assess trajectory performance and transition behavior. Training and inference metrics are also analyzed for the individual phase policies and the complete hierarchical system.

</details>


### [172] [An Optimal Algorithm for Changing from Longitudinal to Latitudinal Formation of Autonomous Aircraft Squadrons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17511)
*Paulo André Sperandio Giacomin,Elder Moreira Hemerly*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于自主飞机编队从纵向到横向编队变换的算法，通过预定义的一组3D基础机动动态定义机动。仿真结果显示这种变换无碰撞发生，并且正确性证明保证了其横向编队特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当编队需要执行诸如着陆、起飞、避障和地理参考等任务时，要求能够灵活地在不同编队形式之间切换，这是研究此转换算法的主要动机。

Method: 该研究采用了一套预定义的3D基本动作来动态定义机动过程，以实现从纵向到横向编队的变化。

Result: 时间复杂度分析表明该转换算法效率最优；仿真结果展示了变换过程中没有发生碰撞情况；此外，还提供了关于形成横向编队特性的正确性证明。

Conclusion: 提出的算法为自主飞机编队提供了一种有效的方法来进行纵向与横向之间的编队变换，支持多种应用场景下的需求。

Abstract: This work presents an algorithm for changing from longitudinal to latitudinal formation of autonomous aircraft squadrons. The maneuvers are defined dynamically by using a predefined set of 3D basic maneuvers. This formation change is necessary when the squadron has to perform tasks demanding both formations, such as landing, lift-off, obstacle avoidance and georeferencing. The time complexity analysis of the transformation algorithm reveals that its efficiency is optimal. Simulations show that the formation change is done without collision, and the proof of correctness ensures its latitudinal formation features.

</details>


### [173] [YUBI-Real2Sim: 3DGS Scene Verification for Reliable Policy Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17521)
*Hiroki Nishizawa,Takehiko Ohkawa,Nicola Scianca,Tatsuya Matsushima,Shigeo Morishima,Kei Ota*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: A novel YUBI-Real2Sim framework is introduced for 3DGS-based scene reconstruction and policy evaluation, addressing challenges in assessing VLA policies with wrist-mounted cameras. It effectively reproduces real-world policy outcomes but underscores the need for careful assessment of reconstruction quality for reliable policy evaluation.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the high cost, time consumption, and difficulty in reproducing evaluations of VLA policies on real robots, which makes real-to-sim simulation crucial. There's also a need to quantify the required reconstruction quality for reliable policy evaluation, especially for policies that rely on wrist-mounted fisheye cameras, which are prone to visual artifacts.

Method: The method involves developing YUBI-Real2Sim, an integrated framework for 3DGS-based scene reconstruction and real-to-sim policy evaluation. This includes 200-degree fisheye camera capture, 3DGS training, 3D foundation models for object modeling, and MuJoCo-based physical simulation. The framework is used to evaluate bimanual robot platforms across different scenes, tasks, and policies, measuring real-to-sim rank agreement and sensitivity to 3DGS artifacts and capture disturbances.

Result: Results indicate that the YUBI-Real2Sim framework successfully reproduces the observed real-world policy ordering in eight out of nine scene-task comparisons. The analysis further shows the task-dependent nature of performance and limitations regarding the use of quality scores to predict policy-ranking reliability. Additionally, it emphasizes the importance of evaluating reconstruction quality in Real2Sim setups.

Conclusion: The study concludes that while the YUBI-Real2Sim framework can effectively reproduce real-world policy ordering in most scene-task comparisons, the reliability of quality-based predictions for policy ranking remains task-dependent. It highlights the necessity to assess reconstruction quality when using Real2Sim for policy evaluation, as a generally reliable scene-acceptance rule has not yet been established.

Abstract: Evaluating VLA policies on real robots is costly, time-consuming, and difficult to reproduce, making real-to-sim simulation essential for scalable evaluation. While 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables rapid real-to-sim scene generation, the reconstruction quality required for reliable policy evaluation remains unquantified. In particular, policies relying on wrist-mounted fisheye cameras (e.g., YUBI handheld gripper data) are heavily exposed to visual artifacts (holes, floaters) in wide-angle views, directly degrading task performance. To address this, we present YUBI-Real2Sim, a unified framework for 3DGS-based scene reconstruction and real-to-sim policy evaluation under dual fisheye wrist views. We develop an integrated pipeline combining 200-degree fisheye camera capture, 3DGS training, 3D foundation models for object modeling, and MuJoCo-based physical simulation, reproducing the observed real-world policy ordering in eight of nine scene-task comparisons. Using this framework, we construct a benchmark across two bimanual robot platforms, three scenes, three tasks, and dual pi0.5-based policies. This systematically measures real-to-sim rank agreement and evaluates policy sensitivity against counterfactual 3DGS artifacts (ceiling holes, floaters, warping) and capture disturbances. We further analyze whether novel-view quality scores can indicate policy-ranking reliability under a scene-held-out evaluation protocol. The results show task-dependent performance and limitations of quality-based reliability prediction, highlighting the need to assess reconstruction quality when using Real2Sim for policy evaluation. These results do not yet establish a generally reliable scene-acceptance rule. Project Page: https://yubi-real2sim.airoa.io.

</details>


### [174] [Box-WAM: Box Trajectories as Compact State Representations for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17526)
*Qinxi Yu,Wenzhou Ding,Gloria X. Wang,Albert J. Zhai,Shenlong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为BoxWAM的方法，该方法通过预测任务相关物体的定向边界框轨迹来替代密集未来帧的预测，从而为机器人操作提供了一个轻量级且高效的预测目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管世界行动模型（WAMs）在多种机器人操作任务中表现优于仅动作模型，但使用全帧视频预测表示未来状态在训练和推理过程中计算成本较高。因此，研究者们探讨了何种紧凑的状态表示能在保留未来预测带来的规划与监督优势的同时降低计算负担。

Method: 提出了BoxWAM方法，它用任务相关物体的定向边界框轨迹代替未来的密集帧预测。这种方法直接设想以物体为中心的未来运动，为目标提供了轻量级预测，并捕捉到了由机器人动作引起的关键物理变化。

Result: 研究表明，通过边界框轨迹预测不仅大幅降低了计算成本，而且提高了动作预测性能，表明以物体为中心的紧凑状态可以作为基于视频的世界行动建模的有效替代方案用于机器人操作。

Conclusion: BoxWAM方法证明了通过关注于物体中心的未来运动预测，能够有效减少计算需求同时保持甚至提高动作预测质量，为机器人操纵领域的世界行动建模提供了一种新的高效途径。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) are a category of policy models that ex- plicitly predict both actions and future world states. They have been shown to outperform action-only models across various robotic manipulation tasks. How- ever, representing the future with full-frame video prediction can be computation- ally expensive during both training and inference. This raises a central question: what compact state representation preserves the planning and supervision bene- fits of future prediction? In this paper, we propose BoxWAM, which replaces dense future frames with oriented bounding box trajectories of task-relevant ob- jects. By explicitly imagining object-centric future motion, our method provides a lightweight prediction target that captures the key physical changes induced by robot actions. We show that box trajectory prediction substantially reduces com- putational cost while improving action prediction performance, suggesting that object-centric compact states can serve as an efficient alternative to video-based world action modeling for robotic manipulation. Result videos can be found on https://box-wam.github.io

</details>


### [175] [Sim4RAD: A MATLAB Toolkit for Design-Phase Sizing, Actuator Selection and URDF-Model Verification of Serial Robotic Arms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17534)
*Jingchen Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为Sim4RAD的MATLAB工具包，用于执行器尺寸设计阶段和后续验证。通过几个参数构建刚体模型，并提供四种分析方法来确定规格：静态分析、动态管道、随机分析以及工作空间和灵巧性分析。该工具包也可用于基于URDF文件的设计验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 执行器尺寸设计是早期的重要决策，它决定了电机、变速箱和制动器的选择。Sim4RAD旨在为这一阶段及后续验证提供支持。

Method: 开发了Sim4RAD工具包，它能从少量参数中构建刚体模型，并通过四种类型的分析（静态分析、动态管道、随机分析、工作空间与灵巧性分析）来辅助确定执行器规格。此外，通过替换URDF文件，这些分析同样可用于设计验证。

Result: 对于7自由度的人形手臂，在三种不同的布局下进行了比较。结果显示，肩轴顺序的变化对最坏情况下的扭矩影响不大，而将最后一个腕关节与负载同轴可以显著减少某些关节的需求扭矩，但会降低灵巧性。此外，5m/s的目标速度比2秒动作所需扭矩高20至37倍。

Conclusion: Sim4RAD工具包有效地帮助了执行器尺寸设计过程及其后的验证工作。不同机械臂布局对性能的影响各异，且场景设定比具体轨迹更重要。

Abstract: Actuator sizing is an early and consequential decision: joint torque and velocity specifications fix the motors, gearboxes and brakes before any CAD model exists. Sim4RAD is a MATLAB toolkit for that phase and for the verification that follows it. It builds a rigid-body model from a handful of parameters, and four analyses supply the specification: a static analysis combining lever-arm sampling with a posture search, giving a rated torque at a chosen payload envelope and a reproducible peak torque; a dynamics pipeline that reshapes a trajectory under one of four constraints, including the actuator torque-speed envelope; a stochastic analysis over many random motions, so a specification rests on a class of motions, not one trajectory; and a workspace and dexterity analysis that separates where the arm can reach from how it can reach there. Replacing the synthesized model by a URDF file turns the same analyses to verification. A 7-DOF anthropomorphic arm is compared in three layouts differing in the shoulder axis order and in whether the last wrist joint is coaxial with the payload. The static demand at half arm length is nearly the same in all three, and it sizes the joints rather than the sampled motion; reordering the shoulder axes leaves the worst-case torque unchanged at 55 N m and the reachable volume within 8 percent. A last wrist joint coaxial with the payload removes its gravity torque, reduces the adjacent wrist joint by almost a third and cuts the last joint demand tenfold in a 5 m/s scan, at the price of dexterity (mean 0.570 to 0.348). The same arm needs 20 to 37 times more torque for a 5 m/s target than for a 2 s motion: the scenario, not the trajectory, must be fixed first. Verification should use the URDF model of the detailed design; the toolkit is available at https://github.com/LasterLi/Sim4RAD.

</details>


### [176] [ATOM: Abstract Transition and Option Modeling - Experience-Informed Hierarchical Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17555)
*Changxuan Yao,Panagiotis Tsiotras*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为ATOM的分层导航框架，它结合了高层图规划和可重用的低层选项策略，在表示为3D场景图等层次结构的大规模3D环境中实现高效的重复导航。通过在多次导航任务中维护并逐步优化选项起始状态的经验分布，ATOM显著减少了累计重复规划时间，并且物理实验验证了其实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在大规模3D环境中提高重复导航任务的效率，研究者们提出了ATOM框架，该框架旨在利用环境的层级结构（如3D场景图）来减少规划时间和提升路径选择质量。

Method: ATOM基于一种层次化的图表示工作，例如使用3D场景图中的房间和地点层，每个抽象转换都与一个导航选项相关联。对于每个转换，ATOM计算并存储一个可重用的选项策略及其状态依赖的成本。随着更多导航任务的执行，ATOM会根据经验调整这些抽象转换成本。

Result: 实验表明，相比于单一MDP基线方法，ATOM能够将累积的重复规划时间降低97.5%，同时保持路径长度接近最优解。此外，通过经验信息进行细化还能进一步减少10.3%的抽象转换成本估计误差。实际测试中，ATOM成功地在一个F1Tenth车辆上完成了所有五次导航请求，其中43%的跨区域选项执行重用了先前生成的解决方案。

Conclusion: ATOM提供了一种有效的方法来解决大规模3D环境下的重复导航问题，不仅大幅降低了规划时间，而且通过持续学习改进了性能，显示出了良好的应用前景。

Abstract: We propose ATOM (Abstract Transition and Option Modeling), a hierarchical navigation framework that combines higher-level graph planning with reusable lower-level option policies for efficient repeated navigation in large-scale 3D environments represented by hierarchical structures such as 3D scene graphs (3DSGs). ATOM operates on a hierarchical graph representation, implemented using, for instance, the Rooms and Places layers of a 3DSG, where each directed abstract transition is associated with a navigation option. For each transition, ATOM computes and stores a reusable option policy along with its state-dependent cost-to-go. Across repeated navigation tasks, ATOM maintains a smoothed empirical distribution over option initiation states and uses it to progressively refine the corresponding abstract transition cost. Ablation studies show that ATOM reduces cumulative repeated-planning time by 97.5% relative to a monolithic MDP baseline while maintaining trajectories within approximately 7% of a Global Places A* shortest-path reference. Experience-informed refinement further reduces abstract transition-cost estimation error by 10.3%. Physical experiments validate ATOM on an F1Tenth vehicle, successfully completing all five navigation queries with 43% of inter-region option executions reusing previously generated solutions.

</details>


### [177] [Mobility-Budget-Constrained Submodular Maximization for Distributed Semantic Multi-Robot Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17562)
*Changxuan Yao,Sandilya Sai Garimella,Lu Gan,Panagiotis Tsiotras*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分布式语义探索框架，结合了Dirichlet映射、预算限制的轨迹选择和资源感知的贪婪协调策略，用于解决多机器人在通信和移动性约束下的探索问题。该方法提高了覆盖率、语义准确性和地图确定性，并且是唯一遵守每条轨迹移动预算的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决多机器人在通信与移动性约束条件下进行探索时如何平衡信息增益与可达性的问题。

Method: 提出了一种新的分布式语义探索框架，包括使用Dirichlet映射、基于预算限制的路径选择以及一种名为资源感知的贪婪（RAG）协调机制。每个机器人都会对其前沿区域进行聚类分析，通过一次考虑成本限制的Dijkstra搜索来计算地形及朝向敏感度最低的成本路径，并根据边际信息增益（以Dirichlet熵预期减少量表示）挑选可行路径。

Result: 实验结果显示，在十次网格世界消融研究中，所提方法相比最短距离前沿选择法在覆盖范围、语义mIoU值和地图确定性方面都有提高，同时单位距离上的熵和遍历成本也更低。此外，通过对预算敏感性的测试发现，一个适中的预算能够最好地平衡路径可达性和滚动视界执行效果。

Conclusion: 提出的分布式语义探索框架不仅有效提升了多机器人系统的探索性能，还确保了对每个轨迹移动预算的尊重，展示了良好的实用价值。

Abstract: Multi-robot exploration under communication and mobility constraints requires balancing information gain with reachability. We present a distributed semantic exploration framework that combines Dirichlet mapping, budget-constrained trajectory selection, and Resource-Aware distributed Greedy (RAG) coordination. Each robot clusters frontiers, computes terrain- and heading-aware least-cost paths with a single budget-limited Dijkstra search, and selects feasible paths by marginal information gain, represented by the expected reduction in Dirichlet entropy. Each robot's per-round selection is exact, the team's joint per-round information gain is at least half the centralized optimum, and the bound degrades gracefully under limited communication. Ten-run gridworld ablations show improved coverage, semantic mIoU, and map certainty with lower entropy and traversal cost per unit distance than shortest-distance frontier selection. Budget-sensitivity experiments show that an intermediate budget best balances path reach and receding-horizon execution. In photorealistic full-system simulation against five coordination rules run in their native published forms, the proposed method attains the highest mean coverage and semantic accuracy while being the only one that respects the per-trajectory mobility budget.

</details>


### [178] [EgoCPT: Learning Dexterous Manipulation from a Single Ego-Centric Video with Contact-Aware Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17572)
*Zhixin Sun,Kairui Shi,Tianao Ren,Ming Yang,Mark Cutkosky,James Jingxi Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为EgoCPT的新方法，可以从第一人称视角视频中学习高质量的灵巧手部轨迹，并直接应用于真实机器人。该方法仅需要RGB视频和参考手部姿态轨迹作为输入，特别擅长处理由于接触点优化框架而产生的噪声手部姿态问题。在五项操作任务上的测试表明，EgoCPT方法在使用不同质量的手部姿态重建数据时都优于多个基准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类的第一人称视角视频容易收集且网络上资源丰富，但要从这些视频中学习到适用于机器人的灵巧操作策略仍然具有挑战性，特别是难以因为较大的身体形态差异来重构物理基础的机器人运动轨迹。

Method: EgoCPT是一种新范式，能够直接从第一人称视角视频中学习可用于实际机器人的高质量重新定向灵巧手部轨迹。它只需要一段第一人称RGB视频和一条通过现成软件或特殊相机轻松获得的参考手部姿势轨迹作为输入。此方法包含一个新颖的接触感知优化框架，能迭代地优化机器人手与物体之间的接触点，从而对含有噪音的手部姿态具有很强的鲁棒性。

Result: 在五个不同的操作任务上进行了测试，EgoCPT方法取得了显著的成绩：对于含噪的EgoDex手部姿态重建达到了67.3%的成功率；而对于采用动作捕捉硬件获取的高质量TACO重建，则实现了92.0%的成功率。两种情况下均超越了多个基线方法的表现。

Conclusion: EgoCPT提供了一个有效的方法来解决从人类视频中学习并转移到机器人执行灵巧操作的问题，证明了即使面对较大身体差距及噪声干扰的情况下也能生成适用于真实世界应用的高质手部轨迹。

Abstract: While expert demonstrations on real robots are difficult to collect, human ego-centric videos are easy to collect and abundant on the internet. However, learning dexterous manipulation policies from human videos remains challenging---one of the most important challenges being the difficulty of reconstructing physically grounded robot trajectories due to the large embodiment gap. In this paper, we propose EgoCPT, a novel paradigm for learning high-quality retargeted dexterous hand trajectories directly from an ego-centric video, which can then be deployed directly on real robots. The only inputs to our method are an ego-centric RGB video and a reference hand pose trajectory, which can be easily obtained through off-the-shelf software or specialized cameras during recording. Our method is particularly robust to noisy hand poses attributed to our novel contact-aware optimization framework, which iteratively optimizes the contact points between the robot hands and objects. Across five manipulation tasks, our method achieves overall success rates of 67.3% on noisy EgoDex hand-pose reconstructions and 92.0% on high-quality TACO reconstructions collected using motion-capture hardware, outperforming multiple baselines on both benchmarks.

</details>


### [179] [TacSpin: Tactile Tracking of In-Gripper Object Rotation on SO(3) via Axis-Magnitude Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17574)
*Yuhao Wu,Linhao Song,Huan Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为TacSpin的方法，利用两个基于视觉的触觉传感器来追踪夹持物体在SO(3)空间中的旋转情况，通过分别估计旋转轴和旋转量，并结合接触边界信息优化每个手指的响应，最后融合两指信息以实现更准确的物体旋转跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决由于接触覆盖有限及不均匀形变导致的触觉估计难题，提高机器人操作中对夹持物体内运动反馈的准确性。

Method: 开发了TacSpin方法，该方法采用两组基于视觉的触觉传感器；独立地估计旋转轴与旋转大小而非直接拟合刚体模型；使用接触边界线索进一步精炼单个手指的反应；依据两者的一致性将来自两个手指的信息进行融合。

Result: 实验表明，在标准物体上，TacSpin相比其他方法能够达到更低的平均检查点误差。此外，通过对真实世界物体的实际演示证明了其能够在不同接触几何条件下有效跟踪物体旋转。

Conclusion: TacSpin提供了一种新的途径来改进机器人抓握过程中物体旋转的追踪精度，展示了在复杂接触场景下增强触觉感知能力的可能性。

Abstract: Tracking object motion within a gripper provides essential feedback for robotic manipulation. However, limited contact coverage and uneven deformation make tactile estimation challenging. In this study, we present TacSpin, a method that tracks in-gripper object rotation on SO(3) using only two vision-based tactile sensors. The key idea is to estimate the axis and magnitude of the rotation response separately, rather than fitting a rigid-body model by least squares. Each finger's response is further refined with contact-boundary cues. The two fingers are then fused according to their agreement. On standard objects, TacSpin achieves lower mean checkpoint errors. Qualitative demonstrations on real-world objects, using calibration transferred from a standard sphere, illustrate tracking under different contact geometries.

</details>


### [180] [Training Generalist Navigation Policies via Action Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17593)
*Bowen Feng,Keqin Wang,Yulong Yang,Amogh Joshi,Christine Allen-Blanchette,Dhruv Shah,Felix Heide*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Action Self-Distillation (ASD)的方法，用于在没有人工标注语言或推理标签的情况下学习机器人导航的语言条件推理。通过利用开源视觉-语言模型(VLM)作为教师网络和学生策略，结合特权轨迹反馈进行训练，从而显著提高了室内、室外及多阶段任务中的导航成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决训练语言条件下的机器人策略和推理通常需要昂贵的人工注释的问题，旨在开发一种无需人工标注语言或推理标签的学习方法，以促进更自然的人机交互方式。

Method: 使用了一个包含广泛世界知识和推理能力的开源VLM作为教师网络和学生策略。训练过程中，自教师接收特权轨迹反馈来推断导航目标以及面向目标的推理；随后，仅基于车载观察训练学生去适应推测出的导航目标、预测未来第一人称视角轨迹，并提供场景语义、计划与推理描述。

Result: 在13个具有挑战性的室内外真实世界导航任务中，该方法相比最强基准，在室内设置下提升了30%的成功率，在多阶段任务上也提升了30%，并在室外任务中达到了95%的成功率。

Conclusion: 实验证明了特权轨迹反馈能够使自我蒸馏成为可能，进而支持无需人工标注即可扩展通用导航策略的有效性。

Abstract: Language provides a natural interface for humans to specify and reason about goals, but training language-conditioned robotic policies and reasoning typically requires expensive manual annotations. We propose Action Self-Distillation (ASD), a method for learning language-conditioned reasoning for robotic navigation without human-annotated language or reasoning labels. ASD uses an open-source VLM, which contains broad world knowledge and reasoning capabilities, as both a teacher network and a student policy. During training, the self-teacher receives privileged trajectory feedback and infers the underlying navigation goal together with goal-oriented reasoning. The student is then trained to condition on the inferred navigation goal, predict future ego-frame trajectory, and provide a description of the scene semantics, plan, and reasoning, from onboard observations alone. Across 13 challenging indoor and outdoor real-world navigation tasks, our method improves success rate over the strongest baseline by $30\%$ in indoor settings, $30\%$ on multi-stage tasks, and achieves $95\%$ success rate on outdoor tasks, validating that privileged trajectory feedback can enable self-distillation for scalable generalist navigation policies without human annotations.

</details>


### [181] [From Pixels to Executable Physics: Behavior-Grounded World Models for Embodied Action Consequence Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17630)
*Ruo Chen,Jiahua Pang,Yingjie Niu,Hongyi Lin,Jun Tian,Yang Zhu,Chao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种新的物理一致的世界模型DR-WM，该模型通过结合机器人-地形接触动力学来预测四足机器人行动的结果。通过从初始RGB图像开始重建场景几何并整合密集无量纲的地形接触属性先验，支持模拟具有空间变化接触特性的物理。实验表明DR-WM在关键物理指标上优于仅基于几何或使用均匀物理参数的基线，并且当与80亿参数视觉-语言模型结合时，在挑战性情境中表现优于未经辅助的320亿参数模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于户外环境中地形和接触条件的变化，相同的动作命令可能对四足机器人产生不同的运动结果。准确预测这些结果对于可靠的导航和路径选择至关重要。然而，现有的视频世界模型往往难以捕捉到机器人的动态和地形交互作用，导致对未来动作结果的预测过于乐观。

Method: 提出的方法名为DR-WM，它首先从一个初始RGB图像出发，重建场景几何结构，并融合了基于现实世界动作响应数据校准得到的、关于地形接触性质的密集无量纲先验信息。此方法能够支持模拟不同摩擦等接触特性随空间变化的物理现象。给定机器人初始状态及候选动作序列后，可以模拟其未来动向，并利用所得相机轨迹和渲染几何体来训练视频世界模型。生成的未来观测值可用于更新地形重建，从而实现无需额外实际图像参与的迭代模型展开。

Result: 广泛的实验证明了DR-WM在描述动作结果的关键物理指标方面明显优于仅有几何基础或使用统一物理参数的基础模型，并且在所有评估过的基线中表现最佳。此外，在90个具有挑战性的场景下，配备了DR-WM的80亿参数视觉-语言模型在六个评估指标中的五个方面超越了一个没有辅助功能的320亿参数视觉-语言模型。

Conclusion: DR-WM提供了一种有效的方法来提高四足机器人在复杂环境下的行为预测准确性，这对于改善决策制定过程非常重要。该方法不仅提升了物理一致性预测的质量，而且还能帮助更大型的语言模型做出更好的决策。

Abstract: In outdoor environments, varying terrain and contact conditions can cause identical action commands to produce different motion outcomes for quadruped robots. Predicting these outcomes is essential for reliable navigation and informed path selection. Existing video world models can generate visually plausible futures, yet often struggle to capture robot dynamics and terrain interactions, leading to overly optimistic predictions of action outcomes. We present DR-WM, a physically consistent world model that incorporates robot-terrain contact dynamics to predict the consequences of quadruped actions. Starting from an initial RGB image, our method reconstructs scene geometry and integrates dense, dimensionless priors over terrain contact properties, calibrated using real-world action-response data. The resulting terrain model supports the simulation of physics with spatially varying contact properties such as friction. Given the initial state of the robot and candidate action sequences, we simulate its future motion and use the resulting camera trajectories and rendered geometry to guide the training of a video world model. The generated future observations are then used to update the terrain reconstruction, enabling iterative model rollouts without additional real-world images. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DR-WM substantially outperforms geometry-only baselines and baselines with uniform physical parameters on key physical metrics characterizing action outcomes, while achieving the best performance among the evaluated baselines. These physically grounded predictions support more informed action selection: across 90 challenging scenarios, an 8B vision-language model augmented with DR-WM outperforms an unassisted 32B vision-language model on five of six evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [182] [Acting Through Registers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17638)
*Yiwei Zhang,Yantai Yang,Jin Gao,Jin Lou,Andong Chen,Yufei Liu,Boyang Xing,Yuchen Zhu,Weiming Hu,Zhipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文发现了WAMs中存在持续高范数空间异常值，并提出RegisterWAM通过添加可学习的非空间寄存器来改善这一情况，同时引入了观测流匹配损失监督进一步优化。该模型在LIBERO-Plus和RoboTwin 2.0数据集上取得了显著的成功率提升，并改进了实际操作表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者观察到，在世界行动模型（WAMs）中的生成视觉表示里，存在着对动作相关的持久高范数空间异常值。即使经过归一化与K/V投影处理降低了这些异常值的极端程度后，它们的动作相关性依旧存在。基于这一发现，提出了新的方法以更好地组织动作相关信息。

Method: 提出了RegisterWAM，它向世界模型中添加了可学习的非空间寄存器，并直接用于动作条件设定。此外，还使用了观测流匹配（FM）损失监督来抑制空间异常值，同时将高范数响应集中在寄存器中。对于没有寄存器的情况，也探索了噪声观测FM目标来从损坏输入中恢复当前场景信息。

Result: 实验结果显示，所提模型在LIBERO-Plus数据集上达到了81.64%的成功率，在RoboTwin 2.0数据集上的平均成功率达到93.0%，并且提高了现实世界的操作性能。

Conclusion: 通过引入RegisterWAM及配套的技术手段，能够有效重新组织WAMs中的信息结构，不仅抑制了不希望的空间异常值现象，而且增强了机器人执行任务的能力。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) use generative visual representations to condition robot actions, yet how these representations organize action-relevant information remains poorly understood. We identify persistent high-norm spatial outliers in WAM representations and find that their action relevance persists even after normalization and K/V projection attenuate their extreme magnitude. Motivated by this observation, we introduce RegisterWAM, which adds learnable non-spatial registers to the world model and uses them directly for action conditioning. Registers alone do not fully eliminate spatial outliers; pairing them with observation flow-matching (FM) loss supervision suppresses spatial outliers while concentrating high-norm responses in registers. Beyond this reorganization, observation FM loss supervision also improves action learning without registers, motivating an additional noised-observation FM objective that learns current-scene recovery from corrupted inputs. The resulting model achieves 81.64\% success on LIBERO-Plus without additional embodied pretraining data or foundation models, reaches 93.0\% average success on RoboTwin 2.0, and improves real-world manipulation performance.

</details>


### [183] [Code2Worlds-2: Physically Plausible Agent Harness for Embodied World Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17651)
*Haoyu Zhang,Siming Li,Zeyu Zhang,Yi Zhang,Tat-Seng Chua,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Code2Worlds-2，这是一个将4D世界生成转变为具体世界模拟的系统。它通过SimReady场景编译器和模拟器循环4D改进器两个模块，为生成的视觉场景添加物理属性和动态，使其能够被物理引擎加载并执行真实物理交互。此外，还提出了SimCode4D基准用于评估该系统的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大型语言模型（LLMs）虽然可以从自然语言合成动态4D世界，但这些内容缺乏物理意义如碰撞几何、质量、摩擦力等特性，使得它们无法真实地模拟物理世界的行为。因此，需要创建一个可以由物理引擎加载并进行物理运算的可执行世界。

Method: 通过扩展Code2Worlds项目，开发了Code2Worlds-2，保留原始双流生成管道的同时增加了两个新模块：SimReady场景编译器负责将视觉场景转换为适合模拟器使用的表示形式；模拟器循环4D改进器则实现了一个自动代理框架，采用编译-模拟-批评-优化循环的方式，把视觉动态转化为可执行的刚体及关节动态，并且优化物理设置而不仅仅是代码本身。

Result: 实验表明，Code2Worlds-2能够在自然、室内、工业、城市和服务等多种场景下生成具有物理合理性的场景，这些场景既兼容模拟器又支持机器人互动。

Conclusion: Code2Worlds-2成功地将基于文本描述的4D世界生成推进到了具备物理语义和仿真能力的具体世界模拟阶段，为未来更加真实的虚拟环境构建奠定了基础。

Abstract: Recent coding large language models (LLMs) can synthesize dynamic 4D worlds from natural language, but the resulting content remains a visual artifact. It lacks physical semantics such as the collision geometry, mass, friction, inertia, and joint structure, required to enable it to simulate the physical world, and its dynamics are scripted to look plausible rather than designed to execute. What is needed is an executable world: one that a physics engine can load and step, resolving contact physically. We present Code2Worlds-2, an extension of Code2Worlds that turns 4D world generation into an embodied world simulation. We keep the original dual-stream generation pipeline intact and append two modules. First, a SimReady Scene Compiler that turns a generated visual scene into a simulator-compatible representation: it assigns collision meshes via convex decomposition, estimates physical attributes with a vision-language model, infers a lightweight support graph, instantiates the engine's contact, friction, and dynamics constraints, and applies a gravity settling pass, before exporting the scene as MuJoCo MJCF. Second, a Simulator-in-the-Loop 4D Refiner that implements an automated agent harness-a compile, simulate, critique and refine loop-that converts visual dynamics into executable rigid and articulated dynamics and evolves the physical settings rather than only the code, replacing the purely visual motion critic with a simulator critic that inspects penetration depth, collision pairs, stability, contact forces, joint limits, and robot reachability. We further propose SimCode4D, a state-based benchmark with five task templates for evaluating embodied simulation. Experiments on SimCode4D and across natural, indoor, industrial, urban, and service scenes show that Code2Worlds-2 produces physically plausible, simulator-compatible, and robot-interactable scenes.

</details>


### [184] [Towards a Systematic Study of Embodied System 2 over Full Task Execution Cycles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17657)
*Yuxi Wei,Ye Fang,Shuai Yang,Tong Wu,Tai Wang,Xiaojuan Qi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种名为SCOPER的系统，旨在通过结合视觉观察、基于规则的低级动作执行以及受控执行失败来研究基于观察的规划、执行和恢复。实验结果表明，该系统能够有效改善任务成功率及全呼叫准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决现有评估方法在测试由代理决策形成的观察或提供有限执行证据时存在的局限性，并且考虑到理想化技能成果的假设可能不切实际的问题，提出了SCOPER这一套用于研究基于观察的规划、执行与恢复的新方案。

Method: 开发了SCOPER工具包，它包括一个共享的基于规则的系统1来处理低级别的动作，同时引入了870个跨BEHAVIOR和ProcTHOR的任务集。此外，还设计了一个名为SCOPER-harness的部分，集成了证据记忆和进度验证等功能，以提高面对意外情况时的恢复能力。

Result: 使用两个开源框架和两种模拟器进行评估的结果显示，在采用SCOPER-harness后，任务成功率提高了11.1%至28.6%；并且在一个配备90亿参数模型的版本上表现优于直接运行270亿参数模型的情况。另外，在利用来自200次真实机器人演示的数据进行离线下一步行动评估时，也观察到了全呼叫准确性的显著提升（分别增加了14.7%和15.3%）。

Conclusion: SCOPER提供了一种新的环境设置，有助于诊断并改进在整个任务执行周期内具身推理过程中遇到的问题。

Abstract: An embodied brain, or System 2, must turn an abstract goal into actions while discovering relevant scene conditions and checking whether executed skills achieved their intended effects. Open-loop evaluations cannot test observations shaped by an agent's decisions, while some closed-loop evaluations provide limited evidence during execution or assume idealized skill outcomes. We introduce SCOPER, a suite for studying observation-grounded planning, execution, and recovery. SCOPER-bench combines high-level goals, ongoing visual observations, a shared rule-based System 1 for low-level actions, and controlled execution failures. Its construction and validation pipeline yields 870 tasks across BEHAVIOR and ProcTHOR, complemented by about 3,500 situated questions tied to the evaluated agent's execution. Benchmark results reveal difficulties in retaining scene evidence, monitoring progress, and recovering from unexpected outcomes. To address these difficulties, SCOPER-harness combines Evidence Memory and Progress Verification with a dynamic task agenda and bounded exploration, without additional model training. Across two evaluated open-source backbones and two simulators, the harness improves task success by 11.1-28.6%; a harnessed 9B model outperforms a directly executed 27B model on both simulators. In an offline next-action evaluation using observations from 200 real-robot demonstrations, the harness also improves full-call accuracy by 14.7% and 15.3%. SCOPER provides a setting for diagnosing and improving embodied reasoning throughout a complete task execution cycle.

</details>


### [185] [PrT-Flow: Probability-Tractable Flow Matching for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17667)
*Congzhong Sun,Ning Tan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 介绍了PrT-Flow，一种能够计算概率密度同时保持多模态、多步骤动作生成的方法。通过使用Jacobian-Tractable Transformer (JTT) 和Conditional Euler-step Training (CET)，使得每一步采样可逆并能有效计算其雅可比行列式。实验表明，该方法在机器人模仿学习和在线学习任务中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然扩散模型和流匹配可以表示灵活的机器人动作分布，但许多强化学习算法还需要每个动作的概率。为了解决这一问题，研究提出了PrT-Flow，旨在计算动作的概率密度的同时保留多模式、多步动作生成的能力。

Method: 提出了一种名为PrT-Flow的新方法，它利用了Jacobian-Tractable Transformer (JTT) 使得采样步骤变得可逆，并且可以高效地计算雅可比行列式的值。此外，通过条件欧拉步训练（CET）来监督采样过程中单个步骤的位移而非瞬时速度，以此方式训练这些有限步骤。

Result: PrT-Flow 在Robomimic Lift上经过300轮模仿学习后达到了96%的成功率，与扩散策略参考相匹配；在OGBench Cube-Triple上经过60万次在线步骤后也取得了96%的成功率。这些结果证明了具有可追踪概率密度的可逆网络能够学习有效的机器人策略，并支持SAC风格的动作评价训练。

Conclusion: 本研究表明，像PrT-Flow这样的具备概率密度可追踪性的可逆网络不仅能够在复杂的机器人控制任务中取得优异的表现，而且还能很好地支持特定类型的强化学习算法如SAC风格的动作评价训练。

Abstract: Diffusion models and Flow Matching can represent flexible distributions over robot actions, but many reinforcement learning algorithms also require the probability of each action. We introduce PrT-Flow (Probability-Tractable Flow Matching), which computes probability density while retaining multimodal, multi-step action generation. Its Jacobian-Tractable Transformer (JTT) makes each sampling step invertible and enables efficient computation of its Jacobian determinant. We train these finite steps with Conditional Euler-step Training (CET), which supervises their displacement over one step during sampling rather than their instantaneous velocity. We prove that, under certain assumptions, CET has the same minimizers as fitting the Euler-solver updates of Flow Matching. PrT-Flow achieves 96% success on Robomimic Lift after 300 epochs of imitation learning, matching the diffusion policy reference, and 96% success on OGBench Cube-Triple after 600k online steps. These results demonstrate that invertible networks with tractable probability densities can learn effective robot policies and support SAC-style actor-critic training. Our code is available at https://github.com/Sunlight-zero/PrT-Flow .

</details>


### [186] [OccWAM: Occupancy World-Action Models with Joint Scene-to-Ego Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17688)
*Qihang Huang,Wenhui Huang,Collister Chua,Shiyu Fang,Zhan Chen,Songyan Zhang,Zhidong Wang,Daiying Tian,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了OccWAM，一种基于流匹配的联合生成密集未来占用和连续自我轨迹的方法，通过两阶段流程预测场景中心占用及绝对自我轨迹，并重新索引至未来的自我框架中优化自我中心占用和运动，以实现动作对齐规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的占用感知规划器通常通过层次耦合或并行多任务学习将占用预测与动作生成联系起来，但这些方法要么保持明确的生成顺序，要么角色不对称，限制了世界预测与动作生成之间的相互作用。研究旨在提出一个更有效的解决方案来克服这些限制。

Method: 提出了OccWAM模型，该模型采用流匹配条件下的场景表示，共同生成密集的未来占用情况和连续的自我轨迹。它包括两个阶段：第一阶段是预测场景为中心的占用情况以及绝对自我轨迹；第二阶段则根据初始轨迹诱导出的未来自我坐标系对同一预测世界进行重索引，并通过第二次联合流进一步精炼自我中心的占用情况和动作一致性规划所需的运动。

Result: 在Bench2Drive和nuScenes上的广泛实验表明，OccWAM在闭环驾驶、开环规划以及未来占用预测方面达到了最先进水平的表现。

Conclusion: OccWAM作为一种原生占据-行动模型，能够有效地结合未来场景占用预测与自我车辆轨迹生成，为自动驾驶提供了一种新的有效途径。

Abstract: Occupancy world models provide a structured, metric representation of future driving scenes, making them a promising basis for end-to-end autonomous driving. However, existing occupancy-aware planners typically couple occupancy prediction and action generation through either hierarchical coupling or parallel multi-task learning. Hierarchical methods use occupancy either as a fully predicted future preceding action generation or as an action-conditioned rollout for trajectory evaluation, whereas parallel multi-task methods predict occupancy alongside planning through a separate branch or auxiliary objective. These interfaces retain either an explicit generation order or a role asymmetry, limiting reciprocal interaction between world prediction and action generation during trajectory formation. We propose OccWAM, an occupancy-native world-action model that jointly generates dense future occupancy and continuous ego trajectories via flow matching conditioned on scene representations. Building on this joint formulation, we introduce scene-to-ego world-action flow matching, a two-stage formulation. The first stage predicts scene-centric occupancy together with an absolute ego trajectory, providing a stable representation of future scene evolution. The same predicted world is then re-indexed into the future ego frames induced by the initial trajectory, where ego motion is represented through adjacent pose increments and a second joint flow refines ego-centric occupancy and motion for action-aligned planning. Unlike video-based world-action models, OccWAM preserves explicit semantic geometry for direct planning reasoning without pixel synthesis. Extensive experiments on Bench2Drive and nuScenes demonstrate state-of-the-art performance in closed-loop driving, open-loop planning, and future occupancy prediction.

</details>


### [187] [HelpMeOut: A Neuropsychology-Grounded Benchmark for Error-Aware Monitoring of Household Task Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17694)
*Haoran Wang,Jingwei Huang,Anqing Duan,Ho Seok Ahn,Bruce Macdonald*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为HelpMeOut的AI基准测试，旨在监控家庭任务执行中的错误。它基于20000个活动片段，覆盖了九种由神经心理学启发的错误类别，要求智能体监测人类活动流并尽早诊断出任何结构化错误。评估结果显示现有方法在作为错误监控者时表现不佳，表明HelpMeOut可以作为面向错误感知辅助代理的研究平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的身体化家庭基准测试通常假设人们能够正确执行任务，但这一假设对认知受损或老年人并不成立，他们在日常活动中会产生结构化的执行错误。为了填补这个空白，本文提出了HelpMeOut来专门研究这种情境下的错误监控问题。

Method: HelpMeOut通过将日常生活动作错误的研究转化为20000个成对的活动片段来进行工作，这些片段涵盖了九种受到神经心理学启发的错误类型。智能体需要观察人类活动的流动轨迹，并监控其是否正确执行，尽可能早地诊断出任何结构化的错误。

Result: 学习型监控、符号学、变点检测以及前沿的大规模语言模型提示基线被用来进行评估。结果表明，这些方法作为错误监控工具的表现较差，经常无法察觉到执行过程中出现的问题。

Conclusion: 本研究表明，标准的目标推理和任务完成基准测试留下了一个缺口，即未能有效处理执行过程中的错误。这使得HelpMeOut成为一个测试错误感知型辅助代理的理想平台。

Abstract: We introduce HelpMeOut, an embodied AI benchmark for error-aware monitoring of household task execution. Existing embodied household benchmarks typically assume that humans execute tasks correctly, but this assumption does not hold for cognitively impaired persons or older adults whose everyday activities produce structured execution errors. To address this gap, HelpMeOut operationalizes research on everyday action errors into 20000 paired activity episodes, covering nine neuropsychologically motivated error categories. In each episode, agents observe a streaming human activity trajectory and must monitor whether the execution is unfolding correctly, diagnosing any structured errors as early as possible. We evaluate learned-monitor, symbolic, change-point, and frontier LLM prompting baselines. Results show that these methods perform poorly as error monitors, often missing when execution goes wrong. These results suggest a gap left by standard goal-inference and task-completion benchmarks, making HelpMeOut a testbed for error-aware assistive agents.

</details>


### [188] [The Human--VLA Communication Gap: What Humans Leave Implicit and What Policies Cannot Recover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17746)
*Yiqing Xu,Jiajun Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了人类与机器人在任务执行上的沟通差距，通过区分信息差距和执行差距，揭示了人们在表达任务目标时越来越依赖于上下文基础和隐含的执行结构。当前策略在这种行为上存在不匹配：它们相对较好地容忍了基础依赖性，但在需要重建任务语义或事件结构时却表现挣扎。即使解决了信息差距，执行仍然对命令粒度和调用状态敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于解决当人类自然地向机器人发出指令时，尽管机器人知道如何完成任务但可能因理解问题而失败的现象。作者旨在探讨这种人机沟通差距，并进一步将其细分为信息差距（任务语义、基础或事件结构未得到解决）和执行差距（即使是完全指定的任务也取决于其组织方式和被调用的方式）。

Method: 通过对人们如何自然表达演示目标进行特征描述，并评估相同物理任务的不同受控变体来实现这一目标。该研究使用$π_{0.5}$进行了详细分析，并且在$π_0$-FAST 和 Cosmos3-Edge 上观察到了一致的趋势。

Result: 研究表明，随着任务复杂性的增加，人们倾向于更加压缩指令，更多地依赖上下文基础以及隐含的执行结构。现有策略虽然能够较好地处理基于上下文的理解需求，但在需要重新构建任务语义或事件结构时则显得力不从心。此外，即便是在信息差距被克服之后，执行过程对于指令的精细程度及调用状态依然十分敏感。

Conclusion: 这些发现为以人类为中心的策略学习指明了具体方向，并提倡开发能更好地将自然的人类意图转化为机器人行为的接口。

Abstract: A robot may know how to perform a task yet fail when a human asks for it naturally. We study this human--VLA communication gap by separating an \emph{information gap}, where task semantics, grounding, or event structure remain unresolved, from a residual \emph{execution gap}, where even a fully specified task depends on how it is organized and invoked. To do this, we characterize how people naturally express demonstrated objectives and evaluate controlled variants of the same physical tasks, with detailed analysis on $π_{0.5}$ and consistent trends on $π_0$-FAST and Cosmos3-Edge. Our study reveals that humans increasingly compress instructions as tasks grow, relying more on contextual grounding and implicit execution structure. Current policies are misaligned with this behavior: they tolerate grounding dependence relatively well, but struggle when task semantics or event structure must be reconstructed. Even after the information gap is resolved, execution remains sensitive to command granularity and invocation state. These findings identify concrete targets for human-centered policy learning and interfaces that better bridge natural human objectives to robot behavior. Project website: https://commgap.github.io/.

</details>


### [189] [I3L: Iterative Interactive Imitation Learning for Whole-Body Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17748)
*Wensi Ai,Christopher Luey,Karthik Dharmarajan,Evans Han,Yunfan Jiang,Stef Ren,Jianwen Xie,Jiajun Wu,Li Fei-Fei,Ruohan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种名为I3L的集成硬件和学习系统，该系统结合了全身修正与迭代策略改进，以解决长周期全身移动操作任务中离线训练策略可靠性差的问题。通过四轮HG-DAgger协议，I3L能够显著提高自主成功率，并减少每集干预时长，证明了其在改善从离线训练到可靠部署之间差距的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于远程操控演示训练得到的策略，在超出训练分布的情况下性能下降明显，特别是在需要长时间协调手臂、躯干和底座动作的任务中更为突出。由于长时间操作导致误差累积以及全面演示覆盖不切实际的问题，使得开发一种能有效提升这类任务可靠性的方法变得尤为重要。

Method: I3L是一个结合了全身调整与迭代策略优化的学习系统，它使用低成本开源的JoyLo+界面来执行政策跟踪的引导臂及触控触发接管机制，允许对身体各部分进行协调校正。此外，通过迭代部署与再训练流程，操作员可以针对性地修复更新后策略出现的失效情况。研究者们还利用四轮HG-DAgger协议评估了该方法的有效性。

Result: 实验表明，在两组受控模拟测试中，采用四轮HG-DAgger协议的方法达到了最高的最终成功率。在此方案下，I3L相较于基础策略平均提高了68.3个百分点的成功率，并且在所有任务上至少达到了90%的成功率；相比仅依靠示范训练的方式，在相同数量训练轨迹的前提下平均高出35.6个百分点。同时，平均每集所需的接管时间减少了72%。

Conclusion: I3L有效地缩小了离线训练与实际可靠部署之间的‘最后一英里’差距，并为收集和利用修正信息以完成长周期全身移动操作任务提供了实用见解。

Abstract: Policies trained offline from teleoperated demonstrations can degrade outside their training distribution. This gap is particularly acute in long-horizon whole-body mobile manipulation, where long horizons amplify compounding errors and coordinated arm, torso, and base motion makes exhaustive demonstration coverage impractical. We introduce I3L, an integrated hardware and learning system that combines whole-body correction with iterative policy refinement to address this last-mile reliability gap. Its low-cost, open-source JoyLo+ interface uses policy-tracking leader arms and touch-triggered takeover to enable coordinated corrections across the body parts, while iterative deployment and retraining allow operators to target failures of the updated policy. We evaluate I3L across three robot embodiments and eight simulated and real-world tasks, including four long-horizon whole-body mobile manipulation tasks. Controlled simulation experiments show that four-round HG-DAgger achieves the highest final success on both tasks. Under this protocol, I3L improves autonomous success over the base policies by an average of 68.3 percentage points and achieves at least 90% success on every task, outperforming demonstration-only training by 35.6 percentage points on average with the same number of training trajectories. Averaged across tasks, takeover duration per episode decreases by 72% over four rounds. Together, these results show that I3L helps close the last-mile gap between offline training and reliable deployment and provides practical insights into collecting and using corrections for long-horizon whole-body mobile manipulation. Videos, visualizations, and appendix are at https://i-3l.github.io/.

</details>


### [190] [Learning Robot Policies for Whole-Body Mobility Assistance via Dynamic Human Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17750)
*Shikun Ban,Shohei Nagai,Qingyang Song,Hanxi Wan,Eric Zhao,Thomas Wang,Zackory Erickson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究人员提出了一种基于模拟的学习框架，用于训练辅助机器人执行诸如坐到站（STS）和抬举转移（L&T）等全身辅助任务。该框架通过对抗性运动先验训练人类策略，以产生对机器人动作的现实反应。从这些演示中，训练3D扩散策略来映射人体点云段和机器人关节位置到移动操作器的全身动作，无需依赖特权人类状态。结果表明，机器人策略在模拟环境中成功执行了STS和L&T协助，并且经过少量真实数据微调后，实现了STS任务从模拟到现实的迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发能够辅助人类移动的机器人技术，特别是在执行如坐到站（STS）和抬举转移（L&T）这样体力要求高的任务时，可以同时为接受护理者带来好处并减轻护理者的负担。然而，学习适用于这类全身辅助任务的机器人策略是具有挑战性的，因为收集人机互动的真实世界数据成本高且关乎安全。因此，使用模拟成为了一个吸引人的替代方案。但以往的工作通常将人视为静态或响应极小，无法捕捉到移动辅助中的合作本质。

Method: 研究者们构建了一个基于模拟的学习框架，其中心是一个利用对抗性运动先验训练出的人类策略，这个策略能够根据机器人的行为生成真实的受助者动作反应。一个适应于人类的脚本控制器与之交互，从而大规模地收集多样化的机器人演示。利用这些演示数据，他们训练了3D扩散模型，该模型可以从分割后的人体点云及机器人关节位置映射到移动操作器的全身动作上，而无需在部署时依赖于特定的人类状态信息。

Result: 所得到的机器人策略在模拟环境下成功完成了STS和L&T辅助任务。此外，初步实验还展示了，在少量真实世界数据上的微调之后，对于STS任务，从模拟到实际环境的迁移是可以实现的。

Conclusion: 这项工作介绍了一种新颖的方法，通过模拟学习来培训辅助机器人进行复杂的、需要全身协调的动作任务，比如帮助人们完成从坐着到站立的过程或者搬运转移。这种方法不仅提高了模拟过程中的人机交互真实性，而且证明了所学策略具有向真实场景迁移的能力，为未来在实际应用中采用此类辅助机器人提供了可能性。

Abstract: Robotic assistance for human mobility could benefit care recipients and reduce caregiver burden during physically demanding tasks such as Sit-to-Stand (STS) and Lift-and-Transfer (L&T). Learning robot policies for these whole-body assistive tasks is challenging: real-world data collection for human-robot interaction is costly and safety-critical, making simulation an attractive alternative. Prior work, however, typically treats the human as static or minimally responsive and thus fails to capture the cooperative nature of mobility assistance.
