### [223] [Benchmarking Interaction-Oriented Aerial Mobility across Heterogeneous Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18381)
*Zhibo Duan,Hanlin Kong,Tianran Ma,Yuze Wu,Yuxuan Xu,Kenghou Hoi,Changheng Xie,Xin Zhou,Jianzhe Gao,Fei Gao,Wenguan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了CrossFly，一个针对异构环境下的长距离交互式空中移动的基准。CrossFly提供了15000条轨迹和超过20个重建场景，引入了交互准备率(IRR)来评估终端导航状态是否为后续交互提供合适的条件。实验表明，跨室内室外设置比单一环境设置更具挑战性，最高成功率在未知场景上达到9.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的空中基准通常局限于单一环境设定，限制了对连续环境过渡中代理的评估。当评估互动时，代理往往被初始化在目标附近，使得从导航到互动的过渡大部分未得到评估。

Method: 通过创建CrossFly基准，该研究提供了涵盖室内、室外及两者之间转换的轨迹，并引入了交互准备率（IRR）来衡量终端导航状态对于下游交互活动的适宜程度。此外，还通过五个代表性空中视觉-语言-动作(VLA)代理和提出的CrossFly-Agent进行了实验。

Result: 实验结果表明，在跨越室内外设置的情况下，相比于单一环境设置来说更为困难，其中在新场景上的最高认证率达到9.7%。进一步的下游操作案例研究证实了以交互为导向到达点对于随后物理交互的相关性。

Conclusion: 希望CrossFly能够促进空中具身智能的发展，使其能够在不同环境下实现统一的移动性和物理交互。

Abstract: Aerial mobile manipulation requires unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) to traverse heterogeneous environments such as indoor and outdoor domains, and connect navigation with subsequent physical interaction within the same trajectories. Existing aerial benchmarks are typically confined to single-environment settings, limiting evaluation of agents across continuous environmental transitions. When interaction is evaluated, agents are often initialized near the target, leaving the transition from navigation to interaction largely unassessed. This work introduces CrossFly, a benchmark for long-horizon interaction-oriented aerial mobility across heterogeneous environments. CrossFly contains 15K trajectories across more than 20 reconstructed scenes under a unified simulation and evaluation framework, covering Indoor, Outdoor, and Indoor$\leftrightarrow$Outdoor trajectories. It further introduces Interaction Readiness Rate (IRR) to assess whether terminal navigation states provide suitable conditions for downstream interaction without assuming a specific manipulation policy. Experiments with five representative aerial vision-language-action (VLA) agents and the proposed CrossFly-Agent show that Indoor$\leftrightarrow$Outdoor settings are more challenging than single-environment settings, with the highest success rate reaching 9.7% on unseen scenes. A downstream manipulation case study further validates the relevance of interaction-oriented arrival to subsequent physical interaction. We hope CrossFly helps advance aerial embodied intelligence toward unified mobility and physical interaction in heterogeneous environments.

</details>


### [224] [Intent-guided Adaptive Stiffening for Visuomotor Policies under Unknown Resistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18386)
*Francesco Cufino,Carmela Mariniello,Mario Selvaggio,Andreas Sochopoulos,Fabio Ruggiero*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种残余硬化层，用于在不改变原有策略的情况下，通过识别策略命令与机器人实际执行动作之间的差异来提高特定方向上的刚度，从而帮助完成如拧开瓶盖或推T型螺母等需要克服阻力的任务。该方法不需要额外的训练或演示，在两个实际任务中将成功率从52%提升至90%，同时产生的峰值偏离轴向力比统一刚度控制器少61%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于需要沿着高摩擦方向施加力量以克服阻力的任务（例如拧开瓶盖或沿表面滑动部件），现有生成视觉运动策略能够预测所需动作但无法确定实现这些动作所需的力度大小。而当遇到超过柔顺控制器所能提供的力时，即使预测非常准确，任务也无法完成。此外，由于接触状态不可见，所需的力也无法事先预知。

Method: 提出一种放置于未修改策略下的残余硬化层，它能基于策略命令的动作和机器人实际完成的动作之间的不匹配来识别出阻力，并通过四模式监管器根据策略自身动作指令选择性地增加单个方向上的刚度、维持此刚度直至任务进展并随后释放。这种方法无需合规标签、重新训练或收集新的示范数据。

Result: 实验结果显示，在拧开瓶盖和对抗非均匀摩擦推动T型螺母这两个真实世界任务上，面对六种不同阻力水平（其中两种被隐藏于演示之外）时，所提出的硬化层方法可将任务成功率从52%提高到90%，且相较于同等条件下使用统一刚度控制器所产生的峰值偏离轴向力减少了61%。

Conclusion: 研究证明了通过引入一个简单的残余硬化层可以有效解决机器人在执行需克服阻力任务时面临的挑战，提高了成功率同时减少了不必要的外力输出。

Abstract: Manipulation tasks such as unscrewing a cap or sliding a part in contact with a surface require imposing force along a high-friction direction: the robot must overcome a resistance rather than yield to it. Generative visuomotor policies predict the motion for such tasks but not the authority needed to realize it, and that authority is bounded by the impedance controller beneath them: when the resistance exceeds what a compliant controller can exert under a bounded tracking error, the task is unattainable however accurate the prediction is. The required amount cannot be anticipated either, since it depends on a contact state the observation does not reveal. In this paper, we propose a residual stiffening layer placed underneath an unmodified policy. Resistance is recognized, without force sensing, from the mismatch between the motion the policy commands and the motion the robot realizes, and a four-mode supervisor raises the stiffness along a single direction read off the policy's own action chunk, retains it while the task progresses and releases it afterwards. The layer needs no compliance labels, no retraining and no re-collected demonstrations. On two real tasks, unscrewing a bottle cap and pushing a T-nut along a T-slot groove against non-homogeneous friction, evaluated at six resistance levels of which two were withheld from the demonstrations, the layer raises the success rate from 52% to 90% while producing 61% less peak off-axis force than a uniformly stiff controller at the same resistance.

</details>


### [225] [CALM: Decentralized Control of Cooperative Aerial Manipulation with Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18425)
*Jiaxi Huo,Petre Ricioppo,Fausto Francesco Lizzio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种名为CALM的分散式控制器，用于多无人机协作搬运篮子并追踪移动地面机器人。该方法结合了多智能体强化学习策略、目标估计等技术，在不同轨迹下实现了比所有评估基线更低的位置和方向误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在风力、负载运动和噪声目标广播的情况下，由于多个无人驾驶飞行器（UAV）通过共享负载而相互关联，使得协同空中操作变得困难。为了解决这个问题，并提高多无人机系统在携带负载同时跟踪移动地面机器人的性能。

Method: 提出的方法是CALM（协作空中学习操控），它是一种分散式的控制器，结合了参数共享的多智能体强化学习（MARL）策略、目标估计、每个无人机的扩展状态观测器（ESOs）、篮子质量估算以及预判编队偏航前馈控制。其中，预判编队偏航前馈控制利用估计的目标运动来提前旋转编队，以减少位置误差发展之前篮子的偏航滞后，且不增加净命令力。此外，扰动条件下的评判者支持集中训练，但在执行时保持分散化。

Result: 通过对未见过的轨迹进行测试，CALM在平均位置和方向误差上都优于所有被评估的基线方法，包括匹配臂基线(7.68厘米对比10.95厘米，3.3度对比4.7度)、缆绳悬挂实现以及两种基于模型的控制器。即使是在未见过的信息路径退化情况下，CALM的表现仍然统计上与臂基线相当或更好。

Conclusion: 研究表明，CALM能够在存在外部干扰因素如风力作用及载荷运动等条件下，有效提升多无人机协作搬运任务中的定位精度与姿态控制能力。

Abstract: Cooperative aerial manipulation is difficult under wind, payload motion, and noisy goal broadcasts because multiple Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) are coupled through a shared payload. We present CALM (Cooperative Aerial Learned Manipulation), a decentralized controller for aerial manipulators carrying a basket while tracking a moving ground robot. CALM combines a parameter-shared Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) policy, trained using Multi-Agent Proximal Policy Optimization (MAPPO), with goal estimation, per-UAV Extended State Observers (ESOs), basket-mass estimation, and anticipatory formation-yaw feedforward. The anticipatory formation-yaw feedforward uses estimated goal motion to rotate the formation before position error develops, reducing basket-yaw lag without adding net commanded force. A disturbance-conditioned critic supports centralized training while execution remains decentralized. Across held-out trajectories, CALM achieves lower mean position and orientation errors than all evaluated baselines, including the matched arm baseline (7.68 versus 10.95 cm and 3.3 versus 4.7$^\circ$), the cable-suspended realization, and two model-based controllers. Under unseen full-information-path degradation, CALM remains statistically comparable or superior to the arm baseline. Videos are available at https://petreric.github.io/CALM/.

</details>


### [226] [PACT: Posture-Aligned Co-Design Technique for Hand Configuration and Retargeting in Multi-Finger Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18443)
*Koyo Sasazaki,Satoshi Funabashi,Pranav Ponnivalavan,Alexander Schmitz,Tetsuya Ogata,Shigeki Sugano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于人类抓握姿势的优化方法PACT，用于同时优化17伺服机器人手的机械参数及其非线性姿势重定向器。PACT手在模仿学习演示中表现出色，能够更准确地复制人类手部姿态，并且在抓取物体时具有更高的成功率和更低的变形程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的机器人手设计往往忽视了类人抓握姿势的重要性，而这些姿势对于通过模仿学习进行远程操作至关重要。此外，机器人执行的手势重定向法则通常是在硬件最终确定后才选定的，这限制了其对人类手势的忠实再现。

Method: PACT是一种基于DexYCB数据集中的手势进行优化的方法，它同时考虑了机器人手的物理装配约束（如伺服干扰、装配间隙和平手突出）以及与人类手势之间的全指对应关系。该方法还涉及到对手部各部分尺寸及关节位置等机械参数的调整。

Result: 实验结果表明，PACT手在重现人类手关键点方面达到了3.9毫米的运动学RMSE，优于基线模型的12.8毫米；在所有抓握姿势下保持指尖距离小于10毫米的比例为100%，而基线仅能达到21.6%。另外，在相同的扭矩限制条件下，PACT手能够举起两倍于基线重量的负载，并且使纸杯变形程度降低了七倍。此外，在YCB日常用品抓取任务中取得了86%的成功率。

Conclusion: PACT提供了一种有效的方法来优化机器人手的设计以更好地适应模仿学习需求，从而提高远程操作演示的保真度。PACT手不仅在抓取性能上优于现有方案，而且在训练模仿学习策略方面也展示了良好的潜力。

Abstract: The fidelity of teleoperated demonstrations used for imitation learning is determined by how faithfully a robot hand can replicate the operator's posture. The posture retargeting law that the robot will execute is typically selected only once the hardware has been finalized, and conventional hand designs often neglect human-like grasping postures. We introduce PACT, an optimization based on human grasping postures that jointly optimizes the mechanical parameters of a 17-servo robot hand (link lengths, joint offsets, mounting angles, and base placements) and its nonlinear posture retargeter (a linear human-to-robot joint map, per-joint posture-IK weights, and damping). The optimization enforces full-finger correspondence (MCP, PIP, DIP, and tip of each finger) to human poses from the DexYCB dataset, and is constrained by servo interference, assembly clearances, and flat-hand protrusion, so the resulting design can be physically assembled. We fabricate the PACT Hand and validate it through teleoperation experiments and an imitation-learning demonstration. The hand reproduces all human hand keypoints with a kinematic RMSE of 3.9 mm, compared to 12.8 mm for the LEAP Hand baseline, and keeps all fingertips within 10 mm on every grasping posture, while the baseline achieves this for only 21.6% of the poses. Under matched per-joint torque limits, it lifts twice the baseline payload, 2 kg against 1 kg, and deforms a held paper cup seven times less, reaching a deformation level comparable to a human hand. It also achieves an 86% grasping success rate on YCB daily objects, compared to 64% for the baseline. Finally, as a demonstration, an imitation-learning policy trained on demonstrations collected with the PACT Hand achieved a 75% success rate at held-out placements in a can pick-and-place task. Project page: https://pact-hand-2027.github.io

</details>


### [227] [MemoryVLN: From Ego-Aware Memory Representation to Cross-Context Memory Reuse in Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18478)
*Xueyao Wei,Yifei Wang,Delun Li,Shaoan Wang,Junzhi Yu,Wenjie Song,Xingyu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为MemoryVLN的系统，通过记忆构建和管理解决了长距离视觉-语言导航中的性能下降问题。该系统采用长序列压缩、自我意识记忆表示、分层记忆库和记忆增强等机制，有效改善了导航表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大型视觉-语言模型在处理长距离导航任务时遇到了因上下文长度限制、历史观察冗余以及跨时间空间对齐不稳定导致的表现下降问题。为了解决这些问题并有效地压缩导航历史及重用过去的空间经验，提出了新的解决方案。

Method: 设计了一个统一的记忆驱动导航系统MemoryVLN，包括：1) 长序列压缩，基于遗忘曲线启发的时间采样方法；2) 自我意识记忆表示，结合2D语义令牌、3D几何令牌和6D代理姿态信息；3) 分层记忆库，支持情境内外的记忆维护；4) 记忆增强，根据当前导航状态检索相关记忆并与当前视觉令牌融合。

Result: 在R2R-CE和RxR-CE数据集上的广泛实验表明，相比最先进基线方法，所提方案在成功率上取得了显著改进（分别提高了2%和6.5%）。

Conclusion: MemoryVLN系统通过创新的记忆构造与管理策略，成功克服了长距离视觉-语言导航任务中面临的主要挑战，并且实验结果证明了其有效性。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) have shown strong potential for embodied vision-language navigation (VLN), yet their performance degrades on long-horizon trajectories due to context-length limits, redundant historical observations, and unstable spatial alignment across time. These issues make it difficult to both compress long navigation histories and reliably reuse past spatial experience. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{MemoryVLN}, a unified memory-driven navigation system built around memory construction and management. During memory construction, \emph{Long Sequence Compression} employs forgetting-curve-inspired temporal sampling to preserve dense recent evidence and sparse yet representative distant observations under a limited token budget. \emph{Ego-Aware Memory Representation} further integrates 2D semantic tokens, 3D geometric tokens, and 6D agent poses, allowing each memory to encode both what was observed and where it was observed. During memory management, \emph{Hierarchical Memory Bank} maintains in-episode and context-level memories through cross-context warm-start and confidence-controlled write-back. \emph{Memory Enhancement} then retrieves the Top-$K$ relevant memories according to the current navigation state and fuses them with the current visual tokens. Together, these mechanisms transform raw navigation history into compact, spatially grounded, selectively retrievable, and reusable navigation experience. Extensive experiments on R2R-CE and RxR-CE demonstrate consistent improvements over the state-of-the-art baselines by a substantial margin (i.e., 2\% and 6.5\% in successful rate).

</details>


### [228] [RTISSLAM: In-air Imaging Sonar SLAM using a Robust Pose Graph and Occupancy Grid Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18479)
*Jan Steckel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种新的仅使用声纳的SLAM系统——RTISSLAM，专为嵌入式3D成像声纳设计。它结合了BatSLAM 2.0的鲁棒位姿图作为后端和占据栅格地图，通过延迟乘积求和波束形成与逐距离CFAR的能量图作为位置描述符用于序列验证的闭环检测，并且实现了声学里程计在线校准轮式里程计、度量注册已识别的重访点等功能。在具有镜面反射墙壁的模拟环境中，RTISSLAM能够显著降低轨迹误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的声纳SLAM依赖于从稀疏距离读数中构建的几何特征或占据栅格地图，在处理室内声纳常见的镜面反射时表现不佳。本文旨在解决这一问题，提出一种新的方法来提高在这些条件下移动机器人定位和建图的准确性。

Method: RTISSLAM是一种新颖的基于声纳的SLAM系统，针对嵌入式3D成像声纳进行了优化。该系统采用了BatSLAM 2.0的稳健姿态图作为后端支持，同时利用占据栅格作为地图表示。通过运用延迟乘积求和波束形成技术和范围级CFAR（恒虚警率）生成的能量景观作为地点描述符，以促进经过序列验证的闭环闭合。此外，还集成了声学里程计对轮式里程计进行在线校准，以及将识别出的重复访问点以度量方式注册等特性。

Result: 实验表明，在包含镜面墙的模拟环境下，RTISSLAM能够将轮式里程计的轨迹误差从8.2米减少到0.31米。相比之下，使用相同声纳数据的传统激光雷达风格的网格SLAM方法则无法取得成功。

Conclusion: RTISSLAM作为一种创新性的声纳SLAM方案，不仅克服了传统方法在处理镜面反射时遇到的问题，而且显著提高了定位精度，特别是在视觉传感器如相机和激光雷达失效的情况下，例如黑暗、烟雾或尘埃环境。

Abstract: Sonar was one of the first sensors with which mobile robots built maps, and in-air sonar keeps working where cameras and lidar fail, in darkness, smoke, dust and next to glass. Classical sonar SLAM, however, relied on geometric features or occupancy grids built from sparse range readings, and both struggle with the specular reflections that dominate indoor sonar. In this letter, we introduce RTISSLAM, a novel sonar-only SLAM system for an embedded 3D imaging sonar, which combines the robust pose graph of BatSLAM 2.0 as back-end with an occupancy grid as map. Energyscapes from delay-multiply-and-sum beamforming with a range-wise CFAR serve as place descriptors for sequence-verified loop closures, acoustic odometry calibrates the wheel odometry online, recognized revisits are registered metrically, and the grid is stored per keyframe and anchored to the pose-graph nodes, so that it follows every loop closure. In simulated worlds with specular walls, RTISSLAM reduces the trajectory error of the wheel odometry from \SI{8.2}{\meter} to \SI{0.31}{\meter} in an office, while lidar-style grid SLAM on the same sonar data fails.

</details>


### [229] [APTree: Extended Behavior Trees for Adaptive and Scalable Automated Robotic Task Planning in Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18523)
*Shermin Sherkat,Thomas Wortmann,Andreas Wortmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为APTree的模型驱动框架，该框架将自动化任务规划与使用行为树作为执行模型的模块化任务执行相结合。APTree通过协调不同的符号规划器自动生成和组合模块化的任务计划，并且开发了特定领域的语言来正式指定和验证任务计划。在实际项目中，APTree能够检测设计时和运行时的规范与规划错误，并生成大规模的任务计划。此外，它还保证了合成执行和局部重新规划的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有建筑预制构件中的机器人任务规划主要依赖人工，这导致规划效率低下，同时也阻碍了机器人解决方案的应用。现有的方法对于可扩展、模块化的自动任务规划以及局部运行时重规划支持有限。

Method: 提出了一个名为APTree的框架，它结合了自动化任务规划和基于行为树的模块化任务执行。为了支撑这一方法，研究人员开发了一种领域特定语言，用于任务计划的形式化定义和验证，并构建了一个兼容的执行引擎。

Result: APTree能够在真实世界项目中建模施工机器人的制造领域，识别出设计阶段和运行期间引入的规格和规划错误，并通过跨多个规划模块协调三个符号规划器来自动生成大规模任务计划。形式化分析表明，在明确假设下，包括规划器的正确性和所生成计划的非干扰性，合成执行和局部重规划是安全的。

Conclusion: APTree使得建筑从业者无需深入了解符号规划技术或APTree内部实现即可利用自动化任务规划。它为可靠计划组合提供了明确条件，促进了机器人在离场建设预制件任务规划中的应用。

Abstract: Task planning for robots in off-site construction prefabrication is primarily manual, leading to planning inefficiencies and reluctance to adopt robotic solutions. Existing approaches provide limited support for scalable, modular automated task planning and localized runtime replanning. This paper proposes APTree, a model-driven framework that integrates automated task planning with modular task execution using behavior trees as the execution model. APTree repeatedly applies and coordinates heterogeneous symbolic planners to automatically generate and compose modular task plans at runtime. To support this approach, we develop a domain-specific language for the formal specification and validation of task plans, together with a compatible execution engine. We evaluate APTree on the real-world livMatS Biomimetic Shell pavilion, where it models the construction robotic fabrication domain, detects injected specification and planning errors at design time and runtime, and automatically generates large-scale task plans by coordinating three symbolic planners across numerous planning modules. A machine-checked formal analysis further shows that composed execution and local replanning are safe under explicitly stated assumptions, including planner soundness and non-interference of generated plans, providing practitioners with explicit conditions for dependable plan composition. APTree thus enables construction practitioners to leverage automated task planning without requiring detailed knowledge of symbolic planning techniques or APTree's internal implementation.

</details>


### [230] [Behavioural self-monitoring for autonomous earth observation agents: The attribute reliability monitor (ARM)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18548)
*Alejandro D. Mousist*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种新的框架——属性可靠性监测器(ARM)，用以评估语言及视觉语言模型中的语义属性可靠性。它通过累积随时间推移产生的可疑证据来工作，并证明了即使是在没有内部访问权限的情况下，也能通过外部行为监控有效地判断是否应该继续信任模型的输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决语言和视觉语言模型在支持自主系统时，因操作条件的变化可能导致其语义输出的可靠性降低的问题。

Method: 引入了属性可靠性监测器（ARM），这是一种通过随着时间积累可疑证据来评估个别语义属性的框架。该框架可以兼容不同的检测器和时间监控方法，并且将干预策略留给使用该系统的消费者决定。首次实现是利用为卫星热红外图像开发的专业视觉语言模型进行的。监控采用黑盒方式，仅使用可观察到的响应，而无需访问内部模型状态、置信度分数或外部参考答案。

Result: 在受控输入退化条件下，响应之间的不一致提供了选择性暂停某些属性的依据。结果表明，外部行为监控可以为继续使用模型输出提供信息，同时区分操作上的怀疑与语义正确性。

Conclusion: 属性可靠性监测器(ARM)能够有效评估特定语义属性的可靠性，并根据外部行为监控的结果为模型输出的持续使用提供指导，这有助于提高在变化的操作条件下的模型输出质量。

Abstract: Language and vision language models can support autonomous systems, but changing operating conditions may compromise the reliability of their semantic outputs. This work introduces the Attribute Reliability Monitor (ARM), a framework that accumulates evidence of suspicion over time to assess individual semantic attributes. ARM accommodates different detectors and temporal monitoring methods, while leaving intervention policies to the consuming system. An initial implementation was evaluated using a specialized vision language model developed for satellite thermal infrared imagery. Monitoring followed a black box approach, using only observable responses without access to internal model states, confidence scores, or external reference answers. Under controlled input degradation, disagreement between responses provided evidence for selective suspension of attributes. The results illustrate how external behavioral monitoring can inform continued use of model outputs, while distinguishing operational suspicion from semantic correctness.

</details>


### [231] [WAVE-0: Bridging VLA Policies and WAMs with Lightweight Latent Future Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18568)
*Haomin Wang,Anqiong Ji,Ke Wang,Zhongshan Liu,Ru Ying,Ran Zheng,Zhipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WAVE-0是一个结合了视觉-语言-动作(VLA)策略和世界行动模型(WAMs)的框架，通过一个轻量级的潜在世界模型来实现。它直接基于真实机器人动作训练，不需要大规模视频预训练或像素重建，并在多个任务中展现了出色的操控性能和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略虽然擅长通用机器人操作，但不明确建模其行为对环境的影响；而WAMs虽然考虑到了未来预测，但通常依赖于昂贵的视频生成。本研究旨在开发一种能够结合两者优势的方法，以低成本方式增强机器人的规划与执行能力。

Method: 提出了WAVE-0框架，该框架通过一个轻量级的世界模型将VLA与WAM相结合。世界模型直接使用真实机器人动作进行训练，采用反事实排名目标函数，无需进行像素重构或大规模视频预训练。此模型可以将VLM生成的潜在行动计划转化为未来状态表示，进而指导行动专家产生具体动作。

Result: WAVE-0在RoboTwin 2.0上达到了91.47%的成功率，在LIBERO及其扩展版本（LIBERO-Plus, LIBERO-Pro, 和 LIBERO-Para）上也取得了显著成绩。对于实际任务，WAVE-0将π_0.5的成功率从38.3%提高到了73.3%。

Conclusion: WAVE-0展示了强大的操作表现及跨不同分布偏移的一般性，表明结合语义理解和显式未来预测可以直接引导动作生成，从而提高机器人在多样化任务中的表现。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies excel at generalist robotic manipulation but generate actions without explicitly modeling their environmental consequences. World action models (WAMs) incorporate future prediction, yet most rely on costly video generation, while latent alternatives either use predicted futures only as auxiliary targets or infer latent actions from suboptimal visual transitions through costly large-scale video pretraining. We introduce WAVE-0 (World-model-Augmented VLA Execution), a framework that bridges VLAs and WAMs through a lightweight latent world model. By connecting the semantic understanding of pretrained VLAs with explicit future prediction, WAVE-0 allows anticipated action consequences to directly guide action generation. Built on a pretrained VLA, its 257M world model translates VLM-generated latent action plans into predicted future representations that condition the action expert, unifying latent planning, future prediction, and action generation. The world model is trained directly on ground-truth robot actions with a counterfactual ranking objective, requiring neither pixel reconstruction nor large-scale video pretraining. WAVE-0 achieves success rates of 91.47% on RoboTwin 2.0 and 98.7% on LIBERO, along with 88.0%, 61.4%, and 75.0% on LIBERO-Plus, LIBERO-Pro, and LIBERO-Para, respectively. On real-world tasks, it improves the success rate of $π_{0.5}$ from 38.3% to 73.3%. These results demonstrate strong manipulation performance and generalization across diverse distribution shifts.

</details>


### [232] [ACCRUE: Continual Off-Policy Reinforcement Learning for Robot Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18596)
*Changyeon Kim,Kimin Lee,Yashraj Narang,Yijie Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为ACCRUE的持续离策略强化学习框架，通过在任务间传递轻量级的行为者和批评者网络，使得机器人基础模型能够在学习新任务的同时保持先前学到的行为。该方法在仿真环境和真实机器人硬件上测试时，不仅加速了后续任务的学习过程，而且显著减少了实际机器人的训练时间，同时几乎不会遗忘之前学习的内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的连续强化学习方法虽然能够减轻遗忘问题，但它们依赖于在线策略更新，这限制了前向迁移能力；而离策略方法虽然提高了单个任务的样本效率，却没有解决跨任务学习者的持久性问题。因此，需要一种新的方法来提高机器人基础模型（RFMs）在学习新任务时的效率，并且不丢失已掌握的行为技能。

Method: 本文提出了ACCRUE框架，这是一种持续性的离策略强化学习方案。它利用轻量级的行为者-批评者网络架构，在不同任务之间转移，允许当前阶段重用之前通过强化学习获得的行为和价值知识。继承下来的批评者仅基于当前任务的转换进行调整，并指导当前任务的行为者学习；为了保留先前的行为模式，行为者会朝少量保存下来的情节中的目标进行蒸馏处理。固定视觉特征确保了继承的学习者及其重放与基础策略更新兼容。

Result: 实验结果表明，在接触丰富的装配序列中无论是模拟环境还是真实机器人平台上，ACCRUE都能以接近零的遗忘率加快后续任务的学习速度，并将实际机器人的训练时间大幅缩短了31.0%至68.5%。

Conclusion: ACCRUE提供了一个有效的方法来增强机器人基础模型在不断学习新任务时的表现，同时保持对已有技能的记忆，为未来更复杂的多任务学习场景提供了潜在解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training improves robot foundation models (RFMs) beyond their demonstrations, but real-world deployment demands more: learning new tasks efficiently while retaining previously acquired behaviors. Existing continual RL methods on top of VLAs mitigate forgetting but rely on on-policy updates with limited forward transfer, whereas off-policy methods improve single-task sample efficiency without addressing learner persistence across tasks. We propose ACCRUE, a continual off-policy RL framework that carries lightweight actor and critic networks across tasks, allowing each stage to reuse behavioral and value knowledge acquired through prior RL. The inherited critic adapts only on current-task transitions and guides current-task actor learning, while prior behavior is preserved by distilling the actor toward cached targets on a small number of retained episodes. Fixed visual features keep the inherited learner and its replay compatible with base-policy updates. Across contact-rich assembly sequences in simulation and on real-robot hardware, ACCRUE accelerates subsequent-task learning with near-zero forgetting and significantly reduces real-robot training time by 31.0\% to 68.5\%.

</details>


### [233] [Grid or Nodes? Probing Object Memory in World Models Under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18637)
*Justin McMillen,Yasin Yilmaz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文比较了基于网格特征的世界模型FloWM与基于节点记忆的物体追踪方法在处理视野外物体时的表现。结果显示，尽管在某些条件下FloWM表现更优，但在对角线运动及高速情况下，基于节点的记忆体表现出更强的适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探索对于离开摄像头视图的对象，机器人世界模型如何有效地记住这些对象的位置和速度信息。

Method: 通过对比两种不同的方法：一种是使用空间网格特征来移动并记忆对象（FloWM），另一种则是直接以数值形式存储每个对象的位置和速度信息（节点记忆）。实验设置在一个专为FloWM设计的方块世界中进行，并采用一个理想状态下的卡尔曼滤波器作为节点记忆的实现方式来进行比较。

Result: 结果表明，在特定读取方式下，FloWM能够找到66%至89%的不在视线内的方块；而在视野内评估时，由于其自身预测回写机制，得分更高。不过，当考虑存储效率以及处理非预设速度（如对角线方向）的能力时，基于节点的记忆体显示出明显优势。

Conclusion: 虽然FloWM在某些场景下展现出良好性能，但面对复杂运动模式时局限性较大。未来工作需要开发出能够应对噪声、长时间间隔及反弹情况的学习型节点记忆系统。

Abstract: A robot's world model must remember objects that leave the camera's view. FloWM, a learned world model, keeps them in a spatial grid of features that it moves with five built-in velocities. A node memory instead keeps each object's position and velocity as numbers. We compare them in FloWM's own block world, with an oracle-fed Kalman tracker as the node memory. How the grid is read changes its score: a single-cell probe finds 66% of out-of-view blocks, while FloWM's own probe 89%. FloWM also scores 8 points higher in view although no frame is shown, which we attribute to its write-back of its own predictions. Out of view, the tracker reaches FloWM's range in 14KB against 5.6MB, with no probe. On diagonal motion, outside its five velocities, FloWM finds 11% of blocks even after fine-tuning, while the unchanged tracker keeps 93%. Faster blocks defeat both until one more allowed speed takes the tracker to 94%, where FloWM's built-in shifts would need 25 copies of its grid. A learned node memory must still replace the oracles and handle velocity under noise, long gaps, and the bounce.

</details>


### [234] [ForkCast: Predictive Recovery for Vision-Language-Action Policies via Counterfactual Futures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18650)
*Chenru Wen,Huajian Zeng,Haokai Ding,Jian Kang,Anqing Duan,Dawei Zhou,Dezhen Song,Xingxing Zuo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ForkCast, a new framework for predictive recovery in vision-language-action (VLA) policies, enhances the success rate of robotic manipulation tasks by evaluating and choosing between continuing with the current action, adjusting the camera view, or replanning the arm's movement, without needing to detect failures or adjust intervention thresholds. It demonstrates significant improvement in task success rates across simulated and real-robot scenarios.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation behind this paper is to address the challenge faced by VLA policies in performing manipulation tasks when confronted with object slip or visual occlusion, which can disrupt their execution. Traditional approaches to handling such disruptions either require learning recovery during policy training or using a monitor that triggers a scripted repair upon detecting a failure. The authors propose ForkCast as an innovative solution that does not rely on failure detection or intervention thresholds, aiming to improve the robustness and effectiveness of VLA policies under challenging conditions.

Method: ForkCast operates by comparing counterfactual futures to decide on the most appropriate course of action at each decision point. It evaluates three options: executing the planned action (Commit), changing the camera viewpoint (Reobserve), and recalculating the arm's trajectory (Replan). A world model conditioned on these options predicts the latent outcomes at a future time, which are then scored based on predicted task progress. This process allows ForkCast to determine both if and how to intervene, using a single comparison, thus streamlining the decision-making process for intervention.

Result: The implementation of ForkCast led to a notable increase in the success rate of five simulated RoboTwin 2.0 tasks, from 13.3% to 30.0%, even when facing challenges like occlusion and object slip. Additionally, it showed transferability to other VLA backbones without requiring modifications to ForkCast itself. In real-world testing, after adapting to candidate rollouts, ForkCast also improved performance over the base policy in both normal and visibility-limited conditions, underscoring its practical value and adaptability.

Conclusion: In conclusion, ForkCast presents a promising approach to enhancing the resilience and efficiency of VLA policies in the face of common execution disruptions. By offering a streamlined method for deciding whether and how to intervene, without the need for failure detectors or intervention threshold tuning, it represents a significant step forward in the field of robotic manipulation. Its demonstrated success across various scenarios, including simulated and real-robot tests, highlights its potential impact on advancing the capabilities of VLA systems.

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies perform manipulation tasks using camera images and language instructions, but object slip or visual occlusion can disrupt execution. Recovery is typically learned during policy training or delegated to a monitor that triggers a scripted repair after detecting a failure. We introduce ForkCast, a predictive recovery framework that compares counterfactual futures to determine whether and how to intervene in a frozen VLA policy. At each decision point, ForkCast evaluates three candidates: executing the policy's nominal action chunk (Commit), adjusting the camera viewpoint (Reobserve), and replanning the arm trajectory (Replan). A candidate-conditioned world model forecasts the latent outcome of each candidate at a common future horizon, and a shared task-progress head scores the predicted outcomes. By evaluating the policy's own continuation alongside the repair candidates, a single comparison based on task progress determines both whether and how to intervene, without a failure detector, an intervention threshold, or updates to the VLA backbone. On five simulated RoboTwin 2.0 tasks with injected occlusion and object slip, ForkCast increases pooled success from 13.3% for the frozen $π_{0.5}$ policy to 30.0% and transfers to two additional VLA backbones without updating ForkCast. After adaptation on real-robot candidate rollouts, ForkCast also improves success over the base policy under both clean and visibility-limited conditions. Code will be released to benefit the research community.

</details>


### [235] [Perception Beyond the Visible: Incompleteness-Aware Scene Understanding for Robot Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18670)
*Rohit Mohan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个针对不完全性场景理解的研究愿景，旨在解决证据、知识和传感器可靠性不完全这三大挑战。通过无模态感知、语义占用预测、开放世界场景理解、标签高效学习以及不确定性引导的多模融合等方法来应对这些挑战，并展望了未来朝向以信念为中心的4D场景表示研究方向，目标是开发出支持动态开放环境中可靠自主性的校准和可行动场景表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前许多感知系统仅建模可见场景区域、依赖固定的语义类别并假设传感器测量可靠，但在遮挡、遇到新物体及传感器退化的情况下这些假设不再成立，因此需要一种能处理不完整性信息的方法来提升机器人在复杂环境下的自主性。

Method: 作者通过无模态感知、语义占用预测、开放世界场景理解、标签高效学习以及不确定性指导下的多模态融合技术来应对场景理解中的不完全证据、不完全知识与传感器不可靠性问题。

Result: 该研究提出了一系列方法论和技术路径，为构建能够同时表达几何、语义、运动和不确定性的4D场景表示奠定了基础，同时也探讨了通过交互减少不确定性的主动感知和安全适应策略。

Conclusion: 为了在动态且未知因素较多的真实世界环境中实现可靠的机器人自主性，发展考虑不完整性的场景理解和相应的不确定性管理策略是非常必要的，未来工作将集中在创建更加准确且实用的场景表示上。

Abstract: Reliable robot autonomy requires reasoning about the world from partial and noisy observations. However, many perception systems model only visible scene regions, rely on fixed semantic categories, and assume reliable sensor measurements. These assumptions break under occlusion, novel object encounters, and sensor degradation. In this paper, I present a research vision for incompleteness-aware scene understanding. I organize this vision around three fundamental challenges: incomplete evidence, incomplete knowledge, and incomplete sensor reliability. I discuss my research contributions toward addressing these challenges through amodal perception, semantic occupancy forecasting, open-world scene understanding, label-efficient learning, and uncertainty-guided multi-modal fusion. Building on these contributions, I outline future research directions toward belief-centric 4D scene representations. These representations aim to jointly model geometry, semantics, motion, and uncertainty. I also discuss active sensing and safe adaptation strategies that allow robots to reduce uncertainty through interaction. The goal is to develop calibrated and actionable scene representations that support reliable autonomy in dynamic, open-world environments.

</details>


### [236] [UniW(A)M: A Unified Mixture-of-Experts Framework for World and World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18697)
*Jingbo Xu,Jianjie Fang,Zhuohang Li,Zihao Song,Zhaolu Wang,Xvyuan Liu,Xin Zhang,Haisheng Su,Yinzhou Tang,Chen Gao,Wei Wu,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为UniW(A)M的模型，该模型基于扩散变换器，统一了以动作条件的世界建模和多种WAM变体。通过共享专家来捕捉共同的动力学特性，并且每个接口保持自己的条件路径。此外，部署时使用插件自适应接口路由器（PAIR）从目标任务场景的小子集中学习，选择合适的WAM接口以平衡任务成功和执行效率。在LIBERO、RoboTwin 2.0以及三个现实世界操作任务上的评估显示，该模型相比最强基线实现了更高的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前世界模型(WMs)和世界-动作模型(WAMs)虽然能学习来自具体轨迹的共享物理动态和交互语义，但通常单独训练，这限制了预测与控制之间知识的重用。研究旨在开发一个既能共享这些动力学特征又能保留每种范式所需独特建模能力的统一学习框架。

Method: 提出了UniW(A)M模型，这是一种基于扩散变换器的混合专家世界-（动作）模型，它统一了以动作为条件的世界建模及多样的WAM变体。该模型通过共享专家来捕捉通用动力学特征，同时原子能力专家跨接口复用，使得每个接口能够维持其特有的条件路径。部署阶段，采用了一个插件自适应接口路由器(PAIR)，通过学习目标任务片段场景的一个小子集来为每次执行块挑选合适的WAM接口，在保持主干冻结的同时优化任务成功率和执行效率。

Result: 在LIBERO、RoboTwin 2.0以及三项实际操控任务中，UniW(A)M达到了88.3%的平均真实世界成功率，相比之下最强基准仅为76.7%。于WorldArena上，在所有三项任务中领先于行动条件下的世界建模表现。AC-WM协同训练对每个动作界面的动作生成都有贡献，而PAIR将OOD（未见数据）的成功率从63.3%提升到了76.7%。

Conclusion: 通过引入UniW(A)M模型及其配套的PAIR机制，本研究有效解决了现有WMs和WAMs之间的知识隔离问题，展示了在提高机器人任务完成度方面的显著优势，特别是在处理未知情况时展现出了更强的鲁棒性和效率。

Abstract: World models (WMs) and world-action models (WAMs) focus on future world prediction and executable action generation, respectively. Although these models learn shared physical dynamics and interaction semantics from embodied trajectories, they are typically trained in isolation, which restricts knowledge reuse between prediction and control. The central challenge is therefore to develop a unified learning framework that shares these dynamics while preserving the distinct modeling capabilities required by each paradigm.
  We propose UniW(A)M, a mixture-of-experts world-(action) model built upon diffusion Transformers that unifies action-conditioned world modeling and diverse WAM variants. Shared experts capture common dynamics, while atomic capability experts are reused across interfaces so that each interface keeps its own conditional pathway. At deployment, a plug-in adaptive interface router (PAIR) learns from a small subset of target-task chunk scenarios to select a suitable WAM interface for each execution chunk, balancing task success and execution efficiency while keeping the backbone frozen.
  We evaluate UniW(A)M on LIBERO, RoboTwin 2.0, and three real-world manipulation tasks, where it achieves 88.3% average real-world success versus 76.7% for the strongest baseline. On WorldArena, it leads action-conditioned world modeling on all three tasks. AC-WM co-training contributes to action generation on every action interface and PAIR raises OOD success from 63.3% to 76.7%.

</details>


### [237] [RoboFQE: Rollout-Free Evaluation of Visual Robot Policies from Offline Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18699)
*Yunfu Deng,Sangwoo Shin,Brahma S. Pavse,Josiah P. Hanna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为RoboFQE的评估器，它可以在不估计行为策略密度的情况下从离线机器人轨迹中估算目标策略的折扣期望回报。通过结合拟合Q-评估与预训练视觉基础模型和学习到的终端成功注释，RoboFQE能够为连续控制视觉操作任务提供准确的折扣回报估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于滚动的方法在评估机器人策略时需要执行新的轨迹、使用模拟器或学习的世界模型，这些方法不仅对基础设施有要求，而且模拟器和世界模型评估器还面临着可能随评估范围增加而累积的预测误差。因此，研究者探索了非策略评估作为替代方案。

Method: 研究人员引入了RoboFQE，这是一种不需要估计行为策略密度即可从离线机器人轨迹估计目标政策折扣预期收益的评估工具。该方法将拟合Q-评估与预训练视觉基础模型以及学习到的终止成功标注相结合，从而解决了标准OPE基准测试与连续控制视觉操控之间的差距问题。

Result: 控制实验表明，在模拟和现实世界的操作任务中，即使是在长周期操作任务下视频世界模型评估器受到自回归预测错误影响的情况下，RoboFQE仍能生成准确的折扣回报估计并保持策略排名。消融实验进一步显示，预训练视觉编码器的表现明显优于从零开始训练的选择，特别是在长时间范围内；并且通过学习得到的终止成功注释能够在没有真实奖励标签的情况下进行评估，仅略微牺牲一点准确性。

Conclusion: RoboFQE作为一种非策略评价方法，对于机器人策略评估来说是一个有效的选择，特别是在处理长期视野下的连续控制视觉操作任务时，它能够克服传统基于展开式方法的一些限制。

Abstract: Evaluating robot policies before deployment typically requires producing new trajectories through real-world execution, simulators, or learned world models. These rollout-based approaches impose infrastructure requirements; simulator and world-model evaluators additionally face prediction errors that can compound over the evaluation horizon. In this paper, we study off-policy evaluation as an alternative to rollout-based robot-policy evaluation methods. Specifically, we introduce RoboFQE, an evaluator that estimates the discounted expected return of a target policy from offline robot trajectories without estimating a behavior-policy density, combining fitted Q-evaluation with a pretrained vision foundation model and learned terminal success annotation to bridge the gap between standard OPE benchmarks and continuous-control visual manipulation. Controlled experiments across simulated and real-world manipulation tasks show that RoboFQE produces accurate discounted-return estimates and preserves policy rankings even on long-horizon manipulation tasks where video world-model evaluators are degraded by autoregressive prediction errors. Ablation experiments show that pretrained vision encoders substantially outperform from-scratch alternatives, particularly on long-horizon tasks, and that learned terminal success annotation enables evaluation without ground-truth reward labels with only slight reduction of accuracy.

</details>


### [238] [Steertuning Policies from a Few Human Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18711)
*Billy Yan,Isaac Tu,Kelly Lee,Blake Chang,Kenny Ma,Kanad Patel,Dhawal Kabra,Neer Patel,Kevin Wu,Lerrel Pinto,Irmak Guzey*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Steertuning, a novel system, allows robots to learn new tasks in different environments using only 5 human demonstrations and within 5 minutes. It combines pretraining on a large dataset with quick fine-tuning for new tasks, achieving a 71% success rate across various settings.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation of this paper is to reduce the amount of data (demonstrations) required for robot learning, making it more efficient and adaptable to new tasks and environments. Current methods often require a large number of demonstrations, which can be time-consuming and resource-intensive.

Method: The method involves two main steps: (1) Pretraining a large diffusion policy model on an egocentric human dataset comprising 100 hours of annotated interactions, which helps the model generalize to new scenarios; (2) Fine-tuning the pretrained model with just 5 demonstrations of a new task, taking only 2 minutes, and guiding the policy's actions during reverse sampling based on the most similar demonstration to the current environment. This approach, coupled with a tailored data processing pipeline, facilitates the transfer of learned behaviors to new physical embodiments and settings.

Result: Through this approach, Steertuning achieves a 71% success rate in performing 12 different tasks across 12 new environments, including lab settings and home kitchens, demonstrating its effectiveness in quickly adapting to unseen tasks and environments. The entire process, from collecting data for a new task to deploying the trained policy, takes less than 5 minutes.

Conclusion: Steertuning represents a significant advancement in the field of robot learning, showcasing the potential for rapid adaptation to new tasks and environments with minimal human intervention. By drastically reducing the need for extensive training data, it opens up possibilities for more widespread and practical applications of robotic systems.

Abstract: Current recipes for robot learning require massive amounts of robot demonstrations to obtain robust and adaptive policies. In this work, we introduce Steertuning, a system that enables robots to learn new tasks in new environments from 5 human demonstrations in under 5
  Steertuning consists of two parts. (1) We pretrain a 400M-parameter diffusion policy on our egocentric human dataset, which consists of 100 hours of densely annotated random interactions. This pretraining makes Steertuning robust to background differences and enables spatial and object generalization when deployed on new tasks. (2) Given 5 demonstrations of a new task, we finetune our model for only 2 minutes (2000 training updates) and steer the policy's actions during reverse sampling using the demonstration whose observation is closest to that of the deployment environment. Together with a carefully designed data processing pipeline for embodiment transfer, this enables Steertuning to achieve a 71% success rate across 12 tasks in 12 new environments, ranging from lab settings to kitchens in new homes. The entire process, from data collection for a new task to robot policy deployment, takes less than 5 minutes. Our full dataset, model weights, codebase, and robot videos will be made available at our website: https://steertuning.github.io.

</details>


### [239] [GreenWAM: A World Foundation Model for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18774)
*Gennady Sidorov,Valerii Kornilov,Denis Grankin,Polina Fedotova,Aleksey Postnikov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了GreenWAM，一种机器人世界基础模型，能够同时作为世界模型和策略应用于不同的机器人类型，并通过共享解剖动作空间、联合训练以及将动作专家从生成器中分离出来等方法实现。在多个基准测试中表现优秀，展示了其在预测和控制任务上的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的机器人模型通常只为单一角色（即世界模型或决策策略）设计，并且每个机器人都以自己的格式记录动作。研究旨在探索是否存在一种可以跨越不同机器人身体同时服务于两个角色的模型，以及实现这一目标的关键因素是什么。

Method: 1. 定义了一个共享解剖动作空间来描述20种不同类型的机器人；2. 在视觉、动作条件及世界-动作模式下对这些机器人进行联合训练；3. 从生成器中分离出一个动作专家作为策略部分。

Result: GreenWAM不仅在WorldArena 1.0上达到了顶级轨迹、深度和3D精度，在RoboTwin 2.0排行榜上也名列前茅，并且在LIBERO-plus零样本成功度方面表现出色。此外，该模型还被适配到实际的人形机器人上并展示出了优于其他方法的表现。

Conclusion: 研究表明，针对特定机器人的优化可以同时改善世界预测与控制性能；从一开始就分离开来的动作专家有助于适应过程；保持世界模型模式有助于政策调整期间对场景变化的鲁棒性。

Abstract: A robot needs a world model that predicts how its actions will change the scene and a policy that chooses those actions. Models are usually built and evaluated for only one of these roles, and every robot records its actions in its own format. We ask whether one robot world model can serve both roles across different bodies, and what makes this work. We present GreenWAM, a world foundation model for robotics, together with the recipe that produces it: (1) a shared anatomical action space that describes 20 robot families, from bimanual arms to dexterous humanoids, in common joint coordinates; (2) joint training on these families in visual, action-conditioned and world-action modes; and (3) policies that split an action expert off the generator. As a world model, GreenWAM attains top-tier trajectory, depth and 3D accuracy on WorldArena 1.0, ahead of every world-action model and video generator in our comparison. As a policy, it scores among the top entries of the RoboTwin 2.0 leaderboard, reaches the highest zero-shot LIBERO-plus success among the compared methods and is competitive with dedicated vision-language-action and world-action policies on CALVIN and LIBERO. We further adapt GreenWAM to a real humanoid, where it succeeds more often than policies adapted from $π_{0.5}$ and from Cosmos 3 Edge, and distill it into a single-step policy for real-time control. Controlled experiments yield three findings: robot specialization improves world prediction and control together; an action expert helps when split off at the start of adaptation; and keeping world-model modes during policy adaptation preserves robustness to scene changes.

</details>


### [240] [EqC: Equilibrium Energy Control for Introspective Lifelong Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18790)
*Haodong Yang,Runhan Huang,Bowen Zhang,Iofai Lei,Zunzhe Zhang,Xiangcheng Zhang,Shaoting Zhu,Linzhan Mou,Yicheng Liu,Hang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为均衡能量控制(EqC)的框架，用于机器人终身学习中的自我反省。EqC通过优化过程来精炼生成动作，并在行为不再与所学模型一致时触发专家干预以更新策略。此方法提高了控制性能和鲁棒性，同时也为从静态生成控制器向自改进机器人系统的转变提供了可行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期自主运行的机器人不仅需要有效的动作生成能力，还需要一种机制能够识别何时其行为缺乏足够的支持，并利用外部帮助来自我提升。为此，文章提出了一个围绕明确条件能量场构建的、旨在促进内省式终生学习的机器人框架——均衡能量控制（EqC）。

Method: 研究引入了均衡能量控制（EqC）框架，它将生成性动作优化视为寻求平衡的过程。同时，该框架能够通过优化过程的几何特性判断当前行为是否仍符合已学习的能量场。基于此，研究者开发了能量门控DAgger算法，在持续违反能量一致性的情况下触发专家接管并收集恢复轨迹供后续策略更新使用。此外，EqC可以自然地整合进扩散变换器政策及流匹配基础的视觉-语言-行动(VLA)政策中。

Result: 实验表明，在模拟环境与真实世界的双臂操作任务中，EqC能够匹配甚至超越现有方法的控制表现与鲁棒性，并且能够在接近策略能力边界时及时介入。进一步的真实世界研究表明，这些适时干预有助于逐步扩大机器人能够自主处理的情景范围。

Conclusion: EqC提供了一个实用的方法论，使得静态生成控制器向着能够自我改进的机器人系统过渡成为可能。通过适时引入外部援助，EqC不仅提升了机器人的控制性能和稳定性，还促进了它们适应更广泛场景的能力发展。

Abstract: Long-term robot autonomy depends on more than effective action generation: a policy must recognize when its behavior is no longer sufficiently supported and use assistance to improve. We introduce Equilibrium Energy Control (EqC), a framework for introspective lifelong robot learning built around an explicit conditional energy landscape. EqC casts generative action refinement as equilibrium-seeking optimization, while the geometry of the refinement process reveals whether the generated behavior remains consistent with the learned landscape. This competence signal enables Energy-Gated DAgger, where persistent energy-consistency violations trigger expert takeover and the resulting recovery trajectories are aggregated for subsequent policy updates. EqC integrates naturally into both Diffusion Transformer policies and flow-matching-based vision-language-action (VLA) policies. Across simulation and real-world bimanual manipulation, EqC matches or improves control performance and robustness while enabling timely intervention near the policy's competence boundary. A real-world study further shows how these interventions progressively expand the range of situations handled autonomously, providing a practical path from static generative controllers toward self-improving robot systems.

</details>


### [241] [SEAR: Self-Evolving Agentic Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18792)
*Xinyi Wang,Bangzheng Li,Hanyu Wang,Lin Zhang,Andrea Yaoyun Cui,Zhenggang Tang,Shyamal Buch,Dejia Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SEAR（自进化代理机器人）框架和基准，旨在研究语言模型代理是否真的在学习以及所学内容能否应用于新任务、新模拟器和真实机器人。实验表明，机器人的学习曲线依赖于时间和任务难度，控制代码和共享程序可以跨任务转移，而特定任务的解决方案则不能。此外，不同的模型以不同方式更有效地进化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了探究前沿的语言模型代理是否真的在学习，还是仅仅尝试更多次，以及它们学到的知识是否有助于解决新任务、适应新的模拟环境，并且能在实际机器人上发挥作用。

Method: 提出了SEAR（Self-Evolving Agentic Robotics）框架与基准测试，该框架允许代理以三种模式运行：将编写的代码作为策略（code-as-policy）、直接参与控制循环作为策略(agent-as-policy)或充当研究员来微调视觉-语言-行动模型(auto-research)。通过这种设置，能够区分保留的经验与重复尝试之间的差异，并评估了多种条件下的表现。

Result: 发现机器人代理的学习曲线既取决于总时间预算也受制于相对于模型能力的任务难度；控制代码和通用流程能够在不同任务间迁移但特定任务解法不行；对于现实中的机器人而言，在人类设计的新场景中存在性能差距；不同类型模型表现出不同的有效进化路径。

Conclusion: SEAR框架和基准测试揭示了语言模型代理在机器人技术领域内学习及泛化能力的关键见解。结果显示，根据任务特性和模型特性选择合适的方法至关重要。同时，研究还指出感知虽有助于错误评估，但在某些情况下，具象推理和工具使用可能更加重要。

Abstract: Frontier language-model agents can now improve robots on their own: they write control code, test it, and keep what works as skills and memories for the next task. We introduce SEAR (Self-Evolving Agentic Robotics), a framework and benchmark built around two questions. Is the agent really learning, or just trying more often? And does what it learns still help on new tasks, in a new simulator, and on a real robot? The framework runs an agent in three modes: it writes code that becomes the policy (code-as-policy), acts live in the control loop (agent-as-policy), or works as a researcher that fine-tunes a vision-language-action model (auto-research). The benchmark adds controls that separate retained experience from repeated attempts, familiar tasks, privileged simulator state, larger budgets, and luck. Across seven frontier language models, 330 simulated and five physical tasks, and over four years of agent runtime, we highlight three findings. First, the learning curve of a robotic agent depends both on the overall time budget and the task difficulty relative to model capability. Harder tasks make longer sessions worthwhile; on easier tasks, re-rolling short attempts does just as well. Second, control code and shared routines transfer across tasks and simulators, while task-specific solutions do not. On a real robot, we observe a performance gap between agent-designed practice and novel human-designed scenes. Third, different models evolve more effectively in different ways. Some do best acting live, others writing code first. And a text-only model keeps pace with a comparable multimodal one: perception helps with error assessment, but agentic reasoning and tool use can matter more. We release the benchmark and codebase to support future research.

</details>


### [242] [EgoMatrix: Physically Faithful Dexterous Robot Demonstrations from Egocentric Human Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18822)
*Ziheng Wang,Yanming Shao,Ting Mao,Kaihan Chen,Yiqun Wang,Xuanye Wu,Yao Mu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EgoMatrix is a data engine that transforms monocular egocentric human videos into dexterous manipulation trajectories and robot observation videos, improving the accuracy of hand-object interaction (HOI) reconstruction and enabling more effective training for robotic tasks. It demonstrates superior performance in simulation and HOI reconstruction compared to existing methods.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation behind EgoMatrix is to address the challenges of accurately reconstructing hand-object interactions from monocular RGB videos and generating high-quality visual observations for robots, which are critical for collecting reliable dexterous manipulation data without relying on robot teleoperation.

Method: EgoMatrix uses an end-to-end approach, focusing on relative geometry and contact for HOI reconstruction, followed by retargeting and reinforcement learning to replicate demonstrated tasks in simulation. It also renders synchronized physical observations of robot hands and objects, expanding training data through varied backgrounds while maintaining motion and contact fidelity.

Result: EgoMatrix achieved 54.84% success in simulation, significantly higher than the 14.52% success rate of the strongest baseline. It also outperformed previous methods in terms of HOI reconstruction quality, as measured by various metrics on public benchmarks, and showed closer alignment with real-robot observations.

Conclusion: EgoMatrix provides a robust solution for converting human demonstrations into actionable data for robot learning, demonstrating its potential in enhancing the effectiveness of dexterous manipulation training and setting new standards for HOI reconstruction.

Abstract: Egocentric human videos offer a scalable alternative to robot teleoperation for collecting dexterous manipulation data. However, two challenges limit their use as reliable training data: accurate hand-object interaction (HOI) reconstruction from monocular RGB and high-quality robot demonstrations, especially visual observations. We present EgoMatrix, an end-to-end data engine that converts monocular egocentric human videos into bimanual dexterous trajectories and paired robot observation videos, with the trajectories directly replayable on real robots. It reconstructs HOI with emphasis on relative geometry and contact rather than exact metric scene recovery, then uses retargeting and reinforcement learning (RL) to reproduce the demonstrated tasks in simulation. We jointly render robot hands and objects from these simulated trajectories to produce physically faithful observations synchronized with state and control records. Rendering the same trajectories in diverse, task-compatible backgrounds expands paired training data while preserving robot-object motion and contact. Across TACO, OakInk2, and self-collected data, EgoMatrix achieves 54.84% end-to-end success in simulation versus 14.52% for the strongest baseline. Reconstruction reaches state-of-the-art performance on most metrics of the two public benchmarks. Qualitative results show more faithful hand-object interactions than prior methods, while feature-space analysis shows closer alignment with real-robot observations than GPT-6 Astra.Our project page is available at https://spikew726.github.io/EgoMatrix/.

</details>


### [243] [WorldOracle: Benchmarking Action-Conditioned World Models against Real-World Executions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18826)
*Haoyi Duan,Liliang Chen,Nan Wang,Yang Zhang,Yuhang Lu,Xiaotong Chen,Wei-Cheng Tseng,Shenyuan Gao,Zhaoshuo Li,Jingyi Jin,Pooya Jannaty,Dhruv Shah,Yuke Zhu,Ming-Yu Liu,Maoqing Yao,Guanghui Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为WorldOracle的基准测试，专门用于评估基于动作条件的世界模型（ACWMs）与真实世界执行情况的对比。通过四个互补维度来评价模型的表现，并提供实验数据集、评估代码和排行榜以支持研究进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了系统地衡量和发展基于动作条件的世界模型在操作任务中的表现，作者们开发了WorldOracle这一基准测试工具，它能够帮助研究人员更好地理解这些模型在实际应用中的强项及不足之处。

Method: 通过物理状态正确性、交互空间对齐度、关键交互区域保真度以及多视角动作一致性这四个方面来综合评价模型性能；同时构建了一套覆盖不同互动动态和实体的真实世界训练与评估集合。

Result: 实验结果展示了七个代表性ACWM的不同优势与失败模式。WorldOracle为测量和发展基于动作条件的世界建模提供了系统的依据。

Conclusion: WorldOracle作为一项新的基准测试，为改进基于动作条件的世界模型提供了重要的评价标准和支持资源，促进了该领域的发展。

Abstract: We introduce WorldOracle, a benchmark specifically designed to evaluate action-conditioned world models (ACWMs) against paired real-world executions. Given the same initial observation and low-level action sequence, WorldOracle asks whether a predicted rollout reproduces what happens in the real world, including physical consequences and task-state transitions. We evaluate models along four complementary dimensions: physical state correctness, interaction spatial alignment, key interaction area fidelity, and multi-view action coherence. To support this evaluation, we curate real-world training and evaluation suites that cover different interaction dynamics and embodiments. The evaluation suite contains long, multi-segment episodes with both successful and failed executions, enabling evaluation across varied action outcomes and behavior distributions. Experiments on seven representative ACWMs reveal distinct model strengths and failure modes. WorldOracle provides a systematic foundation for measuring and advancing action-conditioned world modeling for manipulation. We will release the datasets, evaluation code, and leaderboard.

</details>


### [244] [RoboSimStudio: An Interactive Toolkit for Rapid Real2Sim of Robot Manipulation Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18835)
*Kaiyuan Chen,Shuangyu Xie,Uksang Yoo,Xinyue Zhu,Cael Magner,Alvin Zhu,Daehwa Kim,Eric Heiden,Erwin Coumans,Miles Macklin,Spencer Huang,Ken Goldberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 介绍了RoboSimStudio，一个用于改进机器人与物体交互物理模拟的工具包，通过明确和可编辑的选择求解器、接触模型和物理参数来帮助用户基于观察到的行为迭代优化模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于机器人与其操作对象之间的物理交互（包括接触力学、耦合接触动力学、变形、刚度和摩擦等）建模存在困难，因此需要一种能够更准确地反映这些复杂现象并允许用户调整参数以优化模拟结果的工具。

Method: 开发了名为RoboSimStudio (RSS) 的互动工具箱，该工具箱使用开放的Newton物理仿真系统实现，并且支持人类指导下的真实世界到模拟环境的转换实验，包括视觉评估、力反馈以及代理的真实-模拟-真实循环测试。

Result: 通过实施一系列实验验证了RoboSimStudio的有效性，证明其能够在不同场景下（如视觉评价、力反馈应用及自动化的实现实验）辅助改善机器人与环境交互的仿真精度。

Conclusion: RoboSimStudio提供了一种新的方法来提高机器人操纵任务中物理模拟的真实性，为研究人员提供了一个灵活而强大的平台来进行更精确的仿真研究。

Abstract: It is difficult to accurately model the physics of the interaction between a robot and the objects it manipulates, including contact mechanics, coupled contact dynamics, deformations, stiffness and friction. We present RoboSimStudio (RSS), an interactive toolkit that makes choices of solver, contact model, and physical parameters explicit and editable, allowing both humans and coding agents to iteratively refine simulations based on observed physical behavior. We implement RSS with the open Newton physics simulation system and describe experiments with human-guided real2sim using (1) visual evaluation, (2) force-feedback and (3) agentic Real2Sim2Real. Code, video and additional information can be found on the project website.

</details>


### [245] [LIBERO-RIG: A Hardware-Setup Robustness Benchmark for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18843)
*Andrea Yaoyun Cui,Hanyu Wang,Xinyi Wang,Bangzheng Li,Lin Zhang,Zhenggang Tang,Shyamal Buch,Dejia Xu,Matthew Tancik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为LIBERO-RIG的基准测试，旨在评估机器人策略在面对硬件和设置变化时的表现。该基准涵盖11个扰动轴，每个轴有5个严重程度级别，总共210种条件。研究发现，随着扰动严重程度增加，整体成功率显著下降，并且视觉扰动训练不能解决那些不影响相机图像的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人策略需要能够适应部署环境中的各种变化，包括机器人自身硬件的变化。为了测试这些策略在不同硬件配置下的鲁棒性，作者提出了一个新的基准测试LIBERO-RIG，专门针对机器人的硬件及设置变化进行扰动实验。

Method: 通过设计一个包含11个扰动轴（如摄像头安装、控制器增益等）并为每轴设定5个不同强度级别的基准测试方案，对9个最新的视觉-语言-动作模型进行了评估。每个条件下运行了500次试验，并使用严格的统计方法验证结果。

Result: 随着扰动强度从低到高，所有测试模型的成功率都有所下降，从中等强度的0.767降至最高强度下的0.509。此外，不同类型的扰动以及不同的动作接口对于性能的影响存在很大差异。值得注意的是，仅仅针对视觉方面的扰动进行训练并不能有效缓解那些不改变摄像头图像的问题。

Conclusion: LIBERO-RIG提供了一种有效的方法来衡量机器人策略在面对真实世界中常见的硬件变化时的鲁棒性和适应能力。研究结果表明，现有模型在处理某些类型的变化时表现不佳，这提示未来的研究方向可能需要更多关注如何提高系统在这类情况下的稳定性。

Abstract: Robot policies are expected to generalize across deployment environments, including variations in the robot itself: cameras are bumped and remounted, calibrations drift, controllers run at different rates, and end-effector pads wear down. We present LIBERO-RIG, a benchmark that perturbs only the robot's hardware and setup while holding every LIBERO task fixed. The benchmark spans eleven perturbation axes, including camera mounting and optics, camera-to-arm calibration, actuation gain, control rate, proprioceptive noise, base pose, table height, and gripper pads. Each axis has five severity levels, yielding 210 conditions across the four LIBERO suites. We evaluate nine recent vision-language-action models using 500 episodes per condition and substantiate each finding with rigorous statistical tests. Pooled success falls from 0.932 to 0.767 at the middle severity and 0.509 at the highest severity. Performance degradation varies substantially across perturbation axes and action interfaces, and training on visual perturbations does not mitigate faults that leave camera images unchanged. We release the benchmark together with manifests specifying the fixed perturbation parameters for each episode.

</details>


### [246] [Contact Trajectory Prompting: In-Context Transfer of Contact-Rich Behaviors from a Single Demonstration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18848)
*Xian Nie,Yujie Zang,Yuhang Zheng,Yupeng Zheng,Songen Gu,Wendi Chen,Chuan Wen,Cewu Lu,Wenchao Ding,Junchi Yan,Shuicheng Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了接触轨迹提示(CTP)框架，用于一次性转移接触丰富的操作行为。通过将演示中的运动和接触信息编码为有序的接触轨迹提示来指导策略执行，从而在未训练过的物体上实现了78.7%的成功率，超越了最强基线方法13个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于视觉和运动学规范的方法可能无法区分具有相似动作但需要不同交互强度或模式的接触丰富行为。因此，研究提出了一种新的方法来改进机器人在接触丰富环境下的学习与模仿能力。

Method: 研究者开发了接触轨迹提示(CTP)这一新框架，它能够从单个演示中编码运动和接触信息，并形成一个有序的接触轨迹提示，该提示捕捉到了这些信息的时间演变情况并指定了期望的身体互动行为。执行过程中，策略利用在线感官反馈从提示中检索相关的互动信息，根据展示的接触行为条件生成闭环动作。

Result: 实验结果显示，在真实世界中的擦拭、旋转及插入任务上，对于那些被排除在训练集之外的对象，CTP达到了78.7%的整体成功率，比评估中最强大的基线高出13.0个百分点。此外，仅更改参考演示就允许CTP遵循包括中间擦拭力水平以及顺时针或逆时针帽旋转在内的接触规格。

Conclusion: 研究表明，接触轨迹可以作为有效的行为规范，使得上下文机器人学习不仅限于动作模仿，还扩展到了物理交互的传递上。

Abstract: In-context robot learning enables robots to acquire new behaviors from demonstrations without test-time parameter updates. However, visual and kinematic specifications alone may not distinguish contact-rich behaviors that share similar motions but require different interaction intensities or patterns. We propose Contact Trajectory Prompting (CTP), a framework for one-shot transfer of contact-rich manipulation behaviors. CTP encodes motion and contact information from a single demonstration into an ordered contact trajectory prompt that captures their temporal evolution and specifies the desired physical interaction behavior. During execution, the policy uses online sensory feedback to retrieve interaction-relevant information from the prompt, conditioning closed-loop action generation on the demonstrated contact behavior. Across real-world wiping, rotation, and insertion tasks on objects excluded from training, CTP achieves 78.7% overall success, outperforming the strongest evaluated baseline by 13.0 percentage points. Changing only the reference demonstration enables CTP to follow contact specifications for both intensity and direction, including an intermediate wiping force level and clockwise or counterclockwise cap rotation. These results demonstrate that contact trajectories serve as effective behavioral specifications, extending in-context robot learning beyond motion imitation to physical interaction transfer. Project website: https://ctp-robotics.github.io/project_page.

</details>


### [247] [Collaborative Planning for UAV-UGV Teams under Operational Constraints via Masked Error-bounded Neuro-Symbolic Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18850)
*Zihao Deng,Qianhuang Li,Maggie Wigness,John Rogers,Donghyun Kim,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为MEND的新方法，用于在操作约束下增强UAV-UGV团队协作规划的最优性。通过结合神经符号扩散范式和误差有界推理调度，MEND能够生成符合操作约束的上下文感知动作序列，从而有效促进异构机器人团队间的协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了克服现有Masked Diffusion Models (MDMs)在处理无人机(UAVs)与无人地面车辆(UGVs)协作任务时存在的局限性，特别是关于如何在保证分布一致性的同时满足差异化约束条件的问题，提出了一个新的框架来优化不同种类机器人之间的协同工作流程。

Method: MEND采用神经符号扩散模型，并通过逻辑诱导的可接受性指标重新加权学习到的动作分布，以确保执行过程中遵守操作约束。此外，它还引入了一种基于最小预测熵的选择策略，构建解掩码计划表，以提高在考虑约束条件下合作规划的质量。

Result: 实验结果表明，无论是在仿真环境、真实世界地图上还是物理机器人团队中，MEND都能够显著提升UAV-UGV协同规划的效果，特别是在存在操作约束的情况下表现优异。

Conclusion: 本研究表明，MEND提供了一种有效的解决方案，能够在保持对操作约束敏感性的前提下改善异构机器人团队（如UAV-UGV组合）之间的协作效率与效果。

Abstract: Collaborative planning under operational constraints is essential for heterogeneous robot teams in large-scale missions. Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) provide rapid area coverage but are limited by onboard energy, while Unmanned Ground Vehicles (UGVs) can serve as mobile recharging stations yet are constrained by road-network traversability. Effective collaboration must enforce operational constraints during execution while enabling parallel, coordinated actions. Masked Diffusion Models (MDMs) have shown promise through token unmasking but have two key limitations: entropy-guided scheduling assumes distributional alignment and reliance on differentiable constraints for diffusion-time optimization. We propose Masked Error-bounded Neuro-Symbolic Diffusion (MEND), which formulates collaborative planning as a conditional masked generative process to generate context-aware action sequences for UAV-UGV teams. MEND introduces a neuro-symbolic diffusion paradigm that constrains conditional masked diffusion policies at execution by reweighting the learned action distribution with a logic-induced admissibility indicator, thereby enforcing compliance with operational constraints. MEND introduces an error-bounded inference scheduling at execution that constructs the unmasking schedule by selecting positions with minimal predictive entropy over the constraint-aware action distribution, enhancing collaborative planning optimality under operational constraints. Extensive experimental results in simulation, on real-world maps, and with physical robot teams demonstrate that MEND advances UAV-UGV collaborative planning under operational constraints.

</details>


### [248] [ForceWAM: Decoupling Future and Response Learning from Demo and Play Data for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18852)
*Zijun Xu,Wendi Chen,Xinkai Wang,Jiachen Zou,Hongjin Chen,Hairuo Liu,Bokai Lin,Shihao Ma,Ke Ma,Zhiwei Zhang,Zhuohao Yan,Ke Wu,Haoming Song,Xueqi Li,Chuan Wen,Jieru Zhao,Wenchao Ding,Cewu Lu,Lixin Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为ForceWAM的力世界动作模型，该模型通过解耦任务兼容的交互结果知识与局部动作如何改变接触的知识，来充分利用专家演示和无目标游戏这两种类型的数据。实验表明，在包含受力调节滑动、有无干扰的盒子翻转以及环放置等真实机器人接触丰富任务中，ForceWAM的表现优于基于世界建模和反应式基准方法，并且加入无目标游戏数据可以提高15到45个百分点的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了能够更好地利用专家演示和无目标游戏两种不同类型的数据源，同时解决在执行接触丰富的任务时对不同动作下交互力响应了解有限的问题。

Method: 引入了ForceWAM模型，它包括一个从成功演示中学得的世界模型，用于共同预测未来的视频及六轴力/扭矩轨迹；还有一个逆动力学模型(IDM)，则从任务相关数据和游戏数据中学习，将当前观察和记录的视觉-力矩未来映射到相应的机器人动作上。在部署时，IDM根据生成的未来情况作出反应性转换为动作，并在世界模型更新之间重复使用这些信息与新的力反馈相结合。

Result: ForceWAM在实际机器人的力调控滑动、有或没有干扰下的盒子翻转以及环放置任务中实现了85%至95%的平均成功率，超越了单纯依靠世界建模或者反应式的基线方法。此外，研究还发现增加无目标的游戏数据可进一步提升15到45个百分点的成功率。

Conclusion: 本研究表明，通过结合专家演示与无目标游戏数据，ForceWAM能够有效学习接触丰富的操作技巧，显著提高了执行此类任务的成功率。

Abstract: Expert demonstrations show how to complete contact-rich tasks, but provide limited coverage of how interaction forces respond to different actions. Task-agnostic play provides broader experience of contact dynamics without specifying task goals. To fully leverage these two types of data, we introduce ForceWAM, a force world action model that decouples knowledge of task-compatible interaction outcomes from knowledge of how local actions change contact. A world model learns from successful demonstrations to jointly predict future video and six-axis force/torque trajectories. Together, these futures describe the expected evolution of motion and contact during the task. A separate inverse dynamics model (IDM) learns from both task and play, mapping current observations and recorded visual-wrench futures to their associated robot actions. Play data thus provide knowledge of force responses for goal-directed execution. At deployment, the IDM reactively translates generated futures into actions, reusing them with fresh wrench feedback between world-model updates. Across real-robot force-regulated sliding, box flipping with and without disturbances, and ring placement, ForceWAM achieves $85$-$95\%$ mean success, surpassing world-modeling and reactive baselines. Our experiments further show that adding task-agnostic play data improves success by $15$-$45$ percentage points, demonstrating its value for learning contact-rich execution. Project page and code are available at https://force-wam.github.io/.

</details>


### [249] [Interaction Latents as Actions for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18855)
*Yuhang Zheng,Yujie Zang,Yuchen Liu,Yupeng Zheng,Zhennan Jiang,Zebin Xing,Shuai Tian,Songen Gu,Jingxiang Guo,Chen Gao,Zhongpu Xia,Shuicheng Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的方法ILA，该方法通过将动作表示为包含运动、接触和力应用演化的交互潜变量，来改进机器人在接触密集型操作中的表现。研究显示，在四个实际任务中，与基于触觉直接行动的基线相比，这种方法提高了21.0至36.5个百分点的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大多数机器人策略主要将动作表示为运动轨迹，并未明确地在动作表示中编码接触动力学。为了使机器人能够更好地协调它们的动作以适应复杂的接触情况，需要一种可以同时考虑运动、接触及力量应用的方法。

Method: 提出了ILA（Interaction Latents as Actions），利用可解码的交互潜变量作为策略的动作单元，这些潜变量共同捕捉了运动、接触以及力量应用的变化过程。通过视觉条件下的联合动作-触觉重建学习这些潜变量，并采用时间条件隐式解码器。此外，还开发了一个共享解码器用于将生成的潜变量转换成可执行动作，以及一个基于专家演示的触觉条件参考模型来支持离线改进。

Result: 实验表明，相较于仅依赖触觉信息直接决定动作的传统方法，使用ILA方法在四种真实世界任务上的成功率平均提高了21.0到36.5个百分点。消融研究表明，在动作目标中编码接触演变比单纯的触觉调节更有利，而且相同的潜在空间也支持专家指导下的离线改进。

Conclusion: ILA提供了一种有效的新方式来增强机器人处理接触密集型任务的能力，通过引入一种综合考虑运动、接触及力量应用变化的动作表示形式。这种方法不仅提高了完成任务的成功率，也为后续基于专家经验的学习提供了可能性。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires robots to coordinate not only where to move, but also how contact and force should evolve throughout an interaction. Yet most robot policies represent actions primarily as motion trajectories, using tactile signals as inputs or auxiliary prediction targets without explicitly encoding contact dynamics in the action representation. We propose ILA (Interaction Latents as Actions), which uses decodable interaction latents that jointly capture the evolution of motion, contact, and force application as policy action units, providing a shared representation for action generation, evaluation, and offline improvement. We learn these latents through visually conditioned joint action-tactile reconstruction with a time-conditioned implicit decoder. Using them as flow-matching generation targets supports post-training of pretrained vision-language-action models (VLAs) and world action models (WAMs), as well as training flow-matching policies from scratch. A shared decoder maps generated latents to executable actions. For offline improvement, we learn a tactile-conditioned expert reference model from interaction latents encoded from expert demonstrations. The resulting expert-consistency rewards guide weighted learning from successful rollouts, while observation-compatible expert references guide correction of low-reward predictions from failed rollouts. Across three policy families, ILA improves success rates averaged over four real-world tasks by 21.0-36.5 percentage points relative to tactile-conditioned direct-action baselines. Ablations highlight the benefit of encoding contact evolution in action targets beyond tactile conditioning, while the same latent space supports expert-guided offline improvement. Project website: https://mrsecant.github.io/ILA/.

</details>


### [250] [AutoAgent0: An Agentic Runtime for Safe Closed-Loop Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18866)
*Jiageng Liu,Seth Z. Zhao,Zewei Zhou,Alejandro Huerta,Aidan Bayer-Calvert,Yuxin Bao,Haotian Lin,Robert Spataru,Zijun Cui,Yining Hong,Bolei Zhou,Jiaqi Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了AutoAgent0，一个运行时和框架系统，通过增强现有驾驶专家的互补检测技能、基于规则的动作验证以及反馈驱动的恢复机制来提高闭环驾驶安全性。该系统在Fail2Drive和NavSafe-∞测试中表现出改进的闭环鲁棒性，特别是在长尾和安全关键场景下，无需重新训练底层策略即可实现对多个驾驶专家的在线监督和恢复能力的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管端到端驾驶策略发展迅速，但在动态环境中可靠地执行闭环操作仍然面临挑战，尤其是在未见过的情景中累积的闭环错误可能导致车辆进入难以恢复的状态。

Method: 引入了AutoAgent0，这是一个代理运行时及框架，它为现有的驾驶专家增加了补充性的检测技巧、基于规则的动作验证和由反馈驱动的恢复机制。当感知场景不准确时，独立检测模块使用补充感知技巧构建场景上下文，并且基于规则的验证器会在执行前根据此上下文检查建议轨迹。如果标准轨迹不可靠，则运行时协调器会激活恢复规划，在这里一个代理循环从动作基元构造替代动作提案，并利用验证反馈迭代地精炼这些提案。

Result: 通过在Fail2Drive和NavSafe-∞上的评估显示，在长尾和安全关键情况下，与单个专家执行相比，所提方法展现出了更好的闭环鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，运行时监控可以在不对基础策略进行再训练的情况下，提高多种驾驶专家在不同情况下的闭环鲁棒性。

Abstract: Safe autonomous driving requires more than accurate planning at a single frame: it demands reliable closed-loop execution in dynamic environments. Although end-to-end driving policies have advanced rapidly, the accumulated closed-loop errors, especially in unseen scenarios, can push vehicles into states from which the same policy struggles to recover. We address the challenge by treating closed-loop driving safety as a test-time execution problem. We introduce AutoAgent0, an agentic runtime and harness framework that augments existing driving experts with complementary detection skills, rule-based action verification, and feedback-driven recovery. To address inaccurate scene perception, an independent detection module constructs scene context using complementary perception skills, and a rule-based verifier checks proposed trajectories against this context before execution. When the nominal trajectories are unreliable, the orchestrator of the runtime activates Recovery Planning, where an agentic loop constructs alternative action proposals from action primitives and iteratively refines them using verification feedback. This design combines independent scene assessment with feasibility checks and online recovery, addressing both perception errors and the limited recovery capabilities of individual experts. Evaluations on Fail2Drive and NavSafe-$\infty$ demonstrate improved closed-loop robustness over single-expert execution in long-tail and safety-critical scenarios. These results demonstrate that runtime supervision can improve closed-loop robustness across multiple driving experts without retraining the underlying policies.

</details>


### [251] [EmbodiedEvo: Self-improving Coding Agents for Physical Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18867)
*Wenyi Li,Yizhe Chi,Youjie Zheng,Chunyu Liu,Xiaoqiu Wang,Mingju Gao,Zheng Jiang,Deyao Hong,Calvin Xiao,Kaisen Yang,Qinhuai Na*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种名为EmbodiedEvo的方法，通过让编码代理在单一场景中演化可重用技能和教训来改善物理任务中的表现，并且这种经验可以在不同的新场景中被冻结和重复使用。该方法在RoboDojo-Sim平台上的实验显示了显著的成功率提升，尤其是在需要记忆的任务上，并且能够有效降低执行成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了将通用代理引入物理世界，需要一种可以执行的物理智能表示形式。与直接将观察结果和语言指令映射到动作的视觉-语言-动作模型相比，代码能明确表达控制逻辑并使其可复用。尽管在数字环境中采用计划、执行和验证的循环已取得成功，但在物理任务执行时仍然容易遇到反复失败的问题。因此，提出了一种新的方法以保留并精炼以往的经验，从而更好地适应新的场景。

Method: 提出了EmbodiedEvo方法，允许编码代理通过单一Playground场景中的反馈来发展可重用的技能（Skills）和教训（Lessons），然后将得到的结果固定下来应用于多样化的新型场景。这种方法模仿软件工程风格的工作流程，使代理能够自主检查状态、修改并执行代码，并利用反馈决定下一步行动。

Result: 在RoboDojo-Sim平台上，EmbodiedEvo达到了平均44.48分的成绩以及37.82%的成功率，超过了所有官方认证的结果。配对测试表明，在依赖记忆的任务上收益最大，同时成功实现了协调程序的重用，但精细接触控制仍具挑战性。此外，更强模型的经验也提高了较弱执行者的成功率，例如Astra的经验分别提高了Sol和Luna的成功率8.86个百分点和5.50个百分点。相比于原始交互历史记录，Skills和Lessons在实现相似任务性能的同时减少了53.0%的执行成本。

Conclusion: EmbodiedEvo为物理任务中的通用代理提供了一种有效的解决方案，通过促进经验和知识的累积及再利用，它不仅提高了特定类型任务的成功率，还降低了整体执行成本。这表明，在处理复杂的物理环境问题时，采用基于代码的学习方法具有很大潜力。

Abstract: Bringing general-purpose agents into the physical world calls for an executable representation of physical intelligence. Compared with vision-language-action (VLA) models, which map observations and language instructions directly to actions, code makes control logic explicit and reusable. Code can serve not only as a policy representation but also as the workspace in which the agent solves a task. Rather than following a prescribed workflow of planning, execution, and verification, the agent can autonomously inspect the state, revise and execute code, and use feedback to choose the next step. The success of this SWE-style loop in digital environments motivates its extension to physical tasks. Yet physical execution remains prone to recurring failures. To retain and refine experience for later scenes, we introduce EmbodiedEvo, where a coding agent evolves reusable Skills and Lessons through feedback on a single Playground scene, then freezes the resulting harness for diverse new scenes. On RoboDojo-Sim, EmbodiedEvo achieves an average score of 44.48 and a success rate of 37.82\%, surpassing all officially verified results. Paired tests show the largest gains on memory-dependent tasks and successful reuse of coordinated procedures, while fine contact control remains difficult. Experience from stronger models also improves weaker executors: Astra's experience raises Sol and Luna's success rates by 8.86 and 5.50 percentage points over no experience. Compared with raw interaction history, Skills and Lessons achieve comparable task performance at 53.0\% lower execution cost.

</details>


### [252] [What Matters in Quality-Aware VLA Pretraining: A Benchmark and Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18869)
*Mingxuan Yan,Litian Gong,Dihong Huang,Zhixu Li,Tianyu Zhang,Wenqian Zhang,Hefeifei Jiang,Peihao Li,Jian Zhang,Mengfei Zhao,Yikai Tang,Hai Zhai,Zehao Wang,Ruijian Liang,Yanjia Huang,Lin Shao,Changliu Liu,Kaiyu Hang,Zhiwen Fan,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Jianfei Yang,Jiachen Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在视觉-语言-动作预训练中如何利用演示质量来提高针对特定任务微调后的性能，通过对比不同奖励定义和标签粒度下的加权、过滤和条件设置策略，并引入AXIS-Bench作为支持这些控制比较的工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目的是探索如何在视觉-语言-动作（VLA）预训练过程中有效利用演示质量以提升目标任务微调后系统的表现。

Method: 通过对5000份自然产生的次优人类演示和24个目标任务进行分析，比较了不同奖励定义及标签细度下采用加权、过滤与基于情景的条件设定方法的效果；同时考察了预训练数据量以及质量标签在微调和推理阶段的作用。

Result: 发现对于三种VLA模型而言，基于情景感知的条件设定比简单的模仿学习更能提高平均成功率；条件设定更适用于情景级或块级标签，且这种偏好依赖于具体模型；带有固定标签的微调过程可以在条件预训练之后进一步提升平均成功率，无论是在标记还是未标记的情况下进行推理时。此外，还提出了AXIS-Bench用于支持上述受控比较实验。

Conclusion: 适当的使用高质量演示信息（如通过条件设置等方式）可以显著增强VLA模型对新任务的学习能力，而AXIS-Bench提供了一个强大的平台来促进相关研究。

Abstract: How should demonstration quality be used during vision-language-action (VLA) pretraining to improve performance after target-task fine-tuning? We compare weighting, filtering, and conditioning across reward definitions and label granularities using 5,000 naturally suboptimal human demonstrations and 24 target tasks. Within each VLA, we match demonstrations and training budgets. Additional comparisons examine pretraining data size and the roles of quality tags during fine-tuning and inference. Across three VLAs, we find that approach-aware episode conditioning improves mean success over plain behavioral cloning; conditioning and filtering favor episode and chunk labels, respectively, on average, with model-dependent reversals; and constant-tag fine-tuning improves mean success after conditioned pretraining with either tagged or untagged inference. We introduce AXIS-Bench to support these controlled comparisons. Built on crowdsourced simulation demonstrations, it provides recorded object and contact states for recomputing quality labels on shared trajectories. Replayable trajectories support workspace-aligned rendering and user-defined real-world target tasks. In our real-world experiment, simulation pretraining raises success from 9.6\% to 31.7\%. Project page: https://axis-bench.github.io/.

</details>


### [253] [EgoExplorer: A Vision-Motion-Touch Observatory for Human Loco-Manipulation In Situ](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18872)
*Yunsong Zhou,Xuekun Jiang,Haiqi Shen,Hangxu Liu,Chaojian Shi,Yujie Liu,Jingyuan Li,Haoran Yang,Jiafei Cao,Dongzhan Zhou,Dong Wang,Yao Mu,Chunhua Shen,Weinan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为EgoExplorer的100小时视觉-动作-触觉观测库，用于研究人类位姿操作。该数据集使用可穿戴系统构建，特别关注专业活动，并通过Calibrate-Complete-Constrain流程来提高数据准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数据集只能提供人类位姿操作协调的部分视图，缺乏全面覆盖。因此，作者提出了一个名为EgoExplorer的新数据集，旨在通过轻量级、便携式捕捉系统在多种环境中收集更全面的数据，以观察任务展开过程中行走、姿势和物体互动如何共同演变。

Method: 为了解决稀疏身体感知带来的挑战，开发了Calibrate-Complete-Constrain流程。首先，通过可重用校准固定参与者的几何结构和传感器安装位置，以便在动作推断前分离安装偏移与关节活动。然后，利用时间先验完成未观察到的关节运动；同时施加层次化约束促进测量一致性、时间连续性和几何一致性。这些特性单靠动作合理性是无法保证的。

Result: 与安装跟踪器作为参考相比，该方法将身体点误差相对于最强基线减少了71.9%；匹配评估也显示出在手部稳定性和接触估计方面的改进。此外，初步研究探讨了多模态生成和条件设置以及在仿真中进行类人机器人重新定向时的整体控制。

Conclusion: EgoExplorer作为一个新的大规模数据集，能够提供关于人类位姿操作的丰富信息，支持从人类演示中学习类人机器人的位姿操作。它不仅展示了在减少错误和提高估计准确性上的显著进步，还为进一步的研究提供了可能性，比如多模态生成和模拟环境中的类人机器人行为调整。

Abstract: Learning humanoid loco-manipulation from human demonstrations rests on observing how locomotion, posture, and object interaction evolve together as tasks unfold. Scaling collection across diverse environments, in turn, favors lightweight, portable capture systems. Yet, existing datasets provide only partial views of this coordination. Thus, we introduce EgoExplorer, a 100-hour vision-motion-touch observatory of human loco-manipulation built with a wearable system. With an emphasis on professional activities, its continuous recordings align egocentric video with reconstructed whole-body motion, fine-grained finger articulation, and tactile observations, accompanied by step-level descriptions. To achieve this coverage with sparse body sensing, we develop the Calibrate-Complete-Constrain pipeline. Concretely, reusable calibration fixes participant geometry and sensor attachments before motion inference to disentangle mounting offsets from articulation. A temporal prior then completes unobserved joint motion, while layered constraints promote measurement agreement, temporal continuity, and geometric consistency, properties that motion plausibility alone does not guarantee. Against tracker-fitted references, the method reduces body-point error by 71.9% relative to the strongest baseline; matched evaluations also show gains in hand stability and contact estimation. Preliminary studies explore multimodal generation and conditioning alongside humanoid retargeting with whole-body control in simulation.

</details>


### [254] [LoopWAM: Closed-Loop Generalist Policy Improvement from Deployment Rollouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18873)
*Wenxuan Guo,Yingping Liang,Zhaoguo Wang,Ziyuan Li,Angyuan Ma,Zhenyu Wu,Chengkun Wang,Bingyao Yu,Ze Chen,Borui Zhang,Xiuwei Xu,Meng Zhang,Erjin Zhou,Jiwen Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种名为LoopWAM的闭环世界-动作模型，该模型能够直接从包含成功、次优行为和失败的全质量范围回放中学习。通过部署和训练循环，该方法在多个机器人挑战任务中展示了显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅靠扩展专家演示不足以让通用机器人策略通过部署得到改进。实际操作中包含了成功、次优行为和失败：模仿每一个记录下来的动作可能会加强不良行为；而过滤掉失败则丢弃了物理交互及其后果的重要证据。因此需要一种新方法来利用这些多样化的经验进行学习。

Method: 提出的方法叫做LoopWAM，它是一个封闭式的世界-动作模型，能够直接从包含自身和其他已部署策略的操作回放中学习。这种方法采用基于来源的噪声采样统一了行为学习与条件作用下的世界建模，并通过质量导向和质量加权监督保留了对不同质量水平视频的全面监督，同时控制动作模仿的程度。

Result: 在RoboChallenge Table30-V2上，经过三轮部署与训练后，成功率从23.67%提高到了43.33%，提高了19.7个百分点，最终得分为56.80。此外，在RoboTwin 2.0上达到了平均94.5%的成功率，在RoboCasa365上没有额外体现预训练的情况下总体成功率为49.5%，在这三个基准测试的所有比较方法中获得了最佳整体结果。对于具有挑战性的衬衫折叠任务，在单任务后期训练后的两轮部署驱动改进中，成功率从13.20%提高到55.56%。

Conclusion: 这些结果表明，无论是多任务学习还是单任务后期训练条件下，LoopWAM都能够有效地从混合质量的经验中实现闭环改进。

Abstract: Scaling expert demonstrations alone does not enable generalist robot policies to improve from deployment. Real-world rollouts contain successes, suboptimal behavior, and failures: imitating every recorded action can reinforce poor behavior, while filtering out failures discards evidence of physical interactions and their consequences. We introduce \textbf{LoopWAM}, a closed-loop world-action model that learns directly from this full quality spectrum, using rollouts from both itself and other deployed policies. Source-dependent noise sampling unifies behavior learning and action-conditioned world modeling, while quality steering and quality-weighted supervision retain full video supervision across qualities and regulate action imitation. Video and action tokens interact throughout parallel denoising, allowing learned consequences to inform action generation directly. On RoboChallenge Table30-V2, three deployment-and-training rounds raise success from 23.67\% to 43.33\%, a 19.7-percentage-point gain, with a final score of 56.80. LoopWAM also achieves 94.5\% average success on RoboTwin~2.0 and 49.5\% overall success on RoboCasa365 without additional embodied pretraining, obtaining the best overall results among the compared methods across all three benchmarks. On challenging shirt folding task, two rounds of deployment-driven improvement after single-task post-training raise success from 13.20\% to 55.56\%. These results demonstrate closed-loop improvement from mixed-quality experience under both multi-task learning and single-task post-training. \href{https://loop-wam.github.io/}{\textcolor{orange}{Project Page}}

</details>


### [255] [ChunkAct: Action Chunking for Agentic Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18874)
*Bingxin Xu,Wenrui Bao,Xinxin Liu,Jiawei Zhou,Yuzhang Shang,Emilio Ferrara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为ChunkAct的方法，通过将一系列动作打包成一个块来减少大型语言模型（LLM）在机器人控制循环中的计算需求，从而提高了任务成功率同时减少了LLM的调用次数、输入和输出令牌数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法虽然能够通过大型语言模型（LLM）增强机器人执行新任务的能力，但其效率较低。每次基本操作都需要对整个交互历史进行一轮LLM推理，导致随着基本操作数量增加而成本上升。因此，研究旨在寻找一种方法减少这种冗余推理，提高执行效率。

Method: 提出了ChunkAct方法，该方法引入了动作块的概念。在线时，单次调用即可启动一系列链接的基本操作，并且这些操作可以自主执行直到遇到失败才返回控制权。离线时，ChunkAct将记忆编译成这样的块，使得测试时执行无需依赖记忆，规划器仅用于需要推理的决策上。这样，LLM的计算量就取决于决策的数量而不是基本操作的数量。

Result: 实验结果显示，在LIBERO-Pro数据集上，与当前最先进的代理机器人技术相比，ChunkAct提高了5.0个百分点的任务成功率，同时所需LLM调用次数减少了2.9倍，输入令牌数减少了4.4倍，输出令牌数减少了2.3倍。

Conclusion: 通过引入动作块的概念，ChunkAct有效提升了基于LLM的机器人系统的效率与性能，为解决效率问题提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Agentic robotics places a large language model (LLM) agent in the robot control loop, where it composes perception, motion, and vision-language-action (VLA) tools under the guidance of a memory and thereby generalizes to new tasks without retraining. This generality comes at a steep price in efficiency. Every primitive requires a round of LLM reasoning over the full interaction history, so the cost of an episode grows with the number of primitives. We find this reasoning largely redundant: once a sub-goal is chosen, its primitives follow a routine already prescribed by memory. We present ChunkAct, which introduces action chunking to agentic robotics. Online, a single call launches a chunk of chained primitives, which executes autonomously and returns control only upon failure. Offline, ChunkAct compiles the memory into these chunks, so execution is memory-free at test time and the planner is reserved for decisions that require reasoning. LLM computation therefore scales with the number of decisions rather than the number of primitives. On LIBERO-Pro, ChunkAct improves task success by 5.0 points over the state of the art in agentic robotics while requiring 2.9x fewer LLM calls, 4.4x fewer input tokens, and 2.3x fewer output tokens.

</details>
